Санскритский словарь Шридхарасены, известный также как «Муктавали-коша». Электронная транскрипция издания 1912 года; сохранены страницы и разделы источника. Текст представлен в латинской транслитерации IAST. Источник: sanskrit-kosha/kosha. Оцифровка: Dr. Dhaval Patel и Shree Ramkrishna Knowledge Foundation (SRKKF). Лицензия GNU GPL v3.0. Новая сверка со сканами не выполнялась.

# Viśvalocana-kośa

## Opening

## Opening / 1 \[unit 1\]

śrīparamātmane namaḥ \|

kavipaṇḍita-śrīśrīdharasena-viracitaḥ

viśvalocanakośaḥ \|

(muktāvalī )

maṃgalācaraṇam \|

jayati bhagavānāstāṃ dharmaḥ prasīdatu bhāratī

vahatu jagatī premodgāraṃ tarantvaśubhaṃ janāḥ \|

ayamapi mama śreyāngumphastanotu manomudaṃ

kimadhikamitastyaktāvegā bhavantu vipaścitaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## Opening / 2 \[unit 2\]

paribhāṣā \|

svarakādikramādādirnirṇīto'ntaśca kādibhiḥ \|

dvitīye'pyatra varṇe'pi niyamaḥ kādyanukramāt \|\| 2 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ

## kāntavargaḥ / fragment 3

atha kāntavargaḥ

## kāntavargaḥ / kaikam

## kāntavargaḥ / kaikam / 3 \[unit 4\]

ko brahmānilasūryāgniyamātmadyotabarhiṣu \|

kaṃ sukhe vāri śīrṣe ca kuḥ śabde nā bhuvi striyām \|\| 3 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 5\]

akaṃ duḥkhāghayoraṅko rekhāyāṃ cihnalakṣmaṇoḥ \|

nāṭakādiparicchedotsaṅgayorapi rūpake \|\| 4 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 6\]

citrayuddhe'ntike mantau sthānabhūṣaṇayorapi \|

arkaḥ sūrye'rkaparṇe'pi śakre sphaṭikatāmrayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 7\]

ekastu syāttriṣu śreṣṭhe kevaletarayorapi \|

kaṃkaḥ khage lohapṛṣṭhe kṛtānte kapaṭadvije \|\| 6 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 8\]

karkaḥ karketane vahnau śvetāśve mukure ghaṭe \|

kalko'strī pāpaviṭkiṭṭadoṣadambhabibhītake \|\| 7 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 9\]

pāpāśraye'pi kākastu vāyase pīṭhasarpiṇi \|

śirovakṣālane dhṛṣṭe mānadvīpadrumāntare \|\| 8 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 10\]

kākā syātkākajaṃghāyāṃ kākolīkākanāsayoḥ \|

kākamācīkākatuṇḍīmalapūraktikāsu ca \|\| 9 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 11\]

kākaṃ kākasamūhe syātstrīṇāṃ ca ratabandhane \|

kiṣkurvitastau haste ca prakoṣṭhe kutsite pumān \|\| 10 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 12\]

kokaścakre vṛke jyaiṣṭhyāṃ kharjūrībhekaviṣṇuṣu \|

chekastu gṛhasaṃsaktaviśvastamṛgapakṣiṇoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 13\]

nāgare triṣu vakre ca ṭaṅko'strī grāvadāraṇe \|

ṭaṅkarṇe grāvabhittau ca mānabhedā'bhidhānayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 14\]

kapitthāntarajaṅghā'sikoṣakoṣakhanitrake \|

tarkaḥ kāṅkṣāvitarkohe karmaśāstraprabhedayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 15\]

tokaṃ tvapatye putre ca tokā duhitari striyām \|

trikā kūpasya nemau syāttrikaṃ pṛṣṭhadhare traye \|\| 14 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 16\]

dvikaḥ syāccakravāke'pi nāṅke kāke'pi saṃmataḥ \|

nākuḥ puṃsi munerbhede nākurvalmīkaśailayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 16 \[unit 17\]

nākaḥ svarge'ntarikṣe ca niṣko'strī hemakarṣayoḥ \|

aṣṭādhikasvarṇaśate vakṣo'laṅkaraṇe pale \|\| 16 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 17 \[unit 18\]

hemnaḥ pale'pi dīnāre nyaṅkurjhaṣe munau mṛge \|

paṅko'strī kardame pāpe pākastu pavane śiśau \|\| 17 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 18 \[unit 19\]

pāko jarāparīpāke sthālyādau kledaniṣṭhayoḥ \|

bakaḥ kaṅke śivamallyāṃ rakṣobhedakuberayoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 19 \[unit 20\]

vaṅkastu puṃsi nadyādibhaṅgaparyāṇabhāgayoḥ \|

bhaṅgure vācyavadvaṅko valkaṃ valkalakhaṇḍayoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 20 \[unit 21\]

bhūkaśchidre'vakāśe ca bheko maṇḍūkameghayoḥ \|

muṣko'ṇḍakośe vṛnde ca muṣko mokṣakaśākhini \|\| 20 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 21 \[unit 22\]

mūkastvavāṅmato dīne raṅkaḥ kṛpaṇamandayoḥ \|

atha rākā dṛṣṭarajaḥkanyāyāṃ saridantare \|\| 21 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 22 \[unit 23\]

pūrṇendupūrṇimāyāṃ ca kacchūroge'pi dṛśyate \|

reko vireke śaṅkāyāmadhame tvabhidheyavat \|\| 22 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 23 \[unit 24\]

rokaṃ dattvā kraye randhre nāvi rokastu rociṣi \|

laṅkā rakṣaḥpure śākhākulaṭāśākinīṣvapi \|\| 23 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 24 \[unit 25\]

loko jane'pi bhuvane syādavāttu vilokane \|

śaṅkuḥ kīle śive saṅkhyāyādo'strabhidi kilviṣe \|\| 24 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 25 \[unit 26\]

śaṅkā trāse vitarke ca śalkaṃ śakalavalkayoḥ \|

cūrṇe śākastu śaktau syādvṛkṣadvīpanṛpāntare \|\| 25 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 26 \[unit 27\]

śākaṃ haritake klībe patrapuṣpaphalādike \|

śukaḥ kīre vyāsaputre rāvaṇasya ca mantriṇi \|\| 26 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 27 \[unit 28\]

śukaṃ tu granthiparṇe syācchirīṣe śoṇake'pi ca \|

śulkaṃ ghaṭṭādideye'strī jāmāturapi bandhake \|\| 27 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 28 \[unit 29\]

śūkaḥ syādanukampāyāṃ śūkaḥ śuṅge'pi puṃsyayam \|

śokaḥ syācchumasaṅghāte strīṇāṃ ca karaṇāntare \|\| 28 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvitīyam / 29 \[unit 30\]

śloko yaśasi padye syādupahāsya upātparaḥ \|

sṛko vātotpalaśare stokaḥ syāccātakālpayo. \|\| 29 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 30 \[unit 31\]

katṛtīyam \|

aṇuko nipuṇe'lpe'strī tvanīkaṃ raṇasainyayoḥ \|

anūkaṃ śīlakulayoranūkaṃ gatajanmani \|\| 30 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 31 \[unit 32\]

antikaṃ nikaṭe cullyāmantikā śātalauṣadhau \|

nāṭyoktau cāṃtikā jyeṣṭhabhaginyāṃ parikīrtitā \|\| 31 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 32 \[unit 33\]

andhikā kaitave siddhe śarvaryāmandhayoṣiti \|

abhīko nirbhayakrūrakavikāmiṣu vācyavat \|\| 32 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 33 \[unit 34\]

ambikā pārvatī pāṇḍujananījananīṣvapi \|

tintiḍīkācukrikayoramlodgārepi cā'mlikā \|\| 33 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 34 \[unit 35\]

arbhakastu mato ḍimbhe mūrkhe bhrūṇe kṛśepi ca \|

kuberasyālakā puryāmalakaścūrṇakuntale \|\| 34 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 35 \[unit 36\]

alarko dhavalārke syādyogonmattakakukkure \|

alīkaṃ tridive klībaṃ mithyāyāmāpriye triṣu \|\| 35 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 36 \[unit 37\]

aśoko vañjule māne drume'śokaṃ tu pārade \|

aśokā kaṭurohiṇyāṃ śokaśūnye tu vācyavat \|\| 36 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 37 \[unit 38\]

āḍhako mānabhede'strī tuvaryāmāḍhakī smṛtā \|

ātaṅko rogasantāpaśaṅkāsu murajadhvanau \|\| 37 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 38 \[unit 39\]

ānakaḥ paṭahe bheryāṃ mṛdaṅge dhvanadambude \|

āloko darśane'pi syādudyote baṃdibhāṣaṇe \|\| 38 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 39 \[unit 40\]

āhnikaṃ dinanirvartye bhojane nityakarmaṇi \|

ikṣvākuḥ kaṭutuṃbyāṃ strī sūryānvayanṛpe pumān \|\| 39 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 40 \[unit 41\]

udarka eṣyatkālīyaphale madanakaṇṭake \|

ulūkaḥ pecake śakre kuruyodhe'pi sammataḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 41 \[unit 42\]

uṣṇakastvāture tapte kṣiprakārinidāghayoḥ \|

uṣṭikā mṛttikābhāṇḍabhede karabhayoṣiti \|\| 41 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 42 \[unit 43\]

ūrmikā tvaṅgulīye syāttaraṅge madhupadhvanau \|

ūrmikā vastrabhaṅge'pi tathodbāhulake'pi ca \|\| 42 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 43 \[unit 44\]

aṃśukaṃ sūkṣmavasane vastramātrottarīyayoḥ \|

kañcukaḥ kavace vāṇavāre nirmokacolake \|\| 43 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 44 \[unit 45\]

harṣādāttāṅgavastre ca kañcukī tvauṣadhāntare \|

kaṭakostrī rājadhānyāṃ sānau senānitambayoḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 45 \[unit 46\]

valaye sindhulavaṇe dantidantavibhūṣaṇe \|

kaṭukaṃ kuṭurohiṇyāṃ vyoṣe'pi kaṭumātrake \|\| 45 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 46 \[unit 47\]

kaṭākustu durādharṣe duḥśīle nā vileśaye \|

godhūmacūrṇe kaṇikaḥ striyāṃ sūkṣmā'gnimanthayoḥ \|\| 46 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 47 \[unit 48\]

kaṇṭako'strī drumāṅge'tha dūṣake karṇidūṣake \|

romāñce kṣudraśatrau ca mārau mīnādikīkase \|\| 47 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 48 \[unit 49\]

kanakaṃ hemni dhattūre campake nāgakesare \|

kiṃśuke kāñcanāre ca kālīye'pi kvacinmatam \|\| 48 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 49 \[unit 50\]

karako'strī karaṅke syātkuṇḍyā cātha pumānkhage \|

kusumbhe dāḍime haste karakā tu ghanopale \|\| 49 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 50 \[unit 51\]

karaṅkaḥ sasyasantyaktanālikerā'sthimastake \|

karṇikā karṇabhūṣāyāṃ guvākādicchaṭāṃśake \|\| 50 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 51 \[unit 52\]

karihastāgrabhāge ca karamadhyāṅgulāvapi \|

nalinībījakośe ca kuṭṭinyāmapi kutracit \|\| 51 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 52 \[unit 53\]

kalaṅko'ṅke kālāyasamale doṣā'pavādayoḥ \|

kāvṛkaḥ kṛkavākau syātpītamastakakokayoḥ \|\| 52 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 53 \[unit 54\]

kāmukaḥ kāmini khyāto'śokavṛkṣā'timuktayoḥ \|

kārakaḥ kartari jñeyaḥ karmādau kārakaṃ matam \|\| 53 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 54 \[unit 55\]

kārikā vivṛtiśloke yātanāyāṃ kṛtāvapi \|

naṭastriyāṃ nāpitādiśilpe kartryāṃ ca kārikā \|\| 54 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 55 \[unit 56\]

vaṃśe nā kārmukaṃ cāpe karmaśakte tu vācyavat \|

kālikā caṇḍikāyāṃ syādyoginībhedakārṣṇyayoḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 56 \[unit 57\]

paścāddātavyamūlye ca paṭolakalatāntare \|

romālīdhūmarīmāṃsīkākīvṛścikapatrake \|\| 56 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 57 \[unit 58\]

ghanāvalāvalaṃ dhūmaprabhede navanīrade \|

kimpākastu mahākālaphale mūrkhe ca kīcakaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 58 \[unit 59\]

daityevātadhvanidhvaṃse śuṣkavaṃśe drumāntare \|

kīṭakaḥ kṛmijātau syānniṣṭhure'pi ca kīṭakaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 59 \[unit 60\]

kulakastu kulaśreṣṭhe valmīke kākatinduke \|

kulakaṃ ślokasambaddhagucchake'pi paṭolake \|\| 59 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 60 \[unit 61\]

kuliko nāgabhede syātkulaśreṣṭhe drumāntare \|

kuśikastu munau tailaśeṣe sarje kalidrume \|\| 60 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 61 \[unit 62\]

kuṣāku markaṭe bhānau bṛhadbhānau pumāṃstriṣu \|

parottāpinyapi mataṃ kūrcikā sūcikāntare \|\| 61 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 62 \[unit 63\]

tūlikā kṣīravikṛtikuñcikākuḍmaleṣu ca \|

kūpako guṇavṛkṣe syāttailapātre kukundare \|\| 62 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 63 \[unit 64\]

kūpe jalasthagrāvādau syācca turyā tu kūpikā \|

kūlakaḥ puṃsi valmīke stūpe'strī kūlakaṃ taṭe \|\| 63 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 64 \[unit 65\]

kṛṣakaḥ karṣake puṃsi phāle'pi kṛṣake pumān \|

pāradārakarakte'pi niḥsve'pi triṣu kañcukaḥ \|\| 64 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 65 \[unit 66\]

korakaḥ kuḍmale na strī kakkolakamṛṇālayoḥ \|

kośāṅkastu karīre syādikṣau kīṭāntare'pi ca \|\| 65 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 66 \[unit 67\]

kautukaṃ tvabhilāṣe'pi kusume narmaharṣayoḥ \|

paramparāsamāyāte maṅgale cātiśāyini \|\| 66 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 67 \[unit 68\]

vivāhasūtre viṣayābhogakāle samutsave \|

kauśiko guggulūlūkanakuleṣvahituṇḍike \|\| 67 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 68 \[unit 69\]

indre ca viśvāmitre ca kośajñe cātha kauśikī \|

caṇḍikāyāṃ nadībhede kramuko bhadramustake \|\| 68 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 69 \[unit 70\]

guvākapaṭṭikālodhrakūrpāsabrahmadāruṣu \|

khaṭṭikaḥ saunike'pi syānmāhiṣakṣīraphenake \|\| 69 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 70 \[unit 71\]

khanakaścittatattvajñe sandhicaure'vadārake \|

mūṣake khullakastu syātsvalpe nīce kanīyasi \|\| 70 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 71 \[unit 72\]

kholakaḥ pākavalmīkapūgakośe śirastrake \|

gaṇikā yūthikāveśyātarkārīkariṇīṣvapi \|\| 71 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 72 \[unit 73\]

agnimanthe'pi gaṇikā daivajñe gaṇakaḥ pumān \|

gaṇḍakaḥ khaḍgini khyātaḥ saṅkhyāvidyāprabhedayoḥ \|\| 72 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 73 \[unit 74\]

guhyako gopite yakṣe gṛhyakaśchekanighnayoḥ \|

gairikaṃ dhātubhede syāddhātumātre ca kāñcane \|\| 73 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 74 \[unit 75\]

goraṅkuḥ pakṣijātau ca nagnake śrutipāṭhake \|

golako maṇike jārādvidhavātanaye guḍe \|\| 74 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 75 \[unit 76\]

granthikastu karīre syāddaivajñe gugguladrume \|

mādreyepyadvayorgranthiparṇīpippalimūlayoḥ \|\| 75 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 76 \[unit 77\]

grāhako ghātivihage grahītari tu vācyavat \|

caṭakaḥ kalaviṃkaḥ syāttatputrīyoṣitoḥ striyām \|\| 76 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 77 \[unit 78\]

catuṣkī maśakaharyāṃ yaṣṭikāveśmabhedayoḥ \|

culukaḥ prasṛtau ca syācculukā bhājanāntare \|\| 77 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 78 \[unit 79\]

caṣako'strī pānapātre madhumadyaprabhedayoḥ \|

cārakaḥ pālake'śvādeḥ syātsañcārakabandhayoḥ \|\| 78 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 79 \[unit 80\]

citrakaṃ tilake klībaṃ vahnisaṃjñe tu citrakaḥ \|

eraṇḍe cālavāle ca citrakaḥ śvāpadāntare \|\| 79 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 80 \[unit 81\]

cīrako vikriyālekhe jhillikāyāṃ tu cīrikā \|

cumbakaḥ kāmuke dhūrte bahuvidyopajīvane \|\| 80 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 81 \[unit 82\]

mataḥ puṃsyeva culukaḥ prasṛte bhājanāntare \|

cullakī śiśumāre syātkuṇḍībhede kulāntare \|\| 81 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 82 \[unit 83\]

cūtako'ndhau rasāle ca kapipūrvaḥ kapītane \|

cūlikā nāṭakāṅge syātkarṇamūle ca hastinām \|\| 82 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 83 \[unit 84\]

jatukā'jinapatrāyāṃ jatukaṃ hiṅgulākṣayoḥ \|

janakaḥ snātarājarṣau janakaḥ karaṇāntare \|\| 83 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 84 \[unit 85\]

jambukaḥ pherave'pi syānnīce paścimadikpatau \|

jālakaḥ korake dambhaprabhede jālinīphale \|\| 84 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 85 \[unit 86\]

girisāre jalaukāyāṃ jālikā vidhivatstriyām \|

bhaṭānāmaśmaracitāṅgarakṣiṇyāṃ ca jālikā \|\| 85 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 86 \[unit 87\]

jāhako ghoṅkhamārjārakhajākātuṇḍikāsu ca \|

jīvako vṛkṣabhede syātprāṇake'pyahituṇḍike \|\| 86 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 87 \[unit 88\]

pītaśāle kṣapaṇake vṛddhijīvini sevake \|

jīvikāmāhurājīve jīvantyāmapi jīvikā \|\| 87 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 88 \[unit 89\]

jhillīkā jhillikā'pyeva vilepanamale smṛtaḥ \|

cīrikāyāmapi bhavedātapasya ca rociṣi \|\| 88 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 89 \[unit 90\]

ṭucchako gandhakuṭyāṃ syādvyavahārā'bhyavakāśake \|

ṭuṇṭukaḥ śoṇake'lpe ca krūrake tvabhidheyavat \|\| 89 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 90 \[unit 91\]

ḍiṇḍiko nagnake dārye strīcore tu ratātparaḥ \|

ḍimbikā jalabimbe syātkoṇake kāmukastriyām \|\| 90 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 91 \[unit 92\]

taṇḍako'strī taruskandhe samāsaprāyavācike \|

gṛhadārau pumāṃstu syātphenakhaṃjanamāyiṣu \|\| 91 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 92 \[unit 93\]

tarkakaḥ kāṅkṣiṇi khyātastarke'rke gṛdhrapakṣiṇi \|

takṣako nāgabhede syādvarddhakidrumabhedayoḥ \|\| 92 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 93 \[unit 94\]

tārako daityabhitkarṇadhārayordaśi tārakam \|

ṛkṣe kanīnikāyāṃ ca tārakaṃ tārikā'pi ca \|\| 93 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 94 \[unit 95\]

tilakaṃ drumabhede ca roge ca tilakālake \|

klībaṃ sauvarcale klomni lalāme'strī tu citrake \|\| 94 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 95 \[unit 96\]

tulakaḥ tulakāyāṃ syāttathā dadhikapakṣiṇi \|

turuṣkaḥ sihlake mlecchabhedastrīvāsayorapi \|\| 95 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 96 \[unit 97\]

tūlikā citravinyāsalekhanyāṃ tūlatalpayoḥ \|

triśaṃkurnupabhede'pi śalabhe vṛṣadaṃśake \|\| 96 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 97 \[unit 98\]

darśakastu pratīhāre darśayitṛpravīṇayoḥ \|

dārako bhedake'patye kūpake tu vipūrvakaḥ \|\| 97 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 98 \[unit 99\]

dīpako vāgalaṅkāre pradīpe dīptikārake \|

dīpyakaṃ tvajamode yādyavānīvarhicūḍayoḥ \|\| 98 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 99 \[unit 100\]

dūṣikā locanamale tūlikā ca dūṣikā \|

drāvakastu śilābhede vidagdhe ghoṣake'pi ca \|\| 99 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 100 \[unit 101\]

dhanikaḥ sādhudhānyākadhaveṣu dhanikā striyām \|

dhāvako javake rājagatikarmaṇi yogini \|\| 100 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 101 \[unit 102\]

dhenukā tu bhaveddhenau karipatnīprasūtayoḥ \|

dhenukaṃ karaṇe strīṇāṃ dhenuvṛnde'pi dhenukam \|\| 101 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 102 \[unit 103\]

nagnako vandini granthe nagne gauryāṃ tu nagnikā \|

nandako harikhaḍge'pi harṣake kulapālake \|\| 102 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 103 \[unit 104\]

narako niraye'pi syānnarako dānavāntare \|

nartakaḥ poṭagalake cāraṇe kelake naṭe \|\| 103 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 104 \[unit 105\]

nartakī lāsikāyāṃ syātkariṇyāmapi nartakī \|

nāyako netari śreṣṭhe hāramadhyamaṇāvapi \|\| 104 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 105 \[unit 106\]

nālīkaḥ piṇḍaje'pyajñe nālīkaḥ śaraśalyayoḥ \|

nālīkaṃ padmakhaṇḍe'pi nāḍīkaṃ sarasīruhe \|\| 105 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 106 \[unit 107\]

nipākaḥ pavane khede'pyasatkarmaphale'pi ca \|

nirmoko vyomni sannāhe mocane sarpakañcuke \|\| 106 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 107 \[unit 108\]

vārako'śve mahāmātye hastisaṅkhye'pi nīṭakaḥ \|

nīlikā nīlikīkṣudrarogasephālikāsu ca \|\| 107 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 108 \[unit 109\]

patākā syādvaijayantyāṃ saubhāgyeṅgadhvaje'pi ca \|

padmakaṃ padmakośe'pi karibinduṣu paṅkaje \|\| 108 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 109 \[unit 110\]

parāko vratamātre'pi parākaḥ śayake'pi ca \|

ubhau paryaṅkapalyaṅkau vṛpyāṃ paryastikhaṇḍayoḥ \|\| 109 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 110 \[unit 111\]

pārśvadvāri sapakṣe ca pakṣe pārśve ca pakṣakaḥ \|

pāṭakastu mahākiṣkau vādye'pi kaṭakāntare \|\| 110 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 111 \[unit 112\]

akṣādicālane mūladravyāpacayakūlayoḥ \|

pātukaḥ patayālau syātprapāte jalahastini \|\| 111 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 112 \[unit 113\]

pālaṃkaḥ śākabhede'pi śallakīvājipakṣiṇi \|

pāvako'gnau sadācāre bhallātakavitaṅkayoḥ \|\| 112 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 113 \[unit 114\]

citrake'pyagnimanthe'pi triṣu pācanakāriṇi \|

piṇyākaḥ śihlake hiṅgau tilakalke'pi kuṅkume \|\| 113 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 114 \[unit 115\]

pināko harakodaṇḍe śūle'strī pāṃsuvarṣaṇe \|

piṣṭako yavadhānyādicamase cakṣuṣo ruji \|\| 114 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 115 \[unit 116\]

putrakaḥ śarabhe putre dhūrte vṛkṣanagāntare \|

putrikā puttalīputryostathā yāvakatūlike \|\| 115 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 116 \[unit 117\]

pulakaḥ kṛmibhede syānmaṇidoṣe śilāntare \|

gajānnapiṇḍe romāñce galvarkaharitālayoḥ \|\| 116 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 117 \[unit 118\]

pulākastucchadhānye syātsaṃkṣepe bhaktaśikthake \|

puṣpakaṃ tu kuberasya vimāne ratnakaṅkaṇe \|\| 117 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 118 \[unit 119\]

netraroge ca kāsīse cīrikāyāṃ rasāñjane \|

mṛdaṅgāraśakaṭyā ca lohakāsye ca puṣpakam \|\| 118 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 119 \[unit 120\]

pūrṇakaḥ svarṇacūḍe syānnāsacchittyā ca pūrṇikā \|

pṛthukaścipiṭe bāle pṛdākustu sarīsṛpe \|\| 119 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 120 \[unit 121\]

pṛdākurvṛścike'pi syādvyāghracitrakayorapi \|

ulūke gajalāṅgūlamūlaprānte'pi pecakaḥ \|\| 120 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 121 \[unit 122\]

peṭako'strī pustakādermañjūṣāyāṃ kadambake \|

pratīkaḥ pratikūle triṣvekadeśavilomayoḥ \|\| 121 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 122 \[unit 123\]

pramāde'vayave cātha prasekaḥ secane cyutau \|

prāṇakaḥ sattvajātīye bolake jīvakadrume \|\| 122 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 123 \[unit 124\]

priyakastu kadambe syādalicitrakuraṅgayoḥ \|

priyaṅgau pītaśāle ca kuṅkumapriyayorapi \|\| 123 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 124 \[unit 125\]

phalakaṃ citravinyāse paṭṭikāvraṇabhedayoḥ \|

varāko vācyavacchocye'nukampye saṅgare pumān \|\| 124 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 125 \[unit 126\]

vasukaḥ śivamallyāṃ syādarkaparṇe'pi raumake \|

bahuko'rke karkaṭake dātyūhe jalakhādake \|\| 125 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 126 \[unit 127\]

vārako'śvaviśeṣe ca gatāvapi niṣedhake \|

vārddhakaṃ vṛddhasaṃghāte vṛddhatve vṛddhakarmaṇi \|\| 126 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 127 \[unit 128\]

vālakognau śiśau keśe vājivāraṇavāladhau \|

syādbālakaṃ tu hrīvere pārihāryāṃgulīyake \|\| 127 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 128 \[unit 129\]

vālikā vālukā vālā piṃcholākarṇabhūṣaṇe \|

vālukā sikatā'pi syādvālukaṃ tvelavāluke \|\| 128 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 129 \[unit 130\]

vṛścikaḥ śūkakīṭe'pi druṇe rāśyoṣadhībhidoḥ \|

bhasmakaṃ bhasmaroge syādviḍaṅgakaladhautayoḥ \|\| 129 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 130 \[unit 131\]

bhālāṅko rohite śākaprabhede kacchape hare \|

mahālakṣaṇasampūrṇapuruṣe karapatrake \|\| 130 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 131 \[unit 132\]

syādbhūtīkaṃ tu bhūnimbamālātṛṇakakattṛṇe \|

yavānyāmapi karpūre bhūtīkaṃ kaṭphale'dvayoḥ \|\| 131 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 132 \[unit 133\]

bhūmikā racanāyāṃ syānmūrttyantaraparigrahe \|

bhrāmakaḥ pherave dhūrte sūryāvartaśilāntare \|\| 132 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 133 \[unit 134\]

maṇḍūko dardure bandhaprabhede śoṇake'pyatha \|

maṇḍūkaparṇyāṃ maṇḍūkī madhuko yaṣṭikāhvaye \|\| 133 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 134 \[unit 135\]

bandipakṣiprabhede ca madhuparṇyāṃ striyāmapi \|

malliko mallikā caiva rājahaṃsāntare dvayam \|\| 134 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 135 \[unit 136\]

mallikā tṛṇaśūnye'pi mīnamṛtpātrabhedayoḥ \|

maśakaḥ kṣudrajantūnāṃ prabhede'pi gadāntare \|\| 135 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 136 \[unit 137\]

mātṛkā dhātrikāyāṃ syātkaraṇe mātari svare \|

māmakaṃ mamatāyuktaṃ mātṛbhrātari māmakaḥ \|\| 136 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 137 \[unit 138\]

mālikā puṣpamālāyāṃ mālikā saridantare \|

māliko garuḍe'pi syānmālikā kaṇṭhabhūṣaṇe \|\| 137 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 138 \[unit 139\]

mecakaḥ śyāmale barhicandre dhvānte'tha mecakam \|

vācyavatkṛṣṇavarṇe syānmocakaḥ kadalītarau \|\| 138 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 139 \[unit 140\]

tatprasūne'pi śigrau ca nirmocakavirāgiṇoḥ \|

modako na striyāṃ khādyaprabhede harṣake'nyavat \|\| 139 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 140 \[unit 141\]

yamakaṃ saṃyame śabdā'laṅkāre yamaje triṣu \|

yājako yāgaśīle syātpūjake rājakuñjare \|\| 140 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 141 \[unit 142\]

yājñiko yājake darbhe yajñakāryopajīvini \|

yutakaṃ yautake yugme calanāgre'pi saṃśaye \|\| 141 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 142 \[unit 143\]

vastrāntare vadhūvastrāñcale yukte tu vācyavat \|

yūthikā tu matā yūthyāmamlānakusume kvacit \|\| 142 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 143 \[unit 144\]

raktako'mlānabandhūkaraktavastre tu rāgiṇi \|

rajako dhāvake puṃsi kīre'pi rajakaḥ pumān \|\| 143 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 144 \[unit 145\]

rasikā tu rasālāyāṃ kāñcīrasanayorapi \|

lekhākedārayo rājasarṣape'pi ca rājikā \|\| 144 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 145 \[unit 146\]

rātrakastatra yo veśyāgṛhe gamitavatsaraḥ \|

rātrakaṃ pañcarātre'tha rucako mātuluṅgake \|\| 145 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 146 \[unit 147\]

rucakaṃ mātuladravye dante sauvarcalasraji \|

utkaṭe cāśvabhūṣāyāṃ viḍaṅge kaṇṭhabhūṣaṇe \|\| 146 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 147 \[unit 148\]

bījapūre'pi dīnāre rocanādevavṛkṣayoḥ \|

ruṇḍikā raṇabhūrdvārapiṇḍikādūtikārthikā \|\| 147 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 148 \[unit 149\]

jamadagnipriyāyāṃ ca hareṇvāmapi reṇukā \|

lampākaḥ puṃsi deśe syāllampako lampaṭe triṣu \|\| 148 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 149 \[unit 150\]

lāsako lasake lāsyakārake'pi mayūrake \|

lūnakaḥ syātpaśau bhinne locako netratārake \|\| 149 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 150 \[unit 151\]

māṃsapiṇḍe ca piṇḍe ca yoṣidbhālavibhūṣaṇe \|

kajjale nīlacole ca maurvyāṃ bhrūślathacarmaṇi \|\| 150 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 151 \[unit 152\]

kadalyāṃ karṇapūre ca nirbuddhinṛṣu locakaḥ \|

vañcakastu khale dhūrte gṛhababhrau ca pherave \|\| 151 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 152 \[unit 153\]

bandhakaḥ syādvinimaye vasāsatyostu bandhakī \|

badhūkaṃ bandhujīve syādbandhūkaḥ pītaśālake \|\| 152 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 153 \[unit 154\]

vartako vartikā pakṣiprabhede'śvakhure pumān \|

varṇako bandini kavau cāraṇe'strī tu varṇake \|\| 153 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 154 \[unit 155\]

vilepanādau citrādau lipimasyāṃ ca candane \|

varṇikā kaṭhinīmasyorlekhanyāmapi varṇikā \|\| 154 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 155 \[unit 156\]

valmīko vāmalūre syānmunirogaviśeṣayoḥ \|

vārṣikaṃ trāyamāṇāyāṃ varṣākālabhavenyavat \|\| 155 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 156 \[unit 157\]

govāhake tu vāhīko vāhīko pṛkkadeśaje \|

vāhlīko vāhliko'śve ca deśabhede haye pumān \|\| 156 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 157 \[unit 158\]

vāhlīkaṃ vāhlikaṃ dve ca na dvayorhiṅguvīrayoḥ \|

vitarkaḥ saṃśaye'pyūhe vicāre ca kvacinmataḥ \|\| 157 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 158 \[unit 159\]

vipākaḥ pariṇāme'pi khede svāduni durgatau \|

vivekastu vicāre syājjaladroṇyāṃ rahasyapi \|\| 158 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 159 \[unit 160\]

vṛṣāṅkaḥ śaṅkare sādhau bhallātakamahokṣayoḥ \|

vaijikaṃ śigutaile'pi hetau sadyo'ṅkure'pi ca \|\| 159 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 160 \[unit 161\]

vyalīkaṃ vipriyākāryavailakṣyeṣvapi pīḍane \|

klībameva vyalīkastu nāgare vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 160 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 161 \[unit 162\]

śaṃkhakaṃ valaye kaṃbau śiroroge ca śaṅkhakaḥ \|

śambuko gajakumbhānte śambūkaḥ śuktikāntare \|\| 161 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 162 \[unit 163\]

daityabhede'pi śambūkaḥ śambūkā jalaśuktiṣu \|

śalākā tu śare śalye cātapatrāṇapañjare \|\| 162 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 163 \[unit 164\]

tarkukāṣṭyāṃ ca madane śārikāśvāvidorapi \|

śallakī śvāviddrumayoḥ śāyakaḥ śarakhaṅgayoḥ \|\| 163 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 164 \[unit 165\]

śārkakaḥ śarkarāpiṇḍe dugdhaphene ca śārkakaḥ \|

śiśukaḥ śiśumāre ca śiśau paścādulūpini \|\| 164 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 165 \[unit 166\]

śītakaḥ susthite śītakāle'nāgatadarśini \|

śūkakaḥ prāvaṭaprahau śūkakaḥ pārade'pi ca \|\| 165 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 166 \[unit 167\]

kṛtamālastu śamyākaḥ śamyākastarkudhṛṣṭayoḥ \|

samparkaḥ syānnidhuvane saṃsarge sparśane'pi ca \|\| 166 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 167 \[unit 168\]

sarakaḥ syādavicchinnapānthapaṅktau śare pumān \|

astriyāṃ sīdhupāne ca sīdhupātre ca sīdhuni \|\| 167 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 168 \[unit 169\]

sasyako nālikerādisāre khaṅge maṇāvapi \|

sūcakaḥ khalakākautusūcīṣu śuni bodhake \|\| 168 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 169 \[unit 170\]

sūtakaṃ janmani klībaṃ sūtakaḥ pārade'striyām \|

sṛdākurdāvakuliśā'nileṣu pratisūryake \|\| 169 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 170 \[unit 171\]

secakaḥ sektari bhave triṣu puṃsi tu vāri \|

sevako vallakīmāntavakrakāṣṭhe'nujīvini \|\| 170 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 171 \[unit 172\]

syamīkā nīlikāyāṃ syātsyamīko nākuvṛkṣayoḥ \|

svastiko maṅgaladravye catuṣkagṛhabhedayoḥ \|\| 171 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 172 \[unit 173\]

svastikaḥ piṣṭhakasyā'pi prabhede ratatālike \|

sthāsako gandhavajrāyāṃ jalāderapi budbude \|\| 172 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 173 \[unit 174\]

senāyāṃ samavete'pi senārakṣe'pi sainikaḥ \|

hārakastu śaṭhe caure gadyavijñānabhedayoḥ \|\| 173 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 174 \[unit 175\]

huḍuko vādyabhede syāddātyūhe ca madotkaṭe \|

heruko buddhabhede'pi mahākālagaṇe tathā \|\| 174 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katṛtīyam / 175 \[unit 176\]

kṣārako jālake pakṣimatsyādipiṭake'pi ca \|

kṣurakaḥ kokilākṣe syādgokṣure tilakadrume \|\| 175 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 176 \[unit 177\]

kacaturtham \|

astrī tvaṅgārakodgāre puṃsi bhaume kuraṇṭake \|

aṅgārikā tvikṣukāṇḍe tathā kiṃśukakorake \|\| 176 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 177 \[unit 178\]

pumāna(li)lamako bheke madhuke'mbujake khare \|

pike'pyalipakastu syātpikāliratahiṇḍake \|\| 177 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 178 \[unit 179\]

athā'śmantakamuddhāne mallikācchadane'pi ca \|

ākālikaṃ kṣaṇadhvaṃsyanyakālakṛtasambhave \|\| 178 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 179 \[unit 180\]

ākalpakastamomohagranthāvutkalikāmudoḥ \|

viśeṣyākhanikastu syāccoramūṣakadaṃṣṭriṣu \|\| 179 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 180 \[unit 181\]

ākṣepakastu pavanavyādhau vyādhe ca nindake \|

bhavedutkalikā helotkaṇṭhāsalilabīciṣu \|\| 180 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 181 \[unit 182\]

eḍamūkastriṣu khyātaḥ śaṭhe vākśrutivarjite \|

punarnavākāravellaparṇāseṣu kaṭhillakaḥ \|\| 181 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 182 \[unit 183\]

kaniṣṭhā'ṅgulikānetratārayostu kanīnikā \|

kapardakastu bhūteśe jaṭājūṭe varāṭake \|\| 182 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 183 \[unit 184\]

karkoṭakaḥ kādraveyaprabhede śrīphale'pi ca \|

kalabiṅko bhavedgrāmacaṭake'pi kaliṅgake \|\| 183 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 184 \[unit 185\]

kākarūka ulūke'śve strījite'pi digambare \|

dambhe'pi kākarūkastu triṣu bhīrudaridrayoḥ \|\| 184 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 185 \[unit 186\]

kārpaṭiko'nyamarmajñe chātre syātkāladeśini \|

kuravakaḥ puṃsi śoṇajhiṇṭikā'mlānabhedayoḥ \|\| 185 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 186 \[unit 187\]

kṛkavākustāmracūḍe kṛkalāse ca kekini \|

kośātakaḥ kace jyotsnīpaṭolyāṃ ghoṣake'striyām \|\| 186 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 187 \[unit 188\]

kaukuṭṭiko dāmbhike syādadūrapreritekṣaṇe \|

kauleyako bhavedindre mahākāmikulīnayoḥ \|\| 187 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 188 \[unit 189\]

grāmaṇībhaṇḍinārācopadhāne tu kharālikaḥ \|

bhavedguṇanikā'bhyāse śūnyāṅke pāṭhaniścaye \|\| 188 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 189 \[unit 190\]

nṛtyāntare tvapyatho gokaṇṭako gokṣure pumān \|

gavāṃ gamanasambhūtaśuṣkasthapuṭake'pi ca \|\| 189 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 190 \[unit 191\]

gokuṇikaḥ kekare syātpaṅkasthagavyupakṣake \|

gomedakaḥ pītamaṇau kākole patrake'pi ca \|\| 190 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 191 \[unit 192\]

smṛtā ghargharikā kṣudraghaṇṭikāvādyabhedayoḥ \|

mṛṣṭadhānye saridbhede tathā vāditradaṇḍake \|\| 191 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 192 \[unit 193\]

cāṃḍālikauṣadhībhede gaurīkiṃdirayorapi \|

jaṭāruko jalānūke nāgayaṣṭipaṭīrayoḥ \|\| 192 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 193 \[unit 194\]

jaṭārukastathā śākhāhariṇe'pi tulādhare \|

jarjarīkastriṣu bhavedbahucchidre jarāture \|\| 193 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 194 \[unit 195\]

jīvantikā tu jīvākhyaśākabandāguḍūciṣu \|

jaivātṛkaḥ śaśinyāyuṣmati divyauṣadhe kṛśe \|\| 194 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 195 \[unit 196\]

tartarīkaḥ pārage syāttartarīkaṃ bahitrake \|

tiktaśākastu varuṇe khadire patrasuṃdare \|\| 195 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 196 \[unit 197\]

trivarṇakaṃ trikaṭuke triphalāyāṃ ca gokṣure \|

dandaśūkastu yakṣe syāddandaśūko bhujaṅgame \|\| 196 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 197 \[unit 198\]

dalāḍhakaḥ svayaṃjātatile cāmpeyakundayoḥ \|

śirīṣapṛśnikāvātyākhātakeṣu mahattare \|\| 197 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 198 \[unit 199\]

gairike karikarṇe ca phene'gnikaṇasaṃhatau \|

droṇe ca kāryakūṭe ca kvaciddṛṣṭo dalāḍhakaḥ \|\| 198 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 199 \[unit 200\]

dāserakastu karabhe dāsīputre'pi dhīvare \|

niyāmakaḥ potavāhe karṇadhāre niyantari \|\| 199 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 200 \[unit 201\]

nirgranthikastu kṣapaṇe niṣphale'pyaparicchade \|

niścārako'nile svaire purīṣasya kṣaye'pi ca \|\| 200 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 201 \[unit 202\]

pañcālikā bhavedvambaputrikāgītabhedayoḥ \|

piṇḍītakastu tagare madanādrau phaṇijjake \|\| 201 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 202 \[unit 203\]

stanavṛnte pippalakaḥ klībaṃ sīvanasūtrake \|

puṇḍarīko'gnidīptāṅge vyāghrabhedekṣubhedayoḥ \|\| 202 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 203 \[unit 204\]

puṇḍarīkaṃ sitacchatre sitāmbhoje'pi bheṣaje \|

puṣkalako gandhamṛge kīlake kṣapaṇe'pi ca \|\| 203 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 204 \[unit 205\]

klībaṃ pūrṇānakaṃ pūrṇapātre paṭahapātrayoḥ \|

potakyāṃ vicalatpotādhāne potīnakaṃ matan \|\| 204 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 205 \[unit 206\]

prakīrṇakaṃ granthabhede praśaste cāmare haye \|

pravartakaḥ śarāghāte barhe puṣpabhujaṅgayoḥ \|\| 205 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 206 \[unit 207\]

pharpharīkaścapeṭe syātpharpharīkaṃ tu mārdave \|

bakerukā bakībhede vātāvarjitapallave \|\| 206 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 207 \[unit 208\]

kapardarajjurājīvabījakośe varāṭakaḥ \|

varaṇḍakastu mātaṅgavedyāṃ yauvanakaṇṭake \|\| 207 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 208 \[unit 209\]

tathā saṃvarttule barttarūkastu saridantare \|

jalāvaṭe kākanīḍe daṇḍavāsinyapīṣyate \|\| 208 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 209 \[unit 210\]

barbarīko mahākāle keśavinyāsaśākayoḥ \|

balāhako vārivāhe nāgadaityāntare girau \|\| 209 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 210 \[unit 211\]

vāṇijiko vaṇijyaṃke mṛgāṅke kāminīrate \|

aurve'nurāgabāhye ca mato vāṇijyakaḥ pumān \|\| 210 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 211 \[unit 212\]

vṛndārakaḥ sure śreṣṭhe manojñe yūthaghātini \|

atho bṛhatikā kaṇṭakārīvastrāntaroruṣu \|\| 211 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 212 \[unit 213\]

bhaṭṭārakaḥ sure puṃsi kṣmāpāle ca tapodhane \|

bhayānakastu śārdūle saiṃhikeye vibhīṣaṇe \|\| 212 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 213 \[unit 214\]

bhāryāṭiko bhavedbhāryānirjite hariṇāntare \|

bhramarako'bhre madhupe ca jāle cūrṇakuntale \|\| 213 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 214 \[unit 215\]

maṇḍodakaṃ citrarāge bhavedālimpane'pi ca \|

mataṃ maṇḍalakaṃ bimbe kuṣṭhabhede ca darpaṇe \|\| 214 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 215 \[unit 216\]

mayūrako'pyapāmārge tutthake tu mayūrakam \|

madanadrau marubakaḥ puṣpabhede phaṇijjake \|\| 215 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 216 \[unit 217\]

māṇavako hārabhede bāle kupuruṣe vaṭau \|

mṛṣṭeruko vadānye svāngṛṣṭāśinyatithidviṣi \|\| 216 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 217 \[unit 218\]

ratarddhikaṃ sukhasnāne'pyaṣṭamaṅgalake dine \|

rādharaṅkuntu nā sīre śrīkare jaladopale \|\| 217 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 218 \[unit 219\]

latālikastu lāṭāmre vajramustau ca puṃsyayam \|

lālāṭikaḥ syātkaraṇāntare'pyāliṅganāntare \|\| 218 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 219 \[unit 220\]

kāryākṣame prabhorbhāvadarśinyapi tu vācyavat \|

triṣu lekhīlako lekhahāre yaśca vilekhayet \|\| 219 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 220 \[unit 221\]

svahastaparahastena lekhe lekhīlakaḥ sa ca \|

vitunnakaṃ tu dhānyāke mataṃ jhāṭāmale'pi ca \|\| 220 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 221 \[unit 222\]

vidūṣakaścāṭuvaṭau paranindāvidhāyini \|

vināyako jine buddhe tārkṣye herambavighnayoḥ \|\| 221 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 222 \[unit 223\]

gurau vimānakaṃ tu syānmāne śūnye'bhidheyavat \|

vimānakaṃ devayāne saptabhūmagṛhe striyām \|\| 222 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 223 \[unit 224\]

viśeṣako'strī tilake viśeṣāvāhake drume \|

vaitāliko bodhakare kheṭṭatāle ca kīrtitaḥ \|\| 223 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 224 \[unit 225\]

vaidehako vāṇijake śūdrādveśyāsute'pi ca \|

vaināśikastu kṣaṇike paratantrorṇanābhayoḥ \|\| 224 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 225 \[unit 226\]

śatānīko munerbhede vṛddhe śālāvṛkaḥ śuni \|

śṛgāle vānare vā'tha bile cāndre śilāṭakaḥ \|\| 225 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 226 \[unit 227\]

śṛṅgāṭako bhavedvārikaṇṭake ca catuṣpathe \|

saṅghāṭikā yuge nāsāṭkuṭṭinīlajakaṇṭake \|\| 226 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 227 \[unit 228\]

santānikā dadhikṣīrasāre markaṭajālake \|

saṃdaṃśikā tu mukuṭīlohayantrabhedrayoḥ \|\| 227 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 228 \[unit 229\]

syātsupratīka īśānadiggaje divyavigrahe \|

śṛgālikā śivāyāṃ syāttrāsādapi palāyane \|\| 228 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 229 \[unit 230\]

klībe saikatikaṃ mātṛyātrāmaṅgalasūtrayoḥ \|

triṣu saṃnyastasaṃdehajīvikṣapaṇikeṣvidam \|\| 229 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 230 \[unit 231\]

pumān saikatiko gandhakuṭyāṃ sindhośca saikate \|

svabhāryāṃ parahastasthāṃ yo na sādhayituṃ kṣamaḥ \|\| 230 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 231 \[unit 232\]

tatra saṃnyāsamātreṇa kṣudhā ca kṛtabhojane \|

somavalkaḥ pumān śvetakhadire kaṭphale'pi ca \|\| 231 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacaturtham / 232 \[unit 233\]

saugandhikaṃ tu kahlāre padmarāge ca kattṛṇe \|

gandhake gāndhike puṃsi triṣu saugandhikaṃ kramāt \|\| 232 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / 233 \[unit 234\]

kapañcamam \|

aneḍamūkaḥ kitave triṣu vākśrutivarjite \|

syādācchuritakaṃ hāsanakhāghātaviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 233 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / 234 \[unit 235\]

mātopakārikā rājamandire piṣṭakāntare \|

upakartryāmapīyaṃ syādatha syātkaṭakhādakaḥ \|\| 234 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / 235 \[unit 236\]

khādake kācakalaśe valipuṣṭaśṛgālayoḥ \|

syātkakṣāvekṣako dhīre śuddhāntodyānapālayoḥ \|\| 235 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / 236 \[unit 237\]

api ṣiḍge kavau raṅgājīvini dvārapālake \|

syātkṛmīkaṇṭakaṃ citrāviḍaṅgodumbareṣvapi \|\| 236 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / 237 \[unit 238\]

gojāgarikamityāhurmaṅgale kandukārake \|

kaṇṭhīviśeṣakhadyotavidyutsu cilimīlikā \|\| 237 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / 238 \[unit 239\]

śṛṅgāṭake jalagṛhe pṛśnyāṃ ca jalakaṇṭakaḥ \|

janatāpika hallīśakākolīmatsyayormataḥ \|\| 238 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / 239 \[unit 240\]

bhavejjalakaraṅkastu nālikeraphale'mbude \|

kaṃje jalalatāyāṃ ca bhavennavaphalikā punaḥ \|\| 239 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / 240 \[unit 241\]

navye bhavye prasūnādau navajātarajaḥstriyām \|

nāgavārikamicchanti hastipe rājahastini \|\| 240 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / 241 \[unit 242\]

tārkṣye gaṇanvarāje'pi citramekhalake kvacit \|

śodhanyāmiṃgude lokayātrāyāṃ vyavahārikā \|\| 241 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / 242 \[unit 243\]

syādvrīhirājikaḥ puṃsi kāminīcīnadhānyayoḥ \|

śataparvikā ca dūrvāyāṃ vacāyāṃ śataparvikā \|\| 242 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / 243 \[unit 244\]

śītacampakaśabdo'yamātarpaṇakadīpayoḥ \|

suvasantakamicchanti vāsantyāṃ madanotsave \|\| 243 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcamam / fragment 245

syāddhemapuṣpikā yūthyāṃ campake hemapuṣpakaḥ \|

## kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṣṭham

## kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṣṭham / 244 \[unit 246\]

kaṣaṣṭham \|

grāmamadgurikā śṛṅgyāṃ grāmayuddhe ca dṛśyate \|\| 244 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṣṭham / 245 \[unit 247\]

bhavenmadanaśalākā tu sāryāṃ kāmodayauṣadhau \|

bhavenmātulaje dhūrtaphale mātulaputrakaḥ \|\| 245 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṣṭham / 246 \[unit 248\]

lūtāmarkaṭikā putryāṃ navamālaplavaṅgayoḥ \|

ślokacchāyāhare caure bhavedvarṇaviloḍakaḥ \|\| 246 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṣṭham / 247 \[unit 249\]

sindūratilako nāge sindūratilakastriyām \|

caturmāsopavāsī yaḥ sa syātsnānacikitsakaḥ \|\| 247 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṣṭham / 248 \[unit 250\]

tapasvipuṣpayoścaiva mataṃ snānacikitsakam \|\| 248 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṣṭham / fragment 251

iti kavipaṇḍitaśrīśrīdharasenaviracite muktāvalītyaparābhidhāne viśvalocane svarakādyādikāntavargaḥ \|

## khāntavargaḥ

## khāntavargaḥ / fragment 252

atha khāntavargaḥ \|

## khāntavargaḥ / khaikam

## khāntavargaḥ / khaikam / 1 \[unit 253\]

khaikam \|

khamākāśe divi sukhe buddhau saṃvedane pure \|

śūnyavadindriyakṣetre kuśāhalaphale kvacit \|\| 1 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvitīyam

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 254\]

khadvitīyam \|

ukhā'niruddhabhāryāyāmukhā sthālyāmapi smṛtā \|

nakhastu karaje śuktau gandhadravye nakhī nakham \|\| 2 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 255\]

nyuṅkhaḥ samyagmanojñe ca sāmnaḥ ṣaṭpraṇaveṣvapi \|

preṅkhāḥ paryaṭane nṛtye dolāyāṃ vājināṃ gatau \|\| 3 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 256\]

ciṅkhā gatyantare nṛtye śūkaśimbyāṃ ca dṛśyate \|

mukhaṃ vakre niḥsaraṇe'pyupāyā''rambhayorapi \|\| 4 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 257\]

lekho lekhye sure lekhā rekhārājīlipiṣvapi \|

śaṅkhaḥ kambulalāṭāsthinakhīnidhiṣu na striyām \|\| 5 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 258\]

śākhā syātpallave vedavibhāge'pyantike bhuje \|

śākhā pakṣāntare cātha śikhā śākhāgraraśmiṣu \|\| 6 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 259\]

śikhā śikhāyāṃ cūḍāyāṃ cūḍāyāṃ ca śikhaṇḍinaḥ \|

jvālāyāṃ lāṅgalikyāṃ ca sakhā mitrasahāyayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvitīyam / fragment 260

sukhaṃ śarmaṇyapi svarge sukhā puryāṃ pracetasaḥ \|

## khāntavargaḥ / khatṛtīyam

## khāntavargaḥ / khatṛtīyam / 8 \[unit 261\]

khatṛtīyam \|

gomukhaṃ kuṭilāgāre vādyabhāṇḍopalepayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khatṛtīyam / 9 \[unit 262\]

triśikho rakṣaso bhede klībaṃ bhūṣātriśūlayoḥ \|

durmukho mukhare nāgarāje śākhāmṛgāśvayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khatṛtīyam / 10 \[unit 263\]

pramukhaḥ prathame śreṣṭhe mayūkho jvālarukkare \|

skande tarke viśākhaḥ syādviśākhā bhe kaṭhillake \|\| 10 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khatṛtīyam / 11 \[unit 264\]

viśikhastomare bāṇe viśikhā khanirathyayoḥ \|

nalikāyāṃ ca viśikhā vaiśākho rāghamanyayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khatṛtīyam / 12 \[unit 265\]

sumukhastārkṣyatanaye paṇḍite bhujagāntare \|\| 12 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khacaturtham

## khāntavargaḥ / khacaturtham / 13 \[unit 266\]

khacaturtham \|

bhavedagnimukho deve dvije pāvakasambhave \|

bhallātake tvagnimukhī kvacidagnimukho'pi ca \|\| 13 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khacaturtham / 14 \[unit 267\]

lāṅgalikyāṃ tvagniśikhā kuṅkume'gniśikhaṃ smṛtam \|

indulekhā śaśikalā'mṛtāsomalatāsvapi \|\| 14 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khacaturtham / 15 \[unit 268\]

puṃsi pañcanakhaḥ kūrme gaje gaudhādiṣu kvacit \|

baddhaśikhoccaṭāyāṃ syādbāle baddhaśikhastriṣu \|\| 15 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khacaturtham / 16 \[unit 269\]

mahāśaṅkho narāsthi syānnidhisaṅkhyāprabhedayoḥ \|

śilīmukho bhavedbhṛṅge mārgaṇe ca śilīmukhaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khapañcamam

## khāntavargaḥ / khapañcamam / 17 \[unit 270\]

khapañcamam \|

syānmalinamukhaḥ prete golāṅgūle khale'nale \|

mataḥ śītamayūkho'pi śaśikarpūrayorayam \|\| 17 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khapañcamam / 18 \[unit 271\]

sarvatomukhamākhyātaṃ klībamākāśapāthasoḥ \|

kṣetrajñavidhirudreṣu sa pumān sarvatomukhaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khapañcamam / fragment 272

iti viśvalocane khāntavargaḥ \|

## gāntavargaḥ

## gāntavargaḥ / fragment 273

atha gāntavargaḥ \|

## gāntavargaḥ / gaikam

## gāntavargaḥ / gaikam / 1 \[unit 274\]

gaikam \|

go gandharve gaṇeśe'rke gaṃ gīte śāstragātari \|

gauḥ pumān vṛṣabhe kharge khaṇḍavajrahimāṃśuṣu \|\| 1 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gaikam / 2 \[unit 275\]

strī gavi bhūmidignetravāgbāṇasalile striyaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 276\]

gadvitīyam \|

agaḥ syānnagavadvṛkṣe śailai bhānubhujaṅgayoḥ \|

aṅgā nīvṛtprabhede syuraṅgo deśeṅgamantike \|\| 3 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 277\]

gātropāyāpradhāneṣu pratīkepyaṅgavatyapi \|

aṅga saṃbodhane'saṅkhyaṃ punararthapramodayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 278\]

iṅgaḥ syādiṅgite jñāne jaṅgamādbhutayorapi \|

khago vihaṅge viśikhe khagaḥ sūrye sure grahe \|\| 5 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 279\]

khaḍgaḥ khaḍgini nistriṃśe khaḍgiśṛṅge jināntare \|

gāṅgaḥ ṣaḍānane bhīṣme gaṅgābhūte tu vācyavat \|\| 6 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 280\]

caṅgastu śobhane dakṣe ṭaṅgo'strī syātkhanitrake \|

tathaivāstrāntare'pyastrī jaṅghāyāṃ khaḍgabhedake \|\| 7 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 281\]

unnate vācyavattuṅgastuṅgaḥ punnāgaśailayoḥ \|

barvarāniśayostuṅgī tyāgo dāne ca varjane \|\| 8 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 282\]

durgaḥ syāddurgame durgā caṇḍīnīlikayormatā \|

nagastu parvate vṛkṣe nago bhānubhujaṅgayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 283\]

nāgaḥ pannagapunnāganāgakesaradantiṣu \|

nāgadantakajīmūtamustake krūrakarmaṇi \|\| 10 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 284\]

dehā'nilāntare śreṣṭhe śreṣṭha evottarasthitaḥ \|

nāgaṃ tu sīsake raṅge strībandhakaraṇāntare \|\| 11 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 285\]

piṅgaḥ piśaṅge piṅgī tu śamyāṃ piṅgaṃ tu bālake \|

piṅgā rāmaṭhanīlyāṃ syādumārocanayorapi \|\| 12 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 286\]

pūgastu nikurambe syātpūgaḥ kramukapādape \|

phalgurmalapvāmākhyātā niṣphale phalgu vācyavat \|\| 13 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 287\]

bhagaṃ tu jñānayonīcchāyaśomāhātmyamuktiṣu \|

aiśvaryavīryavairāgyadharmaśrīratnabhānuṣu \|\| 14 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 288\]

bhaṅgastaraṅgarugbhede dambhe jayaviparyaye \|

bhaṅgā śaṇākhyasasye syādbhāgo rūpārdhakāṃśayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 16 \[unit 289\]

ekadeśe ca bhāgye ca vipūrvastu vibhañjane \|

bhṛguḥ śukre prapāte ca jamadagnau pinākini \|\| 16 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 17 \[unit 290\]

bhṛṅgaḥ puṣpatvape khiḍge tathā dhūmyāṭapakṣiṇi \|

napuṃsakaṃ tu bhṛṅgaṃ syātkeśarājabhṛgūṭayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 18 \[unit 291\]

puṃsi bhogaḥ sukhe'pi syādaheśca phaṇakāyayoḥ \|

nirveśe gaṇikādīnāṃ bhojane pālane dhane \|\| 18 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 19 \[unit 292\]

mārgo'grahāyaṇe vāṭe kastūrīviṣayorapi \|

mṛgaḥ kuraṅge'pi paśau mṛgayāmṛgaśīrṣayoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 20 \[unit 293\]

hastibhede'pi yāñcāyāṃ mṛgī syānnāyikāntare \|

praśastarathasārāṅgaṃ yugme'pi syātkṛtādiṣu \|\| 20 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 21 \[unit 294\]

yugaṃ hastacatuṣke'pi vṛddhināmauṣadhe'pi ca \|

yogaḥ saṃnāhasaṃdhānasaṅgatidhyānakarmaṇi \|\| 21 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 22 \[unit 295\]

viṣkambhādiṣu sūtre ca dravye viśvastaghātini \|

care cāpūrvalābhe'pi bheṣajopāyayuktiṣu \|\| 22 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 23 \[unit 296\]

rāgo'nurāgamātsarye kleśādau lohitādiṣu \|

gāndhārādau nṛpe nāge rogaḥ kuṣṭhauṣadhe gade \|\| 23 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 24 \[unit 297\]

laṅgaḥ khiḍge'pi saṅge'pi liṅgaṃ cihnā'numānayoḥ \|

mehane śivabhede ca sāṅkhyoktaprakṛtāvapi \|\| 24 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 25 \[unit 298\]

vaṅgo deśāntare bhaṇṭātakīkārpāsayoḥ pumān \|

vaṅgaṃ raṅge ca nāge ca vaṅgā puṃbhūmni nīvṛti \|\| 25 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 26 \[unit 299\]

vargo'dhyāye ca vṛnde ca vargaḥ pañcākṣarībhidi \|

valgurnā nakule chāge manojñe valgu vācyavat \|\| 26 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 27 \[unit 300\]

vego jave pravāhe ca mahākālaphale'pi ca \|

vyaṅgastu puṃsi maṇḍūke hīnāṅge vyaṅgamanyavat \|\| 27 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 28 \[unit 301\]

klībaṃ śarāsane śārṅgaṃ śārṅgaṃ viṣṇuśarāsane \|

śṛṅgaṃ viṣāṇe śikhare prabhutvotkarṣasānuṣu \|\| 28 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 29 \[unit 302\]

cihnaṃ krīḍāmbuyantre ca śṛṅgaḥ syākūrcaśīrṣake \|

śṛṅgī viṣāyāmṛṣabhe mīnasvargaviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvitīyam / 30 \[unit 303\]

sargaḥ svabhāvanirmokṣaniścayotsāhasṛṣṭiṣu \|

mohe'dhyāye ca śuṅgī tu nyagrodhaplakṣapītane \|\| 30 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 31 \[unit 304\]

gatṛtīyam \|

anaṅgo manmathe'naṅgamākāśamanasormatam \|

aṅgahīne'pyanaṅgaḥ syādaṅgabhūtaviparyaye \|\| 31 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 32 \[unit 305\]

apāṅgastvaṅgavikale netrānte tilake pumān \|

ayogo vidhure kūṭe viśleṣe kaṭhinodyame \|\| 32 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 33 \[unit 306\]

ābhogo vāruṇacchatre yatnapūrṇatvayorapi \|

āyogo gandhamālyādivyasane'pi ca ḍhaukane \|\| 33 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 34 \[unit 307\]

vyāpārarodhayoścā''tha āśugo bāṇavātayoḥ \|

utsargo varjane tyāge sāmānye nyāyadānayoḥ \|\| 34 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 35 \[unit 308\]

udvega udbāhulake pumānudvejane'pi ca \|

bhavedudgamane cāyamudvegaṃ kramukīphale \|\| 35 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 36 \[unit 309\]

kaliṅgaḥ pūtikaraje dhūmyāṭe viṣayāntare \|

nīvṛdbhede kaliṅgastu triṣu dagdhavidagdhayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 37 \[unit 310\]

kaliṅgaṃ kauṭajaphale kaliṅgā yoṣiti striyām \|

kaliṅgo bhūmikūṣmāṇḍe mataṅgajabhujaṅgayoḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 38 \[unit 311\]

kāliṅgī rājakarkaṭyāṃ kāliṅgastriṣu tadbhave \|

cakrāṅgī kaṭurohiṇyāṃ cakrāṅgaścakrapakṣiṇi \|\| 38 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 39 \[unit 312\]

jihmago bhujage puṃsi mandage triṣu jihmagaḥ \|

taḍāgaḥ sarasi khyātastaḍāgo yantrakūṭake \|\| 39 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 40 \[unit 313\]

tātaguḥ kṣudratāte syājjane pitṛhite'pi ca \|

turagī tvaśvagandhāyāṃ turago hayacittayoḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 41 \[unit 314\]

trivargo dharmakāmārthasaṃhatau ca kaṭutrike \|

triphalāyāṃ sattvarajastamasāmapi saṃhatau \|\| 41 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 42 \[unit 315\]

vṛddhisthānakṣayaikoktau dhārāṅgastvasitīrthayoḥ \|

naraṅgaṃ tu varaṇḍe ca vṛttikīlakaśephasoḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 43 \[unit 316\]

nāgaraṅge'pi nāraṅgo nāraṅgo yamaje'pi ca \|

viṭe jantau ca nāraṅgo nāraṅgaṃ pippalīrase \|\| 43 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 44 \[unit 317\]

niṣaṅgo bāṇadhau saṅge nisargaḥ śīlasargayoḥ \|

nīlaṅguḥ kṛmikīṭe syād bhaṃbharālyāmuśīrake \|\| 44 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 45 \[unit 318\]

pataṅgaḥ śalabhe sūrye khage śālyantare'pi ca \|

rase pataṅge patrāṅgaṃ raktacandanabhūrjayoḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 46 \[unit 319\]

padmake cātha sarpe'pi padmakāṣṭhe'pi pannagaḥ \|

parāgaḥ puṣparajasi snānīyādau rajasyapi \|\| 46 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 47 \[unit 320\]

vikhyātāvuparāge'pi candane parvatāntare \|

punnāgaḥ puruṣaśreṣṭhe vṛkṣabhede sitotpale \|\| 47 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 48 \[unit 321\]

jātīphale'pi punnāgaḥ pāṇḍunāge ca dṛśyate \|

prayāgastīrthabhede syādyajñe vāhe viḍaujasi \|\| 48 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 49 \[unit 322\]

prayogaḥ kārmaṇe puṃsi prayuktau ca nidarśane \|

priyaṅguḥ phalinīkaṅgūrājikāpippalīṣviyam \|\| 49 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 50 \[unit 323\]

plavago vānare bheke tīkṣṇadīdhitisārathau \|

bhujaṅgo bhujage ṣiḍge mātaṅgaḥ śvapace gaje \|\| 50 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 51 \[unit 324\]

mṛdaṅgaḥ paṭahe ghoṣe raktāṅgā jīvikauṣadhau \|

raktāṅgo maṅgale klībaṃ dhīrakāmpilyavidrume \|\| 51 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 52 \[unit 325\]

rathāṅgamadvayoścakre rathāṅgaścakrapakṣiṇi \|

varāṅgaṃ mastake yonau guḍatvaci gaje striyām \|\| 52 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 53 \[unit 326\]

vātigastu daśāpāke vārtākīdhātuvādinoḥ \|

viḍaṅgo'strī kṛmighne syād viḍaṅgo nāgare'nyavat \|\| 53 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 54 \[unit 327\]

vihagastu vihaṅge syādagrage vihagastriṣu \|

visargastu bhave dāne tyāge ca malanirgame \|\| 54 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 55 \[unit 328\]

visarjanīye muktau ca bhāsvataścāyanāntare \|

rate bhoge ca sambhogaḥ sambhogo jinaśāsane \|\| 55 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / 56 \[unit 329\]

sarvagaṃ salile klībaṃ sarvagaḥ śaṅkare vibhau \|

sāraṅgo mṛgamātaṅgacātakeṣu khagāntare \|\| 56 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatṛtīyam / fragment 330

bhṛṅge triṣu tu kirmīre hemāṅgastārkṣyavedhasoḥ \|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacaturtham

## gāntavargaḥ / gacaturtham / 57 \[unit 331\]

gacaturtham \|

anuṣaṅgastu nā''rabdhe kāruṇye'pi kvacinmataḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacaturtham / 58 \[unit 332\]

tyāge mokṣe'pavargaḥ syātsāphalye kṛtakṛtyataḥ \|

abhiṣaṅgastu saṃsargaśapathākrośagañjane \|\| 58 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacaturtham / 59 \[unit 333\]

īhāmṛgo vṛke jantau prabhede caṃpakasya ca \|

athoparāgaḥ svarbhānugrastayoḥ puṣpavantayoḥ \|\| 59 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacaturtham / 60 \[unit 334\]

durnayagrahakallole parīvāpe tu puṃsyayam \|

upasargaḥ smṛto rogabhede copaplavepi ca \|\| 60 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacaturtham / 61 \[unit 335\]

kaṭabhaṅgastu śasyānāṃ nakhacchede nṛpātyaye \|

chatrabhaṅgastu vaidhavye'svātantryanṛpanāśayoḥ \|\| 61 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacaturtham / 62 \[unit 336\]

dīrghādhvagastu karabhe lekhahāre tu vācyavat \|

mallanāgo'bhramātaṅge vātsyāyanamunāvapi \|\| 62 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacaturtham / 63 \[unit 337\]

rājaśṛṅgastu kanakadaṇḍamudgarayoḥ pumān \|

samāyogastu saṃyoge samavāye prayojane \|\| 63 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacaturtham / 64 \[unit 338\]

samprayogastu surate kārmaṇepyanvaye'pi ca \|\| 64 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gapañcamam

## gāntavargaḥ / gapañcamam / 65 \[unit 339\]

gapañcamam \|

kathāprasaṅgo vātūle viṣavaidye ca vācyavat \|

nāḍītaraṅgaḥ kākole hiṃḍake ratahiṇḍake \|\| 65 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gapañcamam / fragment 340

iti viśvalocane gāntavargaḥ \|

## ghāntavargaḥ

## ghāntavargaḥ / fragment 341

atha ghāntavargaḥ \|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghaikam

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghaikam / fragment 342

ghaikam \|

gho ghaṇṭāyāṃ ca ghā ghāte kiṅkiṇyāṃ strī dhvanau tu ghaḥ \|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvitīyam

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 343\]

ghadvitīyam \|

pāpe'rttau vyasane cā'ghaṃ syādargho'rcanamūlyayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 344\]

aṅghriḥ syājjānucaraṇe mūle cāpi mahīruhām \|

udgho hastapuṭe dehapavane pāvake pumān \|\| 2 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 345\]

oghaḥ paramparāyāṃ syāddrutanṛtyopadeśayoḥ \|

oghaḥ pāthaḥpravāhe ca samūhe ca pumānayam \|\| 3 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 346\]

maghā daśamanakṣatre maghā syādrutajāntare \|

vārivāhe'pi meghaḥ syānmeghaḥ syānmustake'pi ca \|\| 4 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 347\]

moghastu niṣphale dīne moghā pāṭalipādape \|

laghurmanojñanissārāgurulaghuṣu vācyavat \|\| 5 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 348\]

pṛkkāyāṃ strī laghu klībaṃ kṛṣṇāguruṇi satvare \|

ślāghā tu syātpraśaṃsāyāṃ paricaryā'bhilāṣayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatṛtīyam

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatṛtīyam / 7 \[unit 349\]

ghatṛtīyam \|

amoghaḥ saphale'moghā khyātā pathyāviḍaṅgayoḥ \|

ullāgho nīruje dakṣe śucau harṣayute triṣu \|\| 7 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatṛtīyam / 8 \[unit 350\]

kācighaḥ kāñcane puṃsi mūṣake svacchamaṇḍape \|

nidāgha uṣṇakāle syāttāpe'pi khedavāriṇi \|\| 8 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatṛtīyam / 9 \[unit 351\]

parigho mudgare yogabhede svakulaghātayoḥ \|

palighaḥ kācakalaśe ghaṭaprākāragopure \|\| 9 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatṛtīyam / 10 \[unit 352\]

pratighastu bhavetkrodhe pratighāte'pyatha triṣu \|

mahārghaḥ syānmahāmūlyā'narghayorlāvake pumān \|

sarvaugho guruvegārthasarvasannahanārthayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatṛtīyam / fragment 353

iti viśvalocane ghāntavargaḥ \|

## ṅāntavargaḥ

## ṅāntavargaḥ / fragment 354

atha ṅāntavargaḥ \|

## ṅāntavargaḥ / ṅaikam

## ṅāntavargaḥ / ṅaikam / fragment 355

ṅaikam \|

bhairave viṣaye ṅaḥ syāt \|\|

iti viśvalocane ṅāntavargaḥ \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ

## cāntavargaḥ / fragment 356

atha cāntavargaḥ \|

## cāntavargaḥ / caikam

## cāntavargaḥ / caikam / fragment 357

caikam \|

castu taskaracandrayoḥ \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 358\]

cadvitīyam \|

arccā pūjāpratimayorucco mahati connate \|

kacaḥ keśe'pi hrībere kaco gīṣpatinandane \|\| 1 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 359\]

kacaḥ śuṣkavraṇe bandhe kariṇyāṃ tu kacā striyām \|

kācastu syānmaṇau śikye netraroge mṛdantare \|\| 2 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 360\]

kāñcī tu mekhalādāmni nīvṛdantaraguñjayoḥ \|

kūrcamastrī bhruvormadhye śothaśmaśruvikatthane \|\| 3 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 361\]

krauñcastu pakṣibhede syānnagadvīpaprabhedayoḥ \|

cañco nālādinirmāṇe cañcā tu tṛṇa pūruṣe \|\| 4 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 362\]

cañcuḥ pañcāṅgule troṭyāṃ gonāḍīcakaliñcayoḥ \|

carcā tu sthāsake tarke carcikācintayostale \|\| 5 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 363\]

tvak striyāṃ valkale'pi syāccarmamātre guḍatvaci \|

nīcastu pāmare nimne vāmane'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 6 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 364\]

nyag nimne pāmare kārtsnye picuḥ syātpuṃsi tūlake \|

kṛṣṇe daityāntare karṣe bhairavasyānanāntare \|\| 7 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 365\]

prāk prācye vācyavat kāle digdeśe tvavyayaṃ matam \|

mocaḥ saubhāñjane puṃsi mocā śālmalirambhayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 366\]

ruciricchā rucā ruktā śobhābhiṣvaṅgayorapi \|

ruk śobhāyāṃ ca kiraṇe striyāmapi manorathe \|\| 9 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 367\]

vacaḥ śuke vacā tūgragandhāsārikayoḥ striyām \|

vāgbhāratīgirorvīcirdvayoḥ svalpataraṅgayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 368\]

avakāśe sukhe cātha śacīndrāṇī śatāvarī \|

śuciḥ puṃsyupadhāśuddhamantriṇyāṣāḍhavarhiṣoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 369\]

śṛṅgāragrīṣmayoḥ śvetamadhyānupahate triṣu \|

sūcī karādyabhinaye vedhanīśikhayorapi \|\| 12 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 370\]

sūcī sīmantinīnāṃ ca kathitā karaṇāntare \|\| 13 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catṛtīyam

## cāntavargaḥ / catṛtīyam / 14 \[unit 371\]

catṛtīyam \|

avīcirnarake ghūrmivirahe ghūrmivarjite \|

bhavedudak triṣūdīcye digdeśakālato'vyayam \|\| 14 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catṛtīyam / 15 \[unit 372\]

kaṇīciḥ puṣpitalatāguñjayoḥ śakaṭe'pi ca \|

kavaco vāravāṇe syātpaṭahe gardabhāṇḍake \|\| 15 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catṛtīyam / 16 \[unit 373\]

krakacaḥ karapatre'pi granthilākhyamahīruhe \|

namucirmadane daitye nārāco jalahastini \|\| 16 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catṛtīyam / 17 \[unit 374\]

lohabāṇe'pi nārāco nārācī syāttulāntare \|

pratyak pratīcye digdeśakāle tu matamavyayam \|\| 17 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catṛtīyam / 18 \[unit 375\]

syātprapañcastu vistāre sañcaye ca pratāraṇe \|

marīcirnādyayordīptau munau nā kṛpaṇe'pi ca \|\| 18 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catṛtīyam / 19 \[unit 376\]

mārīco yājakadvije kakkole rākṣasāntare \|

marīco devatābhede praphulle vikacastriṣu \|\| 19 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catṛtīyam / 20 \[unit 377\]

keśaśūnye ca hrīke tu puṃsi ketugrahe'pi ca \|

vipañcī vallakīkelyoḥ saṅkocaṃ kuṅkame matam \|\| 20 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catṛtīyam / 21 \[unit 378\]

saṅkoco matsyabhede'pi saṅkoco bandhane'pi ca \|

satyavatsatyayoḥ samyak samyak saṅgatahṛdyayoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cacaturtham

## cāntavargaḥ / cacaturtham / 22 \[unit 379\]

cacaturtham \|

kākaciñcī tulāvīje vārikrimidilīrayoḥ \|

jalasūcirjalaukāyāṃ śṛṅgāṭe śiśumārake \|\| 22 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cacaturtham / 23 \[unit 380\]

kaṅkatroṭau jhaṣe cātha core vahnau malimlucaḥ \|

amāvāsyādvayaṃ yatra so'pi māso malimlucaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cacaturtham / 24 \[unit 381\]

capaṃcamam

capaṃcamam \|

ratanārīca-śabdo'yaṃ kukkure rativallame \|

parīrambhe samudbhūtaśītkāre ca varastriyāḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cacaturtham / fragment 382

iti viśvalocane cāntavarga \|

## chāntavargaḥ

## chāntavargaḥ / fragment 383

atha chāntavargaḥ \|

## chāntavargaḥ / chaikam

## chāntavargaḥ / chaikam / fragment 384

chaikam \|

chaśchedakārkayośchā ca cchidi chaṃ lācchanā'cchayoḥ \|

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvitīyam

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 385\]

chadvitīyam \|

acchāvyayamābhimukhye acchasphaṭikayoḥ pumān \|

acchaḥ svacche'nyaliṅgaḥ syātkacchaḥ śailādisīmani \|\| 1 \|\|

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 386\]

naukāṅge tunnake'nūpe paridhānāñcalāntare \|

kacchā tu cīrikāyāṃ syād vārāhyāmapi dṛśyate \|\| 2 \|\|

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 387\]

gucchaḥ stabake hārabhede gucchaḥ stambakalāpayoḥ \|

syātpicchamastriyāṃ pucche picchā śālmaliveṣṭake \|\| 3 \|\|

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 388\]

paṅktau pūgacchaṭākośemaṇḍeṣvaśvapadāmaye \|

vijjule'pyatha pucchaḥ syātpicchapaścātpradeśayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvitīyam / fragment 389

mleccho'pabhāṣaṇe jātibhede pāparate'pi ca \|

## chāntavargaḥ / chacaturtham

## chāntavargaḥ / chacaturtham / 5 \[unit 390\]

chacaturtham \|

atha puṃsi mahākacchaḥ sarinnāthapracetasi \|\| 5 \|\|

## chāntavargaḥ / chacaturtham / fragment 391

iti viśvalocane chāntavargaḥ \|

## jāntavargaḥ

## jāntavargaḥ / fragment 392

atha jāntavargaḥ \|

## jāntavargaḥ / jaikam

## jāntavargaḥ / jaikam / 1 \[unit 393\]

jaikam \|

jaḥ syājjavini jodbhūtau jayane jiḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|

jūrākāśe sarasvatyāṃ piśācyāṃ javine triṣu \|\| 1 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 394\]

jadvitīyam \|

ajaḥ kṛṣṇe smarahare vidhau chāge raghoḥ sute \|

abjo dhanvantarau candre nicule klībamambuje \|\| 2 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 395\]

astrī kambunyathā''jiḥ syātsaṅgrāme'pi samakṣitau \|

utsāhe kārttikepyūrjastūrjā vīrye bale dvayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 396\]

kañjaḥ keśe viriñce'pi kuñjaṃ pīyūṣapadmayoḥ \|

kujastu narake'ṅgāre drume kuñjaṃ tu na striyām \|\| 4 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 397\]

hanau vatse nikuñje'pi kubjo nyubje drumāntare \|

striyāṃ tu kharjūḥ kharjūravṛkṣe kaṇḍūtikīṭayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 398\]

khanau surāgṛhe gañjā bhāṇḍāgāre tu na striyām \|

gañjane puṃsi khajā tu manthe darvīprahastayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 399\]

guñjā tu kākaciñcyāṃ syātpaṭahe ca kaladhvanau \|

dvijo vipre'ṇḍaje dante bhārṅgīreṇukayordvijā \|\| 7 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 400\]

dhvajo'strī liṅgakhaṭvāṅgapatākācihnaśauṇḍike \|

nijastriṣu svake nitye nyubjo darbhasruci smṛtaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 401\]

nyubjaṃ tu karmaraṅge syāt kubjādhomukhayosriṣu \|

piñjo vadhe bale piñjaṃ piñjā tūlaharidrayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 402\]

vyākule vācyavatpiñjaḥ prajā santānalokayoḥ \|

bhujo bhujā ca bāhau syāt pāṇimātre'pi tāvubhau \|\| 10 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 403\]

marjustu rajake puṃsi marjūḥ śuddhāvapi striyām \|

rajjurveṇyāṃ guṇe'pi syād rājiḥ strī paṅktirekhayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 404\]

rujā roge'pi bhaṅge'pi lañjaḥ syātpaṭṭakacchayoḥ \|

lājāḥ syurbhṛṣṭadhānyeṣu lājaḥ syādārdrataṇḍule \|\| 12 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 405\]

uśīre lājamuddiṣṭaṃ vājaḥ pakṣe syade'pi ca \|

munibhede khane vājaṃ tvājye yajñānnapāthasoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 406\]

vījaṃ hetāvupādānepyaṅkure'pi ca retasi \|

vījamalpe'pi tattve'pi vyājaḥ sādhyā'padeśayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 407\]

sarjūrvaṇiji puṃsi syātsarjūḥ syādvidyuti striyām \|

sannaddhe saṃbhṛte sajjaḥ sañjaḥ śambhuviriñcayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvitīyam / fragment 408

svajaḥ svede svajaṃ rakte'patye ca svajamanyavat \|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 16 \[unit 409\]

jatṛtīyam \|

aṅgajaḥ keśakandarpe pade putre gade svaje \|\| 16 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 17 \[unit 410\]

aṅgajaṃ rudhire'tha syādaṇḍajaḥ pakṣimīnayoḥ \|

kṛkalāse bhujaṅge ca kastūryāmaṇḍajā'pi ca \|\| 17 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 18 \[unit 411\]

ambujo nicule puṃsi klībaṃ tu sarasīruhe \|

kambojo deśamātaṅgaśaṃkhabhedeṣu deśitaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 19 \[unit 412\]

karajastu karañje syādapi vyāghranakhe nakhe \|

kāmbojaḥ somavalke syācchaṅkhapunnāgavājiṣu \|\| 19 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 20 \[unit 413\]

māṣaparṇīhiṅguparṇyoḥ kāmbojī tadbhave triṣu \|

kārujaḥ śilpināṃ citre svayañjātatile'pi ca \|\| 20 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 21 \[unit 414\]

valmīke gairike phaine kalabhe nāgakeśare \|

kuṭajaḥ śākhināmbhede syāddroṇe kumbhasambhave \|\| 21 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 22 \[unit 415\]

girijā śailatanayāmātuliṅgyorudāhṛtā \|

girijaṃ tvabhrake lauhe śilājatusugandhayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 23 \[unit 416\]

jalajaṃ paṅkaje śaṅkhe nīrajaṃ padmakuṣṭhayoḥ \|

parañjastailayantrāsipheneṣu churikāphale \|\| 23 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 24 \[unit 417\]

vaṇik puṃsyeva vāṇijyajīvake karaṇāntare \|

vāṇijye tu vaṇik strītve valajā valgayoṣiti \|\| 24 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 25 \[unit 418\]

kṣitau tu valajaṃ tu syātkṣetrasasyādigopure \|

syādbhūmijā tu jānakyāṃ bhūmijo narake kuje \|\| 25 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 26 \[unit 419\]

vanajā mudgaparṇyāṃ syād vanajo gajamustayoḥ \|

vanajaṃ paṅkaje klībaṃ vācyavadvanasambhave \|\| 26 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 27 \[unit 420\]

bāhujaḥ kṣatriye khyātaḥ svayañjātatile śuke \|

sahajastu nisarge syātsahajāte'nyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatṛtīyam / 28 \[unit 421\]

sāmajaḥ sāmasambhūte vācyaliṅgaḥ pumān gaje \|

himajā pārvatīśacyormaināke himajaḥ pumān \|\| 28 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacaturtham

## jāntavargaḥ / jacaturtham / 29 \[unit 422\]

jacaturtham \|

ahibhug vinatāputre meghanādānulāsini \|

kāśmīrajā cā'tiviṣākuṣṭhakuṅkumapuṣkare \|\| 29 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacaturtham / 30 \[unit 423\]

graharājaḥ śaśinyarke'nuje śūdre jaghanyajaḥ \|

dvijarājo niśānāthe vinatātmajaśeṣayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacaturtham / 31 \[unit 424\]

dharmmarāḍyamarājau dvau yame buddhe yudhiṣṭhire \|

bharadvājo gurusute vyāghrāṭābhikhyapakṣiṇi \|\| 31 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacaturtham / 32 \[unit 425\]

bhāradvājo munau cogre striyā kārppāsikāntare \|

bhṛṅgarājastu madhupe mārkkave vihagāntare \|\| 32 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacaturtham / 33 \[unit 426\]

yakṣarāṭ tryaṃbakasakhe mallānāṃ raṅgacatvare \|

rājarājastu dhanade sārvabhamamṛgāṅkayoḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacaturtham / 34 \[unit 427\]

kṣīrābdhijaḥ śaśadhare śriyā kṣīrābdhijā striyām \|

kṣīrābdhijaṃ tu sāmudralavaṇe mauktike'pi ca \|\| 34 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / japañcamam

## jāntavargaḥ / japañcamam / 35 \[unit 428\]

japañcamam \|

ṛṣabhadhvajaśabdo'sau śaṅkarepyarhadantare \|

agastau ca harītakyāṃ laṅghane munibheṣajam \|\| 35 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / japañcamam / fragment 429

iti viśvalocane jāntavarga \|

## atha jhāntavargaḥ \| / jhaikam

## atha jhāntavargaḥ \| / jhaikam / fragment 430

jhaikam \|

jhakārastvāravāyau syānnaṣṭe'pi kvacidiṣyate \|

## atha jhāntavargaḥ \| / jhadvitīyam

## atha jhāntavargaḥ \| / jhadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 431\]

jhadvitīyam \|

jhañjhā dhvaniviśeṣe syājjhañjhāṇujalarṣaṇe \|\| 1 \|\|

## atha jhāntavargaḥ \| / jhadvitīyam / fragment 432

iti viśvalocane jhāntavargaḥ \|

## ñāntavargaḥ

## ñāntavargaḥ / fragment 433

atha ñāntavargaḥ \|

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñaikam

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñaikam / 1 \[unit 434\]

ñaikam \|

ñakārastu kvacitkhyāto gāyane ghargharadhvanau \|

jñaḥ paṇḍite budhe vedhasyajño mūḍhe jaḍe triṣu \|\| 1 \|\|

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñadvitīyam

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 435\]

ñadvitīyam \|

prajñā tu buddhau prājñastu paṇḍite vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|

prajñuśca prajñaśca tathā khyātaḥ pragatajānuke \|\| 2 \|\|

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 436\]

sañjñā nāmani gāyatryāṃ cetanāraviyoṣitoḥ \|

arthasya sūcanāyāṃ ca hastamastakalocanaiḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñatṛtīyam

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñatṛtīyam / 4 \[unit 437\]

ñatṛtīyam \|

kṛtajñaḥ sārameyepi vācyavatkṛtavedini \|

striyāmīkṣaṇikāyāṃ syāddaivajño gaṇake pumān \|\| 4 \|\|

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñatṛtīyam / fragment 438

sarvajñaḥ sugate śambhau kṣetrajño nāgarātmanoḥ \|

iti viśvalocane ñāntavargaḥ \|

## ṭāntavargaḥ

## ṭāntavargaḥ / fragment 439

atha ṭāntavargaḥ \|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 1 \[unit 440\]

ṭaikam \|

ṭā pṛthivyāṃ dhvanau ṭaḥ syātkaraṅke ṭaṃ napuṃsakam \|\| 1 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 2 \[unit 441\]

aṭṭaṃ guhāntare kṣaume śuṣke cātyalpabhaktayoḥ \|

iṣṭo nā yāgasaṃskārayogayoḥ kratukarmmaṇi \|\| 2 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 3 \[unit 442\]

klībaṃ triṣu priyatame pūjyepyāśaṃsitepi ca \|

iṣṭiryāgārccanecchāsu saṃgrahaślokasūryayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 4 \[unit 443\]

kaṭuḥ puṃsi rase klībaṃ kaṭu kāryepi dūṣaṇe \|

priyaṅgurājikā'śokarohiṇīkaṭukāsu ca \|\| 4 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 5 \[unit 444\]

striyāṃ kaṭu triṣvapriye nā sugandhau matsare'pi ca \|

kaṭaḥ śroṇau śavetyalpe kiliñjagajagaṇḍayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 6 \[unit 445\]

śmaśāne'pi kriyākāre'pyadbhutepi kaṭā'vyayam \|

kaṭī syātkaṭimāgadhyoḥ kaṣṭaṃ gahanakṛcchrayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 7 \[unit 446\]

kuṭo ghaṭe śilākuṭṭe kuṭī veśmani tu dvayoḥ \|

kuṭī tu syātpayodāsyāṃ surāyāṃ citragucchake \|\| 7 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 8 \[unit 447\]

kūṭo'strī rāśipūrdvāradambhamāyā'nṛteṣvapi \|

tucche'driśṛṅge sīrāṅge yantrāyoghananiścale \|\| 8 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 9 \[unit 448\]

kṛṣṭirvṛdhe nā karṣe'strī koṭiḥ saṃṅkhyāntarāgrayoḥ \|

atyutkarṣaprakarṣāśrikārmukāgreṣu ca striyām \|\| 9 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 10 \[unit 449\]

ṣṭaṃ tu rodane rāve kṛṣṭiḥ syātkṛśasevayoḥ \|

khaṭo'ndhakūpe ṭaṅke ca khaṭaḥ śleṣmacapeṭayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 11 \[unit 450\]

khāṭiḥ striyāṃ śavarathe khāṭirekagrahe kiṇe \|

kheṭastu nindite grāmabhede'pi vasunandake \|\| 11 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 12 \[unit 451\]

gṛṣṭirekaprasavagovarāhakrāntayoḥ striyām \|

viṣṇukrāntauṣadhau ghṛṣṭirghoṇṭā vadarapūgayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 13 \[unit 452\]

caṭuścāṭau piciṇḍe ca vratināmāsane caṭuḥ \|

cāṭaścāṭe ca dhūrte ca mūlamāṃsikayorjaṭā \|\| 13 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 14 \[unit 453\]

jhāṭo nikuñje kāntāre vraṇasaṃmārjane vane \|

truṭistvapacaye leśe sūkṣmailāyāṃ ca saṃśaye \|\| 14 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 15 \[unit 454\]

kālamāne'pyatha troṭiḥ strī cañcumīnakaṭphale \|

tvaṣṭā varddhakigīrvāṇaśilpinostigmadhāmani \|\| 15 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 16 \[unit 455\]

diṣṭirmudi parīmāṇe diṣṭaḥ kālopadiṣṭayoḥ \|

diṣṭaṃ bhāgyetha dṛṣṭiḥ syānnetradarśanabuddhiṣu \|\| 16 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 17 \[unit 456\]

dhaṭaḥ śuddhitulāyāṃ syād dhaṭī khaṇḍe ca vāsasaḥ \|

naṭī haṭṭavilāsinyāṃ naṭaḥ śailūṣaśoṇayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 18 \[unit 457\]

paṭaḥ śobhanacele syātpuraskārapiyālayoḥ \|

paṭurvāgmini nīroge tīkṣṇe dakṣe sphuṭe triṣu \|\| 18 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 19 \[unit 458\]

paṭuḥ puṃsi paṭole strī chatrāyāṃ lavaṇe paṭu \|

paṭṭaḥ peṣaṇapāṣāṇe phalake'pi catuṣpathe \|\| 19 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 20 \[unit 459\]

vraṇādibandharājādiśāsanāsanabhedayoḥ \|

paṭṭī bhālavibhūṣāyāṃ paṭṭī lākṣāprasādane \|\| 20 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 21 \[unit 460\]

paṭṭiḥ paṭavibhede syād valgulau kumbhikādrume \|

puṣṭiḥ syātpoṣaṇe vṛddhau phaṭā tu phaṇadambhayoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 22 \[unit 461\]

taṭe'śramakṛte phāṇṭaṃ vaṭastu syādguṇe triṣu \|

vaṭo varāṭanyagrodhe varttikāyāṃ vaṭī matā \|\| 22 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 23 \[unit 462\]

vīre pāmarabhede nā bhaṭaḥ strī pragame bhaṭiḥ \|

bhṛṣṭistu bharjane śūnyavāṭikāyāmapi striyām \|\| 23 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 24 \[unit 463\]

muṣṭirbaddhakare puṃsi striyāmapi tathā pale \|

mliṣṭaṃ syādvācyavanmlāne mliṣṭamavyaktabhāṣaṇe \|\| 24 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 25 \[unit 464\]

yaṣṭiḥ śastrāntare hāre hāre hārātpare'pi ca \|

bhārṅgyā ca madhuparṇyāṃ ca dhvajadaṇḍe tu puṃsyayam \|\| 25 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 26 \[unit 465\]

riṣṭaṃ kṣeme mṛtyucihne vināśe nā tu sāyake \|

riṣṭastu riṣṭivatkhaḍge samṛddhau puṃstriyoḥ kramāt \|\| 26 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 27 \[unit 466\]

laṭo doṣepi vāgdoṣe lāṭastvaṃśukadeśayoḥ \|

vāṭastu vartmani vṛtau vāṭī syādgṛhaniṣkuṭe \|\| 27 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 28 \[unit 467\]

viṭastu khiḍgalavaṇaśaṅkhākhukhadirādriṣu \|

viṣṭiḥ karmmakare bhadre vetane preṣaṇe striyām \|\| 28 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 29 \[unit 468\]

vyuṣṭaṃ dine prabhāte ca phale paryuṣite triṣu \|

vyuṣṭiḥ samṛddhau vihitā niyamādiphale'pi ca \|\| 29 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 30 \[unit 469\]

saṭā jaṭākesarayoḥ sṛṣṭirnirmmāṇasarggayoḥ \|

sṛṣṭaṃ tu nirmite tyakte triṣu prājye'pi niścite \|\| 30 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / 31 \[unit 470\]

sphuṭo vyakte praphulle ca vyāptavattriṣvapi triṣu \|

sphuṭiḥ sphuṭikakarkaṭyāṃ pādasphoṭe'pi ca sphuṭiḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / fragment 471

hṛṣṭo romāñcite jātaharṣe prahasite smṛte \|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 32 \[unit 472\]

ṭatṛtīyam \|

avaṭaḥ kuhake kūpe khile gartte'pyathā'vaṭuḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 33 \[unit 473\]

gartte kūpe ca ghāṭāyāmargaṭoṃtargale gale \|

ariṣṭaḥ phenile nimbe laśune kākakaṅkayoḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 34 \[unit 474\]

ariṣṭaṃ sūtikāgāre takre cihne śubhe'śubhe \|

utkaṭastīvre matte ca karaṭo nindyajīvite \|\| 34 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 35 \[unit 475\]

ekādaśāhaśrāddhe ca kākavādyāntare'pi ca \|

kubrāhmaṇe kusumbhe'pi durddāntagajagaṇḍayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 36 \[unit 476\]

karkaṭaḥ karaṇe strīṇāṃ rāśibhedakulīrayoḥ \|

khage tu karkaṭī tu syādbāluṅkyāṃ śālmalīphale \|\| 36 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 37 \[unit 477\]

karddaṭaḥ paṅkapaṅkārakarahāṭeṣu kīrtitaḥ \|

karyaṭastriṣu kāryajñe pumāñjatuni karyaṭaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 38 \[unit 478\]

kīkaṭo magadhe'pi syānniḥsve cāśve mitaṃpace \|

kukkuṭastāmracūḍe syātkukkume vāgnikukkuṭe \|\| 38 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 39 \[unit 479\]

niṣādaśūdrayoścaiva tanaye triṣu kukkuṭaḥ \|

rasonabhedoccaṭayostālamadhyepi kukkuṭī \|\| 39 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 40 \[unit 480\]

kukkuṭī tāmracūḍākhyayoṣinmithyopacaryayoḥ \|

kuruṇṭī śālabhaṃjyāṃ syātkuruṇṭo jhiṇṭikāntare \|\| 40 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 41 \[unit 481\]

kṛpīṭamudare nīre keśaṭastu kaṇe harau \|

cakrāṭaḥ puṃsi dīnāre dhūrtte jāṅgulike triṣu \|\| 41 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 42 \[unit 482\]

carppaṭaḥ sphāravipule capeṭe caiva carppaṭaḥ \|

carpaṭaḥ parpaṭe'pi syātpiṣṭabhede tu carppaṭī \|\| 42 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 43 \[unit 483\]

cipiṭaścipiṭe puṃsi piccite vistṛte'nyavat \|

ciraṇṭī tu suvāsinyāṃ syādvitīyavayaḥstriyām \|\| 43 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 44 \[unit 484\]

vārttākupuṣpe jakuṭaṃ jakuṭo malaye śuni \|

trikūṭaṃ sindhulavaṇe trikūṭaḥ syātsuvelake \|\| 44 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 45 \[unit 485\]

tripuṭastu bhavettīre pumānapi satīnake \|

tripuṭā mallikābhede sūkṣmailātrivṛtorapi \|\| 45 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 46 \[unit 486\]

tryaṅgaṭaṃ śikyabhede syāddhautāñjanyāmapīṣyate \|

drohāṭastu mato gāthāprabhede mṛgalubdhake \|\| 46 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 47 \[unit 487\]

baiḍālavratike'pi syāddhārāṭaścātakāśvayoḥ \|

nirdaṭo nirdaye nyāyavādarakte ca niṣphale \|\| 47 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 48 \[unit 488\]

niṣkuṭastu gṛhodyāne svātkedārakapāṭayoḥ \|

parpaṭastu dvayoḥ piṣṭavikṛtau bheṣajāntare \|\| 48 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 49 \[unit 489\]

parīṣṭiḥ paricaryāyāṃ prākāśye'pi gaveṣaṇe \|

parkaṭī plakṣaṇakalyoḥ pātraṭaḥ karpare kṛśe \|\| 49 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 50 \[unit 490\]

piccaṭo netrarogepi piccaṭaṃ sīsake trapau \|

varaṭāyāṃ sayodhanyāṃ gandholyāṃ varaṭo dvayoḥ \|\| 50 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 51 \[unit 491\]

barbaṭī gaṇikāyāṃ syād vrīhibhede'pi barbaṭī \|

barbaṭo makare pote bālake'pi ca barbaṭaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 52 \[unit 492\]

striyāṃ puñjepi bhākūṭā bhākūṭo mīnaśailayoḥ \|

bhāryāṭaḥ paṭahājīve lobhālvakīsamarpake(?) \|\| 52 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 53 \[unit 493\]

bhāvāṭaḥ kāmuke sādhuniveśe bhāvake naṭe \|

markaṭaḥ kapilūtākīkaraṇeṣvatha markaṭī \|\| 53 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 54 \[unit 494\]

vānarīśūkaśimbyāṃ syād cakrāṅgyāṃ karajāntare \|

bīje tu rājakarkaṭyāṃ prācīnāmalakasya ca \|\| 54 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 55 \[unit 495\]

gavedhukāphale caiva markaṭaḥ puṃsi dṛśyate \|

mocāṭaścandane kṛṣṇajīrarambhāsthnyupaskare \|\| 55 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 56 \[unit 496\]

moraṭaṃ tvikṣumūle syādaṅkoṭakusume'pi ca \|

saptarātrātparakṣīre mūrtikāyāṃ tu moraṭā \|\| 56 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 57 \[unit 497\]

ravaṭo dakṣiṇāvartaśaṅkhe jāṅgulike'pi ca \|

varāhe moraṭe reṇau vātūle'pi ca revaṭaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 58 \[unit 498\]

varṇṇāṭo gāyane kāmicitrakṛdvārajīvini \|

vikaṭo vikarāle syādviśāle suṃdare vare \|\| 58 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 59 \[unit 499\]

vekaṭaḥ syādvaikaṭike mīne ca navayauvane \|

varaṭo miśrite nīce veraṭaṃ badarīphale \|\| 59 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / 60 \[unit 500\]

śailāṭo devale siṃhe sitakācakirātayoḥ \|

saṃsṛṣṭaṃ triṣu vāntyādisaṃśuddhe saṅgate'pi ca \|\| 60 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatṛtīyam / fragment 501

harmmaṭastu pumānsūrye kacchape'pi ca harmmaṭaḥ \|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham / 61 \[unit 502\]

ṭacaturtham \|

pumānucciṅgaṭe mīnabhede kopanapūruṣe \|\| 61 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham / 62 \[unit 503\]

karahāṭo'bjakande'pi śalyadrau kusumāntare \|

kāmakūṭastu gaṇikāvibhrame gaṇikāpriye \|\| 62 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham / 63 \[unit 504\]

triṣu kāryapuṭo hrīke pramattā'narthakāriṇoḥ \|

kuṭannaṭastu kaivarttimustake śoṇake pumān \|\| 63 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham / 64 \[unit 505\]

kuṇḍakīṭastu cārvākavāṇyabhijñepi puṃścale \|

jāraje brāhmaṇīputradāsīkāmukayorapi \|\| 64 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham / 65 \[unit 506\]

khaṅgarīṭastu phalakāsidhārāvratacāriṇoḥ \|

gāḍhamuṣṭistu kṛpaṇe kṛpāṇachurikādiṣu \|\| 65 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham / 66 \[unit 507\]

cakravāṭaḥ kriyārohe paryante ca śikhātarau \|

catuḥṣaṣṭistu saṃkhyāyāṃ bahvṛce'pi kalāsvapi \|\| 66 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham / 67 \[unit 508\]

nārakīṭo'śmakīṭe syātsvadantāśāvihantari \|

parapuṣṭaḥ parabhṛte parapuṣṭā'paṇastriyām \|\| 67 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham / 68 \[unit 509\]

pratikṛṣṭaṃ mataṃ guhye dvirāvṛttyavakarṣite \|

pratiśiṣṭaḥ pratihate dante khyāte ca vācyavat \|\| 68 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham / 69 \[unit 510\]

pratisṛṣṭaṃ bhavetpratyākhyātaproṣitayostriṣu \|

barkarāṭaḥ kaṭākṣe'pi taruṇādityadīdhitau \|\| 69 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham / 70 \[unit 511\]

nārīpayodharotsaṅgakāntadantanakhakṣate \|

śipiviṣṭastu khalatau duścarmaṇi maheśvare \|\| 70 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacaturtham / 71 \[unit 512\]

prāñcalohe śrutikaṭaḥ prāyaścitte bhujaṅgame \|

siṃhacchaṭā tu punnāgakesare nāgakesare \|\| 71 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭapañcamam

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭapañcamam / 72 \[unit 513\]

ṭapañcamam \|

atha syāddaśanocchiṣṭaścuṃbe niḥśvāsite'dhare \|

lohe kāṃsye mṛdaṅgāraśakaṭyāṃ ratnakaṅkaṇe \|\| 72 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭapañcamam / 73 \[unit 514\]

pāvake paṭahasyāpi badare pātracarghaṭaḥ \|\| 73 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭapañcamam / fragment 515

iti viśvalocane ṭāntavargaḥ \|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / fragment 516

atha ṭhāntavargaḥ \|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhaikam

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhaikam / fragment 517

ṭhaikam \|

ṭhaścandre maṇḍale śūnye syāt kareṇūccaśabdite \|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvitīyam

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 518\]

ṭhadvitīyam \|

kaṭho munāvṛcāṃ bhede tadadhyetari tadvidi \|\| 1 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 519\]

svare'pi kaṇṭhastu gale pārśve śalyadruśabdayoḥ \|

kāṣṭhotkarṣe diśi sthāne kālamāne ca sīmani \|\| 2 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 520\]

kāṣṭhā dāruharidrāyāṃ kāṣṭhaṃ tu klībamindhane \|

kuṇṭho mūrkhepyakarmaṇye kuṣṭhaṃ bheṣajarogayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 521\]

koṣṭho'ntaḥkukṣigṛhayoḥ kusūlātmīyayorapi \|

goṣṭhī sabhāyāṃ saṃlāpe goṣṭhaṃ gosthānake matam \|\| 4 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 522\]

jyeṣṭho māse'graje śreṣṭhe vṛddhe jyeṣṭhā tu tārake \|

musalyāmaṅgulībhede duṣṭhaḥ syād durbale'dhame \|\| 5 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 523\]

niṣṭhā nirvahaniṣpattināśāntotkarṣayācane \|

kleśe'tha pāṭhāmbaṣṭhāyāṃ pāṭhastu paṭhane pumān \|\| 6 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 524\]

pṛṣṭhaṃ śarīrāvayavāntare'pi carame'pi ca \|

praṣṭho'gragāmini śreṣṭhe praṣṭhā cāṇḍālikauṣadhau \|\| 7 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 525\]

vaṇṭhaḥ syādakṛtodvāhe kuntadhārakakharvayoḥ \|

śaṭhastu puṃsi dhattūre dhūrttamadhyasthayostriṣu \|\| 8 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 526\]

śoṭho'lase ca mūrkhe ca śreṣṭho varakuberayoḥ \|

ṣaṣṭhī tu ṣaṇṇāṃ pūraṇyāṃ triṣu strī harayoṣiti \|\| 9 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvitīyam / fragment 527

haṭhastu syādbalātkāre vāriparṇyāṃ tu puṃsyayam \|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatṛtīyam

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatṛtīyam / 10 \[unit 528\]

ṭhatṛtīyam \|

apaṣṭuḥ samaye vāme'mbaṣṭho vaiśyāsute dvijāt \|\| 10 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatṛtīyam / 11 \[unit 529\]

deśeṃ'baṣṭhā tu cāṅgeryāṃ pāṭhayūthikayorapi \|

kaniṣṭho'lpe'nuje yūni kaniṣṭhā tvantimāṅgulau \|\| 11 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatṛtīyam / 12 \[unit 530\]

kamaṭhaḥ kacchape puṃsi kamaṭhaṃ bhājanāntare \|

jaraṭhaḥ kaṭhine pāṇḍau karkaśepyabhidheyavat \|\| 12 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatṛtīyam / 13 \[unit 531\]

narmaṭhaścubuke puṃsi narmaṭho nāgare'nyavat \|

prakoṣṭho vistṛtakare kūrparādadhare'pi ca \|\| 13 \|\|
