## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatṛtīyam / 14 \[unit 532\]

nṛpakakṣāntare cātha pratiṣṭhā gaurave matā \|

yā(yo)ganiṣpādane sthānacaturakṣarapadyayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatṛtīyam / 15 \[unit 533\]

variṣṭhaḥ pravare corutare syādabhidheyavat \|

variṣṭhaṃ marice tāmre variṣṭhaḥ puṃsi tittirau \|\| 15 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatṛtīyam / 16 \[unit 534\]

makuṣṭho manthare'pi syād vrīhibhitsavayorapi \|

laghiṣṭho bhelake'tyalpe vaikuṇṭhau viṣṇuśakrayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatṛtīyam / 17 \[unit 535\]

śrīkaṇṭhaḥ pārvatīnāthe kurujāṅgalake'pi ca \|

bhavedārthe'pi sādhiṣṭhaḥ sādhiṣṭho'pi dṛḍhe'pi ca \|\| 17 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacaturtham

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacaturtham / 18 \[unit 536\]

ṭhacaturtham \|

kalakaṇṭhaḥ pike pārāvate haṃse kaladhvanau \|

kaṇṭhe mṛgāntare kālapṛṣṭhaḥ klīvaṃ tu kārmuke \|\| 18 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacaturtham / 19 \[unit 537\]

karṇabāṇe'pyatho dantaśaṭho jambhakapitthayoḥ \|

karmaraṅge'pi nāraṅge rukkriyāyāṃ striyāmiyam \|\| 19 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacaturtham / 20 \[unit 538\]

nīlakaṇṭhastu dātyūhe khañjane prabalākini \|

kalaviṃke hare pītasārake kālakaṇṭhavat \|\| 20 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacaturtham / 21 \[unit 539\]

pūtikāṣṭhaṃ tu sarale devadārumahīruhe \|

sūtrakaṇṭhaḥ kapote syātkhañjarīṭe dvijanmani \|\| 21 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacaturtham / 22 \[unit 540\]

hārikaṇṭhaḥ parabhṛte hārānvitagale triṣu \|\| 22 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacaturtham / fragment 541

iti viśvalocane ṭhāntavargaḥ \|

## ḍāntavargaḥ

## ḍāntavargaḥ / fragment 542

atha ḍāntavargaḥ \|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍaikam

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍaikam / fragment 543

ḍaikam \|

ḍakāraḥ pārvatīnāthe cāse śabde'pi dṛśyate \|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 544\]

ḍadvitīyam \|

aṇḍaṃ tu khagamīnādikośe syānmuṣkavīryayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 545\]

iḍā budhavadhūvācorilāvadbhūgavorapi \|

kāṇḍo'strī vargabāṇārthanālāvasaravāriṣu \|\| 2 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 546\]

daṇḍe prakāṇḍe rahasi staṃbe kutsitakutsayoḥ \|

pativatnīsute jārātkuṇḍaḥ kuṇḍī kamaṇḍalau \|\| 3 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 547\]

kuṇḍaṃ devajalādhāre piṭhare tu mataṃ na nā \|

krīḍā kelāvavajñāyāṃ khelāyāmapi sammatā \|\| 4 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 548\]

kroḍaḥ śanau varāhe ca kroḍaṃ kroḍā ca vakṣasi \|

khaṇḍorddhe'strī pumānikṣuvikāre maṇidūṣaṇe \|\| 5 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 549\]

gaḍo mīne'ntarāye ca kubje pṛṣṭhaguḍe gaḍuḥ \|

gaṇḍastu piṭake yogabhede khaḍgikapolayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 550\]

vare pravīre cihne ca vājibhūṣaṇabudbude \|

guḍaḥ syādgajasannāhe golakekṣuvikārayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 551\]

guḍā snuhīguḍikayoḥ kaṃduke coḍanātparaḥ \|

goṇḍaḥ pāmarabhede syād vṛddhanābhau tu vācyavat \|\| 8 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 552\]

caṇḍastīvre daityabhede yamadāse'tikopane \|

striyāṃ caṇḍā dhanaharīśaṅkhapuṣpikayormmatā \|\| 9 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 553\]

bhaveccaṇḍī tu pārvatyāṃ hiṃsrakopanayoṣitoḥ \|

cūḍā valayabhede syācchikhāyāṃ vaḍa(la)bhāvapi \|\| 10 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 554\]

coḍo(lo) deśaviśeṣe syāccoḍaḥ prāvaraṇāntare \|

mūrkhe mūke himagraste jaḍā strī kandarauṣadhau \|\| 11 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 555\]

tāḍo muṣṭyādisaṃmeyatṛṇādau tāḍane rave \|

tāḍī tāḍītarau daṇḍaścaṇḍāṃśoḥ pāripārśvike \|\| 12 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 556\]

daṇḍaḥ sainyavyūhabhede mānabhede dame yame \|

maṃthāne'śve'bhimāne ca koṇadaṇḍaprakāṇḍayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 557\]

vigrahe ca grahe yajñe laguḍe'pi mato'striyām \|

nāḍī nāḍyāṃ śirāyāṃ syādvārttāyāṃ kuhanasya ca \|\| 14 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 558\]

nīḍaṃ sthāne kulāye'strī samīpe tu sapūrvakaḥ \|

paṇḍaḥ ṣaṇḍhe dhiyāṃ paṇḍā pāṇḍuḥ kuntīpatau site \|\| 15 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 16 \[unit 559\]

piṇḍo dehāṃsayorastrī nivāpe sihlake pumān \|

piṇḍo japāprasūne'pi piṇḍaḥ syādbhojane triṣu \|\| 16 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 17 \[unit 560\]

piṇḍaṃ sāṃdre bale bole gṛhāṅge jīvikāyasoḥ \|

piṇḍī tu piṇḍikā'lāvūkharjūrītagarāntare \|\| 17 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 18 \[unit 561\]

piṇḍī syājjñānajijñāse jijñāse'pi satāṃ matā \|

pīḍā'pamarddakṛpayoḥ saraladruśirodhvaje \|\| 18 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 19 \[unit 562\]

baṇḍā tu kulaṭāyāṃ syād baṇḍo hastādivarjjite \|

bhāṇḍaṃ tu bhājane vaṇigmūlavitte vibhūṣaṇe \|\| 19 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 20 \[unit 563\]

bhūṣaṇe ca turaṅgāṇāṃ nadīpātre ca kutracit \|

bhavenmaṇḍastu kūṣmāṇḍe karkaṭyāmapi puṃsyayam \|\| 20 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 21 \[unit 564\]

sāre picche'pi maṇḍe'strī pumāneraṇḍabhūṣayoḥ \|

maṇḍā dhātryāmatho maṇḍaṃ śākabhede ca mastuni \|\| 21 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 22 \[unit 565\]

muṇḍo rāhuśirodaityabhedeṣu triṣu muṇḍini \|

raṇḍā mūṣikapaṇarṇyākhyabheṣaje vidhavāstriyām \|\| 22 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 23 \[unit 566\]

vyāḍastu hiṃsrapaśvādye śvāpade'pi sarīsṛpe \|

śuṇḍā surāyāṃ veśyāyāṃ nalinīhastihastayoḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 24 \[unit 567\]

śuṇḍā jalakariṇyāṃ ca śuṇḍastu madanirbhare \|

śauṇḍī kuśāyāṃ cavike śauṇḍo matte'bhidheyavat \|\| 24 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 25 \[unit 568\]

ṣaḍaḥ peyāntare puṃsi ṣaḍo bhidyapi vidyate \|

padmādivṛnde ṣaṇḍo'strī ṣaṇḍaḥ syādgopatau caye \|\| 25 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 26 \[unit 569\]

kṣveḍastu puṃsi garale dhvāne karṇe maheśvare \|

kṣveḍastriṣu syātkuṭile kṣveḍā tu gajayoṣiti \|\| 26 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvitīyam / 27 \[unit 570\]

vīrāṇāṃ siṃhanāde'pi vaṃśaśalye'pi ca striyām \|

kṣveḍastu raktārkkaphale ghoṣapuṣpe durāsade \|\| 27 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 28 \[unit 571\]

ḍatṛtīyam \|

kāraṇḍo madhukoṣā'sikāraṇḍavadalāḍhake \|

kūṣmāṇḍo gaṇabhede syātkarkārubhrūṇayorapi \|\| 28 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 29 \[unit 572\]

kūṣmāṇḍī caṇḍikāyāṃ syādapi syādauṣadhībhidi \|

kodaṇḍo deśade'pi kodaṇḍaḥ kārmuke bhruvi \|\| 29 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 30 \[unit 573\]

gāruḍaṃ syānmarakate viṣaśāstre'pi gāruḍam \|

gāruḍaṃ gāruḍabhave taraṇḍo bhelake pumān \|\| 30 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 31 \[unit 574\]

vaḍiśīsūtrasaṃbaddhataradvastuni nāvi ca \|

tittiḍo daityabhede syāt tittiḍo yamaceṭake \|\| 31 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 32 \[unit 575\]

vṛkṣabhede'pi vṛkṣāmlabiṃbayorapi tintiḍī \|

drāviḍo vedhamukhye syānnīvṛdantarasaṅkhyayoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 33 \[unit 576\]

nirguṇḍīndrāṇikānīlaśephālyoḥ karahāṭake \|

picaṇḍaḥ puṃsi jaṭhare paśoravayavepi ca \|\| 33 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 34 \[unit 577\]

pūtyaṇḍaḥ śvāvidgandhamṛgayorgandhakīṭake \|

prakāṇḍostrī taruskandhe praśaste viṭape'pi ca \|\| 34 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 35 \[unit 578\]

pracaṇḍo durvahe śvetakaravīre pratāpini \|

varaṇḍo mukharoge syādaṃtarāvedivṛndayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 36 \[unit 579\]

mato duṣṭhiṇi vārtaṇḍo vārtaṇḍaḥ syādvihaṅgame \|

vāruṇḍī dvārapiṇḍyāṃ syād vāruṇḍaḥ karṇadṛṅmale \|\| 36 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 37 \[unit 580\]

phaṇirāje'tha vāruṇḍaḥ sekapātre'pi mudgare \|

bheruṇḍā yakṣiṇīdevībhedayostriṣu bhīṣaṇe \|\| 37 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 38 \[unit 581\]

mārttaṇḍastu mataścaṇḍakiraṇakroḍayorayam \|

māraṇḍastu bhujaṅgāṇḍe pathi gomayamaṇḍale \|\| 38 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 39 \[unit 582\]

varaṇḍā sārikākhaḍgadhenuvarttiṣu vartate \|

vitaṇḍā vādabhede syāt karavīryāṃ śilāhvaye \|\| 39 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatṛtīyam / 40 \[unit 583\]

kacchīśāke ca sā jñeyā śikhaṇḍo barhicūḍayoḥ \|

sapiṇḍaḥ puṃsi dāyāde sapiṇḍastanaye'pi ca \|

saraṇḍaḥ saraṭe dhūrtte saraṇḍo bhūṣaṇāntare \|\| 40 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacaturtham

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacaturtham / 41 \[unit 584\]

ḍacaturtham \|

āpogaṇḍastu śiśuke vikalāṅge'tibhīruke \|\| 41 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacaturtham / 42 \[unit 585\]

cakravāḍo'dribhede syāccakravāḍaṃ tu maṇḍale \|

jalaruṇḍo jalāvartte jalareṇubhujaṅgayoḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacaturtham / 43 \[unit 586\]

devatāḍo bṛhadbhānau svarbhānau ghoṣake'pi ca \|

dvayorvātaguḍaḥ khyāto vātyāyāṃ vātaśoṇite \|\| 43 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacaturtham / fragment 587

picchilāsphoṭikāyāṃ ca dhāmamātre'pi dṛśyate \|

iti viśvalocane ḍāntavargaḥ \|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / fragment 588

atha ḍhāntavargaḥ \|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhaikam

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhaikam / 1 \[unit 589\]

ḍhaikam \|

syāḍ ḍhakārastu ḍhakkāyāṃ nirgguṇe viṣamadhvanau \|\| 1 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvitīyam

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 590\]

ḍhadvitīyam \|

gūḍhaṃ rahasi guhye ca savṛte tvabhidheyavat \|

bhaveddāḍhā tu daṃṣṭrāyāmicchāyāmapyatha triṣu \|\| 2 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 591\]

sthāddṛḍhaḥ sthūlavalinordṛḍhaṃ vāḍhapragāḍhayoḥ \|

māḍhiḥ patrādibhaṅgau syād balināṃ dainyadīpane \|\| 3 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 592\]

mūḍhastu tandrite mūrkhe rāḍhā syādguhyaśobhayoḥ \|

bāḍhaṃ bhṛśe pratijñāyāṃ voḍhā bhārikasūtayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 593\]

vyūḍhaḥ pṛthulavinyastasaṃhateṣu hate triṣu \|

ṣaṇḍo vṛṣe varṣavare klībe syādvandhyapūruṣe \|\| 5 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvitīyam / fragment 594

vācyavanmarṣaṇe soḍhā soḍhā śakte'pi vācyavat \|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatṛtīyam

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatṛtīyam / 6 \[unit 595\]

ḍhatṛtīyam \|

adhyūḍha īśvare'dhyūḍhā kṛtasāpatnyayoṣiti \|\| 6 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatṛtīyam / 7 \[unit 596\]

āṣāḍho vratināṃ daṇḍe māse'pi malayācale \|

udūḍha ūḍhe sthūle syādupoḍho nikaṭoḍhayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatṛtīyam / 8 \[unit 597\]

pragāḍhastu dṛḍhe kṛcchre pramīḍho mūtrite ghane \|

prarūḍho jāṭhare baddhamūle syādabhidheyayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatṛtīyam / 9 \[unit 598\]

prārūḍhaḥ sambale vahnau vastrāñcalakapāṭayoḥ \|

pañjare'pi vigūḍhastu guptagarhitayostriṣu \|\| 9 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatṛtīyam / 10 \[unit 599\]

vigūḍhastriṣu sañjāte varddhite churite mataḥ \|

saṃmūḍhastu nave bhugne puṃjite'pyanupaplute \|\| 10 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatṛtīyam / fragment 600

saṃrūḍho vācyavatprauḍhe tathaivāṅkurite'pi ca \|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhacaturtham

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhacaturtham / 11 \[unit 601\]

ḍhacaturtham \|

adhyārūḍhaṃ samārūḍho'tyadhike'pi triliṅgakaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhacaturtham / fragment 602

iti viśvalocane ḍhāntavargaḥ \|

## ṇāntavargaḥ

## ṇāntavargaḥ / fragment 603

atha ṇāntavargaḥ \|

ṇakāro nirṇaye jñāne \|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 604\]

ṇadvitīyam \|

sūkṣme vrīhyantare'pyaṇuḥ \|

aṇirāṇivadakṣāgrakīlasīmāśriṣu dvayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 605\]

uṣṇaḥ syādātape grīṣme vācyavattaptadakṣayoḥ \|

ūrṇā bhrūmadhyajāvartte bhavenmeṣyādilomni ca \|\| 2 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 606\]

pippalījīrakumbhīramakṣikāsu kaṇā smṛtā \|

kaṇo'tisūkṣme dhānyāṃśe karṇaḥ śrotre pṛthāsute \|\| 3 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 607\]

suvarṇālau ca kāṇastu maudgalyādhikalocane \|

kiṇastu vraṇe cihne syādatha sūkṣmavraṇe guṇe \|\| 4 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 608\]

kīrṇaṃ channe parikṣipte hiṃsite'pyabhidheyavat \|

kuṇistu kukare tunne kṛṣṇe viṣṇau pike'rjune \|\| 5 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 609\]

vyāse kṛṣṇaṃ tu marice lohe ca triṣu tadvati \|

kṛṣṇā tu draupadīnīlīhārāhūrāsu pippalau \|\| 6 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 610\]

koṇo'srau laguḍe vādyaprabhede cārkkasambhave \|

vīṇādivādanopāye'pyekadeśe'pi vācyavat \|\| 7 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 611\]

gaṇaḥ samūhe pramathe saṃkhyāsainyaprabhedayoḥ \|

guṇo rūpādisattvādibiṃbādiharitādiṣu \|\| 8 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 612\]

sūde'pradhāne sandhyādau rajjau maurvyāṃ vṛkodare \|

geṣṇurnaṭe gāyane syād ghṛṇā kāruṇyanindayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 613\]

ghrāṇaṃ ghrāṇe'pi nāsāyāṃ cūrṇī tu syātkapardake \|

cūrṇaḥ kṣode kṣārabhede cūrṇāni gandhaśuktiṣu \|\| 10 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 614\]

jarṇaḥ kalānidhau vṛkṣe jiṣṇuḥ pārthendravahniṣu \|

jitvare triṣu jīrṇaṃ tu pakve vṛddhe jarāntare \|\| 11 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 615\]

jhaṇiḥ pūge duṣṭadaivaśrutau strī kaṭhine'nyavat \|

tīkṣṇaṃ kṣāre'tha niśitatigmātmatyāgiṣu triṣu \|\| 12 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 616\]

nirālasye subuddhau ca triṣu tīkṣṇaṃ ca muṣkake \|

tīkṣṇaṃ lohe viṣe tigme yavāgre lavaṇe raṇe \|\| 13 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 617\]

tūṇī nīlyāṃ niṣaṅge nā tṛṣṇā lipsāpipāsayoḥ \|

droṇaṃ tu rakṣite rakṣye rakṣaṇatrāyamāṇayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 618\]

dīrṇaṃ vidārite bhīte sphuṭite'pyabhidheyavat \|

deṣṇurddātari durddānte druṇo vṛścikamṛgayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 16 \[unit 619\]

druṇī tu kacchapīdroṇyordruṇaṃ cāpakṛpāṇayoḥ \|

droṇastu droṇakāke syādapi droṇaḥ kṛpīpatau \|\| 16 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 17 \[unit 620\]

āḍhakānāṃ catuṣkepi droṇaṃ syādāḍhake'striyām \|

droṇī kāṣṭhāmbuvāhinyāṃ gavāṃ ghāsabhujisthitau \|\| 17 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 18 \[unit 621\]

kāṣṭhāgāre gireḥ sandhau nīvṛdbhede'pi dṛśyate \|

varṇaḥ svarṇe'pi rūpe'pi paṇo mūlye bhṛtau glahe \|\| 18 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 19 \[unit 622\]

paṇo'śītivarāṭe'pi paṇaḥ kārṣāpaṇe dhane \|

dyūte vikrayyaśākāderbaddhamuṣṭāvapi smṛtaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 20 \[unit 623\]

paṇo dyūtādiṣūtsṛṣṭe vyavahāre'pyayaṃ paṇaḥ \|

parṇaṃ patre patatre ca parṇaḥ syātpuṃsi kiṃśuke \|\| 20 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 21 \[unit 624\]

pārṣṇiścaraṇamūle nā kumbhīpāścāttyabhāgayoḥ \|

senāpṛṣṭhe'pi pārṣṇiḥ syātpārṣṇiḥ syādunmadastriyām \|\| 21 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 22 \[unit 625\]

samagre pūrite pūrṇastriṣu śakte tu puṃsyayam \|

prāṇā asuṣvatha prāṇe viḍvāte'pyanile bale \|\| 22 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 23 \[unit 626\]

kāvyajīve ca bole ca prāṇaṃ tu triṣu pūrite \|

phāṇirguḍe karaṇḍe ca vāṇī dyūtau ca vāci ca \|\| 23 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 24 \[unit 627\]

vāṇistu hārake mūlye bhrūṇaḥ strīgarbhaḍimbhayoḥ \|

maṇirdvayormehanāgre ratne chāgīgalastane \|\| 24 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 25 \[unit 628\]

aliñjare'pi muktādau moṇastu paṭamutsake \|

moṇo bāṇepi kumbhīre makṣikāhikaraṇḍayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 26 \[unit 629\]

raṇaḥ koṇe kvaṇe yuddhe reṇurdhūlyaṇuparppaṭe \|

atha puṃsyeva varṇaḥ syātstutau rūpayaśoguṇe \|\| 26 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 27 \[unit 630\]

rāge dvijādau muktādau śobhāyāṃ citrakambale \|

vrate gītakrame deśe'pyastrī syādvarṇake'kṣare \|\| 27 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 28 \[unit 631\]

vāṇo valisute kāṇḍe kāṇḍāśe kevale pumān \|

bāṇo vāṇā ca jhiṃṭyāṃ syād vāṇako vyantare kvacit \|\| 28 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 29 \[unit 632\]

viṣṇuḥ kṛṣṇe vasau sūrye viṣṇurnnārāyaṇārkayoḥ \|

vasurdaivatabhede'pi vīṇā vallakividyutoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 30 \[unit 633\]

vṛṣṇiḥ syādyādave meṣe vṛṣṇiḥ pāṣaṇḍicaṇḍayoḥ \|

veṇī nadīnāṃ saṅge syāt keśabandhāntare'pi ca \|\| 30 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 31 \[unit 634\]

devatāḍe'pi veṇī strī veṇurvaṃśe nṛpāntare \|

śāṇorddhamāṣake karṣe kaṣaṇe karapatrake \|\| 31 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 32 \[unit 635\]

śītatrāṇāntare śāṇī śīrṇamalpaviśīrṇayoḥ \|

śoṇo nade kokanadacchavau śyonākabarhiṣoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 33 \[unit 636\]

lohitāśve'pyatha śroṇirdvayoḥ syātkārusaṃhatau \|

keśapātrāntare śroṇiḥ śreṇiḥ paṅktāvaniḥ striyām \|\| 33 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 34 \[unit 637\]

śrāṇā yavāgvāṃ śrāṇaṃ tu pakve syādabhidheyavat \|

sthāṇuḥ kīle hare puṃsi sthāṇustvastrī dhruvepi ca \|\| 34 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvitīyam / 35 \[unit 638\]

sthūṇā tu syād gṛhastambhe lohapratikṛtāvapi \|

kṣaṇaḥ syādutsave kālabhedāvasaraparvasu \|\| 35 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 36 \[unit 639\]

ṇatṛtīyam \|

abhīkṣṇaṃ tu bhṛśe nitye'pyaruṇo'nūrusūryayoḥ \|

kuṣṭhe cāvyaktarāge ca sandhyārāge ca puṃsyayam \|\| 36 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 37 \[unit 640\]

nīravā''raktakapilavyākuleṣu ca vācyavat \|

aruṇā tivṛtāśyāmāmañjiṣṭhā'tiviṣāsu ca \|\| 37 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 38 \[unit 641\]

araṇistu bhavedagnigranthe manthānadaṇḍake \|

indrāṇī tu śacīsinduvārayoḥ karaṇe striyām \|\| 38 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 39 \[unit 642\]

īriṇaṃ tūṣare śūnye'pīkṣaṇaṃ darśane dṛśi \|

ūṣaṇā tu kaṇāyāṃ syādūṣaṇaṃ marice matam \|\| 39 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 40 \[unit 643\]

eṣaṇī vraṇamārgā'nusāriṇyāṃ tu tulābhidi \|

eṣaṇo lohabāṇe syādanveṣe tvanupūrvakam \|\| 40 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 41 \[unit 644\]

kaṅkaṇaṃ karabhūṣāyāṃ hastasūtre'pi śekhare \|

kattṛṇaṃ tṛṇabhede'pi vāriparṇyāṃ ca kattṛṇam \|\| 41 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 42 \[unit 645\]

karaṇastu bhavedvaiśyācchudrāyāstanuje pumān \|

karaṇaṃ sādhakatame kāryakāyasthakarmasu \|\| 42 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 43 \[unit 646\]

kriyāyāmindriye kṣetre karaṇaṃ bālavādiṣu \|

gītāṅgahārasaṃveśakriyābhede'pi ceṣyate \|\| 43 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 44 \[unit 647\]

karuṇastu rase vṛkṣe kṛpāyāṃ karuṇā striyām \|

kareṇustu vasāyāṃ strī karṇikārebhayoḥ pumān \|\| 44 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 45 \[unit 648\]

kalyāṇamakṣayasvarge maṅgale tadvati triṣu \|

syānmānadaṇḍapaṇayoścaturthāṃśe hi kākiṇī \|\| 45 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 46 \[unit 649\]

guñjāyāṃ vāṭamāne'pi kuṣṭhabhede'pi kākaṇe \|

kāraṇaṃ hetuvadhayoḥ pīḍāyāṃ karaṇe'pi ca \|\| 46 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 47 \[unit 650\]

kāraṇā yātanāyāṃ syātkārmaṇaṃ syāttu karmaṭhe \|

paradehapraveśe ca yojane maṃtratantrayoḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 48 \[unit 651\]

bhṛtye kartari kurvāṇaḥ kṛpaṇaḥ kutsite kṛmau \|

khaḍge kṛpāṇaḥ śastrī tu kṛpāṇī karttarāvapi \|\| 48 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 49 \[unit 652\]

koṅkaṇo deśabhede syādastrabhede tu koṅkaṇam \|

gokarṇo'śvatare sarpamṛgabhede gaṇāntare \|\| 49 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 50 \[unit 653\]

aṅguṣṭā'nāmikonmāne gokarṇī mūrvikauṣadhī \|

grahaṇaṃ tūpalavdhau syātsvīkārādarayoḥ kare \|\| 50 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 51 \[unit 654\]

grahoparāge vandyāṃ ca pratyāye grahaṇīruji \|

grāmaṇīrnāpite puṃsi śreṣṭhā'dhipe ca bhaugike \|\| 51 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 52 \[unit 655\]

triṣu striyāṃ tu gaṇikā grāmiṇī nīlikā striyām \|

caraṇo'strī bahvṛcādau mūle'pi padagotrayoḥ \|\| 52 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 53 \[unit 656\]

caraṇaṃ bhramaṇeṅghrau syāccaraṇaṃ bhakṣaṇe'pi ca \|

jaraṇaṃ jīraṇo'jājīhiṅgusauvarcale matam \|\| 53 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 54 \[unit 657\]

taraṇiḥ sūrye'pi taraṇe kumārīnaukayoḥ striyām \|

taruṇo yūni navake kubjapuṣporuvūkayoḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 55 \[unit 658\]

dakṣiṇaḥ saralāvāmaparacchandānuvarttiṣu \|

triṣu syādvācyaliṅgo'yamavācī saṃbhave mataḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 56 \[unit 659\]

yajñadānapratiṣṭhāyāmavācyāmapi dakṣiṇā \|

durvarṇaṃ vāluke rūpye draviṇaṃ syātparākrame \|\| 56 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 57 \[unit 660\]

dharaṇaṃ dhāraṇe mānabhede'pi dharaṇī kṣitau \|

dharuṇaḥ salile svarge dharuṇaḥ parameṣṭhini \|\| 57 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 58 \[unit 661\]

dharmmaṇaḥ sarpabhedepi dharmmaṇaḥ pādapāntare \|

dharṣaṇī kulaṭāyāṃ syād dharṣaṇaṃ gañjite rate \|\| 58 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 59 \[unit 662\]

buddhoktamantrabhede ca nāṭikāyāṃ ca dhāraṇī \|

dhiṣaṇastu surācārye dhiṣaṇā buddhinidrayoḥ \|\| 59 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 60 \[unit 663\]

nirmāṇo nirmitau sāre racanāyāṃ samañjase \|

niryāṇaṃ nirgame mokṣe gajāpāṅge ca taddvayoḥ \|\| 60 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 61 \[unit 664\]

nirvāṇaṃ nirvṛtau mokṣe stambhane gajamajjane \|

niśreṇiradhirohiṇyāṃ kharjūrīpādape striyām \|\| 61 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 62 \[unit 665\]

patrorṇaṃ dhautakauśeye patrorṇaḥ śoṇakadrume \|

purāṇaṃ cirakālīyadravye syādabhidheyavat \|\| 62 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 63 \[unit 666\]

pūraṇaḥ pūrake puṃsi pūraṇe piṣṭakāntare \|

pūraṇī śālmalīvastrārambhasūtrāntare'pi ca \|\| 63 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 64 \[unit 667\]

praghaṇastāmrakuṇḍe syādalinde lohamudgare \|

pramāṇamekateyattāhetiyantṛpramātṛṣu \|\| 64 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 65 \[unit 668\]

satyavādini nitye ca maryādāhantṛśāstrayoḥ \|

pravaṇaḥ praguṇe prahve kramanimnaḥkṣitau kṛśe \|\| 65 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 66 \[unit 669\]

eteṣu triṣu puṃsyeva pravaṇaḥ syāccatuṣpathe \|

praveṇiḥ strī kuthe veṇyāṃ prokṣaṇaṃ vadhasekayoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 67 \[unit 670\]

varaṇastiktaśāke'pi prākāre varaṇaṃ vṛtau \|

varuṇastarubhede syāt pracetaḥsūryavāriṣu \|\| 67 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 68 \[unit 671\]

vāraṇo dantini khyātaḥ pratiṣedhe tu vāraṇam \|

atha pratīcīmadirāgaṇḍadūrvāsu vāruṇī \|\| 68 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 69 \[unit 672\]

brāhmaṇī phañjikāsṛkkādvijapatnīṣvatha dvije \|

brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇaṃ mantrabhede'pi dvijasaṃhatau \|\| 69 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 70 \[unit 673\]

bharaṇī śoṇake ṛkṣe bharaṇaṃ vetane bhṛtau \|

bhīṣaṇe dāruṇe gāḍhe bhīṣaṇaṃ sallakīrase \|\| 70 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 71 \[unit 674\]

kāruṇḍyāmīśvarakrīḍābhramaṇe bhramaṇī strīyām \|

mārgaṇo yācake bāṇe klībamanveṣayācñayoḥ \|\| 71 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 72 \[unit 675\]

yantraṇaṃ syānniyamane bandhane rakṣaṇe'pi ca \|

paṭolamūle ramaṇaṃ ramaṇastu priye smare \|\| 72 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 73 \[unit 676\]

ravaṇo rāsabhe śabdo roṣāṇo roṣaṇe triṣu \|

pāradoṣarayoḥ svarṇagharṣaṇe'pi pumānayam \|\| 73 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 74 \[unit 677\]

rohiṇī kuṭurohiṇyāṃ lohitāsomavalkayoḥ \|

gonāgakarṇarugbhede lavaṇaṃ tu dvijāntare \|\| 74 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 75 \[unit 678\]

lavaṇo rasarakṣobdhibhedeṣu lavaṇā dyutau \|

lakṣaṇaṃ nāmni cihne ca rāmabhrātari lakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 75 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 76 \[unit 679\]

lakṣmaṇaḥ puṃsi saumitrau lakṣmaṇaṃ nāmalakṣmaṇoḥ \|

lakṣmaṇā sārasījyotiṣmatyoḥ śrīmati vācyavat \|\| 76 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 77 \[unit 680\]

vipaṇistu striyāṃ paṇyavīthyāmāpaṇapaṇyayoḥ \|

viṣāṇaṃ tu paśoḥ śṛṅgau viṣāṇaṃ dviradadantayoḥ \|\| 77 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 78 \[unit 681\]

triṣu triṣu viṣāṇī tu meṣaśṛṅgākhyabheṣaje \|

śaraṇaṃ gṛharakṣitroḥ śaraṇaṃ rakṣaṇe vaghe \|\| 78 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 79 \[unit 682\]

siṅghāṇaṃ kācapātre'pi nāsikālohakiṭṭayoḥ \|

śrāvaṇo māsi pāṣaṇḍe daghyānyā śrāvaṇī striyām \|\| 79 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 80 \[unit 683\]

śrīparṇī kumbhigambhāryāṃ klībaṃ padmāgnimanthayoḥ \|

saṅkīrṇaṃ saṅkaṭe'śuddhe saraṇiḥ śreṇivartmanoḥ \|\| 80 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 81 \[unit 684\]

sāraṇo rāvaṇā'mātye'pyatīsāre'pi sāraṇaḥ \|

sāraṇī svalpasariti prasāraṇyāṃ ca sāraṇī \|\| 81 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 82 \[unit 685\]

suparṇaḥ svarṇacūḍe'pi garuḍe kṛtamālake \|

suparṇā kamalinyāṃ ca suparṇā tārkṣyamātari \|\| 82 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 83 \[unit 686\]

suvarṇastu suvarṇālau kṛṣṇā'gurumakhāntare \|

suvarṇaṃ varṇitaṃ svarṇe suvarṇaṃ karṣavittayoḥ \|\| 83 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 84 \[unit 687\]

suṣeṇo harisugrīvavaidyayoḥ karamardake \|

haraṇaṃ yautakadravye'pyaṅgarāge bhuje hṛtau \|\| 84 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 85 \[unit 688\]

hariṇastu mṛge puṃsi hariṇaḥ pāṇḍure'nyavat \|

hariṇī haritāmṛgyorvṛttastrībhedayorapi \|\| 85 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 86 \[unit 689\]

suvarṇapratimāyāṃ ca harṣaṇastu pramodake \|

akṣirogāntare yogāntare'pi śrāddhadaivate \|\| 86 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / 87 \[unit 690\]

strī kulastrīreṇukayoḥ hareṇurnā satīnake \|

hiraṇaṃ ca hariṇyaṃ ca varāṭe svarṇaretasoḥ \|\| 87 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatṛtīyam / fragment 691

kṣepaṇī ca bhavennaukādaṇḍe jālāntare'pi ca \|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 88 \[unit 692\]

ṇacaturtham \|

aṅgāriṇī hasantyāṃ syād bhāskaratyaktadiśyapi \|\| 88 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 89 \[unit 693\]

ātarpaṇaṃ tu sauhitye maṅgalālepane'pi ca \|

ātharvaṇastvatharvajñadvijanmani purohite \|\| 89 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 90 \[unit 694\]

ārohaṇaṃ tu sopāne samārohaprarohayoḥ \|

utkṣepaṇaṃ tu vyajane dhānyamardanavastuni \|\| 90 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 91 \[unit 695\]

vāntonmūlananistāronnayeṣūddharaṇaṃ matam \|

atha kāmaguṇo rāge'pyābhoge viṣaye'pi ca \|\| 91 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 92 \[unit 696\]

kārṣāpaṇaḥ purāṇe syādastriyāmapi kārṣike \|

cīrṇaparṇastu kharjūrīpādape picumardake \|\| 92 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 93 \[unit 697\]

cūḍāmaṇiḥ śiroratne kākaciñcāphale'pi ca \|

juhurāṇo'nale'dhvaryau taṇḍurīṇastu kīṭake \|\| 93 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 94 \[unit 698\]

syāttandulodake caiva yāmyadeśīyabarbare \|

tailaparṇī malayaje sihlaśrīvāsayorapi \|\| 94 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 95 \[unit 699\]

dākṣāyaṇī ca durgāyāṃ rohiṇyāṃ tārakāsu ca \|

devamaṇiḥ śive vājikaṇṭhāvartte ca kaustubhe \|\| 95 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 96 \[unit 700\]

nārāyaṇo'cyute'bhīrugauryornārāyaṇī striyām \|

gale nigaraṇaḥ puṃsi bhojane tu napuṃsakam \|\| 96 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 97 \[unit 701\]

nirūpaṇaṃ vicāre syādālokananidarśane \|

nistaraṇaṃ syānnistāre'pyupāye nirgame'pi ca \|\| 97 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 98 \[unit 702\]

nissaraṇaṃ dvāramuktiniryāṇopāyamṛtyuṣu \|

parīraṇaḥ syātkamaṭhe daṇḍe ca paṭṭaśāṭake \|\| 98 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 99 \[unit 703\]

parvarīṇastu parṇasya śirāyāṃ ghūtakambale \|

parṇavṛntarase'pi syāt sitasaurabhaparvaṇoḥ \|\| 99 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 100 \[unit 704\]

paravāṇistu kathito dharmā'dhyakṣe'pi vatsare \|

triṣu syāttatpare'bhīṣṭe'pyāśraye tu parāyaṇam \|\| 100 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 101 \[unit 705\]

pārāyaṇaṃ pāragatau samyagāsaṅgakārtsnyayoḥ \|

pīluparṇī tu mūrvāyāṃ bimbāyāmoṣadhībhidi \|\| 101 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 102 \[unit 706\]

puṣkariṇī sarojinyāṃ hastinyāṃ ca jalāśaye \|

syātpratipaṇaḥ saṃskāre'pyupagrahaniṣaṅgayoḥ \|\| 102 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 103 \[unit 707\]

pravāraṇaṃ niṣedhe syāt kāmyadāne pravāraṇam \|

vāravāṇastu kavace sarvasannahane'pi ca \|\| 103 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 104 \[unit 708\]

mīnāstrīṇo mataḥ puṃsi dardarāmre'pi khañjane \|

raktareṇustu sindūre palāśakalikodbhave \|\| 104 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 105 \[unit 709\]

rāgacūrṇaḥ smare raktavāluke dantadhāvane \|

rerihāṇaḥ paśupatau rerihāṇo vihāyasi \|\| 105 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 106 \[unit 710\]

lambakarṇo mataśchāge syādaṅkoramahīruhe \|

astrī vidāraṇaṃ yuddhe bhedane ca viḍambane \|\| 106 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 107 \[unit 711\]

bhavedvaitaraṇī pretanadyāṃ rākṣasamātari \|

śaravāṇiḥ śaramukhe pāpiṣṭhe śarajīvini \|\| 107 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 108 \[unit 712\]

striyāṃ śikhariṇī vṛttabhede takraprabhedayoḥ \|

strīratne mallikāyāṃ ca romāvalyāmapi smṛtā \|\| 108 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 109 \[unit 713\]

samīraṇaḥ syātpavane prasthapuṣpakapānthayoḥ \|

saṃsaraṇaṃ syātsaṃsāre puranirgamagopure \|\| 109 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacaturtham / 110 \[unit 714\]

ghaṇṭāpathe raṇārambhe'pyasaṃbādhacamūgatau \|

hastikarṇo'yameraṇḍe palāśagaṇabhedayoḥ \|\| 110 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcamam

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcamam / 111 \[unit 715\]

ṇapañcamam \|

avagrahaṇamākhyātaṃ pratirodhe'pyanādare \|

athā'vatāraṇaṃ bhūtādyāveśepyambare'rcane \|\| 111 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcamam / 112 \[unit 716\]

ākhyeyabhāge'dhyāhāragranthe syādavatāraṇā \|

nindopālambhaniyamālāpeṣu paribhāṣaṇam \|\| 112 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcamam / 113 \[unit 717\]

pravidāraṇamityetatsammataṃ dāraṇe raṇe \|

maṇḍūkaparṇaḥ syonāke'pyalake ca kapītane \|\| 113 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcamam / 114 \[unit 718\]

maṇḍūkaparṇī mañjiṣṭhābrāhmīgojihvikāsvapi \|

syānmattavāraṇaḥ puṃsi madadurdāntavāraṇe \|\| 114 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcamam / 115 \[unit 719\]

klībaṃ prāsādavīthīnāṃ varaṇḍe cāpyapāśraye \|

vibhītakatarau puṃsi romāñce romaharṣaṇam \|\| 115 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcamam / 116 \[unit 720\]

vātarāyaṇa unmatte mataḥ kūṭe ca mārgaṇe \|

śarasaṃkramaṇe kiñcitkarepi karapatrake \|\| 116 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇaṣaṣṭham

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇaṣaṣṭham / 117 \[unit 721\]

ṇaṣaṣṭham \|

vayaḥsaṃdhau ca garbhe ca bhaveddohadalakṣaṇam \|

payodhare ca lāvaṇye mataṃ yauvanalakṣaṇam \|\| 117 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇaṣaṣṭham / fragment 722

iti viśvalocane ṇāntavargaḥ \|

## tāntavargaḥ

## tāntavargaḥ / fragment 723

atha tāntavargaḥ \|

## tāntavargaḥ / taikam

## tāntavargaḥ / taikam / fragment 724

taikam \|

pālane pālake taḥ syāttuścaurakroḍapucchayoḥ \|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 725\]

tadvitīyam \|

antaṃ viśuddhe vyāpte syādanto nāśe manohare \|\| 1 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 726\]

svarūpe'ntaṃ mataṃ klībaṃ na strī prānte'ntike triṣu \|

arttiḥ pīḍāghanuṣkoṭyorastaḥ pratyaṅmahīdhare \|\| 2 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 727\]

triṣu kṣipte gate'pyastamāptaḥ satyagṛhītayoḥ \|

āptiḥ saṃvaraṇe prāptau vijñātagatayorgatam \|\| 3 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 728\]

ītiḥ syādativṛṣṭyādipaṭṭe ḍimbapravāsayoḥ \|

uktamekākṣaracchandasyuktastu triṣu bhāṣite \|\| 4 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 729\]

sphūrtirakṣaṇayorūtirṛtamuñchaśile jale \|

mataṃ triliṅgakaṃ satye gatau dīpte'bhipūjite \|\| 5 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 730\]

ṛtirgatau jugupsāyāṃ sparddhāyāmapyamaṅgale \|

ṛtuḥ svādārttave vīre vasantādiṣu māsi ca \|\| 6 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 731\]

etastu karbure vācyaliṅgaḥ syādāgate'pi ca \|

śobhā'bhilāṣayoḥ kāntiḥ kānto ramye priye triṣu \|\| 7 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 732\]

kānto'śmani pumānkāntā priyaṅgau nāyikāntare \|

kīrttiryaśasi vistāre prasāde'pi ca kardame \|\| 8 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 733\]

kunto gavedhuke prāse daṇḍabhāve'lpajantuṣu \|

kuntī syātpāṇḍukāntāyāṃ śallakyāṃ gugguludrume \|\| 9 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 734\]

kṛtirvadhe'pi karaṇe klībaṃ satyayuge kṛtam \|

triṣu hiṃsitaparyāptavihite niṣphale'vyayam \|\| 10 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 735\]

kṛttaṃ tu kathitaṃ chinne veṣṭhite'pyabhidheyavat \|

kṛttistvakcarmabhūrjeṣu kṛttikāyāṃ ca kīrtitā \|\| 11 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 736\]

keturgrahāntarotpātadyutilakṣmadhvajādiṣu \|

kraturyajñe munerbhede gataṃ syājjātayādasoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 737\]

gatirdaśāyāṃ gamane jñāne marmmā'bhyupāyayoḥ \|

nāḍīvraṇe saraṇyāṃ ca gatirjanmāntare'pi ca \|\| 13 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 738\]

gartastrigarttadeśe syād bhūśvabhre'pi kukundare \|

gāturgandharvarolambaroṣaṇe kokilāpatau \|\| 14 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 739\]

gītiśchandontare jñāne gītaṃ gāne ca śabdite \|

guptastu rakṣite gūḍhe vṛṣale candrapūrvakaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 16 \[unit 740\]

guptiḥ kārāgṛhe gartte gopāye rakṣaṇe yuge \|

grastaṃ grāsīkṛte'pi syālluptavarṇapadodite \|\| 16 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 17 \[unit 741\]

ghātaḥ prahāre kāṇḍe ca ghṛtaṃ dīptājyavāriṣu \|

citiḥ samūhe cityāyāmupādupacaye citiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 18 \[unit 742\]

citaḥ kūṭīkṛte'pi syāccitā sahaticityayoḥ \|

citā channe cullikāyāṃ jātaṃ janmaughajantuṣu \|\| 18 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 19 \[unit 743\]

jātiḥ sāmānyamālatyośchandobhidgotrajanmasu \|

tāto'nukampye janake tikto rasasugandhayoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 20 \[unit 744\]

tiktā tu kaṭurohiṇyāṃ tiktaṃ parpaṭake matam \|

tretā yuge'gnitritaye dattaṃ viśrāṇite'vite \|\| 20 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 21 \[unit 745\]

dantaḥ kuñje rade sānau dantī syādauṣadhīmidi \|

dāntastriṣu tapaḥkleśasahe'pi damite'pi ca \|\| 21 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 22 \[unit 746\]

ditirdanau khaṇḍane ca dīptaṃ jvalitadagdhayoḥ \|

triṣu nirvāsite'pi syāddṛtiścarmapuṭe kaṣe \|\| 22 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 23 \[unit 747\]

dṛpto nivārite śakte dyutirdīdhitiśobhayoḥ \|

drutaṃ śīghre ca vidrāṇe vilīne śīghrage triṣu \|\| 23 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 24 \[unit 748\]

dhātā tu brahmaṇi ravau triṣu syātparipālake \|

dhātuḥ kriyārthe śukrepi viṣayeṣvindriyeṣu ca \|\| 24 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 25 \[unit 749\]

śleṣmādirasaraktādibhūtādivasudhādiṣu \|

manaḥśilādike lohe viśeṣādgairikesthini \|\| 25 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 26 \[unit 750\]

dhutaṃ vidhūte tyakte ca dhūtaḥ kampitabhartsite \|

dhūrttaṃ tu khaṇḍalavaṇe dhattūre nāviṭe triṣu \|\| 26 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 27 \[unit 751\]

dhṛtirdhāraṇasaṃtuṣṭidhairye yogāntare'dhvare \|

natastagaravṛkṣe syāt kuṭilānatayostriṣu \|\| 27 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 28 \[unit 752\]

nītirnnaye prāpaṇe ca nṛttaḥ syānnartane krimau \|

paktiḥ strī gaurave pāke paṅktiḥ śreṇau daśasvapi \|\| 28 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 29 \[unit 753\]

syāddaśākṣaravṛttepi striyāṃ mūlye gatau patiḥ \|

pattiḥ padātau vīre nā gatau senāntare striyām \|\| 29 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 30 \[unit 754\]

pātastu patane trāte pītamācāntagaurayoḥ \|

triṣu pītā tu parṇinyāṃ pītaṃ pāne napuṃsakam \|\| 30 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 31 \[unit 755\]

pītiḥ pāne sapūrvā tu sahapāne haye pumān \|

pustaṃ tu pustake klībaṃ vijñāne lepyakarmmaṇi \|\| 31 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 32 \[unit 756\]

pūtaṃ pavitre śabde ca triṣu syādbahulīkṛte \|

pūritacchannayoḥ pūrttaṃ pūrttaṃ khātādikarmmaṇi \|\| 32 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 33 \[unit 757\]

poto bāle bahitre ca prātiḥ pūrttipradeśayoḥ \|

prāptirmahodaye lābhe prāptaṃ labdhasamañjase \|\| 33 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 34 \[unit 758\]

prītiḥ smarasutāyogabhedayoḥ premamodayoḥ \|

harṣite narmmaṇi prītaṃ preto bhūtāntare mṛte \|\| 34 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 35 \[unit 759\]

protaṃ tu grathite vastre plutastu syāttrimātṛke \|

plutamaśvasya gamane plutaṃ saptavane triṣu \|\| 35 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 36 \[unit 760\]

bhaktirvibhāge sevāyāṃ bhartā svāmini dhārake \|

bhittiḥ kuḍye ca kāśe ca pradeśe bhedabhāgayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 37 \[unit 761\]

bhītaṃ bhaye'pi samaye bhītiḥ sādhvasakaṃpayoḥ \|

atha bhṛtaḥ pumāndevayonibhede'pi devale \|\| 37 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 38 \[unit 762\]

triṣu prāpte vivṛtte ca bhūtaṃ syānnyāyyasatyayoḥ \|

upamāne pṛthivyādau piśācādau same triṣu \|\| 38 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 39 \[unit 763\]

bhūtirmmātaṅgaśṛṅgāre bhasmasampattijanmasu \|

bhṛtistu bharaṇe khyātā tathā vetanamūlyayoḥ \|\| 39 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 40 \[unit 764\]

bhrāntiḥ syādbhramaṇe'pi syānmatau vā'pyanavasthitau \|

mato'rcite'pyanumate matirbuddhau smṛtīcchayoḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 41 \[unit 765\]

mantuḥ syādaparādhe'pi mānave parameṣṭhini \|

mātā brāhmyādigokādiprasūgaurīṣvapi kṣitau \|\| 41 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 42 \[unit 766\]

triṣu syānmāpake mātā gītādhyakṣe prapūrvakaḥ \|

mitirmāne'pyavacchede muktirmokṣe'pi mocane \|\| 42 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 43 \[unit 767\]

mukto mokṣagate'pyuktastriṣu muktā tu mauktike \|

mūrttaṃ mūrttyanvite mūrcchā'nvite kāṭhinyavatyapi \|\| 43 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 44 \[unit 768\]

mūrttiḥ kāye'pi kāṭhinye mṛtyuyācitayormmatam \|

mṛtaṃ mṛtyupariprāpte vijñeyamabhidheyavat \|\| 44 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 45 \[unit 769\]

yatiryatini puṃsi strī pāṭhabhedanikārayoḥ \|

yantā sādini sūte ca nipūrvo'sau niyāmake \|\| 45 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 46 \[unit 770\]

yuktaṃ syāducite yuktaṃ saṃyute'pyabhidheyavat \|

yuktirnniyojane nyāye pṛthaksaṃyuktayormatam \|\| 46 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 47 \[unit 771\]

yutaṃ hastacatuṣke'pi saṃkhyābhede napūrvakam \|

raktonurakte nīlyādirañjite lohite'nyavat \|\| 47 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 48 \[unit 772\]

riktaṃ śūnye vane'pi syādaśarītirgirāṃ pathi \|

rītiḥ syande pracāre ca lohakiṭṭārakūṭayoḥ \|\| 48 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 49 \[unit 773\]

latā tu mādhavīvallīśākhāspṛkkāpriyaṅguṣu \|

latā kastūrikājyotiṣmatīdūrvāsu ca smṛtā \|\| 49 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 50 \[unit 774\]

liptaṃ vilepite bhukte viṣāktaviśiṣādiṣu \|

lūtā pipīlikāyāṃ syādūrṇanābhe gadāntare \|\| 50 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 51 \[unit 775\]

loptaṃ tu corite bāṣpe vaktā vāggmini paṇḍite \|

vaptā pitari tantūnāṃ vāpake bījavāpake \|\| 51 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 52 \[unit 776\]

varttirgātrānulepanyāṃ varttirdīpadaśāsu ca \|

dīpe bheṣajanirmmāṇe locanāñjanalekhayoḥ \|\| 52 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 53 \[unit 777\]

nīrugvṛttimatorvārto vārttamārogyaphalgunoḥ \|

vārttā kṛṣyādivṛttāntavārttākīvṛttiṣu sthitā \|\| 53 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 54 \[unit 778\]

vittaṃ tu vibhave jñātakhyātalavdhavicārite \|

vittirjñānepi lābhe'pi vicāre sambhave'pi ca \|\| 54 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 55 \[unit 779\]

vītaṃ tvasārahastyaśve tyaktepyaṅkuśakarmmaṇi \|

vītistyāge gatau dīptau prajane dhāvane'śane \|\| 55 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 56 \[unit 780\]

vṛttiḥ pravṛttau vṛttau ca kauśikyādipravarttane \|

vṛttastu vartule'tīte mṛte khyāte dṛḍhe vṛte \|\| 56 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 57 \[unit 781\]

triṣu vṛttaṃ tu carite vṛttaṃ chandasi varttate \|

vṛtirvivaraṇe vāṭe veṣṭite varaṇe vṛtam \|\| 57 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 58 \[unit 782\]

vṛntaṃ prasavabandhe'pi kucāgre ghaṭadhārayoḥ \|

śastaṃ kṣeme praśaste ca śātaḥ śakuniśātayoḥ \|\| 58 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 59 \[unit 783\]

śātaṃ śarmmaṇi śāntastu rase dānte'pi muktake \|

śāntiḥ śame'pi kalyāṇe śāstā śāsakabuddhayoḥ \|\| 59 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 60 \[unit 784\]

śitaḥ kṛṣṇe site bhūrje śitaṃ śakuniśāntayoḥ \|

vānīrabahuvāre ca śītaḥ śītaṃ tu candane \|\| 60 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 61 \[unit 785\]

himasambhūtajāḍye'pi śītalālasayostriṣu \|

śuktiḥ śaṅkhanakhe śaṅkhe muktāsphoṭe'pi kambuni \|\| 61 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 62 \[unit 786\]

dīrghakośīyāvartte kapālaśakale striyām \|

śukto'mle karkaśe pūte śāstrāvaghṛtayoḥ śrutam \|\| 62 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 63 \[unit 787\]

śrutiḥ śrotre ca vede ca vārtāyāṃ śrautakarmmaṇi \|

śvetaṃ rūpyaṃ triṣu site śveto dvīpādribhedayoḥ \|\| 63 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 64 \[unit 788\]

śvetā varāṭikāyāṃ syācchaṅkhinyāṃ kāṣṭhapāṭalau \|

satsādhau vidyamāne'pi praśaste pūjite triṣu \|\| 64 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 65 \[unit 789\]

satī sādhvīcaṇḍikayoḥ sattu satye'bhidheyavat \|

sātirdānevasāne'pi sitaṃ śvetasamāptayoḥ \|\| 65 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 66 \[unit 790\]

triṣu jñāte'pi baddhe'pi śarkarāyāṃ sitā matā \|

sītā tu jānakīvyomagaṅgālāṅgalavarttmasu \|\| 66 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 67 \[unit 791\]

sutastu pārthive putre suptirviśvāsaghātini \|

svāpe sparśājñatāyāṃ ca sukhasvāpe supūrvikā \|\| 67 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 68 \[unit 792\]

sūtastu pārade takṣṇi sūtaḥ sārathibandinoḥ \|

prasūte prerite sūtaḥ kṣatriyād brāhmaṇīsute \|\| 68 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 69 \[unit 793\]

sṛtiḥ strī gamane mārge kupūrvā nikṛtau sṛtiḥ \|

seturnnālau ca varuṇe sthitamūrddhve'pi saṃsthite \|\| 69 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 70 \[unit 794\]

niścite sapratijñe'pi gatyabhāve tu na dvayoḥ \|

maryādāyāmavasthāne sthāne sīmani ca sthitiḥ \|\| 70 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 71 \[unit 795\]

smṛtistu dharmaśāstre syāt smaraṇe dhīcchayorapi \|

saṃtatau sīvane syūtiḥ syūtaḥ kṣataprasevayoḥ \|\| 71 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 72 \[unit 796\]

svāntaṃ napuṃsakaṃ vitte svāntaṃ syādapi gahvare \|

dvayostu hasto nakṣatre hastaḥ karikare kare \|\| 72 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 73 \[unit 797\]

saprakoṣṭhātatakare hastaḥ keśātparaścaye \|

hitaṃ gate dhṛte pathye hetirjvālārkatejasoḥ \|\| 73 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 74 \[unit 798\]

striyāṃ śastrepyatha kṣattā sārathidvāḥsthadhātṛṣu \|

bhujiṣyaje niyukte ca śūdrācca kṣatriyāsute \|\| 74 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvitīyam / 75 \[unit 799\]

kṣamāyāṃ tu matā kṣāntiḥ kṣāntiḥ syānniyame'pi ca \|

kṣitiḥ pṛthivyāṃ vāse ca sthānamātre kṣaye kṣitiḥ \|\| 75 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 76 \[unit 800\]

tatṛtīyam \|

agastirvaṅgasenadrau syādagastye'pyathāṅkatiḥ \|

agnibrahmā'gnihotreṣu sthire dāmodare'cyutaḥ \|\| 76 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 77 \[unit 801\]

ajito'nirjite viṣṇāvaditirdevasūbhuvoḥ \|

anṛtaṃ syād mṛṣākṛpyorananto viṣṇuśeṣayoḥ \|\| 77 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 78 \[unit 802\]

anantaṃ gagane'nantaṃ bhavedanavadhau triṣu \|

anantā pṛthivīdūrvāpārvatīlāṅgalīṣvapi \|\| 78 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 79 \[unit 803\]

sārivāyāṃ guḍūcyāṃ ca samudrāntāviśalyayoḥ \|

amṛtaṃ mokṣapīyūṣasalile hṛdyavastuni \|\| 79 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 80 \[unit 804\]

ayācite yajñaśeṣe ghṛte dugdhe'tisundare \|

amṛtastu mataḥ puṃsi dhanvaṃtarisuparvaṇoḥ \|\| 80 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 81 \[unit 805\]

guḍūcyāmalakīpathyāmāgadhīṣvamṛtā matā \|

amatirbhāvikāle syādarhaṃstu jinapūjyayoḥ \|\| 81 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 82 \[unit 806\]

arditaḥ pavanavyādhau yācitā'hatayostriṣu \|

arvatī ceṭikāvāmyoraśve'rvan kutsite'nyavat \|\| 82 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 83 \[unit 807\]

avyaktastu harau hīre mūrkhe vācyavadasphuṭe \|

vācyavatkṣatahīne syādākṛtiḥ kāyarūpayoḥ \|\| 83 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 84 \[unit 808\]

sāmānye'pi tathākhyātamākhyātaṃ kathite tiṅi \|

atha vācyavadākhyātaṃ ghrāṇite hiṃsite'pi ca \|\| 84 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 85 \[unit 809\]

ācitastu cite channe saṃgṛhīte triliṅgakaḥ \|

ācitaḥ śakaṭonmeye palānāmayutadvaye \|\| 85 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 86 \[unit 810\]

ādṛtaḥ sādare'pi syāt pūjite'pyabhidheyavat \|

ādhmātaḥ pavanavyādhau dagdhaśabditayosriṣu \|\| 86 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 87 \[unit 811\]

ānartto nartanasthāne deśabhede raṇe jale \|

pāte tadātve'pyāpāta āpatiḥ prāptidoṣayoḥ \|\| 87 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 88 \[unit 812\]

āplutaḥ snātake puṃsi snāte syādabhidheyavat \|

āyattiḥ snehamayodāvaśitāvalavāsare \|\| 88 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 89 \[unit 813\]

āyatistu yame dairghye prabhāvottarakālayoḥ \|

āyastastejite kṣipte kupite kleśite hate \|\| 89 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 90 \[unit 814\]

āvarttaścintane cā''vartane vāpyambhasāṃ śrame \|

āsphotastvarkaparṇe syādāsphotaḥ kovidārake \|\| 90 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 91 \[unit 815\]

āsphotā girikarṇyāṃ ca vanamalyāmapi striyām \|

āsattiḥ saṅgame lābhe āhataṃ tu mṛṣārthake \|\| 91 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 92 \[unit 816\]

syātpurātanavastre'pi navavastre'pi vāhatam \|

āhataṃ cānake'pi syāttāḍite grasite triṣu \|\| 92 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 93 \[unit 817\]

iṅgitaṃ ceṣṭite gatyāmucitaṃ tu samañjase \|

anumatyāṃ mitā'bhyastajñāteṣu triṣu ca triṣu \|\| 93 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 94 \[unit 818\]

ucchritaṃ tu pravṛddhe syāt sañjāte'pyunnate'nyavat \|

uttaptaṃ śuṣke'piśite saṃtapte ca pariplute \|\| 94 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 95 \[unit 819\]

vṛddhimatyunmanaske'pi prodyate matamutthitam \|

ucchritaṃ tu triṣūtpanne prodyate vṛddhimatyapi \|\| 95 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 96 \[unit 820\]

uditaṃ sūdite prāpte'pyudgataproktayostriṣu \|

uddhāto mudgare vāyuyogārthaṃ kumbhakādiṣu \|\| 96 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 97 \[unit 821\]

udraṅge skhalane'pyarthā''dhāne'pi samupakrame \|

syādudantastu vārtāyāmudantaḥ sajjane'pi ca \|\| 97 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 98 \[unit 822\]

triṣūdvāntaḥ samudgīrṇe pumānnirmadadantiṣu \|

udāttaḥ svarabhede syāt kāvyālaṅkaraṇe'pi ca \|\| 98 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 99 \[unit 823\]

udātto dātṛmahatormatā hṛdye'pi vācyavat \|

uddhṛtaṃ tu site bhuktojjhite'pyātolite mṛte \|\| 99 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 100 \[unit 824\]

unnatistūdaye vṛddhāvudgatau tārkṣyayoṣiti \|

unmatta unmādavati dhattūramucakundayoḥ \|\| 100 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 101 \[unit 825\]

uṣitaṃ vyuṣite dagdhe'pyūrmmitaṃ kṣiptadagdhayoḥ \|

edhatuḥ puruṣe vahnāvaṃhatistyāgarogayoḥ \|\| 101 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 102 \[unit 826\]

kapotaḥ syātkalarave kavakākhye vihaṅgame \|

kalitaṃ viditepyāpte svīkṛte'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 102 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 103 \[unit 827\]

kāpotaṃ tadguṇe sroto'ñjanakhañjikayorapi \|

kirātaḥ puṃsi bhūnimbe mlecchasvalpaśarīrayoḥ \|\| 103 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 104 \[unit 828\]

vālavyajanadhāriṇyāṃ kuṭṭinīsuragaṅgayoḥ \|

syātkirātīti kurvaṃstu bhṛtye karmmakare triṣu \|\| 104 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 105 \[unit 829\]

kṛtānto yamasiddhāntadaive'pyaśubhakarmmaṇi \|

kranditaṃ rodite'pi syādāhvāne kṛtarodane \|\| 105 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 106 \[unit 830\]

gabhastiḥ kiraṇe sūrye puṃsi strī vahniyoṣiti \|

garmut kārttasvare klībaṃ garmucchākhābhidhāyini \|\| 106 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 107 \[unit 831\]

garjito mattamātaṅge garjitaṃ jaladadhvanau \|

godanto haritāle syāddaṃśite varmmite triṣu \|\| 107 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 108 \[unit 832\]

gopatiḥ pārthive ṣaṇḍe ravipaṇḍitaśūliṣu \|

graṃthitaṃ gumphitākrāntahiṃsiteṣu triṣu smṛtam \|\| 108 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 109 \[unit 833\]

cintāto mocane gāṅgacitte ca cirajīvini \|

jaganvāte pumānklībaṃ bhuvane jaṅgame triṣu \|\| 109 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 110 \[unit 834\]

jagatī jagati kṣmāyāṃ chandobhade jane'pi ca \|

jayantī tvatha gaurīndraputrī jarā drumāntare \|\| 110 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 111 \[unit 835\]

vaijayantyāṃ jayantastu pākaśāsanihīrayoḥ \|

jāmātā dayite sūryāvartte tu duhituḥ patau \|\| 111 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 112 \[unit 836\]

jīmūto jalade śakre ghoṣepi vṛddhijīvini \|

devatāḍe'pi jīmūto jīmūtaḥ parvate'pi ca \|\| 112 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 113 \[unit 837\]

jīvāturastriyāṃ bhakte jīvite jīvanauṣadhau \|

jīvantī jīvanīvṛkṣe śamīvandā'mṛtāsu ca \|\| 113 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 114 \[unit 838\]

jṛmbhitaṃ karaṇe strīṇāṃ veṣṭite sphuṭite triṣu \|

jvalito bhāskare dagdhe vānitaṃ tanitāṃśuke \|\| 114 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 115 \[unit 839\]

vādyabhāṇḍe guṇe vistāre teṣu triṣu tānitam \|

tṛṇatā tu tṛṇatve syāt tṛṇatā kārmuke'pi ca \|\| 115 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 116 \[unit 840\]

trigarttaḥ syājjanapade trigartto gaṇitāntare \|

viṣaye'pi trigarttā tu ghurghurīkāmukastriyoḥ \|\| 116 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 117 \[unit 841\]

tvaritaṃ prajave śīghre durgatirniraye striyām \|

dāridrye'pyatha durjjātaṃ kujāte vyasane tathā \|\| 117 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 118 \[unit 842\]

dṛṣṭāntastu pumāñśāstre syādudāharaṇepi ca \|

daṃśitaṃ varmmite daṣṭe dravantī saridantare \|\| 118 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 119 \[unit 843\]

maghau caiva dvijātistu dvijanmani vihaṅgame \|

dhīmānvācaspatau puṃsi dhīre buddhimati triṣu \|\| 119 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 120 \[unit 844\]

nikṛtaṃ vipralambhe'pi nīce viprakṛte'pi ca \|

nikṛtirbhartsane kṣepe nikṛtiḥ śaṭhaśāṭhyayoḥ \|\| 120 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 121 \[unit 845\]

nimittaṃ lakṣaṇe hetau nimittaṃ parvaṇi smṛtam \|

āganturdevādeśe ca niyatirniyame vidhau \|\| 121 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 122 \[unit 846\]

nirastaḥ preṣitaśare saṃtyakte tvaritodite \|

niṣṭhyūte'pi pratihate nirmmitastvanupadrute \|\| 122 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 123 \[unit 847\]

dikpālakālaparṇau tu puṃstriyoḥ syādanukramāt \|

nirvṛttiḥ susthitāsaukhyanirvāṇā'staṅgamādhvasu \|\| 123 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 124 \[unit 848\]

nirmuktastyaktasaṅge syāt tyaktakañcukapannage \|

nirvāto vātavigate vyāśraye dṛḍhavarmmaṇi \|\| 124 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 125 \[unit 849\]

niśāntastriṣu śānte syānniśānto bhavanoṣasoḥ \|

pañcatā mṛtyumātre'pi pañcabhāve'pi pañcatā \|\| 125 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 126 \[unit 850\]

paṇḍitaḥ sihlake dhīre patatpātukapakṣiṇoḥ \|

paddhatiḥ pathi paṅktau ca pareto vācyavanmṛte \|\| 126 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 127 \[unit 851\]

bhūtabhede'pyatha girau surarṣāvapi parvataḥ \|

paryāptaṃ vāraṇatuṣṭiyatheṣṭeṣvāptaśaktayoḥ \|\| 127 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 128 \[unit 852\]

vināśadoṣakṛcchreṣu daṇḍe tu matamavyayam \|

paryāptistu prakāme syātprāptau ca parirakṣaṇe \|\| 128 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 129 \[unit 853\]

paryastaḥ patitakṣiptanihateṣu triṣu triṣu \|

palitaṃ keśapāṃḍutve paṅke tāpe'pi śailaje \|\| 129 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 130 \[unit 854\]

pakṣatiḥ pakṣamūle syātpratipadyapi pakṣatiḥ \|

pārvatī draupadī durgā jīvantī śallakīdrume \|\| 130 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 131 \[unit 855\]

piṇḍito gaṇite sāndre pitsan pāte'pi pakṣiṇi \|

piśitā māsikāyāṃ syātpiśitaṃ palale matam \|\| 131 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 132 \[unit 856\]

pīḍitaṃ karaṇe strīṇāṃ yantrite bādhite'pi ca \|

puṭitaṃ syātkarapuṭe prasūtissyūtapoṭite \|\| 132 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 133 \[unit 857\]

pṛṣato'pi pṛṣadbindau mṛge tu pṛṣataḥ pṛṣan \|

syādduḥsvare'hite'pyevaṃ śvetabinduyute'nyavat \|\| 133 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 134 \[unit 858\]

prakṛtistu sattvarajastamasāṃ sāmyamātrake \|

svabhāvā'mātyapaureṣu liṅge yonau tathā''tmani \|\| 134 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 135 \[unit 859\]

prakṛtaṃ prastute'pi syātprakṛtaḥ prakṛtisthite \|

pravitaḥ śakaṭonmeye palānāmayutadvaye \|\| 135 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 136 \[unit 860\]

praṇītaḥ saṃskṛtāgnau syādvācyaliṅgaḥ praveśite \|

saṃskṛte copapanne nikṣipte vihite'pi ca \|\| 136 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 137 \[unit 861\]

pratītaḥ sādare khyāte hṛṣṭe dṛṣṭe virakṣaṇe \|

pratīta ete jñāte ca pratatirvratatau tatau \|\| 137 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 138 \[unit 862\]

prapāto nirjhare kṛcchre patanāvaṭayorapi \|

prabhūtamudgate prājye pramītaḥ prokṣite mṛte \|\| 138 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 139 \[unit 863\]

pravṛttirvṛttivārttāntapravāheṣu pravarttane \|

prasūtiḥ prasavotpattiputreṣu duhitaryapi \|\| 139 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 140 \[unit 864\]

prasūtaṃ kusume klībaṃ vācyavallabdhajanmani \|

prasṛtā tu prajātāyāṃ jaṃghāyāṃ prasṛtā matā \|\| 140 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 141 \[unit 865\]

prasṛto'rdhāñjalau samprasāre vegivinītayoḥ \|

pravṛtaṃ vitate kṣuṇṇe prokṣitaṃ sikta āhate \|\| 141 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 142 \[unit 866\]

prārthitaṃ yācite śatruruddhe'pyabhihate triṣu \|

vārddhitaṃ pūrite chinne vārddhitaṃ vṛddhiśālini \|\| 142 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 143 \[unit 867\]

bṛhatī mahatīkaṇṭakārikākalaśīṣu ca \|

vāci ca kṣudravārtākyāṃ chandobhedottarīyayoḥ \|\| 143 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 144 \[unit 868\]

bharatastu naṭe nāṭyaśāstre rāmā'nuje pumān \|

dauṣyantau śabare tantuvāye'pi bharataḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 144 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 145 \[unit 869\]

bhavatī bāṇabhede syāttriṣu yuṣmatsadarthayoḥ \|

vyāsarṣibhāṣite granthe jambūdvīpe'pi bhārataḥ \|\| 145 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 146 \[unit 870\]

vāgvāṇīpakṣiṇībhedavṛttibhedeṣu bhāratī \|

bhāvitaṃ vāsite lavdhe dhyāte'pyutpādite triṣu \|\| 146 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 147 \[unit 871\]

bhāsanto bhāsavihage sundare'pyabhidheyavat \|

bhāsvānābhāsvare sūrye bhūbhṛdbhūpālaśailayoḥ \|\| 147 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 148 \[unit 872\]

mathitaṃ nirjalodaśvityanavaghṛṣṭaloḍite \|

marutpuṃsi sure vāte mahadrājye napuṃsakam \|\| 148 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 149 \[unit 873\]

nāradasya tu vīṇāyāṃ mahatī syātpṛthau triṣu \|

mālatī jātiyuvatijyotsnānikṣu saridbhidi \|\| 149 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 150 \[unit 874\]

kākamācyagniśikhayormuṣitaṃ khaṇḍite hṛte \|

mūrcchitaṃ mohasaṃprāpte socchraye'pi dṛḍhe'pi ca \|\| 150 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 151 \[unit 875\]

rajataṃ rūpyahārebhadanteṣu viśade triṣu \|

ramatirnāyake svarge rasitaṃ khanite rute \|\| 151 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 152 \[unit 876\]

svarṇādikhacite tu syāttriṣveva rasitaṃ matam \|

revatī halikāntāyāṃ tārābhede'pi mātṛṣu \|\| 152 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 153 \[unit 877\]

raivataḥ śailabhede syātsuvarṇālau hareśvare \|

sarale'ndrāyudhe vīre rudhire'pi ca rohitam \|\| 153 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 154 \[unit 878\]

rohito lohite mīne mṛgabhede'pi rohiṇi \|

rohidarke pumāneva matā rohillatāntare \|\| 154 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 155 \[unit 879\]

lalitaṃ hārabhede syāttriṣveva laliteṣṭayoḥ \|

lohitaṃ kuṅkume rakte gośīrṣe raktacandane \|\| 155 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 156 \[unit 880\]

puṃsyeva maṅgale rakte nade nāge ca lohitaḥ \|

vanitā janitā'tyartharāgayoṣiti yoṣiti \|\| 156 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 157 \[unit 881\]

vanitaṃ yācite klībaṃ śodhite vanitaṃ triṣu \|

vasatiḥ syānniśāveśmāvasthāneṣvarhadāśrame \|\| 157 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 158 \[unit 882\]

vahaturvṛṣabhe pānthe vahatiḥ sacive gavi \|

vāpitaṃ vācyavadvījākṛtamuṇḍitayormmatam \|\| 158 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 159 \[unit 883\]

vāsantaḥ kokile mudge karabhe'vahite viṭe \|

vāsantī mādhavīyūthyorvāsantī pāṭalāvapi \|\| 159 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 160 \[unit 884\]

vāsitā kariṇīnāryorvāsitaṃ vihagārave \|

jñāne triṣveva vasanaveṣṭite surabhīkṛte \|\| 160 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 161 \[unit 885\]

vikṛtastriṣu bībhatse rogite syādasaṃskṛte \|

ḍimbe roge ca vikṛtirvigato niṣprabhe gate \|\| 161 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 162 \[unit 886\]

vicchittiraṅgarāge syādapi vicchedahāvayoḥ \|

vijātā tu prasūtāyāṃ vikṛte janite triṣu \|\| 162 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 163 \[unit 887\]

vitataṃ tu mataṃ vyāpte vistṛte'pyabhidheyavat \|

vidyuttaḍiti sandhyāyāṃ striyāṃ triṣveva niṣprabhe \|\| 163 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 164 \[unit 888\]

viditaṃ svīkṛte jñāte vidhātā vedhasi smare \|

vinataḥ praṇate bhugne śikṣite'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 164 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 165 \[unit 889\]

vinatā vainateyasya jananyāṃ piḍikāntare \|

vinītaḥ suvahāśve syādvinayāḍhye jitendriye \|\| 165 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 166 \[unit 890\]

upanīte'panīte'pi nibhṛte vaṇiji triṣu \|

vinetā''deśake rājñi vipattiryācanāpadoḥ \|\| 166 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 167 \[unit 891\]

vivṛtā kṣudraroge syādvivṛtaṃ tu triṣu triṣu \|

vivarttaṃ samudāye syādapravarttananṛtyayoḥ \|\| 167 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 168 \[unit 892\]

viviktaṃ vijane pūte'pyasaṃpṛktavivekini \|

viśrutaṃ jñātasaṃhṛṣṭapratīteṣu triṣu triṣu \|\| 168 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 169 \[unit 893\]

viśvastastriṣu viśrabdhe viśvastā vidhavā striyām \|

vihasto hastarahite vihvale ṣaṇḍhake'pi ca \|\| 169 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 170 \[unit 894\]

vṛttānto bhāvakārtsnye syādapi vārtāprakārayoḥ \|

prakriyāyāṃ prakaraṇe'pyekānte'pi kvacinmataḥ \|\| 170 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 171 \[unit 895\]

vellitaṃ kampite vakre plute syādvellitaṃ gatau \|

veṣṭitaṃ karaṇe strīṇāṃ lasake cāvṛte triṣu \|\| 171 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 172 \[unit 896\]

vyāghātastvantarāye syādyogabhedaprahārayoḥ \|

vyāyataṃ tu dṛḍhe dīrghe vyāpṛte'tiśaye'nyavat \|\| 172 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 173 \[unit 897\]

śakunto vihage pakṣibhede bhāsākhyapakṣiṇi \|

śuddhāntontaḥpure kakṣāntare rahasi ca smṛtaḥ \|\| 173 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 174 \[unit 898\]

rājayoṣiti śuddhāntā śrīpatiḥ nṛpakṛṣṇayoḥ \|

śrīmāṃstilakavṛkṣe syādīśvare'pi manohare \|\| 174 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 175 \[unit 899\]

saṅghātaḥ saṃhate puṃsi prahāre narakāntare \|

saṅgatiḥ saṅgate jñāne sannatirnutiśabdayoḥ \|\| 175 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 176 \[unit 900\]

santatistanayāputragotravistārapaṅktiṣu \|

paramparābhāve'pi syātsamāptistu samarthane \|\| 176 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 177 \[unit 901\]

vināśe saṃmatistu syādanumatyabhilāṣayoḥ \|

samitiḥ saṅgare sāmye sabhāyāṃ saṅgame'pi ca \|\| 177 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 178 \[unit 902\]

saṃvidājau pratijñāyāmācārajñānayoḥ striyām \|

saṃvittiḥ pratipattau syādavivāde janasya ca \|\| 178 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 179 \[unit 903\]

saṃvarttaḥ puṃsi kalpānte hāyane ca kalidrume \|

sikatā sikatāyuktadeśe syādāmayāntare \|\| 179 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 180 \[unit 904\]

sikatā vālukāyāṃ syuḥ śarkarāyāmapīṣyate \|

sukṛtaṃ tu śubhe puṇye klībaṃ suvihite triṣu \|\| 180 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 181 \[unit 905\]

sunītiḥ śobhananaye sunītirdhruvamātari \|

suvratā sukhasandohyagavarhatsadvrateṣu ca \|\| 181 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 182 \[unit 906\]

surataṃ syānnidhuvane suratve suratā matā \|

suhitastriṣu tṛpte syādukte suṣṭhuhite'pi ca \|\| 182 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 183 \[unit 907\]

sūnṛtaṃ maṅgale satyapriyavāci na vācyavat \|

saṃskṛtaṃ lakṣaṇopete kṛtrime triṣu saṃskṛtaḥ \|\| 183 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 184 \[unit 908\]

bhūṣite'pi praśaste'pi saṃhataṃ saṅgate dṛḍhe \|

skhalitaṃ tūcitādbhraṃśe skhalitaṃ calite triṣu \|\| 184 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 185 \[unit 909\]

stamitaṃ vītacāñcalyepyārdrībhūte'pi vācyavat \|

sthapatiḥ śalyabhede syādapi kañcukisūtayoḥ \|\| 185 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 186 \[unit 910\]

jīveṣṭiyājake cā'tha sthāpitaṃ nyastaniścite \|

suvarṇā tu matā nadyā saridauṣadhibhedayoḥ \|\| 186 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 187 \[unit 911\]

haritā maṇḍalāyāṃ syād haridvarṇayute triṣu \|

haridvāhe ca puṃsyeva haritaḥ kakubhi striyām \|\| 187 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 188 \[unit 912\]

klībaṃ tṛṇaprabhede'tha harmmitaṃ kṣiptadagdhayoḥ \|

hasantyāṅgāradhānyāṃ syānmallikāśākinībhidoḥ \|\| 188 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / 189 \[unit 913\]

hārītaḥ kaitave'pi syānmunipakṣiprabhedayoḥ \|

hṛṣitaṃ vismṛte prīte nate romāñcite hṛte \|\| 189 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatṛtīyam / fragment 914

kṣāritaṃ srāvite kṣāre'bhiśaste'pi ca vācyavat \|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 190 \[unit 915\]

tacaturtham \|

aṅgāritaṃ tu dagdhe syātpalāśakalikodgame \|\| 190 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 191 \[unit 916\]

atimuktastu vāsantyāṃ tiniśe niṣkale triṣu \|

atyāhitaṃ tu jīvanāpekṣakṛtye mahābhaye \|\| 191 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 192 \[unit 917\]

adhikṣiptaḥ parābhūte triṣu praṇihite'pi ca \|

syātpurātanavastre'pi navavastrepyanāhatam \|\| 192 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 193 \[unit 918\]

anumatistvapūrṇe tu pūrṇimānujñayoḥ striyām \|

matamantargataṃ madhye triṣu prāpte ca vismṛte \|\| 193 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 194 \[unit 919\]

bhavedapacito nyūne pūjitepyabhidheyavat \|

striyāmapacitiḥ pūjāniṣkṛtikṣayahāniṣu \|\| 194 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 195 \[unit 920\]

apāvṛtastu pihite svatantre syādapāvṛtaḥ \|

abhijātastriṣu nyāyye kulīnaprāptarūpayoḥ \|\| 195 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 196 \[unit 921\]

abhiyuktastriṣu dveṣisaṃruddhe'pyatitatpare \|

abhinīto bhavennyāyyasaṃskṛtāmarṣiṣu triṣu \|\| 196 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 197 \[unit 922\]

abhiśastistu lokāpavāde yācñābhiśāpayoḥ \|

udite'bhyudito yasminsupte'rkaḥ samudeti ca \|\| 197 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 198 \[unit 923\]

pumānarthapatirbhūpe īśvare kinnare triṣu \|

jñāte mūḍho'pyavasitaṃ klībaṃ gatyavasānayoḥ \|\| 198 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 199 \[unit 924\]

klībamācchuritaṃ hāsye śabdānvitanakhārpaṇe \|

āyuṣmān yogabhede nā cirajīvini vācyavat \|\| 199 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 200 \[unit 925\]

ujjṛmbhitaṃ tu ceṣṭāyāmutphulle tvabhidheyavat \|

udāsthitaścaredhyakṣe praṇidhau dvārapālake \|\| 200 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 201 \[unit 926\]

udgrāhitamupanyaste baddhagrāhitayorapi \|

upākṛto yajñahate paśāvupahate triṣu \|\| 201 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 202 \[unit 927\]

bhavedupacitaṃ digdhe samṛddhe ca samāhite \|

upāhito'nalotpāte pumānāropite triṣu \|\| 202 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 203 \[unit 928\]

rāhau sopaplave coparaktaḥ syādvyasanāntare \|

upasattistu sevāyāṃ saṅge'pi pratipādane \|\| 203 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 204 \[unit 929\]

matamullikhitaṃ tu syāttriṣūtkīrṇaṃ tanūkṛte \|

ṛṣyaproktā śatāvaryāṃ śūkaśiṃbyāṃ balābhidi \|\| 204 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 205 \[unit 930\]

airāvato'bhramātaṅge nāraṅge lakucadrume \|

airāvataṃ mataṃ dīrghasaralendraśarāsane \|\| 205 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 206 \[unit 931\]

striyāmairāvatī saudāmanīsaudāmanībhidoḥ \|

aṃśumānbhāskare śālaparṇyāmaṃśumatī striyām \|\| 206 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 207 \[unit 932\]

kaladhautaṃ kalārāve klībaṃ kanakarūpyayoḥ \|

kumudvatī kumudinyāṃ kuśapatnyāṃ kumudvatī \|\| 207 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 208 \[unit 933\]

kumudvānkumudaprāyadeśe syādabhidheyavat \|

klībaṃ kuharitaṃ dhvāne pikālāpe ratasvane \|\| 208 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 209 \[unit 934\]

kṛṣṇavṛntā pāṭalāyāṃ māṣaparṇyāmapi smṛtā \|\| 209 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 210 \[unit 935\]

matā gandhavatī madye medinyāṃ ca purībhidi \|

api yojanagandhāyāṃ garutmāṃstārkṣyapakṣiṇoḥ \|\| 210 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 211 \[unit 936\]

gṛhasthasatriṇorarthā''dhāne gṛhapatiḥ pumān \|

cakrāhutirddīrghabāhubhrame pūrṇāhutāvapi \|\| 211 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 212 \[unit 937\]

candrakānto maṇerbhede candrakāntaṃ tu kairave \|

carmmaṇvatī nadībhede kadalīcāravṛkṣayoḥ \|\| 212 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 213 \[unit 938\]

āṣāḍhaparvatasyāntaḥ kārutī nāma nimnagā \|

tasyāṃ māsopavāsinyāmapi cāruvratā smṛtā \|\| 213 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 214 \[unit 939\]

citragupto mato daṇḍadhāre tasya ca lekhake \|

divākīrtistu cāṇḍāle nāpite kākavairiṇi \|\| 214 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 215 \[unit 940\]

divābhīta ulūke syātkutsite kumudākare \|

dvīpavānabdhinadayordpīpavatyāpagābhuvoḥ \|\| 215 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 216 \[unit 941\]

dhūmaketurbṛhadbhānāvutpātagrahabhedayoḥ \|

nadīkānto jalanidhau sindhuvāre'pi hijjale \|\| 216 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 217 \[unit 942\]

nadīkāntā latājambūkākajaṅghāsu viśrutā \|

nandyāvarttaḥ pumānveśmaprabhede tagaradrume \|\| 217 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 218 \[unit 943\]

nāgadanto gajarade gṛhānnirgatadāruṇi \|

nāgadantī tu kumbhāyāṃ śrīhastinyāṃ ca dṛśyate \|\| 218 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 219 \[unit 944\]

asvādhyāye pratikṣepe nirākāre nirākṛtiḥ \|

triṣu nistuṣitaṃ tyakte tvacāśūnye laghūkṛte \|\| 219 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 220 \[unit 945\]

niṣkāśito nirgamite dhikkṛtepyujjhite triṣu \|

pañcaguptastu cārvākadarśane kamaṭhe'pi ca \|\| 220 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 221 \[unit 946\]

gatāptaceṣṭite jñāte lābhe parigataṃ matam \|

parighātaḥ samāghātā''yudhayoratha hāyane \|\| 221 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 222 \[unit 947\]

parivartto vinimaye kūrmmarāje palāyane \|

dante saprasave lākṣārakte pallavitaṃ triṣu \|\| 222 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 223 \[unit 948\]

pārāvataḥ kalarave śaile markaṭatinduke \|

pārāvatī tu gopālagīte'pi lavalīphale \|\| 223 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 224 \[unit 949\]

pārijātaḥ pāribhadre mandāre'pi ca pādape \|

pāśupataḥ paśupatidaivate bakapuṣpake \|\| 224 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 225 \[unit 950\]

puraskṛtaṃ bhavedagnakṛtābhyarcitayostriṣu \|

śaste śikte ripugraste svīkṛte'pi triṣu smṛtam \|\| 225 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 226 \[unit 951\]

puṣpadantastu dignāganāgavidyādharāntare \|

prajāpatiḥ kṣitipatau viriṃcye ca prajāpatiḥ \|\| 226 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 227 \[unit 952\]

triṣu praṇihitaṃ khyātaṃ nyaste lavdhe samāhite \|

bhavetpratihato dviṣṭe pratiskhalitaruddhayoḥ \|\| 227 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 228 \[unit 953\]

pratipaccetanāyāṃ syātpratipattāvapi smṛtā \|

pratipattiḥ padaprāptiḥ pratiprāptiśca gaurave \|\| 228 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 229 \[unit 954\]

pratipattiḥ prabodhe'pi saṃvitprāgalbhayorapi \|

pratikṛtiḥ pratīkāre pratibimbe ca pūjane \|\| 229 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 230 \[unit 955\]

pratikṣiptaṃ pratihate preṣite ca nirākṛte \|

pradhūpitastriṣu kliṣṭe sūryagamyadiśi striyām \|\| 230 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 231 \[unit 956\]

pravrajitā tu muṇḍīrīmāṃsyostriṣu tapasvini \|

bhagavānsugate pūjye triṣu gauryāṃ tu yoṣiti \|\| 231 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 232 \[unit 957\]

bhogavānnāṭyagānayorbhogavānahibhoginoḥ \|

matā bhogavatī nāgapuri nāgasarityapi \|\| 232 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 233 \[unit 958\]

raṅgamātā tu lākṣāyāṃ kuṭṭinyāmapi dṛśyate \|

lakṣmīpatirnṛpe viṣṇau pūgīphalalavaṅgayoḥ \|\| 233 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 234 \[unit 959\]

vanaspatirvinā puṣpaṃ phalivṛkṣe'pi pādape \|

vijṛmbhitaṃ vikasite'pyudgate veṣṭite triṣu \|\| 234 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 235 \[unit 960\]

vinipātastu daivādivyasane patane'pi ca \|

vivasvāṃstu pumānvāsareśvare tridiveśvare \|\| 235 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 236 \[unit 961\]

vivakṣitaṃ vaktumiṣṭe śobhane'pi vivakṣitam \|

vaijayanto dhvaje śakraprāsāde śarajanmani \|\| 236 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 237 \[unit 962\]

vaijayantī patākāyāṃ jayantī vahnimanthayoḥ \|

vyatīpāto yogabhede mahotpāte'pamānane \|\| 237 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 238 \[unit 963\]

mataḥ śatadhṛtiḥ pākaśāsane kamalāsane \|

śubhradantī maruddantī dantinīsuṃdarastriyoḥ \|\| 238 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 239 \[unit 964\]

saṃkhyāvānpaṇḍite puṃsi triṣu saṅkhyāyute mṛte \|

sadāgatirgandhavāhe nirvāṇe'pi sadīśvare \|\| 239 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 240 \[unit 965\]

samudrāntā tvanantāyāṃ kārpāsīpṛkkayorapi \|

samuddhataḥ samutkīrṇe'pyavinīte samuddhataḥ \|\| 240 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 241 \[unit 966\]

samāghāto vadhe yuddhe samādhisthe samāhitaḥ \|

triṣu nyastapratijñātasaṃsiddhe yama ātmani \|\| 241 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 242 \[unit 967\]

samāhitaṃ samādhāne vyasane'pi samāhitam \|

sarasvānrasike sindhau nade'pyatha sarasvatī \|\| 242 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 243 \[unit 968\]

nadībhede nadīdivyastrīgovāgdevatāgiri \|

sudhāsūtiḥ pumānyajñe kuraṅgatilake'pi ca \|\| 243 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 244 \[unit 969\]

sūryabhakto mato bandhujīve bhāskaradaivate \|

senāpatiranīkādhikṛte haimavatīsute \|\| 244 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacaturtham / 245 \[unit 970\]

himārātiḥ khale sūrye'nale haimavatī tu yā \|

gauryā harītakīsvarṇakṣīrīśvetavacāsu sā \|\| 245 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcamam

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcamam / 246 \[unit 971\]

tapaṃcamam \|

syādadhyavasiṃta jñāte gate kruddhe'pi veṣṭite \|

puṃsi śrīkaṇṭhavaikuṇṭhayajñabhede'parājitaḥ \|\| 246 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcamam / 247 \[unit 972\]

jayantīpārvatīviṣṇukāntāsu tvaparājitā \|

vācyaliṅgaḥ pipatiṣanpatanecchau khage pumān \|\| 247 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcamam / 248 \[unit 973\]

dṛṣṭe'valokitaṃ khyātaṃ lokanāthe'valokitaḥ \|

upadhūpita āsannamaraṇe paridhūpite \|\| 248 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcamam / 249 \[unit 974\]

gaṇādhipatirityeṣa pinākini vināyake \|

śvetāyāmapyasau vācyaliṅgastu syādanirjite \|\| 249 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcamam / 250 \[unit 975\]

sarvamukte'bhinirmuktaḥ supte yatrāstago raviḥ \|

pṛthivīpatirityukto bhūpāle ṛṣabhauṣadhe \|\| 250 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcamam / 251 \[unit 976\]

mūrdhābhiṣiktaḥ kṣmāpāle mantriṇi kṣatriye'pi ca \|

yādasāṃpatirambhodhau varuṇe yādasāṃpatiḥ \|\| 251 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcamam / 252 \[unit 977\]

vasantadūtaścūte'sau pikapañcamarāgayoḥ \|

vasantadūtīśavdastu pāṭalāvatimuktake \|\| 252 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / taṣaṣṭham

## tāntavargaḥ / taṣaṣṭham / 253 \[unit 978\]

taṣaṣṭham \|

arddhapārāvataścitrakaṇṭhe ca tittirāvapi \|

samudranavanītaṃ syādamṛte ca sudhānidhau \|\| 253 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / taṣaṣṭham / fragment 979

iti viśvalocane tāntavargaḥ \|

## thāntavargaḥ

## thāntavargaḥ / fragment 980

atha thāntavargaḥ \|

## thāntavargaḥ / thaikam

## thāntavargaḥ / thaikam / fragment 981

thaikam \|

thaḥ syācchiloccaye bhītatrāṇe thaṃ maṅgale'pi tham \|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 982\]

thadvitīyam \|

arthaḥ prayojane citte hetvabhiprāyavastuṣu \|\| 1 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 983\]

śabdābhidheye viṣaye syānnivṛttiprakārayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 984\]

āsthā tvālambanāpekṣāyatnāsthāneṣu dṛśyate \|

kanthā tu mṛttikābhittau kanthā prāvaraṇāntare \|\| 3 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 985\]

kuthaḥ strīpuṃsayorvarṇakambale puṃsi barhiṣu \|

kothastu netrarugbhede mathane śaṭite'pi ca \|\| 4 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 986\]

kvāthaḥ syādvyasane puṃsi dravaniṣpākaduḥkhayoḥ \|

gāthā vṛtte'pi vāgbhede granthastu dhanaśāstrayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 987\]

granthaḥ syādgranthanāyāṃ ca dvātriṃśadvarṇanirmmitau \|

granthirnā parvaṇi granthiparṇe rugbhidi ca striyām \|\| 6 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 988\]

kauṭilye bandhabhede ca tīrthaṃ śāstrāvatārayoḥ \|

puṇyakṣetramahāpātropāyopādhyāyadarśane \|\| 7 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 989\]

ṛṣijuṣṭe jale yajñe jātau ca vanitārttave \|

nīlīsūkṣmailayostutthā tutthognau tutthamañjane \|\| 8 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 990\]

duḥsthastu durgate mūrkhe pārthaḥ syātkakubhe'rjune \|

pātho divākare puṃsi pāthaḥ payasi na dvayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 991\]

pṛthurnṛpe kṛṣṇajīre vāpyāṃ strī mahati triṣu \|

sānau māne'striyāṃ prasthaḥ syādapyunmitavastuni \|\| 10 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 992\]

prothaḥ pānthe'śvaghoṇāyāmastrī nā kaṭigarbhayoḥ \|

vīthī gṛhataṭīpaṅktau nāṭyarūpakavartmanoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 993\]

mantho manthānadaṇḍe syāddvādaśātmani sāktave \|

mantho nayanaroge'pi yūthaṃ tiryakcaye caye \|\| 12 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 994\]

astrī yūthī tu māgadhyāṃ puṣpabhede kuraṇṭake \|

rathastu syandane kāye vetase caraṇe'pi ca \|\| 13 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 995\]

sārthaḥ syādvaṇijāṃ vṛnde vṛndamātre'pi dṛśyate \|

sikthaṃ nīlyāṃ madhūcchiṣṭe siktho naudanasambhave \|\| 14 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 996\]

saṃsthā nāśe vyavasthāyāṃ vyaktisādṛśyayoḥ sthitau \|

saṃsthā kratau samāptau ca care ca nijarāṣṭrage \|\| 15 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam / 16 \[unit 997\]

thatṛtīyam \|

atithiḥ syātprāghuṇake kopepi kuśaputrake \|

triṣvavyatho vyathāhīne pathyāyāṃ pannage'vyathaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam / 17 \[unit 998\]

aśvatthaḥ pūrṇimāyāṃ ca gardabhāṇḍe ca pippale \|

udrathastamracūḍe'pi mahendre mahakāmuke \|\| 17 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam / 18 \[unit 999\]

unmāthaḥ kūṭayantre syādapi māraṇaghātayoḥ \|

upasthastu bhage liṅge'pyutsaṅge'pi gude pumān \|\| 18 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam / 19 \[unit 1000\]

kāyasthastu nṛṇāṃ jātiprabhede paramātmani \|

kāyasthā syādvayasthāyāṃ pathyāyāṃ kāyage triṣu \|\| 19 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam / 20 \[unit 1001\]

gogranthistu karīṣe syādgoṣṭhe gojihvikauṣadhau \|

damathastu dame daṇḍe nirgranthaḥ kṣapaṇe'dhane \|\| 20 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam / 21 \[unit 1002\]

vāliśe'pi niśīthastu niśāmātrārddharātrayoḥ \|

pramathaḥ śaṅkaragaṇe pathyāyāṃ pramathā tathā \|\| 21 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam / 22 \[unit 1003\]

vayaḥsthā śālmalīpathyākākolyāmalakīṣu ca \|

brāhmītruṭiguḍūcīṣu vayasthataruṇe triṣu \|\| 22 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam / 23 \[unit 1004\]

manmathaḥ kāmacintāyāṃ kāmadevakapitthayoḥ \|

vamathuḥ puṃsi vamane mātaṅgakaraśīkare \|\| 23 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam / 24 \[unit 1005\]

varūtho rathaguptau nā varūthaṃ carmaveśmani \|

vidatho yogikṛtinoḥ śamathaḥ śāntyamātyayoḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam / 25 \[unit 1006\]

ṣaḍgranthā tu vacāśaṭyoḥ ṣaṅgranthaḥ karañjāntare \|

samarthastūdbhaṭe śakte sambaddhārthe hite triṣu \|\| 25 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatṛtīyam / 26 \[unit 1007\]

sarvārthasiddhe siddhārthaḥ siddhārthā sitasarṣape \|

kṣavathuḥ puṃsi kāse syācchikkāyāmapi sammataḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thacaturtham

## thāntavargaḥ / thacaturtham / 27 \[unit 1008\]

thacaturtham \|

anīkastho raṇasthale cihneṣu bhaṭamardane \|

rājarakṣiṣu mātaṅgaśikṣaṇātivicakṣaṇe \|\| 27 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thacaturtham / 28 \[unit 1009\]

bhaveditikathā grāmyakathāpranaṣṭadharmayoḥ \|

bācyavaddaśamīsthaḥ syātsthavirakṣīṇarāgayoḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thacaturtham / fragment 1010

vānaprastho madhuṣṭhīle tṛtīyāśramikiṃśuke \|

## thāntavargaḥ / thapañcamam

## thāntavargaḥ / thapañcamam / 29 \[unit 1011\]

thapaṃcamam \|

bhaṭe puṃsyapratirathaṃ yātrāyāṃ sāmni maṅgale \|\| 29 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thapañcamam / fragment 1012

iti viśvalocane thāntavargaḥ \|

## dāntavargaḥ

## dāntavargaḥ / fragment 1013

atha dāntavargaḥ \|

## dāntavargaḥ / daikam

## dāntavargaḥ / daikam / 1 \[unit 1014\]

daikam \|

daḥ śuddhau devane dāstu dātari cchedadānayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 1015\]

dadvitīyam \|

anduḥ striyāmalaṅkāre vedabaṃdhanavastunoḥ \|

abdaḥ saṃvatsare meghe mustake parvatāntare \|\| 2 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 1016\]

kando'strī śūraṇe vṛkṣamūle puṃsi payodhare \|

kundo māghye pumāṃścakre bhramau nidhisuradviṣoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 1017\]

viṣṇubhrātari roge ca mataḥ śastrāntare gadā \|

chadaḥ patre patatre ca granthiparṇatamālayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 1018\]

chando'bhiprāyavaśayordhīdā kanyāmanīṣayoḥ \|

nadī sarityapi nadaḥ sindhau śoṇāvinādayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 1019\]

nandiḥ śivapratīhāre dyūtabhāṇḍabhidormudi \|

nandā maṇikasampattyornindā kutsā'pavādayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 1020\]

padaṃ vākye pratiṣṭhāyāṃ vyavasāyā'padeśayoḥ \|

pādātaccihnayoḥ śabde sthānatrāṇāṅghrivastuṣu \|\| 7 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 1021\]

pādo'strī caraṇe mūle turīyāṃśe'pi dīdhitau \|

śailapratyantaśaile nā vidā jñāne matāvapi \|\| 8 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 1022\]

binduḥ syāddantadaśane śukre veditṛvipruṣoḥ \|

vediraṅgulimudrāyāṃ budhe saṃskṛtabhūtale \|\| 9 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 1023\]

bhandaṃ(draṃ) śarmmaṇi kalyāṇe bhedo dvaidhaviśeṣayoḥ \|

vidāraṇe copajāpe saṃpūrvaḥ sindhusaṅgame \|\| 10 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 1024\]

mado mṛgamade madye dānamudgarvaretasi \|

mahāpūrvo mataṅge syānmadī kṛṣakavastuni \|\| 11 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 1025\]

mandaḥ svaire khale mandarate mūrkhālparogiṣu \|

abhāgye'pi triṣu pumān gajajātyantare śanau \|\| 12 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 1026\]

mṛdvatīkṣṇe triṣu ślakṣṇe rado dante vilekhane \|

śādastu kardame śaṣpe sūdaḥ syādvyañjane guṇe \|\| 13 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 1027\]

svādurmiṣṭhe manojñe ca svedaḥ svedanagharmmayoḥ \|

hṛccittabukkayoḥ klībaṃ kṣodaścūrṇe'pi peṣaṇe \|\| 14 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 15 \[unit 1028\]

datṛtīyam \|

aṅgado vāliputre syātkeyūre tvaṅgadaṃ matam \|

bhaveddakṣiṇadigdantīdantinyāṃ tu matā'ṅgadā \|\| 15 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 16 \[unit 1029\]

astrī saṅkhyāntare māṃsakīle śaile'pi nā'rbudaḥ \|

arddhendurarddhacandre syādgalahastanakhāṅkayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 17 \[unit 1030\]

ardhenduḥ syādatiprauḍhastrīguhyāṅguliyojane \|

ākrando dāruṇaraṇe mitre tātārirodane \|\| 17 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 18 \[unit 1031\]

pārṣṇigrāhātparo rājā yastasminnārade'pi ca \|

sugandhimudi vāmoda āspadaṃ padakṛtyayoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 19 \[unit 1032\]

strī kakut kakudo'pyastrī vṛṣāṅge rājalakṣmaṇi \|

śṛṅge śreṣṭhe kapardastu vaṭe śambhujaṭāṭayoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 20 \[unit 1033\]

karkanduḥ sākṣare śāke vārijāle gudāmaye \|

utkṣiptikāyāṃ karṇānduḥ karṇapālyāmapi striyām \|\| 20 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 21 \[unit 1034\]

kāmadā dhenukāyāṃ syādvācyavatkāmadogdhari \|

kumudo nāgadignāgadaityāntaravanaukasi \|\| 21 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 22 \[unit 1035\]

kumudaṃ kairave klībaṃ kṛpaṇe kumudanyavat \|

kusīdike kusīdaḥ syātkusīdaṃ vṛddhijīvane \|\| 22 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 23 \[unit 1036\]

kaumudaḥ kārttike jyotsnāparvaṇorapi kaumudī \|

kravyātkravyādavatpuṃsi māṃsabhakṣakarakṣasoḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 24 \[unit 1037\]

govinda indrāvaraje gavādhyakṣe ca gīṣpatau \|

goṣpadaṃ gopadaśvabhre gavāṃ ca gatigocare \|\| 24 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 25 \[unit 1038\]

balāhako'pi jalado jalado mustake'pi ca \|

jīvado dviṣi vaidye ca taratkāraṇḍave plave \|\| 25 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 26 \[unit 1039\]

toyado mustake meghe toyadaṃ tu ghṛtaṃ matam \|

daradbhaye prapāte'drau dāyādo jñātiputrayoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 27 \[unit 1040\]

dāradaḥ pārade sindhau hiṅgule garalāntare \|

dṛṣatpeṣaṇapāṣāṇapaṭṭapāṣāṇayoḥ striyām \|\| 27 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 28 \[unit 1041\]

dhanado dātari śrīde krīḍāmātye tu narmmadaḥ \|

narmadā narmadāyinyāṃ revāyāmapi narmadā \|\| 28 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 29 \[unit 1042\]

naladaṃ makarande syānmāsikośīrayorapi \|

nirvādastu parīvādaparaninditavādayoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 30 \[unit 1043\]

niṣādaḥ svarabhede'pi niṣādaḥ pacapace'pi ca \|

praṇādo'tyuccaśabde syātpraṇādaḥ karṇarugbhidi \|\| 30 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 31 \[unit 1044\]

pramadā mattakāśinyāṃ pramado garvitāmudi \|

prasādastu prasannatve kāvyālaṅkaraṇāntare \|\| 31 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 32 \[unit 1045\]

svāsthye cānugrahe cātha prahlādaḥ praṇade'sure \|

prāsādaḥ puṃsi devasya naradevasya vā''laye \|\| 32 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 33 \[unit 1046\]

kanyāyāṃ varadā śānte prasanne varadastriṣu \|

bhasatpuṃsyeva kāle syādbhasanmāṃse prabhāsure \|\| 33 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 34 \[unit 1047\]

maryādā tu sthitau sīmni kūle kūle ca vāridheḥ \|

mākandatu rasāle syānmākandyāmalakīphale \|\| 34 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 35 \[unit 1048\]

mainādaśchāgamārjārameghanādānulāsiṣu \|

vātardirvalkale kāṣṭhalohīvedekayoḥ striyām \|\| 35 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 36 \[unit 1049\]

viśadaḥ pāṇḍare vyakte śaratstrī śaradabdayoḥ \|

śāradā jalapippalyāṃ saptaparṇe'tha śāradaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 37 \[unit 1050\]

navā'pratimaśālīnapītamudgenduvarṣayoḥ \|

striyāṃ sampadguṇotkarṣe bhūtihāraprabhedayoḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 38 \[unit 1051\]

saṃvitpratijñāsaṅketajñānācāreṣu nāmani \|

striyāṃ toṣe kriyākāre raṇe sambhāṣaṇe'pi ca \|\| 38 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / 39 \[unit 1052\]

sambhedastu vikāśe syātsambhedaḥ sindhusaṅgame \|

sunandā rocanānāryoḥ kṣaṇado gaṇake pumān \|\| 39 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datṛtīyam / fragment 1053

triṣūtsavaprade vāri kṣaṇadaṃ kṣaṇadā niśi \|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 40 \[unit 1054\]

dacaturtham \|

apavādastu nidrāyāmājñāviśvāsayorapi \|\| 40 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 41 \[unit 1055\]

abhiṣyando vivṛddhau syādāstāve locanāmaye \|

abhimarddastu puṃsyeva raṇamanthānadaṇḍayoḥ \|\| 41 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 42 \[unit 1056\]

aṣṭāpadaṃ śāriphale klībamastrī tu kāñcane \|

śarabhe markaṭe puṃsi candramallyāṃ striyāmapi \|\| 42 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 43 \[unit 1057\]

ekapadaṃ syāttatkāle klībamekapadī pathi \|

kaṭukandaḥ pumān śṛṅgavere śigrurasonayoḥ \|\| 43 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 44 \[unit 1058\]

kuruvindastu mustāyāṃ kulmāṣavrīhibhedayoḥ \|
