kuruvindaṃ tu mukure padmarāge ca hiṅgule \|\| 44 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 45 \[unit 1059\]

klībaṃ kokanadaṃ raktakairave raktapaṅkaje \|

cakrabundastu bhākūṭe pṛṣṭhaśṛṅge mṛṣāntare \|\| 45 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 46 \[unit 1060\]

catuṣpado gavāśvādipaśau strīkaraṇāntare \|

pumāñjanapado deśe tathā janapado jane \|\| 46 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 47 \[unit 1061\]

tamonudastamonucca candrasūryakṛśānuṣu \|

parīvādo'pavāde syādvīṇāvādanavastuni \|\| 47 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 48 \[unit 1062\]

pṛṣṭhamarddo'tidhṛṣṭe syānnāṭyoktyāṃ nāyakapriye \|

puṭabhedo nadīvakre nagarātodyayorapi \|\| 48 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 49 \[unit 1063\]

pratipattu striyāmādyatithau saṃvidi sā smṛtā \|

priyaṃvadaḥ khecare syātpriyavāci tu vācyavat \|\| 49 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 50 \[unit 1064\]

mahānādo mahāśabde varṣukābde śayānake \|

gaje ca mucukundastu munidaityadrumāntare \|\| 50 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 51 \[unit 1065\]

meghanādo daśagrīvasute paścimadikpatau \|

viśāradaḥ paṇḍite syāttriṣu dhṛṣṭe viśāradaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacaturtham / 52 \[unit 1066\]

pṛtvākūṭe prapañce ca mṛge śūke pade matam \|

samaryādaṃ samīpe syānmaryādinyapi vācyat \|\| 52 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dapañcamam

## dāntavargaḥ / dapañcamam / 53 \[unit 1067\]

dapañcamam \|

dharme rahasyupaniṣadvedānte pārśvaveśmani \|

sahasrapādo mārttaṇḍe kāraṇḍepi ca yajvani \|\| 53 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dapañcamam / fragment 1068

iti viśvalocane dāntavargaḥ \|

## dhāntavargaḥ

## dhāntavargaḥ / fragment 1069

atha dhāntavargaḥ \|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhaikam

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhaikam / fragment 1070

dhaikam \|

dho dhane ca dhaneśe ca dhāstu dhātari dhī matau \|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 1071\]

dhadvitīyam \|

andhaṃ syāttimire dṛṣṭihīne tvandho'bhidheyavat \|\| 1 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 1072\]

abdhirvārāṃnidhau puṃsi puṃsyevā'bdhiḥ sarovare \|

arddhaṃ samāṃśake klībamarddhaḥ khaṇḍe pumānapi \|\| 2 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 1073\]

puṃsyādhiścittapīḍāyāṃ pratyāśāyāṃ ca bandhake \|

vyasane cāpyadhiṣṭhāne syādiddhastvātape pumān \|\| 3 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 1074\]

pradīpte triṣu ṛddhaṃ tu sampannānnasamṛddhayoḥ \|

ṛddhiḥ syādauṣadhībhede yogaśaktau ca bandhane \|\| 4 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 1075\]

gandho gandhakasambandhaleśeṣvāmodagarvayoḥ \|

gādhaḥ sthāne'pi lipsāyāṃ godhā talanihākayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 1076\]

dagdhā sthitārkakāṣṭhāyāṃ dagdhaṃ pluṣṭe'nyaliṅgakaḥ \|

dadhi syācchrīghane klībaṃ dadhi śrīvāsavāsayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 1077\]

viṣāktaviśikhe digdho digdhaṃ liptārthake'nyavat \|

triṣu prapūrite dugdhaṃ dugdhaṃ kṣīre'pi na dvayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 1078\]

vatse gope kavau dogdhā dogdhā'pyarthopajīvini \|

sajje saṃpūrvakaṃ naddhaṃ naddhaṃ tadvṛtabaddhayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 1079\]

ādhibandhanayorvedho bandhaḥ saṃpūrvako'nvaye \|

bandhūkapādape bandhurvadhūbhrātari bāndhave \|\| 9 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 1080\]

bādhā duḥkhe niṣedhe ca vipūrvā tu viheṭhane \|

budhastu sugate dhīre saumye ca bughite triṣu \|\| 10 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 1081\]

budhaḥ syātpaṇḍite saumye budhaḥ kvāpi tathāgate \|

ṛddhistu varddhane ṛddhyauṣadhe mudi kalāntare \|\| 11 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 1082\]

vṛddhiḥ kuruṇḍaroge ca vṛddhiryoge'pi dṛśyate \|

vṛddho rūḍhe kavau jīrṇe triṣu vṛddhaṃ tu śailaje \|\| 12 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 1083\]

bodhiḥ samādhibhede syādbodhirbodhimahīruhe \|

madhu puṣparase kṣaudre madyakṣīrā'su na dvayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 1084\]

madhurmmadhūke surabhau caitre daityāntare pumān \|

jīvāśāke striyāmevaṃ madhu-śabdaḥ prayujyate \|\| 14 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 1085\]

siddhaṃ cittābhisaṃkṣepe siddhamālasyanidrayoḥ \|

sundare vācyavanmugdho mugdho mūḍhe'pi vācyavat \|\| 15 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 16 \[unit 1086\]

meghaḥ kratau matau medhā medhistu khaladāruṇi \|

rādhā tu vallavībhede citrabhede ca dhanvinām \|\| 16 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 17 \[unit 1087\]

syādviśākhātaḍidviṣṇukrāntātiṣyaphalāsu ca \|

rādhastu puṃsi vaiśākhe lubdho mṛgayukāṃkṣiṇoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 18 \[unit 1088\]

vadhūḥ snuṣāyāṃ bhāryāyāṃ vadhūryoṣinnavoḍhayoḥ \|

śaṭyāṃ ca sārivāyāṃ ca spṛkkāyāṃ ca matā vadhūḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 19 \[unit 1089\]

bhavedvidhaṃ tu sādṛśye vedhitakṣiptayostriṣu \|

vidhirvedhasi kāle nā vidhāne niyatau striyām \|\| 19 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 20 \[unit 1090\]

vidhā prakāre ṛddhau ca gajānne vetane vidhau \|

vidhuḥ śaśāṅke viṣṇau ca kapūre rākṣasāntare \|\| 20 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 21 \[unit 1091\]

vyādhiḥ syādāmaye vyāpye vyādho mṛgayuduṣṭhayoḥ \|

śuddhaṃ tu kevale pūte śraddhā śrāddhordhvakāṅkṣayoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 22 \[unit 1092\]

śrāddhaṃ nivāpe śrāddhastu triṣu śraddhāsamanvite \|

sandhā sthitau pratijñāyāmavadhāne'pi sā smṛtā \|\| 22 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 23 \[unit 1093\]

sandhiḥ puṃsi suraṅgāyāṃ randhrasaṃghaṭṭane bhage \|

sandhirbhāge'vakāśe'pi vāṭasaṃjñe'pi puṃsyayam \|\| 23 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 24 \[unit 1094\]

sādhurvārddhuṣike puṃsi cārusajjanayostriṣu \|

siddhastu nitye niṣpanne prasiddhe devayoniṣu \|\| 24 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 25 \[unit 1095\]

yoge'pyāḍiprabhede ca siddhirnniṣpattiyogayoḥ \|

sadvyākhyābheṣaje siddhiḥ siddhivṛddhyākhyabheṣaje \|\| 25 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 26 \[unit 1096\]

sindhurabdhau nade deśībhede nā sariti striyām \|

sudhā'mṛte sudhā mūrvāsnuhīgāṅgeṣṭikāsu ca \|\| 26 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 27 \[unit 1097\]

sṛdhūrvṛddhau gude'pi syātskandhaḥ kāyaprakāṇḍayoḥ \|

bāhūmūle samūhe ca samīhāyāṃ samīhatau \|\| 27 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / 28 \[unit 1098\]

skandho narāśvamātaṅgavṛnde bhadrādikṛtyake \|

snigdho vātsalyasaṃpanne cikkaṇe'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 28 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvitīyam / fragment 1099

sparddhā saṃharṣaṇe sāmye sparddhā kramasamunnatau \|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 29 \[unit 1100\]

dhatṛtīyam \|

agādhamatalasparśe triṣu śvabhre napuṃsakam \|\| 29 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 30 \[unit 1101\]

avadhirnā'vadhau na syātsīmni kāle vile'vaṭe \|

ānaddhaṃ triṣu baddhe syādānaddhaṃ murajādike \|\| 30 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 31 \[unit 1102\]

āvandhaḥ premṇyalaṅkāre dṛḍhabandhe'pi kīrttitaḥ \|

āviddhaḥ prahate vakre'pyutsedhaḥ kāya ucchraye \|\| 31 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 32 \[unit 1103\]

vyāje'pi cakre'pyupadhirupādhirnā viśeṣaṇe \|

kaitave dharmmacintāyāṃ kuṭumbavyāpṛte'pi ca \|\| 32 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 33 \[unit 1104\]

kavandhastu hare rāhau rakṣobhede mataḥ pumān \|

kabandhaṃ vāri na strī tu gatamūrddhakalevare \|\| 33 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 34 \[unit 1105\]

durvidhau duḥkhikhalayornirodho rodhanāśayoḥ \|

niṣadhaḥ parvate deśe tadrāje kaṭhinepi ca \|\| 34 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 35 \[unit 1106\]

nyagrodhastu vaṭe śamyāṃ nyagrodho vyāmamātrake \|

nyagrodhī viṣapaya ca mohanākhyauṣadhāvapi \|\| 35 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 36 \[unit 1107\]

paridhiryajñiyataroḥ śākhāyāmupasūryake \|

praṇidhiryācyācarayoḥ prasiddhaḥ khyātabhūṣite \|\| 36 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 37 \[unit 1108\]

māgadho magadhodgate kṣatriyāvaiśyaje triṣu \|

bandijīrakayoḥ puṃsi kaṇāyūthyostu māgadhī \|\| 37 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 38 \[unit 1109\]

paryāhārādhvabhāreṣu paṇye vivadhavīvadhau \|

vibudhaḥ paṇḍite deve viśrabdhaṃ tu bhṛśārthakam \|\| 38 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 39 \[unit 1110\]

viśrabdhaḥ syāttu viśvastā'nudbhaṭeṣu triṣu triṣu \|

latāyāṃ viṭape vīrutsannaddho vyūḍhavarmmite \|\| 39 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 40 \[unit 1111\]

sannidhiḥ sannidhāne sī pumānindriyagocare \|

samādhirdhyānanīvākaniyameṣu samarthane \|\| 40 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / 41 \[unit 1112\]

sambādhaḥ saṅkaṭe yonau saṅgarepi sugandhi tu \|

śaileye'bhīṣṭagandhe ca saṃrodhaḥ kṣeparodhayoḥ \|\| 41 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatṛtīyam / fragment 1113

saṃsiddhistu matā śrīmattinīprakṛtisiddhiṣu \|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacaturtham

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacaturtham / 42 \[unit 1114\]

dhacaturtham \|

aniruddhaḥ smarasute puṃsi cānargale triṣu \|\| 42 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacaturtham / 43 \[unit 1115\]

anubandhaḥ prakṛtyādernaśvare'pyanuyāyini \|

doṣotpāde śiśau ca syātpravṛttasyānuvarttane \|\| 43 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacaturtham / 44 \[unit 1116\]

anubandhī tu hikkāyāṃ tṛṣṇāyāmapi dṛśyate \|

avarodhastu śuddhānte'pyantarddhau rājasadmani \|\| 44 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacaturtham / 45 \[unit 1117\]

syādavaṣṭavdha ākrānte'pyadūre'pyavilambite \|

āśābandhaḥ samāśvāse markaṭasya ca vāsake \|\| 45 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacaturtham / 46 \[unit 1118\]

ikṣugandhā kokilākṣe kāśe kroṣṭyāṃ ca gokṣure \|

ugragandhā bacāyāṃ syādyavānyāṃ chikkikauṣadhau \|\| 46 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacaturtham / 47 \[unit 1119\]

upalabdhiḥ striyāṃ prāptimatijñāneṣu lakṣaṇe \|

kālaskandhastamāle'pi tinduke jīvakadrume \|\| 47 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacaturtham / 48 \[unit 1120\]

tīkṣṇagandho mataḥ śigrau vacārājikayoḥ striyām \|

tṛṇagodhā bhaveccitrakolake kṛkalāsake \|\| 48 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacaturtham / 49 \[unit 1121\]

parivyādhaḥ pumānnīravānīre'pi drumotpale \|

brahmabandhuradhikṣipte nirdeśe'brāhmaṇasya ca \|\| 49 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacaturtham / 50 \[unit 1122\]

mahauṣadhaṃ viṣāśuṇṭhī śṛṅgavere rasonake \|

samunnaddhaḥ samudbhūte paṇḍitammanyagarvite \|\| 50 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhapañcamam

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhapañcamam / 51 \[unit 1123\]

dhapañcamam \|

yojanagandhā tu kastūryāṃ vyāsasūsītayorapi \|\| 51 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhapañcamam / fragment 1124

iti viśvalocane dhāntavargaḥ \|

## nāntavargaḥ

## nāntavargaḥ / fragment 1125

atha nāntavargaḥ \|

## nāntavargaḥ / naikam

## nāntavargaḥ / naikam / 1 \[unit 1126\]

naikam \|

nāstu netari nāvi strī nakāro jinapūjyayoḥ \|

nuḥ stotari nutau strī ca syādannaṃ bhaktamuktayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 1127\]

nadvitīyam \|

inaḥ patyau nṛpe sūrye'pyunnaṃ klinne ratāntare \|

raṇodyoge bhavedūnamūne nyūnā'bhidheyavat \|\| 2 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 1128\]

niśśeṣe triṣu kṛtsnaṃ syākṛtsnaṃ syādudare jale \|

gānaṃ gīte'pi śabde'pi garhaṇe tu vipūrvakam \|\| 3 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 1129\]

ghanaṃ syātkāṃsyatālādivādye madhyamatāṇḍave \|

ghanastu meghe mustāyāṃ vistāre lohamudgare \|\| 4 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 1130\]

kāṭhinye cātha kaṭhine sāndre'pi ca ghanastriṣu \|

cihnamaṅke patākāyāṃ dhvajamātre'pi na dvayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 1131\]

cīno deśāṃśukavrīhitantubhede mṛgāntare \|

rahasi cchādite channamutpūrvaṃ channamujjvale \|\| 6 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 1132\]

chinnā'mṛtāyāṃ puṃścalyāṃ chinnaṃ bhinne'bhidheyavat \|

jano loke maharlokātpare loke ca pāmare \|\| 7 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 1133\]

janī sīmantinīvadhvoḥ striyāṃ tu janirudbhave \|

jinastvarhati buddhe'tivṛddhajitvarayostriṣu \|\| 8 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 1134\]

jyotsnā tu candrikāyāṃ syātsyāllatāyāṃ vibhāvarau \|

jyotsnī paṭolikāyāṃ ca candrakānvitaniśyapi \|\| 9 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 1135\]

jyānirhānau taṭinyāṃ ca tanurdehatvacoḥ striyām \|

tanuḥ keśe'pi virale svalpamātre'pi vācyavat \|\| 10 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 1136\]

dānaṃ tyāge gajamade chede śuddhau ca rakṣapau \|

vikrānte vācyavaddānurddānadātari vācyavat \|\| 11 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 1137\]

kātare durgate dīno dīnā mūṣikayoṣiti \|

dyumnaṃ parākrame vitte prapūrvaṃ puṃsi manmathe \|\| 12 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 1138\]

dhanuḥ puṃsi priyāladrau rāśibhede'pi kāmuke \|

dhanaṃ tu godhane vitte dhānā bhṛṣṭayave striyām \|\| 13 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 1139\]

dhānyāke'pyaṅkure'bdhau tu dheno dhenī sarityapi \|

nagnastriṣu vivastre syātpuṃsi kṣapaṇabandinoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 1140\]

nyūnamūne'pi garhye'pi pānaṃ pītau ca rakṣaṇe \|

vanaṃ tu kānane nīre'pyutse vāsapravāsayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 16 \[unit 1141\]

vastraṃ tu vasane mūlye vetanadravyayorapi \|

budhnaḥ śiphāyāmīśāne bhānuḥ sūrye'pi dīdhitau \|\| 16 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 17 \[unit 1142\]

bhinnaṃ vācyavadanyārthe dārite saṅgate sphuṭam \|

mānaṃ pramāṇe prasthādau mānaścittonnatau grahe \|\| 17 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 18 \[unit 1143\]

mānaḥ syādapi pūjāyāṃ mīno rāśyantare jhaṣe \|

munirvācaṃyame buddhe priyālā'gastikiṃśuke \|\| 18 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 19 \[unit 1144\]

iṅgudyāmapi mṛtsnā tu tuvarīmṛtsnayormatā \|

yānaṃ bāhyagatau yonirdvayoḥ syādākare bhage \|\| 19 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 20 \[unit 1145\]

ratnaṃ maṇāvapi śreṣṭhe ratnaścakṣakacaṇḍayoḥ \|

rāsnā tu syādbhujaṅgākṣyāmelāparṇyāmapi smṛtā \|\| 20 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 21 \[unit 1146\]

rāśīnāmudaye lagnaṃ lagnaṃ sakte'pi lajjite \|

vānaṃ śuṣkaphale śuṣkasyūtayostriṣvatha dvayoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 22 \[unit 1147\]

vanyāsuraṅgāvātormisaurabheṣu kaṭe gatau \|

vinnaṃ jñāte sthite labdhe śīno'jagaramūrkhayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 23 \[unit 1148\]

puṃsyeva patriṇi śyenaḥ śyenaḥ śvete'bhidheyavat \|

sānuḥ śṛṅge budhe'raṇye vātyāyāṃ pallave pathi \|\| 23 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 24 \[unit 1149\]

sūnuḥ putre'nuje sūrye sūnurduhitari striyām \|

sūnaṃ prasūne prasave sūnamucchvasite triṣu \|\| 24 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 25 \[unit 1150\]

sūnā putryāṃ vadhasthāne galaśuṇḍyāmapīṣyate \|

styānaṃ lomni pratiśrutyāṃ matā snigdhe tu vācyavat \|\| 25 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 26 \[unit 1151\]

sthānaṃ sthitau ca sādṛśye saṃniveśā'vakāśayoḥ \|

sthāne syādavyayaṃ khyātaṃ yuktārthakaraṇārthayoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / 27 \[unit 1152\]

syūno'rke kiraṇe svapnaḥ suptadhīsvāpadarśane \|

hanuḥ kapolāvayave mṛtyau praharaṇe'striyām \|\| 27 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvitīyam / fragment 1153

gade haṭṭavilāsinyāṃ hīnaṃ garhyonayostriṣu \|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 28 \[unit 1154\]

natṛtīyam \|

aṅganaṃ prāṅgaṇe yānepyaṅganā nāyikāntare \|\| 28 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 29 \[unit 1155\]

aṅganā vāmanebhasya hastinyāmapi dṛśyate \|

añjano dikkarīndre syādañjanaṃ tu rasāñjane \|\| 29 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 30 \[unit 1156\]

akṣikajjalasauvīre giribhede'nyathāñjane \|

jyeṣṭhībhede marutpatnyāmañjanī lepyayoṣiti \|\| 30 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 31 \[unit 1157\]

adhvā vartmani saṃkleśe skande saṃsthānakālayoḥ \|

apāno gudavāte syādapānaṃ tu gude matam \|\| 31 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 32 \[unit 1158\]

ābjinī visinītyādipadānyabjasarovare \|

mahāsahāyāmāmlānaḥ puṃsyeva triṣu nirmale \|\| 32 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 33 \[unit 1159\]

ayanaṃ pathi bhānośca dakṣiṇottaratogatau \|

nā'ratniḥ kaphaṇau haste prakoṣṭhavitatāṅgulau \|\| 33 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 34 \[unit 1160\]

arjunaḥ pārthakakubhakārttavīryaśikhaṇḍiṣu \|

māturekasute'pi syādarjuno dhavale'nyavat \|\| 34 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 35 \[unit 1161\]

arjunī gavyuṣāyāṃca kuṭṭinīkaratoyayoḥ \|

arjunaṃ tu tṛṇe netraroge'pi klībamarjunam \|\| 35 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 36 \[unit 1162\]

arthī syādyācake yakṣe sevake ca vivādini \|

arvā haye pumānarvā kutsite'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 36 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 37 \[unit 1163\]

arśoghnī tālaparṇyāṃ syādarśoghnaḥ śūraṇe pumān \|

alī tu vṛścike bhṛṅge'pyavanaṃ rakṣaṇe mudi \|\| 37 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 38 \[unit 1164\]

aśanistu dvayorvajre taḍityapi matā'śaniḥ \|

asanaṃ kṣepaṇe klīvamasanaḥ pītasārake \|\| 38 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 39 \[unit 1165\]

asiknī sariti preṣyāśuddhāntā'vṛddhayoṣiti \|

ātmā brahmamanodehasvabhāvadhṛtibuddhiṣu \|\| 39 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 40 \[unit 1166\]

ātmāyatte'pyathā''dānaṃ grahaṇe vājibhūṣaṇe \|

āpannastu vipatprāpte prāpte cāpyabhidheyavat \|\| 40 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 41 \[unit 1167\]

āsanaṃ dviradaskandhapīṭhe pīṭhasthitāvapi \|

āsanī paṇyavīthyāṃ syādāsano jīvakadrume \|\| 41 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 42 \[unit 1168\]

uttānamunmukhe supte'pyagambhīre'pi vācyavat \|

utthānamudgame tantre'pyudyame harṣaṇe raṇe \|\| 42 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 43 \[unit 1169\]

prāṅgaṇe pauruṣe caiva malavege ca pustake \|

udānastūdarāvartte kaṇṭhavātāhibhedayoḥ \|\| 43 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 44 \[unit 1170\]

uddhānaṃ cullikāyāṃ syānmatamudgamane'pi ca \|

udyānaṃ klībamākrīḍe niḥsṛtau ca prayojane \|\| 44 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 45 \[unit 1171\]

kaṭhinā tu matā sthālyāṃ śarkarāyāṃ guḍasya ca \|

khaṭikāyāṃ tu kaṭhinī kaṭhinaṃ niṣṭhure triṣu \|\| 45 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 46 \[unit 1172\]

kadanaṃ yudhādye kāme kampanaṃ kamprakampayoḥ \|

kamanaḥ kāmuke cābhirūpe cāśokakāmayoḥ \|\| 46 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 47 \[unit 1173\]

karmma vyāpye kriyāyāṃ ca pare syādaṅgasaṃskṛtau \|

karttanaṃ chedane tūlatantukarmaṇi yoṣitām \|\| 47 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 48 \[unit 1174\]

kalaglāyāntu kalanaṃ kalanaṃ bandhane'pi ca \|

kalpanaṃ chedane klṛptau kalpanā gajasajjane \|\| 48 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 49 \[unit 1175\]

paṇasya mānadaṇḍasya caturthāṃśe'pi kākinī \|

kāñcano dhūrttapunnāganāgakesaracampake \|\| 49 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 50 \[unit 1176\]

udumbare kāñcanāre haridrāyāṃ ca kāñcanī \|

klībaṃ tu kāñcane hemni keśare'pi ca kāñcanam \|\| 50 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 51 \[unit 1177\]

kānanaṃ vipine'pi syāccaturmmukhamukhe gṛhe \|

vyāse karṇepi kānīnaḥ kānīnaḥ kanyakāsute \|\| 51 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 52 \[unit 1178\]

kāminī nāyikābhede vandāyāmapi kāminī \|

kāmī tu kāmuke koke kāmī pārāvate'pi ca \|\| 52 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 53 \[unit 1179\]

kunnānaṃ tu hyalaṅkāre bhājane golakāntare \|

kuhanā dambhacaryāyāmīrṣyālau dāmbhike triṣu \|\| 53 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 54 \[unit 1180\]

kṛtī tu paṇḍite yogye ketanaṃ lāñchane gṛhe \|

ketanaṃ syātpatākāyāṃ kārye copanimantraṇe \|\| 54 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 55 \[unit 1181\]

cīnaikadeśe kaupīnaṃ syādguhyākāryayorapi \|

kaulīnaṃ tu parīvāde kulīnatve kukarmmaṇi \|\| 55 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 56 \[unit 1182\]

guhye'pi saṅgarepi śvabhujaṅgapaśupakṣiṇām \|

bhavetkrandanamāhvāne matamaśruvimocane \|\| 56 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 57 \[unit 1183\]

khaḍgī tu gaṇḍake puṃsi khaḍgī khaḍgāyudhe'pi ca \|

gandhanaṃ sūcane hiṃsāsamutsāhaprakāśane \|\| 57 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 58 \[unit 1184\]

garjanaṃ tu mataṃ kope nisvane meghanisvane \|

gahanaṃ kānane duḥkhe gahvare kalile'pi ca \|\| 58 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 59 \[unit 1185\]

gāyanaṃ svapne klībaṃ ca gītajīvini gāyane \|

viṣadigdhapaśormmāṃse gṛñjanaṃ laśune pumān \|\| 59 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 60 \[unit 1186\]

gomī gavīśvare harau syānmaheṣvāsake'pi ca \|

gostanī hārahūrāyāṃ hārabhede tu gostanaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 61 \[unit 1187\]

grāvā tu puṃsi pāṣāṇe girivāridayorapi \|

ghaṭṭanā calanāyāṃ syādāvṛttyāmapi ghaṭṭinī \|\| 61 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 62 \[unit 1188\]

cakrī harikulālā'hikokeṣu grāmajāline \|

candanā kālibhede syāccandanaṃ malayodbhave \|\| 62 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 63 \[unit 1189\]

candanī tu nadībhede carmma syātphalakatvayoḥ \|

carmmī phalakapāṇau syādbhṛṅgarīṭe mṛdutvaci \|\| 63 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 64 \[unit 1190\]

calanaṃ bhramaṇe kampe vācyavatkampaśālini \|

calanī vastraghargharyāṃ vārībhede'pi dṛśyate \|\| 64 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 65 \[unit 1191\]

cetanaścetanāyukte triṣu saṃvidi cetanā \|

patre patatre chadanaṃ chadma śāpakilāsayoḥ \|\| 65 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 66 \[unit 1192\]

lakṣye'pi chardanastu syānnimbālambuṣavāntiṣu \|

chedanaṃ bhedane chede jagaṃstu jantuśuṣmaṇoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 67 \[unit 1193\]

jaghanaṃ vanitāśroṇīpurobhāge kaṭāvapi \|

jayanaṃ tu jaye vājigajaprabhṛtikañcuke \|\| 67 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 68 \[unit 1194\]

yavano yavamātre'pi yavādhikaturaṅgame \|

deśabhede turuṣke'pi javanaḥ prajave triṣu \|\| 68 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 69 \[unit 1195\]

tapano ravisantāpe mallake narakāntare \|

tamoghnaścandrasūryā'gnibuddhaśrīkaṇṭhaviṣṇuṣu \|\| 69 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 70 \[unit 1196\]

talinaṃ virale stoke svacchagambhīrayorapi \|

talunaḥ pavane yūni vācyavattalunī striyām \|\| 70 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 71 \[unit 1197\]

temanaṃ vyañjane klede cullikābhidi temanī \|

todanaṃ vyathane totre tyāgī sūre'pi dātari \|\| 71 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 72 \[unit 1198\]

puṣpe vīre'pi damano darśanaṃ dṛśi darppaṇe \|

svapne vartmani buddhau ca śāstradharmmopalavdhiṣu \|\| 72 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 73 \[unit 1199\]

daṃśanaḥ śiśire puṃsi daṃśanaṃ kavace rade \|

dahane duṣṭacarite bhallāte citrake'nale \|\| 73 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 74 \[unit 1200\]

dṛśānastu gṛhapatau dṛśānaṃ jyotiṣi smṛtam \|

devanaḥ pāśake puṃsi dhanva cāpe sthale'pi ca \|\| 74 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 75 \[unit 1201\]

dhanvī dhanurddhare khiḍge'pyarjune cārjunadrume \|

dhamanastvanale bhastrādhmāpakakrūrayostriṣu \|\| 75 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 76 \[unit 1202\]

dhamanī kaṃdharāyāṃ ca haridrāśirayorapi \|

dhāma raśmau gṛhe dehe prabhāvasthānajanmasu \|\| 76 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 77 \[unit 1203\]

dhāvanaṃ dhāvite śuddhau pṛṣṭiparṇyāṃ tu dhāvanī \|

syāddhāvanī rajanyāṃ ca dhautājanyāṃ ca tarttare \|\| 77 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 78 \[unit 1204\]

dhvajī dvije rathe śaile turaṅge ca bhujaṅgame \|

nandano harṣake putre nandanaṃ miśrakāvane \|\| 78 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 79 \[unit 1205\]

nandanī tu matā devadhunīdhenunanāndṛṣu \|

nandī nandīśvare gardabhāṇḍanyagrodhavṛkṣayoḥ \|\| 79 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 80 \[unit 1206\]

nalinī tu sarojinyāṃ saroje ca sarovare \|

vyomagaṅgāmalikayoḥ nalinaṃ tu jalābjayoḥ \|\| 80 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 81 \[unit 1207\]

nidānaṃ roganiyame'pyādihetvavamānayoḥ \|

vatsadāmni nidānaṃ syānnidhanaṃ kulanāśayoḥ \|\| 81 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 82 \[unit 1208\]

patrī kāṇḍakhagaśyenanagadrurathike rathe \|

padminī padmanalinīsarassu vanitāntare \|\| 82 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 83 \[unit 1209\]

parva syādutsave granthau darśapratipadorapi \|

tatsandhau viṣuvādau ca prastāve lakṣaṇāntare \|\| 83 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 84 \[unit 1210\]

pavano'strī kulālasya pākasthāne'nile pumān \|

nirvikalpe'pi pavanaḥ pakṣma locanalomani \|\| 84 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 85 \[unit 1211\]

pakṣma sūtrādisūkṣmāṃśe pakṣma syātkeśare'pi ca \|

pāvanaṃ tu jale kṛcchre pāvakādhyāsayoḥ pumān \|\| 85 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 86 \[unit 1212\]

pāṭhīnastu vadāle syādapi citravadālake \|

pāṭhake gugguludrau ca prāyaścitte tu pācanam \|\| 86 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 87 \[unit 1213\]

pācanī tu harītakyāṃ pācano vahnisihlayoḥ \|

pāvanaṃ pāvayitari triṣu pūte'pi pāvanam \|\| 87 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 88 \[unit 1214\]

varuṇe puṃsi syātpāśī pāśī pāśadhare'nyavat \|

piśuno nārade puṃsi khalasūcakayostriṣu \|\| 88 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 89 \[unit 1215\]

piśunaṃ kuṅkume klībaṃ pṛkkāyāṃ piśunā matā \|

pītanaḥ kapicūte syātpītanaṃ pītadāruṇi \|\| 89 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 90 \[unit 1216\]

kuṅkume haritāle ca pūtanā rākṣasībhidi \|

pathyāyāṃ cātha pṛtanā'nīkinīsainyabhedayoḥ \|\| 90 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 91 \[unit 1217\]

syāccamūsenayoścātha prajñānaṃ lāñchane dhiyi \|

pradhanaṃ dāruṇe saṅkhye pradhānaṃ paramātmani \|\| 91 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 92 \[unit 1218\]

kṣetrajñadhīmahāmātre'pyekatve tūttame sadā \|

prasūno vācyavajjāte prasūnaṃ phalapuṣpayoḥ \|\| 92 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 93 \[unit 1219\]

prasannā madirāyāṃ syātprasādasahite triṣu \|

pretvā tu sārase vāte prema tu snehanarmmaṇoḥ \|\| 93 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 94 \[unit 1220\]

phālgunastu tapasye syādarjune cārjunadrume \|

phālgunaḥ syānnadīje'pi phālgunī pūrṇimāntare \|\| 94 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 95 \[unit 1221\]

bandhanaṃ tu śatabandhe bandhamātre'pi bandhanam \|

varddhanaṃ chedane vṛddhau vāridhānyāṃ tu varddhinī \|\| 95 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 96 \[unit 1222\]

saṃpūrvādvarddhanaṃ poṣe vasanaṃ chādanāṃśuke \|

vāṇinī tu mattānarttakyorvidagdhāyāṃ striyāmatha \|\| 96 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 97 \[unit 1223\]

vāsanā vasane vārāsanajñāne ca dhūpane \|

vāhinī syādanīkinyāṃ sainyabhede sarityapi \|\| 97 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 98 \[unit 1224\]

gurau puṃsi budhānaḥ syādbudhānaḥ paṇḍite'pi ca \|

bodhanī bodhipippalyorbodhanaṃ gandhadīpane \|\| 98 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 99 \[unit 1225\]

suravartmani ca vyoma vyomacāriṇi ca smṛtam \|

brahmā viriñce vipre'pi ṛtvikcandrārkayogayoḥ \|\| 99 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 100 \[unit 1226\]

brahma klībaṃ śrutijñāne'pyadhyātmatapasorapi \|

brahmāṇyāṃ bhaṭṭinī nāṭye rājayoṣiti bhaṭṭinī \|\| 100 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 101 \[unit 1227\]

bhaṇḍanaṃ tu khalīkāre yuddhasannāhayorapi \|

bharmma svarṇe bhṛtau sāre bhavanaṃ bhāvasadmanoḥ \|\| 101 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 102 \[unit 1228\]

bhājanaṃ pātre yogye'pi bhāvanā dhyānalepayoḥ \|

bhuvanaṃ tu jagallokasalileṣu vihāyasi \|\| 102 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 103 \[unit 1229\]

bhogī bhogānvite sarpe grāmaṇyāṃ rājñi nāpite \|

saṃgṛhītastriyāṃ rājabhāryābhede'pi bhoginī \|\| 103 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 104 \[unit 1230\]

maṃjanaṃ bhojane klībamalaṃkartari vācyavat \|

madanaḥ smaradhattūravasantadrumasikthake \|\| 104 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 105 \[unit 1231\]

malanaḥ paṭhavāse'pi syānmalanaṃ karddame matam \|

puṣpavatyāṃ tu malinī malinaṃ dūṣite'site \|\| 105 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 106 \[unit 1232\]

mārjjanaṃ tu mataṃ mārṣṭau mārjano lodhrapādape \|

mālinī vṛttabhede syādgaṅgāmālikayoṣitoḥ \|\| 106 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 107 \[unit 1233\]

gauryāṃ campānagaryāṃ ca rāśau tu mithunaḥ pumān \|

mithunaṃ dampatīyugme sambandhagrāmyadharmmayoḥ \|\| 107 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 108 \[unit 1234\]

muṇḍanaṃ vapane trāṇe mehanaṃ śiśnamūtrayoḥ \|

maithunaṃ syānnidhuvane maithunaṃ saṅgatāvapi \|\| 108 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 109 \[unit 1235\]

yamanaṃ syāduparame bandhane ca yame tathā \|

yāpanaṃ varttane kālakṣepe nirasane'pi ca \|\| 109 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 110 \[unit 1236\]

prajāno brāhmaṇe'pi syātprajānaḥ sārathāvapi \|

yuvā tu taruṇe śreṣṭhe nisargabalaśālini \|\| 110 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 111 \[unit 1237\]

yojanaṃ tu catuḥkrośyāṃ yoge ca paramātmani \|

rajanī tu haridrāyāṃ lākṣāyāṃ nīlikārase \|\| 111 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 112 \[unit 1238\]

rañjano rāgajanake rañjanaṃ raktacandane \|

rañjanī nīlikāśuṇḍāmañjiṣṭhārocanīṣvapi \|\| 112 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 113 \[unit 1239\]

jihvākāṃcīrasajñeṣu rasanā rasane svane \|

svedane mūrchane bhastrāvāte nāsāmarutpathe \|\| 113 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 114 \[unit 1240\]

rāgī tu kopane rakte rāgayukte'pi kāmini \|

rājā candre nṛpe śakre kṣatriye prabhuyakṣayoḥ \|\| 114 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 115 \[unit 1241\]

rādhanaṃ sādhane prāptau toṣaṇe'pi ca rādhanam \|

recanī trivṛtā śuṇḍā rocanī dantikārthikā \|\| 115 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 116 \[unit 1242\]

rocano raktakahlāre kūṭaśālmaliśākhini \|

api gopittamaṅgalaracitastrīṣu rocanā \|\| 116 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 117 \[unit 1243\]

rodanaṃ krandane'pi syādaśrumātre'pi rodanam \|

rohī rohitake bodhidrume nyagrodhapādape \|\| 117 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 118 \[unit 1244\]

laṅghanaṃ kramaṇe pīḍākṛtopavasane plutau \|

lalanā tu nitambinyāṃ jihvāyāṃ nāḍikāntare \|\| 118 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 119 \[unit 1245\]

lakṣma cihne pradhāne'pi lāñchanaṃ nāmalakṣmaṇoḥ \|

lekhanaṃ tu lipinyāse charde bhūrje'pi lekhanam \|\| 119 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 120 \[unit 1246\]

vacaknurvākpaṭau vipre vaśī sugataśakrayo \|

vapanaṃ muṇḍane vāpe vamanaṃ charddane'rddane \|\| 120 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 121 \[unit 1247\]

āhatāvapyatha klībaṃ varjanaṃ tyāgahiṃsayoḥ \|

varttanaṃ jīvane jīvye tūlanāle ca varttanam \|\| 121 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 122 \[unit 1248\]

varttanī tarkkupiṇḍe'pi maline pathi varttanī \|

varṇī citrakare brahmacārilekhakayorapi \|\| 122 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 123 \[unit 1249\]

ākāre śobhane varṣma varṣma dehapramāṇayoḥ \|

vartma netracchade mārge vāgmī vācaspatau paṭau \|\| 123 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 124 \[unit 1250\]

vājī vāhe khage bāṇe kharveṣu triṣu vāmanaḥ \|

vāmano viṣṇubhede syādaśve yānyādidiggaje \|\| 124 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 125 \[unit 1251\]

viklinnastimite jīrṇe jarājīrṇepi vācyavat \|

vicchinnastu samālabdhe vibhakte kuṭile'nyavat \|\| 125 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 126 \[unit 1252\]

vijñānaṃ kārmmaṇe jñāne vitānaṃ riktamandayoḥ \|

triṣu na strī vitānaṃ syādvistārollocayormakhe \|\| 126 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 127 \[unit 1253\]

vastraveśmanyavasare vṛtte ca kratukarmmaṇi \|

vipanno bhujage puṃsi triṣu naṣṭe vipadgate \|\| 127 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 128 \[unit 1254\]

vimāno vyomayāne'strī saptabhūmau gṛhe'pi ca \|

vilagnastvaṃgamadhye syāttriṣveva cāṅgalagnayoḥ \|\| 128 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 129 \[unit 1255\]

viṣaghnastu śirīṣe syādguḍūcītrivṛtoḥ striyām \|

vṛjinaṃ kaluṣe klībaṃ keśe nā kuṭile triṣu \|\| 129 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 130 \[unit 1256\]

vṛṣā sureśvare karṇe vedanā jñānapīḍayoḥ \|

veṣṭanaṃ karṇaśaṣkulyāmuṣṇīṣe mukuṭe vṛtau \|\| 130 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 131 \[unit 1257\]

vyañjanaṃ temane śmaśrucihnāvayavakādiṣu \|

svātaṃtryakṛtye vyutthānaṃ virodhācaraṇe'pi vā \|\| 131 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 132 \[unit 1258\]

vyasanaṃ tvaśubhe saktau pānastrīmṛgayādiṣu \|

daivāniṣṭaphale pāke vipattau viphalodyame \|\| 132 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 133 \[unit 1259\]

saktimātre sucaritādbhraṃśe kopajadūṣaṇe \|

śakunaṃ maṅgalāśaṃsinimitte śakunaḥ khage \|\| 133 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 134 \[unit 1260\]

śakuniḥ puṃsi vihage sauvaśve karaṇāntare \|

śaṅkhinī śaṅkhayūdhe syādbhujaṅgastrīprabhedayoḥ \|\| 134 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 135 \[unit 1261\]

śaṅkhinī vetapunnāge corapuṣpyāṃ ca śaṅkhinī \|

śataghnī śastrabhede'pi vṛścikālīkarañjayoḥ \|\| 135 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 136 \[unit 1262\]

śamanastu yame śāntivadhayoḥ śamanaṃ matam \|

śayanaṃ talpamātre'pi nidrāsuratayorapi \|\| 136 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 137 \[unit 1263\]

śākhī mahīruhe vede turuṣkākhyajane'pi ca \|

śāstrājñārājadattorvīrājalekheṣu śāsanam \|\| 137 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 138 \[unit 1264\]

śikhī ketugrahe vahnau mayūre kukkuṭe śare \|

balīvardde bake vṛkṣe vratibhedasacūḍayoḥ \|\| 138 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 139 \[unit 1265\]

śilpī tu vācyavatkārau nāsikāyāṃ tu śilpinī \|

śṛṅgī nāge'pi vṛṣabhe parvate'pi mahīruhe \|\| 139 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 140 \[unit 1266\]

śobhano yogabhede nā śobhanaḥ sundare triṣu \|

śrīghanaḥ sugate bhikṣau śrīghanaṃ dadhi na dvayoḥ \|\| 140 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 141 \[unit 1267\]

śleṣmanghī mallikāyāṃ syātkampillakaphaṇijjayoḥ \|

śvasanaḥ pavane śvāse śvasano madanadrume \|\| 141 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 142 \[unit 1268\]

sandhānaṃ syādabhiṣave klībaṃ saṅghaṭṭane'pi ca \|

sandhinī tu vṛṣākrāntā'kāladugdhagavoḥ smṛtā \|\| 142 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 143 \[unit 1269\]

samāno nābhipavane sadekasadṛśe triṣu \|

sampannaṃ triṣu sampattisahite sādhite'pi ca \|\| 143 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 144 \[unit 1270\]

saṃvyānamuttarāsaṅge saṃvyānaṃ chādane tathā \|

savanaṃ yajane snāne somanirddamane matam \|\| 144 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 145 \[unit 1271\]

sādī tu sārathau vāhavāhake hastivāhake \|

sādhanaṃ mehane sainye nivṛttigatisiddhiṣu \|\| 145 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 146 \[unit 1272\]

karaṇe copakaraṇe mṛtasaṃskaraṇe vadhe \|

dravaṇe cānuvrajyāyāmupāye dāpane dhane \|\| 146 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 147 \[unit 1273\]

sādhano yajñakarmmānte yajamānapracetasoḥ \|

maryādāyāṃ striyāṃ sīmā kṣetre ghāṭe sthitāvapi \|\| 147 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 148 \[unit 1274\]

sūcanā'bhinaye dṛṣṭau gandhane vyadhane'pi ca \|

secanaṃ sekapātre syātsekarakṣaṇayorapi \|\| 148 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 149 \[unit 1275\]

senāpatau tu senānīḥ sainānīḥ śarajanmani \|

sevanaṃ sīvane klībaṃ sevāyāmapi sevanam \|\| 149 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 150 \[unit 1276\]

saṃsthānamākṛtau sanniveśe mṛtyau catuṣpathe \|

stananaṃ jaladadhvāne dhvanimātre'pi kuñcane \|\| 150 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 151 \[unit 1277\]

sthāpanaṃ syātpuṃsavane samādhāvarpaṇe'pi ca \|

sparśanaḥ pavane puṃsi sparśanaṃ sparśadānayoḥ \|\| 151 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 152 \[unit 1278\]

syandanaṃ prasrave nīre syandanastiniśe rathe \|

sraṃsanaṃ recane pāte pṛthagbhāvātisārayoḥ \|\| 152 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 153 \[unit 1279\]

svāmī prabhau viśākhe ca halī syātkarṣake bale \|

aṅgāradhānyāṃ hasanī hasanaṃ hasite matam \|\| 153 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / 154 \[unit 1280\]

hastinī nāyikābhede hastinī hastiyoṣiti \|

hāyano vatsare na strī vrīhibhedārcciṣoḥ pumān \|\| 154 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natṛtīyam / fragment 1281

hiṇḍanaṃ surate kelau hrādinī vajravidyutoḥ \|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 155 \[unit 1282\]

nacaturtham \|

atharvā dvijabhede syādvede'tharva napuṃsakam \|\| 155 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 156 \[unit 1283\]

adhiṣṭhānaṃ prabhāve'pi pure'nyāsanacakrayoḥ \|

anūcāno vinīte'pi sāṅgavedavicakṣaṇe \|\| 156 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 157 \[unit 1284\]

nayanāgre'pyanūcānaḥ pumāneva kvacinmataḥ \|

anvāsanaṃ tu sevāyāṃ snehavastāvupāsane \|\| 157 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 158 \[unit 1285\]

apācīnaṃ triṣu viparyaste dakṣiṇasambhave \|

janmabhūmyāmabhijanaḥ kule khyātau kuladhvaje \|\| 158 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 159 \[unit 1286\]

abhipanno'parāddhe'bhidrute graste vipadgate \|

dakṣiṇe svīkṛte'pi syādabhipanno'bhidheyavat \|\| 159 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 160 \[unit 1287\]

abhimānaḥ pumāngarve'jñāne'praṇayahiṃsayoḥ \|

aryamā mihire sūryamuktāyāṃ pitṛdaivate \|\| 160 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 161 \[unit 1288\]

avadānaṃ matamiti vṛttakarmmaṇi khaṇḍane \|

tanumadhye'valagnaḥ syātsaṃlagne tvabhidheyavat \|\| 161 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 162 \[unit 1289\]

syādākalanamākāṅkṣāparisaṅkhyāvibandhane \|

ācchādanaṃ pidhāne syādvasanenāpavāraṇe \|\| 162 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 163 \[unit 1290\]

ātañcanaṃ pratīvāpajavanāpyāyane matam \|

ātmayonirviriñce syādātmayonirmanobhave \|\| 163 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 164 \[unit 1291\]

āveśanaṃ śilpigṛhe bhūtāveśe praveśane \|

āyodhanaṃ bhavedyuddhe vadhepyāyodhanaṃ matam \|\| 164 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 165 \[unit 1292\]

ārādhanaṃ tu pūjāyāṃ pākaprāpaṇayorapi \|

āskandanaṃ tiraskāre tathā saṃśoṣaṇe raṇe \|\| 165 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 166 \[unit 1293\]

utpatanaṃ samutpattau bhavedūrddhvagatāvapi \|

utsādanaṃ samullekhodvarttanodvāsanārthakam \|\| 166 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 167 \[unit 1294\]

bhavedudayano vatsarāje kalaśasambhave \|

udvarttanamutpatanā'pāvarttanavilepane \|\| 167 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 168 \[unit 1295\]

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 184 \[unit 1311\]

mataṃ bhavennidhuvanaṃ surate kampane'pi ca \|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 202 \[unit 1329\]

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 203 \[unit 1330\]

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 204 \[unit 1331\]

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 206 \[unit 1333\]

viṣayi tvindriye klībaṃ vācyavadviṣayānvite \|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 207 \[unit 1334\]

anadhīte bhujiṣye ca viṣāṇī śṛṅgināgayoḥ \|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 209 \[unit 1336\]

mataṃ vihananaṃ ghāte piñjane tūladhūnane \|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 211 \[unit 1338\]

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 214 \[unit 1341\]

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 215 \[unit 1342\]

## nāntavargaḥ / nacaturtham / 216 \[unit 1343\]

samādānaṃ samīcīnagrahaṇe nityakarmmaṇi \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 227 \[unit 1354\]

napañcamam \|

agrajanmā vidhau vipre jyeṣṭhabhrātari ca smṛtaḥ \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 229 \[unit 1356\]

apavarjanamityetad dāne'pi parivarjanam \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 231 \[unit 1358\]

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 232 \[unit 1359\]

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 233 \[unit 1360\]

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 234 \[unit 1361\]

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 235 \[unit 1362\]

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 236 \[unit 1363\]

tiktaparvā hilamocīguḍūcīmadhuyaṣṭiṣu \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 237 \[unit 1364\]

lokeśvare vidhau sūrye dhanade padmalāñchanaḥ \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 238 \[unit 1365\]

pītacandanamityetatkālīyakaharidrayoḥ \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 239 \[unit 1366\]

pravalākī bhujaṅge'pi meghanādānulāsini \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 240 \[unit 1367\]

vanamālī hṛṣīkeśe vārāhyāṃ vanamālini \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 241 \[unit 1368\]

rocanāyāṃ haridrāyāmapi syādvaravarṇinī \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 242 \[unit 1369\]

vyomacārī vihaṅge'pi sure vidyādhare'pi ca \|

vanamālini rolambe vijñeyo madhusūdanaḥ \|\| 242 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 243 \[unit 1370\]

śātakumbhe kusumbhe'pi mahārajanamadvayoḥ \|

kṛttivāsasi kākole śrīphale mṛtyuvañcanaḥ \|\| 243 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 244 \[unit 1371\]

vighnakārī mato bhīmadarśane'pi vighātini \|

viśvakarmā tu mārtaṇḍe munibhiddevaśilpinoḥ \|\| 244 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 245 \[unit 1372\]

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 246 \[unit 1373\]

piñjanyāṃ kaṭukāyāṃ ca sammatā śakulādanī \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 247 \[unit 1374\]

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 248 \[unit 1375\]

śvetadhāmā sudhādhāmni ghanasārābdhiphenayoḥ \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 249 \[unit 1376\]

saṃprayogī kalākelau kāmuke suprayogini \|

gośīrṣe daivatatarau haricandanamastriyām \|\| 249 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcamam / 250 \[unit 1377\]

jyotsnāyāṃ kuṅkume padmapārage haricandanam \|

pumānahaskare meghavāhane karivāhanaḥ \|\| 250 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṣṭham

## nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṣṭham / 251 \[unit 1378\]

naṣaṣṭham \|

antāvasāyī śvapace nāpite ca munerbhidi \|

kalānunādī rolambe kalaviṅke kapiñjale \|\| 251 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṣṭham / 252 \[unit 1379\]

jāyānujīvī bharate durgatākhilayorbake \|

mataḥ sahasravedhī tu rāmaṭhe cāmlavetase \|\| 252 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṣṭham / fragment 1380

iti viśvalocane nāntavargaḥ \|

## pāntavargaḥ

## pāntavargaḥ / fragment 1381

atha pāntavargaḥ \|

## pāntavargaḥ / paikam

## pāntavargaḥ / paikam / 1 \[unit 1382\]

paikam \|

po vāte pā tu pāne syātpāstu pātari vācyavat \|\| 1 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvitīyam

## pāntavargaḥ / padvitīyam / 2 \[unit 1383\]

padvitīyam \|

kalpo brāhmadine nyāye pralaye vidhiśāntayoḥ \|

kūpo'ndhugarttamṛnmānakūpake guṇavṛkṣake \|\| 2 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvitīyam / 3 \[unit 1384\]

kṛpā dayāyāṃ vyāse tu kṛpo bhāratapūruṣe \|

khaṣpaḥ krodhe balātkāre gopo gopālabhūpayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvitīyam / 4 \[unit 1385\]

gopo grāmaughagoṣṭhādhikāriṇośca kvacitkarau \|

chupaḥ kṣupe sparśanepi santāne mārute jupaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvitīyam / 5 \[unit 1386\]

taplaṃ kalatre śayyāyāṃ talpamaṭṭe'pi na dvayoḥ \|

santāpe davathau tāpastāpī tu saridantare \|\| 5 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvitīyam / 6 \[unit 1387\]

trapā lajjākulaṭayostrapu sīsakaraṅgayoḥ \|

darppo bhavedahaṅkāre darppo mṛgamade'pi ca \|\| 6 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvitīyam / 7 \[unit 1388\]

nipo valikadaṃbe syānnīlavañjulabandhane \|

puṣpaṃ rajasi nārīṇāṃ vikāse kusume'pi ca \|\| 7 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvitīyam / 8 \[unit 1389\]

rūpamākārasaundaryasvabhāvaślokanāṇake \|

nāṭakādau mṛge granthāvṛttau ca paśuśabdayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvitīyam / 9 \[unit 1390\]

repaḥ syānnindite krūre ropo bāṇe'pi ropaṇe \|

lepastu lepane khyātaḥ sudhājemanayorapi \|\| 9 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvitīyam / 10 \[unit 1391\]

vapā tu vivare bhede vāṣpo netrajaloṣmaṇoḥ \|

śaṣpaṃ bālatṛṇaṃ klībaṃ śaṣpastu pratibhākṣaye \|\| 10 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvitīyam / 11 \[unit 1392\]

śapathākrośayoḥ śāpaḥ śiṣpaṃ kṛtyocita śruve \|

sūpo vyañjanabhede'pi sūpakāre'pi ca smṛtaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

piṇḍapuṣpamaśoke syājjapāpuṣpe'pi paṃkaje \|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvitīyam / 3 \[unit 1424\]

hrasve saṅkhyāntare kharvaścārvī syācchobhanādhiyoḥ \|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvitīyam / 4 \[unit 1425\]

ḍimbastu viplavaplīhaphupphusairaṇḍabhītiṣu \|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvitīyam / 5 \[unit 1426\]

dārvī dāruharidrāyāṃ haridrādevadāruṇoḥ \|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvitīyam / 6 \[unit 1427\]

tiktatumbīśriyorlambā bimbaṃ syādbimbikāphale \|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvitīyam / 7 \[unit 1428\]

śaṃbaḥ śubhānvite vajre musalāgrasthamaṇḍale \|

## bāntavargaḥ / batṛtīyam

## bāntavargaḥ / batṛtīyam / 9 \[unit 1430\]

## bāntavargaḥ / batṛtīyam / 11 \[unit 1432\]

kaṭīcakre nitambaḥ syācchikhariskaṃgharodhasoḥ \|

herambo mahiṣe lambodaraśūratvagarvite \|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhaikam / fragment 1442

bhūrbhuvi sthānamātre'pi striyāṃ bhavitari triṣu \|

sambuddhāvavyayaṃ bho syāt-

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvitīyam

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 1443\]

bhadvitīyam \|

-kumbho rāśyantare ghaṭe \|\| 2 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 1444\]

samādhau gajamūrddhvāṃśe kumbhakarṇasute viṭe \|

kumbhī syātpāṭalā vāriparṇī piṭharakaṭphale \|\| 3 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 1445\]

kumbhaṃ gugguluvṛkṣe syāttrivṛtāyāṃ ca na dvayoḥ \|

garbho bhrūṇe'rbhake kukṣau sandhau panasakaṇṭake \|\| 4 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 1446\]

jambho dante'pi jambīre daityabhede'pi bhakṣaṇe \|

jṛmbho vikāse puṃsyeva jṛmbhastu triṣu jṛmbhaṇe \|\| 5 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 1447\]

ḍimbhastu bāliśe pote dambhaḥ kaitavakalkayoḥ \|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 1448\]

dvayoḥ pradhānacakrāntaḥprāṇyaṅgeṣu made striyām \|

nibhastu sadṛśe vyāje saṃpūrvaḥ stulya eva saḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 1449\]

rambhā kadalyapsaraso rambho vaiṇavadaṇḍake \|

paripūrvastu saṃśleṣe vibhurnitye śive prabhau \|\| 8 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 1450\]

śumbhaḥ syādbrahmaśivayorarhatyapi ca keśave \|

yoge śubhaḥ śubhaṃ kṣeme śobhā kāntīcchayormatā \|\| 9 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 1451\]

sabhā sāmājike goṣṭhyāṃ dhūnamandirayoḥ sabhā \|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 11 \[unit 1452\]

bhatṛtīyam \|

pāpe'pyariṣṭe'pyaśubhamātmabhūḥ smaravedhasoḥ \|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 12 \[unit 1453\]

ṛṣabhaḥ śreṣṭhavṛṣayoraṣṭavargauṣadhāntare \|

svarādribhede varāhapucche randhre ca karṇayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 13 \[unit 1454\]

ṛṣabhī tu narākāranārīvidhavayoṣitoḥ \|

śūkaśiṃbyāṃ śirālāyāṃ śreṣṭhe syāduttarasthitaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 14 \[unit 1455\]

kakubho'rjunavṛkṣe'pi rāgabhede prasevake \|

kakub dikśobhayoḥ śāstre kambale campakasraji \|\| 14 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 15 \[unit 1456\]

karabho maṇibandhādikaniṣṭhānte kramelake \|

aṣṭāpade'pi karabhaḥ śarabhe ca mṛgāntare \|\| 15 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 16 \[unit 1457\]

kusumbhaṃ hemani mahārajane nā kamaṇḍalau \|

garddabhī rāsabhe gandhabhede klībaṃ tu kairave \|\| 16 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 17 \[unit 1458\]

garddabhī svalparugjantubhedayoratha puṃsyayam \|

dundubhirdaityabheryoḥ strī tvakṣabindutrike dvaye \|\| 17 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 18 \[unit 1459\]

duṣprāpe vallabhe kaccharogiṇi triṣu vallabhaḥ \|

nikumbhaḥ kumbhakarṇasya putre dantyāmapi smṛtaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 19 \[unit 1460\]

vallabho nā kulīnāśve dayitādhyakṣayostriṣu \|

punarnavāyāṃ varṣābhūḥ strī nā kiṃculake plave \|\| 19 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 20 \[unit 1461\]

viṣkambho yogabhede'pi bandhabhede'pi yoginām \|

rūpakāṅge pariṣṭambhe vistārapratiyatnayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 21 \[unit 1462\]

viṣkambhaḥ pratibandhe'pi vaidarbhe vismṛtāvapi \|

viśrambhaḥ kelikalahe viśvāse praṇaye vadhe \|\| 21 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 22 \[unit 1463\]

vṛṣabhastu vṛṣe śukre vṛṣabhaḥ puṅgave'pi ca \|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 23 \[unit 1464\]

sanābhiḥ pūjane puṃsi sanābhiḥ sadṛśe triṣu \|

surabhiścampake caitre vasante gandhake kavau \|\| 23 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatṛtīyam / 24 \[unit 1465\]

svarṇe jātīphale cābje triṣu madyasugandhayoḥ \|

khyāte ca strī tu śallakyāṃ surabhī mātṛbhedayoḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / maikam

## māntavargaḥ / maikam / fragment 1471

mā niṣedhe'vyayaṃ me ca mametyarthe mamāvyayam \|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 2 \[unit 1472\]

madvitīyam \|

amo roge'pi tadbhede syādapakve tu vācyavat \|\| 2 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 3 \[unit 1473\]

idhmaḥ puṃsi vasante syādidhmaḥ syānmīnaketane \|

umā gauryāmatasyāṃ ca haridrākāntikīrtiṣu \|\| 3 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 4 \[unit 1474\]

umastu syātpumāneva mato nagaraghaṭṭayoḥ \|

ūrmmirdvayostaraṅge syādbhaṅge vegaprakāśayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 5 \[unit 1475\]

vastrasaṃkocarekhāyāmutkaṇṭhāpīḍayorapi \|

kāmaḥ smarecchayoḥ kāmye kāmaṃ retonikāmayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 6 \[unit 1476\]

sammate syādanumatau kāmamityetadavyayam \|

kāmiḥ strī kāmakāntāyāṃ kāmiḥ syātkāmuke pumān \|\| 6 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 7 \[unit 1477\]

sarvanāmni kimityetadvijñeyamabhidheyavat \|

kiṃ vitarke'vyaye praśne kṣepe nindāprakārayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 8 \[unit 1478\]

kirmmiḥ strī svarṇaputryāṃ syādapi mālāpalāśayoḥ \|

kṛmirnā krimivatkīṭe lākṣāyāṃ kṛmile khare \|\| 8 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 9 \[unit 1479\]

kramaḥ śaktiparīpāṭīcalane kampane'pi ca \|

kharmaḥ kṣaumaprabhede'pi kharme syādapi pauruṣe \|\| 9 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 10 \[unit 1480\]

gamo dyūtāntare mārge'pyaparyālocite'pi ca \|

gulmaḥ stambe camūrakṣāsainyayoḥ plīhaghaṭṭayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 11 \[unit 1481\]

gulmī syādāmalakyelāvanikāvastraveśmasu \|

grāmaḥ svare saṃvasathe vṛnde śabdādipūrvakaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 12 \[unit 1482\]

gharmmaḥ syādātape grīṣme ūṣmasvedajale'pi ca \|

jālmaḥ syātpāmare krūre jālmo'samīkṣyakāriṇi \|\| 12 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 13 \[unit 1483\]

jihmaṃ tu tagare jihmastriṣu syānmandavakrayoḥ \|

haridyave'pi harite tokmastokmaṃ śrutermmale \|\| 13 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 14 \[unit 1484\]

damastu damane daṇḍe damathe karddame'pi ca \|

dasmo vaiśvānare caure yajamāne'pi ca smṛtaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 15 \[unit 1485\]

drumastu pādape pārijāte kiṃpuruṣeśvare \|

dharmmaḥ syādastriyāṃ puṇye dharmmo nyāyasvabhāvayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 16 \[unit 1486\]

upamāyāṃ yamācāravedānte'pi dhanuṣyapi \|

yāge yoge'pyahiṃsāyāṃ somape'pi kvacinmataḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 17 \[unit 1487\]

dhyāmo gandhatṛṇe puṃsi dhyāmo damanake'pi ca \|

śyāmavarṇe triṣu dhyāmo numā nāmni paradyutau \|\| 17 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 18 \[unit 1488\]

nemiḥ kūpatrikāyāṃ syāccakrānte tiniśadrume \|

nemo'rddhakīlasīmāsu garttaprākārakaitave \|\| 18 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 19 \[unit 1489\]

padmo'strī padmanāle'bje vyūhasaṃkhyāntare nidhau \|

padmake nāgabhede nā padmā bhārṅgīśriyoḥ striyām \|\| 19 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 20 \[unit 1490\]

brāhmī tu bhāratīpaṅkagatikābrahmaśaktiṣu \|

phañjikāyāṃ tathā somavallarīśākayorapi \|\| 20 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 21 \[unit 1491\]

bhāmaḥ krodhe ravau bhāsi bhīmaḥ śambhau vṛkodare \|

syādamlavetase bhīmastriṣu ghore bhayānake \|\| 21 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 22 \[unit 1492\]

bhīṣmastu haragāṅgeyarakṣasi triṣu bhīṣaṇe \|

sthānamātre kṣitau bhūmirbhaumastu narake kuje \|\| 22 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 23 \[unit 1493\]

bhramo bhrāntau ca kundākhyayantre ca jalanirgame \|

saṃyame yamaje dharmarāje dhvāṅkṣe yuge yamaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 24 \[unit 1494\]

nityakarmaprabhede ca yamunāyāṃ yamī striyām \|

prahare saṃyame yāmo yāmiḥ svasṛkulastriyoḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 25 \[unit 1495\]

pradhmaścāpepi saṃgrāme rāmamādhavayoṣiti \|

ramastu manmathe kānte ramo'śokamahīruhe \|\| 25 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 26 \[unit 1496\]

raśmiraṃśupragrahayo raśmirlocanalomani \|

rāmastu rāghave jāmadagnye haladhare'pi ca \|\| 26 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 27 \[unit 1497\]

paśubhede sitaśyāmamanojñeṣu tu vācyavat \|

rāmāṅganāhiṅgulinyo rāmaṃ vāstukakuṣṭhayoḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 28 \[unit 1498\]

manorame'bhipūrvāyāṃ rukmaṃ tu svarṇalohayoḥ \|

rumā sugrīvakāntāyāṃ rumā tu lavaṇākare \|\| 28 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 29 \[unit 1499\]

lakṣmīḥ śrīriva saṃpattau padmaśobhāpriyaṅgaṣu \|

lakṣmīḥ syādauṣadhībhede nañaḥ pūrvā tu nirṛtau \|\| 29 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 30 \[unit 1500\]

vamiḥ syātpāvake puṃsi vamistu vamane striyām \|

vāmaḥ savye hare kāme dhane vitte tu na dvayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 31 \[unit 1501\]

valgupratīpayorvāmastriṣu vāmā tu yoṣiti \|

vāmī śṛgālyāṃ vaḍavārāsabhīkarabhīṣvapi \|\| 31 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 32 \[unit 1502\]

śamī śaktuphalāyāṃ syācchivāyāṃ valgulāvapi \|

śuṣmaḥ pumāndinapatau mataṃ śuṣmaṃ tu tejasi \|\| 32 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 33 \[unit 1503\]

śyāmastu harite kṛṣṇe prayāgasya vaṭadrume \|

pike payodhare vṛddhadārake'pi pumānayam \|\| 33 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 34 \[unit 1504\]

śyāmavarṇe haridvarṇe triṣu śyāmā tu valgulau \|

aprasūtāṅganāyāṃ ca śyāmā somalatoṣadhau \|\| 34 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 35 \[unit 1505\]

trivṛtāśārivāgundrāniśānīlīpriyaṅguṣu \|

śyāmaṃ lavaṇabhede'pi śyāmaṃ syānmarice'pi ca \|\| 35 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 36 \[unit 1506\]

śrāmastu maṇḍape kāle vipūrvaḥ śramavañcane \|

samā varṣe sadṛksarvamānyeṣu ca samaṃ triṣu \|\| 36 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 37 \[unit 1507\]

sīmā'vadhau ca velāyāṃ kṣetre ghāṭe sthitāvapi \|

sūkṣmaṃ tu nabhasi kṣīre sūkṣmamalpe'bhidheyavat \|\| 37 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 38 \[unit 1508\]

katakā'dhyātmayoḥ sūkṣmaṃ sūkṣmaḥ puṃsyaṇumātrake \|

somaḥ sudhāṃśukarpūrakuberapitṛdaivate \|\| 38 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 39 \[unit 1509\]

divyauṣadhīśyāmalatāvasubhidvātavānare \|

tuṣāre candane śīte himaṃ triṣu tu śītale \|\| 39 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 40 \[unit 1510\]

homiragnau ghṛte cātha kṣitau kṣāntāvapi kṣamā \|

kṣamaṃ yukte kṣamaḥ śakte hite kṣāntyanvite'nyavat \|\| 40 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / 41 \[unit 1511\]

kṣumā'tasīnīlikayoḥ kṣemaṃ syāllabdharakṣaṇe \|

maṅgale corake vā strī kṣemā caṇḍāharastriyoḥ \|\| 41 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvitīyam / fragment 1512

kṣaumaṃ syādatasīvastre kṣaumamaṭṭadukūlayoḥ \|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 42 \[unit 1513\]

matṛtīyam \|

adhamaḥ kutsite nyūne'pyāgamaḥ śāstra āgatau \|\| 42 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 43 \[unit 1514\]

āśramo brahmacaryādau munisthāne maṭhe striyām \|

uttamā dugdhikāyāṃ syādutkṛṣṭe tu triṣūttamam \|\| 43 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 44 \[unit 1515\]

kalamaḥ śālilekhanyoścaure lākṣārase'pi ca \|

kusumaṃ puṣpaphalayorārttave locanāmaye \|\| 44 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 45 \[unit 1516\]

kṛtrimaṃ lavaṇe puṃsi sihlake kṛtake triṣu \|

guḍārmaḥ syādguḍakṣode kṣīradāruṇi ca smṛtaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 46 \[unit 1517\]

godhūmo vrīhibhede syānnāraṅge bheṣajāntare \|

golomī śvetadūrvāyāṃ vārastrīvacayorapi \|\| 46 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 47 \[unit 1518\]

gautamaḥ śākyasiṃhe'pi munibhede'pi gautamaḥ \|

gautamī caṇḍikāyāṃ ca rocanyāmapi gautamī \|\| 47 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 48 \[unit 1519\]

talimaṃ kuṭṭime talpe vitāne yāvake'pi ca \|

dāḍimaḥ puṃsi dāḍimba elāyāmapi dāḍimaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 49 \[unit 1520\]

nigamo haṭṭapūrvedakaṭaluṇḍīṣu vāṇije \|

niyamo niścaye bandhe yantraṇe saṃvidi vrate \|\| 49 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 50 \[unit 1521\]

niṣkramo nirgame buddhisampattau duṣkule'pi ca \|

naigamaḥ kṣurivedāntavaṇigvāṇijyanāgare \|\| 50 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 51 \[unit 1522\]

pañcamo rāgabhede syātpañcānāṃ pūraṇe triṣu \|

triṣu dakṣiṇameghe'pi pañcamī pāṇḍavastriyām \|\| 51 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 52 \[unit 1523\]

paramastu triṣūtkṛṣṭe pradhānādyośca puṃsi tu \|

oṃkāre paramaṃ tu syādanujñāyāmasaṃjñakam \|\| 52 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 53 \[unit 1524\]

prakramo'vasare cānukrame cāpakrame krame \|

pratimā'nukṛtau dantabandhane'pi ca dantinām \|\| 53 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 54 \[unit 1525\]

ādāvapi pradhāne'pi prathamaṃ vācyaliṅgakam \|

praharmmaḥ saudhakūṭasthakalaśādrinitambayoḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 55 \[unit 1526\]

madhyamo madhyadeśe syātsvare madhye'tha madhyamā \|

triṣu dṛṣṭarajonārīrākayormadhyamā striyām \|\| 55 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 56 \[unit 1527\]

karṇikātryakṣaracchandakaramadhyāṅgulīṣu ca \|

vikramastūdyamakrāntau kṣamāyāṃ śaktisaṃpadi \|\| 56 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 57 \[unit 1528\]

vidrumo ratnavṛkṣe'pi pravāle navapallave \|

vibhramastu vilāse syād vibhramo bhrāntihāvayoḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 58 \[unit 1529\]

vilomo viparīte'pi bhujaṅgeṅguliromani \|

vilomī tu vyavasthāyāṃ vilomamaraghaṭṭake \|\| 58 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 59 \[unit 1530\]

vyāyāmo durgasaṃcāre viyāme pauruṣe śrame \|

saṅkramaḥ saṅkamaṇe'strī tu vārisaṃcārayantrake \|\| 59 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 60 \[unit 1531\]

triṣūttame pūjyatame sādhīyasi ca sattamaḥ \|

sambhramastvādare puṃsi saṃvege sādhvase'pi ca \|\| 60 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / 61 \[unit 1532\]

suṣamaṃ cārusamayostriṣu syātsuṣamā dyutau \|

atidyutau ca suṣamā suṣīmaḥ pannagāntare \|\| 61 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matṛtīyam / fragment 1533

suṣīmaṃ śiśire klībaṃ cāruśītalayostriṣu \|

## māntavargaḥ / macaturtham

## māntavargaḥ / macaturtham / 62 \[unit 1534\]

macaturtham \|

sundare'pyupamāśūnye bhavedanupamo'nyavat \|\| 62 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macaturtham / 63 \[unit 1535\]

gaurīnāyakadiṅnāgayoṣityanupamā matā \|

abhyāgamo'ntike ghāte virodhe'pyudgame yudhi \|\| 63 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macaturtham / 64 \[unit 1536\]

upakramaścikitsāyāmupadhāne ca vikrame \|

bhavedupagamaḥ pārśvagamane'ṅkīkṛtāvapi \|\| 64 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macaturtham / 65 \[unit 1537\]

jalagulmo jalāvarttajalacatvarakacchape \|

daṇḍayāmastu divase kīnāśe kumbhasambhave \|\| 65 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macaturtham / 66 \[unit 1538\]

parākramastu sāmarthye vikramodyogayorapi \|

plavaṅgamaḥ kapau bheke mahāpadmaṃ tu mānake \|\| 66 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macaturtham / 67 \[unit 1539\]

mahāpadmaḥ pumānsaṅkhyānidhināgāntare mataḥ \|

yātayāmo mato jīrṇe paribhuktojjhite triṣu \|\| 67 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macaturtham / 68 \[unit 1540\]

sārvabhaumastu dignāgabhede sarvamahīpatau \|

abhyupagamaḥ svīkāre samīpāgamane'pi ca \|\| 68 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macaturtham / fragment 1541

iti viśvacalocane māntavargaḥ \|

## yāntavargaḥ

## yāntavargaḥ / fragment 1542

atha yāntavargaḥ \|

## yāntavargaḥ / yaika

## yāntavargaḥ / yaika / fragment 1543

yaikam \|

yo vātayaśasoḥ puṃsi yā yānatyāgayātṛṣu \|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 1544\]

yadvitīyam \|

anyo'samāne bhinne ca syādantyo'ntabhave'dhame \|\| 1 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 1545\]

arthyo budhe triṣu nyāyye śilājatuni na dvayoḥ \|

arghārthaṃ yattadarghyaṃ syāttriṣu yaścārghamarhati \|\| 2 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 1546\]

arghyaḥ syādyogyamātre'pi syādaryaḥ svāmivaiśyayoḥ \|

puṃsyāryaḥ sauvidalle syādāryastvabhyarhite triṣu \|\| 3 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 1547\]

āsyā sthitau mukhe cāsyaṃ mukhamadhye mukhodbhave \|

ijyo gurau pumānijyā dānārcāsaṅgameṣṭiṣu \|\| 4 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 1548\]

ibhya āḍhyaṃ bhavedibhyā kareṇvāmapi śallakau \|

kanyā kumārikānāryo rāśibhedauṣadhībhidoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 1549\]

prātarhyodinayoḥ kalyaṃ kalyo nīrogadakṣayoḥ \|

sajje'pi triṣu kalyā tu madye kalyā ca vāci ca \|\| 6 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 1550\]

kaśyaṃ madye kaśārhe ca kaśyaṃ madhye ca vājinām \|

kakṣyā bṛhatikākāñcormadhyabandhe ca dantinām \|\| 7 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 1551\]

harmyādīnāṃ prakoṣṭhe tu kāṃsyaṃ syātpānabhājane \|

taijasadravyabhedepi vādyabhede'pi na dvayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 1552\]

kāyo varṣma svabhāve ca saṅghe lakṣye kadaivate \|

kāyaṃ manuṣyatīrthe syātkāryaṃ hetau prayojane \|\| 9 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 1553\]

kāvyaḥ śukagrahe puṃsi kāvyā syātpūtanādhiyoḥ \|

kāvyaṃ granthāntare klībaṃ kuḍyaṃ bhittau vilepane \|\| 10 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 1554\]

kulyo mānye kulodbhūtakulātihitayostriṣu \|

kulyaṃ syādāmiṣe śūrpepyaṣṭadroṇyāṃ ca kīkase \|\| 11 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 1555\]

kulyā'lpakṛtrimanadīnadījīvāsu nirjhare \|

kṛtyā kriyādevatayostriṣu bhedye ghanādibhiḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 1556\]

vidviṣṭakāryayoścāyaṃ kṛtyāstavyādiṣu smṛtāḥ \|

kriyā karmmaṇi ceṣṭāyāṃ karaṇe saṃpradhāraṇe \|\| 13 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 1557\]

upāyārambhaśikṣārcācikitsāniṣkṛtiṣvapi \|

gavyaṃ napuṃsakaṃ jyāyāṃ gavāṃ kṣīrādike'pi ca \|\| 14 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 1558\]

rāgadravye'pi gavyā tu gokule gohite triṣu \|

guhyaṃ rahasyupasthe ca guhyo dambhepi kacchape \|\| 15 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 16 \[unit 1559\]

gṛhyā śākhāpure gṛhyastvasaktamṛgapakṣiṇoḥ \|

guhyaṃ purīṣamārge'pi gṛhyamasvairipakṣayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 17 \[unit 1560\]

geyastu triṣu gātavye geyaḥ syādgāyane pumān \|

gopyo dāsyā apatye syādrakṣaṇīye'pi vācyavat \|\| 17 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 18 \[unit 1561\]

grāmyo jane triṣu grāmyaṃ tvaślīlaratabandhayoḥ \|

cayastvāharaṇe vṛnde prākāre mūlabandhane \|\| 18 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 19 \[unit 1562\]

cavyaṃ tu cavike yacca cavyā dūrvogragandhayoḥ \|

cityā mṛtacitāyāṃ syāccityaṃ mṛtakacaityake \|\| 19 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 20 \[unit 1563\]

caityamāyatane klībaṃ syāccitācūḍake'pi ca \|

buddhabimbe pumāṃścaityaścaitye uddeśyapādape \|\| 20 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 21 \[unit 1564\]

codyaṃ praśne'dbhute codyaṃ vācyavaccodanocite \|

chāyā syādātapābhāve satkāntyutkocakāntiṣu \|\| 21 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 22 \[unit 1565\]

pratibimbe'rkakāntāyāṃ tathā paṅktau ca pālane \|

janyastāte varavadhūjñātibhṛtyapriye hite \|\| 22 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 23 \[unit 1566\]

janyastu jananīye syāttriṣu janyaṃ tu saṃyuge \|

parīvāde'pi haṭṭe'pi janyā mātṛsakhīmudoḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 24 \[unit 1567\]

janyuḥ prāṇini vahnau ca janyuḥ syātparameṣṭhini \|

jayo jayante vijaye jayā tithyantaromayoḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 25 \[unit 1568\]

umāsakhījayantyośca pathyāyāmagnimanthake \|

jātyaṃ kulīne śreṣṭhe'pi tārkṣyo'nūrusuparṇayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 26 \[unit 1569\]

rathe'śve cāśvakarṇadrau mataṃ tārkṣyaṃ rasāñjane \|

tiṣyaḥ puṣye kalau tiṣyā dhātryāṃ tiṣyaiva puṣyavat \|\| 26 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 27 \[unit 1570\]

trayī trivedyāṃ tritaye purandhryāṃ sumatāvapi \|

dasyurvidviṣi caure ca dāyaḥ solluṇṭhabhāṣite \|\| 27 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 28 \[unit 1571\]

yautakādidhane dāne bhāgārhapitṛvastuni \|

divyaṃ tu śapathe vāle lavaṅgakusume'pi ca \|\| 28 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 29 \[unit 1572\]

divyā''malakyāṃ divyaṃ tu valgau divibhave'nyavat \|

dūṣyaṃ vastragṛhe vastre dūṣaṇīye tu vācyavat \|\| 29 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 30 \[unit 1573\]

daityā surāsurācaṇḍauṣadhīṣu ditije pumān \|

dravyaṃ tu pittale vitte druvikāre jatunyapi \|\| 30 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 31 \[unit 1574\]

bheṣaje ca pṛthivyādau triṣu bhavyavilepayoḥ \|

dhanyā dhātryāmalakyoḥ syāddhanyaḥ puṇyavati triṣu \|\| 31 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 32 \[unit 1575\]

dhānyaṃ vrīhiṣu dhānyāke dhiṣṇyaḥ syādanale pumān \|

dhiṣṇyaṃ sadmani nakṣatre sthāne śaktau ca na dvayoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 33 \[unit 1576\]

nayo dyutāntare nītau vyañjake tvabhipūrvakaḥ \|

nāṭyaṃ tauryatrike lāsye nityaṃ tu satate dhruve \|\| 33 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 34 \[unit 1577\]

harītakyāṃ matā pathyā mataṃ pathyaṃ hite triṣu \|

padyaḥ śūdre pumānpadyaṃ śloke padyā tu vartmani \|\| 34 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 35 \[unit 1578\]

napuṃsakaṃ tu pākyaṃ syādyavakṣāre biḍāhvaye \|

pādyaṃ payasi nindye ca pīyuḥ kālārkapecake \|\| 35 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 36 \[unit 1579\]

puṇyaṃ tu sukṛte dharme triṣu madhyamanojñayoḥ \|

śvaśure puṃsi pūjyaḥ syātpūjyo vandyo'bhidheyavat \|\| 36 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 37 \[unit 1580\]

peyaṃ pātavyapayasoḥ peyā śrāṇācchamaṇḍayoḥ \|

prāyaḥ pumānanaśane mṛtyubāhulyayostathā \|\| 37 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 38 \[unit 1581\]

priyastu triṣu hṛdye syāddhave vṛddhyauṣadhe pumān \|

vanyaṃ triṣu vanodbhūte vanyā vṛnde vanāmbhasoḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 39 \[unit 1582\]

aprajātastriyāṃ vandhyā vandhyastriṣu halidrume \|

valyaṃ pradhānadhātau syādvalyaṃ balakare triṣu \|\| 39 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 40 \[unit 1583\]

vareṇye vācyavadvaryo varyaḥ pañcaśare pumān \|

vindhyā truṭau lavalyāṃ ca vindhyo vyādhādribhedayoḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 41 \[unit 1584\]

vīryaṃ prabhāve śukre ca tejaḥsāmarthyayorapi \|

veśyā tu gaṇikāyāṃ syād veśyaṃ veśyāniketane \|\| 41 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 42 \[unit 1585\]

bhayaṃ ghore pratibhaye prasūne kubjavīrudhaḥ \|

karmmaraṅgatarau bhavyo bhavyā karikaṇomayoḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 43 \[unit 1586\]

bhāgyaṃ śubhātmakavidhau syācchubhāśubhakarmmaṇi \|

bhṛtyo dāse bhṛtau bhṛtyā matsyo mīne janāntare \|\| 43 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 44 \[unit 1587\]

viṣṇormūrtyantare matsyo virāṭākhye ca yādave \|

madhyaṃ nyāyye'vakāśe ca madhyaṃ madhyasthite triṣu \|\| 44 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 45 \[unit 1588\]

lagnake'pyadhame madhyamastriyāmavalagnake \|

manyurdainye kratau krodhe vāsave tu śatātparaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 46 \[unit 1589\]

mayaḥ śilpini daityānā karabhe'śvatare mayaḥ \|

mayurmṛge kiṃpuruṣe māyaḥ pītāmbare'sure \|\| 46 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 47 \[unit 1590\]

māyā dambhe kṛpāyāṃ ca syānmāyā śāmbarīdhiyoḥ \|

mālyaṃ puṣpe'pi mālāyāṃ mūlyaṃ vetanavasnayoḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 48 \[unit 1591\]

mṛtyuḥ syānmaraṇe daive medhyaṃ pūte'pi medure \|

medhyā raktavacāyāṃ ca rocanāyāmapi striyām \|\| 48 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 49 \[unit 1592\]

klībaṃ syādāśrame medhyaṃ yayuḥ kratuhaye haye \|

yāmyā'pācyāṃ bharaṇyāṃ ca yāmyo'gastye'pi candane \|\| 49 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 50 \[unit 1593\]

yogyaḥ pravīṇayogārhaśaktopāyiṣu vācyavat \|

yogyā'bhyāse'rkkakāntāyāṃ yogyamṛddhyākhyabheṣaje \|\| 50 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 51 \[unit 1594\]

rathyā tu viśikhāyāṃ syādrathaughe pathi catvare \|

mato rathodvahe rathyo rathyaṃ triṣu manorame \|\| 51 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 52 \[unit 1595\]

ramyā vibhāvarī ramyaḥ puṃsi campakapādape \|

rūpyaṃ syādāhatasvarṇarajate rajate tathā \|\| 52 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 53 \[unit 1596\]

triṣu praśastarūpe'pi labhyaṃ lavdhavyamuktayoḥ \|

layo nṛtyādisāmye syādvināśāśleṣayorlayaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 54 \[unit 1597\]

saṅkhyāśaravyayorlakṣyaṃ lakṣyaṃ syācchadmani smṛtaḥ \|

atha tauryatrike lāsyaṃ lāsyaṃ strīnṛtyanṛtyayoḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 55 \[unit 1598\]

vācyaṃ doṣe'pi vaktavye vacorhe kutsite'nyavat \|

vīkṣyo'vilāsake vīkṣyo draṣṭavyādbhutayostriṣu \|\| 55 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 56 \[unit 1599\]

vargaprasthānayorvrajyā vrajyā paryaṭane'pi ca \|

śayaḥ śayyāhihasteṣu śayyā tu śayanīyake \|\| 56 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 57 \[unit 1600\]

śabdagumphe'pi śalyastu śvāvinmadanavṛkṣayoḥ \|

śalyaṃ śaṅkau śare vaśakarṇikāyāṃ ca tomare \|\| 57 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 58 \[unit 1601\]

śunyā tu nalikāyāṃ syācchūnyaṃ tu triṣu nirjane \|

mataṃ śauryaṃ tu śūratve cārabhaṭyāṃ ca tanmatam \|\| 58 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 59 \[unit 1602\]

saṅkhyaṃ tu saṅgare klībaṃ saṅkhyaikatvādicarcayoḥ \|

tapolokātpare satyaṃ satyaṃ satyapragarttayoḥ \|\| 59 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 60 \[unit 1603\]

divyepi satyā pāmāyāṃ satyaṃ tu triṣu tadvati \|

sandhyā sāye saridbhede sandhāne kusumāntare \|\| 60 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 61 \[unit 1604\]

pratijñāyāṃ ca ciṃtāyāṃ maryādāyāmapi striyām \|

vāmadakṣiṇayoḥ savyaṃ sahyaṃ śastrakale guṇe \|\| 61 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 62 \[unit 1605\]

sahyaḥ śaile'pi soḍhavye nairujye sahyamadvayoḥ \|

sādhyastu yogabhede syātsādhyo'pi gaṇadaivate \|\| 62 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 63 \[unit 1606\]

vācyavatsāghanīye'pi sāyaḥ kāṇḍā'parāhṇayoḥ \|

sūryo'rke tatpriyāyāṃ tu sūryā syādoṣadhībhidi \|\| 63 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 64 \[unit 1607\]

sevyaṃ triliṅgaṃ sevārhe sevyaṃ tu nalade dvayoḥ \|

senāyāṃ samavete tu sainyaḥ sainyaṃ bale matam \|\| 64 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 65 \[unit 1608\]

ilvalāsu striyaḥ saumyā budhe saumyo'tha vācyavat \|

bauddhe manorame'nugre pāmare somadaivate \|\| 65 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 66 \[unit 1609\]

vivādapakṣanirṇetaryapi stheyaḥ purohite \|

stheyaṃ syāddravyamātre'pi puṃsi garve'dbhute smayaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 67 \[unit 1610\]

hāryo bibhītakīvṛkṣe harttavye hāryamanyavat \|

hṛdyastu vaśakṛdvedamantre vṛddhyākhyabheṣaje \|\| 67 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvitīyam / 68 \[unit 1611\]

syācchvetajīrake hṛdyaṃ hṛtpriye hṛdgave triṣu \|

kṣayo'pacayakalpāntanivāseṣu rugantare \|\| 68 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 69 \[unit 1612\]

yatṛtīyam \|

atyayo dūṣaṇe kṛcchre'tikrame nāśadaṇḍayoḥ \|

adhṛṣyastu pragalbhe syādadhṛṣyā saridantare \|\| 69 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 70 \[unit 1613\]

anayo vyasanānītidaivāśumavipattiṣu \|

apatyaṃ putrayoḥ klībamabhayo nirbhaye triṣu \|\| 70 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 71 \[unit 1614\]

matā'bhayā tu pathyāyāmabhayaṃ syāduśīrake \|

abhikhyā tu yaśaḥkīrttiśobhāvikhyātināmasu \|\| 71 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 72 \[unit 1615\]

triṣvavadhyaṃ vadhānarhe klībe'narthakabhāṣite \|

syādavandhyaṃ tu saphale triṣu triṣvaphalegrahau \|\| 72 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 73 \[unit 1616\]

aśvīyamaśvasaṅghāte'śvīyamaśvahite triṣu \|

ahalyāpsarasobhede tathā gautamayoṣiti \|\| 73 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 74 \[unit 1617\]

ahāryaḥ parvate puṃsi syādahāryaḥ sthire triṣu \|

ātithyamātitheye syādātithyastvatithau pumān \|\| 74 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 75 \[unit 1618\]

ātreyī puṣpavatyāṃ syādātreyī nimnagāntare \|

ātreyastu munerbhede syādādityaḥ sure ravau \|\| 75 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 76 \[unit 1619\]

āmnāya upadeśepi syādāmnāyaḥ śrutāvapi \|

āśayaḥ syādabhiprāye'pyādhāre panase dhane \|\| 76 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 77 \[unit 1620\]

koṣṭhāgāre'pyajīrṇe'pi kiṃpacāne'pi cāśayaḥ \|

indriyaṃ retasi klībamindriyaṃ viṣayīndriye \|\| 77 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 78 \[unit 1621\]

puṃsi syādudayaḥ pūrvaparvate'pi samunnatau \|

upāyaḥ sāmabhedādāvupāyaḥ syādupāgatau \|\| 78 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 79 \[unit 1622\]

ūrṇāyureḍake meṣakambalakṣaṇabhaṅgayoḥ \|

eṇeyameṇyāścarmādye ratabandhāntare striyāḥ \|\| 79 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 80 \[unit 1623\]

aucityamucitatve syādaucityaṃ satyayogyayoḥ \|

astrī kaṣāyo niryāse rase rakte vilepane \|\| 80 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 81 \[unit 1624\]

aṅgarāge sugandhe tu triṣu syāllohite'pi ca \|

kāleyo daityabhede syātkāleyaṃ kālakhaṇḍakam \|\| 81 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 82 \[unit 1625\]

kulāyo nīḍavatpakṣinilayasthānayoḥ pumān \|

kaukṛtyamanutāpe syādayuktakaraṇe'pi ca \|\| 82 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 83 \[unit 1626\]

gāṅgeyastu mahāsene bhīṣme gaṅgābhave triṣu \|

cakṣuṣyaḥ ketake puṇḍarīkavṛkṣe rasāñjane \|\| 83 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 84 \[unit 1627\]

astrī strī tu kulathyāṃ syādayuktakaraṇe'pi ca \|

gāṅgeyaṃ mustakasvarṇakaseruṣu napuṃsakam \|\| 84 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 85 \[unit 1628\]

gāṅgeyastu mahāsene bhīṣme gaṅgodbhave triṣu \|

cakṣuṣyaḥ ketake pusi śubhage'kṣihite triṣu \|\| 85 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 86 \[unit 1629\]

cāmpeyaścampake nāgakesare puṣpakesare \|

svarṇe klībaṃ jaghanyaṃ tu nindye caramaśiśnayoḥ \|\| 86 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 87 \[unit 1630\]

jaṭāyuḥ pakṣibhede syātpuṃsi guggulapādape \|

tapasyā vratacaryāyāṃ tapasyaḥ phālgune pumān \|\| 87 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 88 \[unit 1631\]

devayurddhārmike devayātrike'pyabhidheyavat \|

dvitīyā tithibhitpatnyoḥ pūraṇe'pi dvayostriṣu \|\| 88 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 89 \[unit 1632\]

nādeyī nīravānīre bhūjambūnāgaraṅgayoḥ \|

japājayantyorvyaṅguṣṭhe nikāyastvātmaveśmanoḥ \|\| 89 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 90 \[unit 1633\]

sadharminivahe lakṣye saṃhatānāṃ ca melake \|

raṅgabhūmau tu nepathyaṃ nepathyaṃ ca prasādhane \|\| 90 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 91 \[unit 1634\]

payasyā kṣīrakākolyāṃ svarṇakṣīryāmapi smṛtā \|

payasyā dugdhikāyāṃ ca payohitabhave'nyavat \|\| 91 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 92 \[unit 1635\]

parjanyo vāsave meghadhvanau ca dhvanadambude \|

paryāyaḥ kramanirvāṇaprakārāvasare pumān \|\| 92 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 93 \[unit 1636\]

peyavāriṇi pānīyaṃ pāruṣyastu bṛhaspatau \|

pāruṣyaṃ paruṣatve syādapi śukrasya kānane \|\| 93 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 94 \[unit 1637\]

paulastyaḥ kinnarādhīśe paulastyo daśakandhare \|

prakīryaḥ pūtikaraje vinikīrṇe tu vācyavat \|\| 94 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 95 \[unit 1638\]

praṇayaḥ premaviśrambhapraśrayaprasare'rthane \|

praṇāyyo'saṃmate tṛṣṇāvarjite'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 95 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 96 \[unit 1639\]

pratyayaḥ śapathe hetau jñānaviśvāsaniścaye \|

sannādyadhīnarandhreṣu khyātatvācārayorapi \|\| 96 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 97 \[unit 1640\]

pralayo mṛtyukalpāntamūrcchāsu viditaḥ pumān \|

prasavyamanyaliṅgaṃ syātpratikūlānukūlayoḥ \|\| 97 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 98 \[unit 1641\]

valayaḥ kaṅkaṇe na strī balākaṇṭharujorapi \|

vāleyaḥ phañjikāyāṃ syātkhare bālahite mṛdau \|\| 98 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 99 \[unit 1642\]

brahmaṇyastu śanau yūpe brahmasādhau tu vācyavat \|

brāhmaṇyaṃ brāhmaṇatve syādbrāhmaṇānāṃ ca saṃhatau \|\| 99 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 100 \[unit 1643\]

bhujiṣyastu sahāye'pi hastasūtre'pyatha triṣu \|

anadhīte bhujiṣyā tu veśyāceṭikayormatā \|\| 100 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 101 \[unit 1644\]

bhuvayuḥ syādbṛhadbhānubhānuśītalabhānuṣu \|

bhrātṛvyo bhrātṛtanaye triṣu puṃsi tu vidviṣi \|\| 101 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 102 \[unit 1645\]

maṅgalyaṃ dadhni maṅgalyaṃ tatrasādhau manohare \|

maṅgalyaḥ śrīphale svacche masūratrāyamāṇayoḥ \|\| 102 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 103 \[unit 1646\]

maṅgalyā rocanāyāṃ syātpriyaṅguśatapuṣpayoḥ \|

malligandhi ca yatkṛṣṇāguru tatrāpi sā smṛtā \|\| 103 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 104 \[unit 1647\]

adhaḥpuṣpīśamīkhaṇḍapuṣpīśvetavacāsu ca \|

malayaḥ puṃsi deśādribhedayoḥ parvatāṃśake \|\| 104 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 105 \[unit 1648\]

ārāme candane cātha malayā tṛvṛtauṣadhau \|

mṛgayurbrahmaṇi prokto gomāyuvyādhayorapi \|\| 105 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 106 \[unit 1649\]

rahasyaṃ vācyavadgopye rahasyā tu nadībhidi \|

lauhityaṃ raktatāyāṃ syātpuṃsi brīhau nadāntare \|\| 106 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 107 \[unit 1650\]

vaktavyaḥ kutsite hīne'pyadhīne vācyavattriṣu \|

vadānyastu suvāgdātrorvijayo jayapārthayoḥ \|\| 107 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 108 \[unit 1651\]

vijayā tu matā gauryāṃ tatsakhītithibhedayoḥ \|

vinayastu natau nītau śikṣāyāṃ vinayo dvayoḥ \|\| 108 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 109 \[unit 1652\]

viśalyā'gniśikhādantīguḍūcītṛvṛti striyām \|

vācyavadgataśalye syādvismayo'dbhutagarvayoḥ \|\| 109 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 110 \[unit 1653\]

viṣayo gocare deśe indriyārthe'pi nīvṛti \|

prabandhādyasya yo jñātaḥ sa tasya viṣayaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 110 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 111 \[unit 1654\]

vyavāyaḥ suratentarddhau vyavāyaṃ tejasi smṛtam \|

śāṇḍilyo munibhede'pi śrīphale pāvakāntare \|\| 111 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 112 \[unit 1655\]

śāleyaḥ śatapuṣpāyāṃ triṣu śālyudbhavocite \|

śīrṣaṇyaḥ puṃsi viśade kace klībaṃ tu śīrṣake \|\| 112 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 113 \[unit 1656\]

śaileyaṃ sindhulavaṇe tālaparṇyāṃ ca śailaje \|

bhṛṅge puṃsi śvaśuryastu devare śyālake'pi ca \|\| 113 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 114 \[unit 1657\]

pṛṣṭhasthāyibale nītau samavāye'pri sannayaḥ \|

samayaḥ puṃsi siddhāntaśapathācārasaṃvidi \|\| 114 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 115 \[unit 1658\]

kālasiddhāntanirdeśakriyākāreṣu saṅgame \|

melake yogiyoginyoḥ samayaḥ kvāpi dṛśyate \|\| 115 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 116 \[unit 1659\]

saraṇyurvāride vāte sāmarthyaṃ yogyatābale \|

saukaryaṃ syādanāyāse kriyāyāṃ sūkarasya ca \|\| 116 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 117 \[unit 1660\]

saubhāgyaṃ subhagatve syādyogabhede pumānayam \|

saurabhyaṃ tu sugandhatve gurutve guṇagaurave \|\| 117 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatṛtīyam / 118 \[unit 1661\]

saṃstyāyaḥ sanniveśe'pi saṃsthāne vismṛtau gaṇe \|
