## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 269 \[unit 1960\]

varohe pādapānāṃ ca samāvedoktavaijave \|

raṇḍāyāṃ tālapatrī syāttālapatraṃ tu kuṇḍale \|\| 269 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 270 \[unit 1961\]

tuṅgabhadrā nadībhede tuṅgabhadro madotkaṭe \|

tuṇḍikerī tu karpāsyāṃ bimbikāyāmapi striyām \|\| 270 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 271 \[unit 1962\]

tulādhārastulārāśau tulādhāro vaṇikṣvapi \|

bhavettoyadharo meghe mustake suniṣaṇṇake \|\| 271 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 272 \[unit 1963\]

yame nṛpe daṇḍadharo daṇḍadhāro yame nṛpe \|

daṇḍayātrā digvijaye saṃyānavarayātrayoḥ \|\| 272 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 273 \[unit 1964\]

klībaṃ daśapuraṃ deśe puragonardayorapi \|

digambarastu kṣapaṇe nagne dhvānte ca śūlini \|\| 273 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 274 \[unit 1965\]

darodaraṃ paṇe dyūte dyūtakāre durodaraḥ \|

dehayātrā matā mṛtyau dehayātrā'pi bhojane \|\| 274 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 275 \[unit 1966\]

dvaimāturo jarāsandhe dvaimātura ibhānane \|

dharādharaścakradhare kṣmādhare ca dharādharaḥ \|\| 275 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 276 \[unit 1967\]

bhaveddhārādharo vārivāhinistriṃśayoḥ pumān \|

dhārāṅkurastu nā sīre karakāyāṃ ca śīkare \|\| 276 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 277 \[unit 1968\]

dhārāṣṭro'sitaiścañcupadairhaṃse'pi kaurave \|

sarpe'pyatho dhavatarau dhūrvahe ca dhurandharaḥ \|\| 277 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 278 \[unit 1969\]

dhundhumāraḥ śakragope gṛhadhūme padālike \|

dhṛtarāṣṭrastvāṃbikeye pakṣibhede surājñi ca \|\| 278 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 279 \[unit 1970\]

dhṛtarāṣṭrī matā haṃsapadīnāmauṣadhāntare \|

nabhaścaro ghane vidyādhare vāte vihaṅgame \|\| 279 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 280 \[unit 1971\]

niśācaraḥ pheravabhūtarakṣobhujaṅgaghūkeṣu niśācarī tu \|

bhavedasatyāṃ hi niṣadvaraḥ syātpaṅke niśāyāṃ tu niṣādvarī syāt \|\| 280 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 281 \[unit 1972\]

paramparaḥ prapautrādau mṛgabhede paramparaḥ \|

paramparā tu santāne khaḍgakośe paricchade \|\| 281 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 282 \[unit 1973\]

bhavetparisaro daivopātte mṛtyupradeśayoḥ \|

yūthabhraṣṭapṛthakcārigaje pakṣacaro vidhau \|\| 282 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 283 \[unit 1974\]

pātraṭīro jaratpātre muktavyāpāramantriṇi \|

siṅghāṇe lauhakāṃsye ca jatupātre ca pāṭhake \|\| 283 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 284 \[unit 1975\]

pārāvāraḥ sarinnāthe pārāvāraṃ taṭadvaye \|

pāribhadraḥ pumānnimbatarau mandārapādape \|\| 284 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 285 \[unit 1976\]

mataḥ pītāmbaraścakrapāṇau pītāmbaro naṭe \|

pītasārastu gomede maṇau malayasambhave \|\| 285 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 286 \[unit 1977\]

pūrṇapātraṃ tu sampūrṇapātre vardhāpake'pi ca \|

yātrāyāṃ paṭahe caiva pūrṇapātramiti smṛtam \|\| 286 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 287 \[unit 1978\]

dvāri dvāḥsthe pratīhāraḥ pratīhārī tvanantarā \|

puṃsi pratisaro mālye camūpṛṣṭhe'pi kaṅkaṇe \|\| 287 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 288 \[unit 1979\]

bhūṣāyāṃ vraṇaśuddhau ca niyojyā''rakṣayorapi \|

mantrabhede striyāṃ puṃsi hastasūtre'pi na striyām \|\| 288 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 289 \[unit 1980\]

same pratikriyāyāṃ ca pratīkāro bhaṭe'pi ca \|

prabhākarorke dahane vakranakraḥ śuke khale \|\| 289 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 290 \[unit 1981\]

balabhadrā kumāryāṃ syāttrāyamāṇe vale pumān \|

vārvaṭīrastrapau cūtāsthyaṅkure gaṇikāsute \|\| 290 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 291 \[unit 1982\]

ukaṇe vārakīraḥ syānnīrājitahaye'pi ca \|

vīrabhadro'śvameghāśve mahāvīre'pi vīraṇe \|\| 291 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 292 \[unit 1983\]

klībaṃ vīrataraṃ vīraśreṣṭhe vīraṇagundrayoḥ \|

maṇicchidrā tu medāyāmṛṣabhākhyauṣadhāvapi \|\| 292 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 293 \[unit 1984\]

mahāvīrastu garuḍe śūre kaṇṭhīrave pavau \|

mahāvīraḥ pike cāśvamakhāgnau ca jarāṭake \|\| 293 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 294 \[unit 1985\]

mahāmātro hastipake samūhāmātyayorapi \|

rathakārastu māhiṣyātkaraṇīje'pi takṣaṇi \|\| 294 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 295 \[unit 1986\]

rāgasūtraṃ tulāsūtre paṭṭasūtre'pi na dvayoḥ \|

vasantakaṅkaṇābhikhyaśaṅkhe nogaṇḍipaṭṭake \|\| 295 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 296 \[unit 1987\]

dagdhadīpadaśāṣveṣa mato laṅgaṃścatuḥ pumān \|

lambodaraḥ syādudhmāne herambe lambakukṣike \|\| 296 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 297 \[unit 1988\]

lakṣmīputrastu kandarpe lakṣmīputrasturaṅgame \|

vātaputro mahādhūrtte hanūmadbhīmayorapi \|\| 297 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 298 \[unit 1989\]

bindutantraḥ pumāñśāriphalake caturaṅgake \|

vibhākaro bṛhadbhānau citrabhānau vibhākaraḥ \|\| 298 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 299 \[unit 1990\]

vibhāvarī tamasvinyāṃ haridrāyāṃ vibhāvarī \|

vivāhavastraguṇṭhyāñca kuṭṭinyāṃ vakrayoṣiti \|\| 299 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 300 \[unit 1991\]

viśvambharo harau śakre striyāṃ viśvambharā bhuvi \|

viśvakadruḥ khale dhvāne syādākheṭikakukkure \|\| 300 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 1 \[unit 1992\]

vītihotro bṛhadbhānau vītihotro divākare \|

bhavedvyatikaraḥ puṃsi vyasanavyatiṣaṅgayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 2 \[unit 1993\]

vyavahāro vyavahṛtau vṛkṣabhede sthitāvapi \|

śatapatro rājakīre dārvāghāṭe śikhaṇḍini \|\| 2 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 3 \[unit 1994\]

śatapatraṃ tu rājīve varīśuṇṭhyoḥ śatāvarī \|

śiśumāro jalakapau tārātmakaharāvapi \|\| 3 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 4 \[unit 1995\]

samudrārurmataḥ setubandhe grāhe timiṅgile \|

saṃprahāro mṛtau yuddhe jhiṇṭyāṃ sahacarī dvayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 5 \[unit 1996\]

syādvayasye sahacarastriṣu pratikṛtau pumān \|

sālasāro mato hiṅgau sālasāro mahīruhe \|\| 5 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 6 \[unit 1997\]

sukumārastu puṇḍrekṣau komale tvabhidheyavat \|

sūtradhāro mataḥ śilpiprabhede'pi purandare \|\| 6 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racaturtham / 7 \[unit 1998\]

nāndyanantarasañcāripātrabhede'pi sa smṛtaḥ \|

sthiradaṃṣṭro bhujaṅge syādvarāhākṛtikeśave \|\| 7 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcamam

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcamam / 8 \[unit 1999\]

rapañcamam \|

utpalapatraṃ tūtpalacchade yoṣinnakhakṣate \|

svarganadyāṃ tu kapiladhārā tīrthāntare pumān \|\| 8 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcamam / 9 \[unit 2000\]

tamālapatraṃ tilake tāpicche patrake'pi ca \|

tālīśapatraṃ tālīśe tāmalakyā ca na dvayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcamam / 10 \[unit 2001\]

saikate karake chāge pippale pādacatvaraḥ \|

paradīpaprakāśaikatatpare'pi mato nare \|\| 10 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcamam / 11 \[unit 2002\]

klībaṃ tu pītakāberaṃ pittale kuṅkume'pi ca \|

syātpāṃśucāmaro dhūlīgucchake'pi praśaṃsane \|\| 11 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcamam / 12 \[unit 2003\]

varddhāpake puroṭau ca dūrvāñcitataṭībhuvi \|

bakule vedhake nāgakusume nāgakesaraḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcamam / 13 \[unit 2004\]

syādrājabadaraṃ raktāmalake lavalīphale \|

romagucche ca mantau ca romakesara iṣyate \|\| 13 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcamam / 14 \[unit 2005\]

vasvaukasārā śrīdasya nalinyāmalakāpuri \|

vipratīsāraḥ kaukṛtye roṣe'pyanuśaye'pi ca \|\| 14 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcamam / 15 \[unit 2006\]

mataḥ samabhihārastu paunaḥpunye bhṛśārthake \|

puṃsyeva sarvatobhadraḥ kāvyacitre gṛhāntare \|\| 15 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcamam / 16 \[unit 2007\]

nimbe'tha sarvatobhadrā gambhāryāṃ naṭayoṣiti \|\| 16 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcamam / fragment 2008

iti viśvalocane'parābhidhānāyāṃ muktāvalyāṃ rephāntavargaḥ samāptaḥ \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ

## lāntavargaḥ / fragment 2009

atha lāntavargaḥ \|

## lāntavargaḥ / laikam

## lāntavargaḥ / laikam / 1 \[unit 2010\]

laikam \|

la indre lā tu dāne syādāśleṣe'pi laye'pi ca \|

api lūśchedake puṃsi lavaṇe lūrapi smṛtā \|\| 1 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 2 \[unit 2011\]

ladvitīyam \|

amlo rasaprabhede syādamlī cāṅgerikauṣadhau \|

alirbhṛṅge surāyāṃ strī syādāliḥ piṇḍale striyām \|\| 2 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 3 \[unit 2012\]

sakhyāṃ paḍktāvapi khyātā vācyavadviśadāśaye \|

ālurgalantikāyāṃ strī klībe bhelakakandayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 4 \[unit 2013\]

ilā gobhūmipīyūṣe bhāratyāṃ saumyayoṣiti \|

ollastu sūraṇe puṃsi syādārdre tvabhidheyavat \|\| 4 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 5 \[unit 2014\]

kalastu madhurāvyaktaśabde'jīrṇe kalaṃ site \|

kalā tu ṣoḍaśāṃśe syādindorapyaṃśamātrake \|\| 5 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 6 \[unit 2015\]

mūlārthavṛddhau śilpādau kalanākālabhedayoḥ \|

kalirantyayuge kande kandale subhaṭe pumān \|\| 6 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 7 \[unit 2016\]

kālastu samaye mṛtyau mahākāle yame śitau \|

kṛṣṇe triṣvatha kālī syātkālikāmātṛbhedayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 8 \[unit 2017\]

gauryāṃ navāmbudānīke kṣīrakīṭāpavādayoḥ \|

kālā tu kṛṣṇatrivṛtti nīlīmañjiṣṭhayorapi \|\| 8 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 9 \[unit 2018\]

kīlā kaphoṇighāte syātkīle śaṅkau ca kīlavat \|

kulaṃ sajātīyagaṇe gotrāṅgagṛhanīvṛti \|\| 9 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 10 \[unit 2019\]

kūlaṃ pratīre sainyasya pṛṣṭhe stūpataḍāgayoḥ \|

koloṅkapālāvutsaṅge kroḍe bhelakacitrayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 11 \[unit 2020\]

khañje kolaṃ tu kuvale kolā pippalicavyayoḥ \|

khalaḥ śaṭhe'dhame nīce triṣu syāttu khalaṃ bhuvi \|\| 11 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 12 \[unit 2021\]

khalaṃ sthāne'pi kalke'pi sasyasthāne'pi na dvayoḥ \|

khallā carmmaṇi nimne'pi vastrabhede'pi cātake \|\| 12 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 13 \[unit 2022\]

khallī tu hastapādāvamarddanākhyaruji striyām \|

khilaṃ bhavedaprahate sārasaṅkṣiptavedhasoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 14 \[unit 2023\]

galaḥ kaṇṭhe sarjjarase galaḥ skandhe mahīruhe \|

golā godāvarīsakhyorgolā patrāñjane matā \|\| 14 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 15 \[unit 2024\]

kunaṭyāmapi golaṃ tu maṇike maṇḍale'pi ca \|

calaścalācale kampe kamalāvidyutoścalā \|\| 15 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 16 \[unit 2025\]

cālaśchadiṣi puṃsyeva cālaḥ syātkampane'pi ca \|

klinnākṣitāyinaścillaścillī syātkṣudravāstuke \|\| 16 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 17 \[unit 2026\]

klinnanetrayute tu syāccillaḥ khullaśca vācyavat \|

cullaḥ klinne'kṣṇi cullī tu citāvuddhānavādyayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 18 \[unit 2027\]

celaṃ syādaṃśuke nīce garhitepyabhidheyavat \|

challī tu valkale puṣpabhede sannativīrudhoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 19 \[unit 2028\]

chalaṃ tu skhalite'pi syādvyāje'pi chalamadvayoḥ \|

jalaṃ śokarave nīre hrīvere'pi jaḍe triṣu \|\| 19 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 20 \[unit 2029\]

jālastu kṣārakānāyagavākṣe dambhavṛkṣayoḥ \|

jālī paṭolikāyāṃ syājjālau nīpamahīruhe \|\| 20 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 21 \[unit 2030\]

jhalā syādātapasyormau tathā putrīsulukkayoḥ \|

jhillī tvātaparugvandyāṃ jhīrukodvarttanāṃśayoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 22 \[unit 2031\]

talastāle talaṃ khaḍgamuṣṭau jyāghātavāraṇe \|

vane capeṭe na strī tu svarūpā''dhārayostalam \|\| 22 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 23 \[unit 2032\]

tallī taruṇyāṃ tallastu bille puṃsi napuṃsake \|

tālo drumāntareṅguṣṭhamadhyamābhyāṃ ca sambhite \|\| 23 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 24 \[unit 2033\]

gītakālakriyābhāve tālaḥ khaḍgādimuṣṭiṣu \|

tālaḥ syātkāṃsyaracitavādyabhāṇḍāntare tathā \|\| 24 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 25 \[unit 2034\]

karāsphāre karatale tālaṃ tu haritālake \|

tulā rāśau palaśate tulyatāmānabhedayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 26 \[unit 2035\]

bandhāya gṛhadārūṇāṃ pīṭhikāyāṃ sabhājane \|

tūlaḥ picau pumāṃstūlamākāśe brahmadāruṇi \|\| 26 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 27 \[unit 2036\]

apadravye chadocchrāyakhaṇḍe śastrīchade dalam \|

ḍuliḥ puṃsi munerbhede kamaṭhyāṃ tu striyāṃ ḍuliḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 28 \[unit 2037\]

dolā yānāntare nīlyāṃ dhūliḥ saṅkhyāntare raje \|

nalaḥ poṭagale rājñi kapīśe pitṛdaivate \|\| 28 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 29 \[unit 2038\]

nalī manaḥśilāyāṃ syānnalaṃ tu sarasīruhe \|

padmadaṇḍe na nā nālā nālī śākakadambake \|\| 29 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 30 \[unit 2039\]

nālā pānakaraṅkādirandhre nālastu pañjare \|

nīlastu kṛṣṇavarṇe syāttriṣu nīlaḥ kapīśvare \|\| 30 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 31 \[unit 2040\]

nīlo nagāntare kṛṣṇe nīlaṃ vṛkṣāṅkabhedayoḥ \|

pallī tu kuṭyāṃ kugrāme pallaḥ sthūlakusūlayoḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 32 \[unit 2041\]

palaṃ māse tathonmāne pāliḥ paṅkipradeśayoḥ \|

prasthe karṇalatāgreṃ'śe yūkāsaśmaśruyoṣitoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 33 \[unit 2042\]

indrāderdeyabhāge ca viśrāmya cāgatajvare \|

aśrau cihne ca pillastu klinne'kṣṇi triṣu tadvati \|\| 33 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 34 \[unit 2043\]

pīlurdrume gaje puṣpe kāṇḍatālāsthikhaṇḍayoḥ \|

aṇumātre'pyatha pulaḥ pulake vipule triṣu \|\| 34 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 35 \[unit 2044\]

phalaṃ tu sasye hetūtthe phalake vyuṣṭilābhayoḥ \|

jātīphale'pi kaṅkole mārgaṇāgre'pi na dvayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 36 \[unit 2045\]

syātphalaṃ triphalāyāṃ ca phalinyāṃ tu phalī striyām \|

phālaṃ sīrasya lauhe syātkarpāsādeśca vāsasi \|\| 36 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 37 \[unit 2046\]

balo halini daityeṅge kāke balini vācyavat \|

balaṃ gandharase sainye sthāmani sthaulyarūpayoḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 38 \[unit 2047\]

balā vāṭyālake proktā baliḥ puṃsyasurāntare \|

baliścāmaradaṇḍepi karapūjopahārayoḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 39 \[unit 2048\]

saindhavepi baliḥ strī tu jarasā ślathacarmmaṇi \|

kukṣibhāgaviśeṣe ca gṛhakāṣṭhāntare dvayoḥ \|\| 39 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 40 \[unit 2049\]

ballī syādajamodāyāṃ latāyāṃ kusumāntare \|

bālaḥ puṃsi śiśau keśe vājivāraṇabāladhau \|\| 40 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 41 \[unit 2050\]

mūrkhe'pi bālo bālaṃ tu hrībere puṃnapuṃsakam \|

bilaṃ guhāyāṃ randhre ca bilastvindrahaye pumān \|\| 41 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 42 \[unit 2051\]

velā kāle'pi sīmāyāmīśvarāṇāṃ ca bhojane \|

dattamāṃse'dhivelā syātpayonāśe'pi nīradheḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 43 \[unit 2052\]

tannīre'kliṣṭamaraṇe rāśau vāci budhasriyām \|

bhallo bāṇe'pi bhallūke bhallī bhallātabāṇayoḥ \|\| 43 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 44 \[unit 2053\]

bhālaṃ tu na dvayoreva lalāṭamahasormatam \|

ṛṣibhede plave bhelo bhelaṃ bhīruhṛdi triṣu \|\| 44 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 45 \[unit 2054\]

malastriṣveva kṛpaṇe na strī viṭkiṭṭakilviṣe \|

mallaḥ pātre kṛpāle ca matsyabhede kapālini \|\| 45 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 46 \[unit 2055\]

bhallo balāḍhye subhage mallī tu kusumāntare \|

māluḥ patralatāyāṃ syādvanitāyāmapi striyām \|\| 46 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 47 \[unit 2056\]

mālaṃ kṣetre jane mālo mālā puṣpādidāmani \|

mūlamādyaśiphāpārśvakuñje mūle'pi tārake \|\| 47 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 48 \[unit 2057\]

masimelakayormelā maulirdhammillacūḍayoḥ \|

kirīṭe'pi dvayoreva puṃsi vañjulapādape \|\| 48 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 49 \[unit 2058\]

līlā hāvāntare strīṇāṃ kelau khelāvilāsayoḥ \|

lolā jihvāśriyorlolaḥ satṛṣṇacalayostriṣu \|\| 49 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 50 \[unit 2059\]

vyālaḥ śaṭhe bhujaṅge ca śvāpade duṣṭadantini \|

śalaṃ tu śallakīlomni śalo bhṛṅgigaṇe vidhau \|\| 50 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 51 \[unit 2060\]

śālo matsyāntare vṛkṣasāmānye hālabhūbhuji \|

śālā veśmani veśmaikapradeśe skandhaśākhayoḥ \|\| 51 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 52 \[unit 2061\]

śāluḥ kaṣāyadravyepi śāluścorākhyabheṣaje \|

mataḥ śāliḥ pumān gandhamārjāre kalamādiṣu \|\| 52 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 53 \[unit 2062\]

śilā kunaṭyāṃ dvārādhodāruṇi grāvaṇi striyām \|

śilamuñchaśile klībaṃ gaṇḍūpadyāṃ śilī matā \|\| 53 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 54 \[unit 2063\]

śīlaṃ svabhāve sadvṛtte śukle dhavalayogayoḥ \|

śuklaṃ tu rūpyake śuklāṃ triṣu śuklaguṇānvite \|\| 54 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 55 \[unit 2064\]

śūlaṃ mṛtyau dhvaje nā tu yoge na strī rugastrayoḥ \|

śūlā tu paṇyayoṣāyāṃ duṣṭanāśāya kīlakaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 56 \[unit 2065\]

śailaḥ kṣamābhṛti śailaṃ tu śaileye tārkṣyaśailake \|

śālaḥ syāddharaṇe hāle pādape sarjapādape \|

sthālaṃ bhājanabhede syātsthālī syātpāṭalokhayoḥ \|\| 56 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvitīyam / 57 \[unit 2066\]

sthūlastu vācyavatpīne kūṭaniṣprajñayorapi \|

hālā madye nṛpe hālo helā'vajñāvilāsayoḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 58 \[unit 2067\]

latṛtīyam \|

syādaṅgulī tu mātaṅgakarṇikākaraśākhayoḥ \|

acalaḥ parvate kīle niścale'pyacalā bhuvi \|\| 58 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 59 \[unit 2068\]

añjaliḥ puṃsi kuḍave karasaṃpuṭake'ñjaliḥ \|

analo vasubhede'gnāvanilo vasuvātayoḥ \|\| 59 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 60 \[unit 2069\]

avelaḥ pūgarāge'pi ravatoyacaśālayoḥ \|

apalāpe'pyavelaṃ syādavelā pūgacūrṇayoḥ \|\| 60 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 61 \[unit 2070\]

amalā kamalāyāṃ syādamalaṃ viśade'bhrake \|

syādarālaḥ pumānsarje mattebhe kuṭile'nyavat \|\| 61 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 62 \[unit 2071\]

antaḥkapāṭayordaṇḍe kallole'pyargalaṃ triṣu \|

ābhīlaṃ na dvayoḥ kaṣṭe triṣvābhīlaṃ bhayānake \|\| 62 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 63 \[unit 2072\]

mṛgaśīrṣaśirastārāsvilvalāḥ syurathelvalaḥ \|

mīne daityaprabhede ca śṛṅgāra ujjvalaḥ pumān \|\| 63 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 64 \[unit 2073\]

ujjvalo vācyavaddīpte parivyaktavikāśiṣu \|

uttālo markaṭe śreṣṭhe vikarālotkaṭe triṣu \|\| 64 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 65 \[unit 2074\]

utpalaṃ kuvale kuṣṭhe nirmmāṃse tu triṣūtpalam \|

utphullaḥ karaṇe strīṇāmuttāne vikace'nyavat \|\| 65 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 66 \[unit 2075\]

uttāla udgate śreṣṭhepyūrdhvanāle'pi vācyavat \|

upalā śarkarāyāṃ syādupalo grāvaratnayoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 67 \[unit 2076\]

kadalībhapatākāyāṃ patākāyāṃ mṛgāntare \|

rambhāyāṃ cātha kadalī pṛśnyāṃ ḍimbyāṃ ca śālmalau \|\| 67 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 68 \[unit 2077\]

kandalaṃ kalahe yuddhe navāṅkurakapālayoḥ \|

kaladhvanau cātha tarau mṛgabhede'pi kandalī \|\| 68 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 69 \[unit 2078\]

kapilo munibhede'gnau śuni piṅge tu vācyavat \|

kapilā śiṃśapāgotrabhidvahnidigdantayoṣiti \|\| 69 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 70 \[unit 2079\]

reṇukāyāṃ ca kapilā kapālo'strī śirosthani \|

ghaṭādiśakale kuṣṭharogabhede vraje'pi ca \|\| 70 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 71 \[unit 2080\]

kamalaṃ jalaje nīre klomni toṣe ca bheṣaje \|

kamalo mṛgabhede syātkamalā śrīvarastriyām \|\| 71 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 72 \[unit 2081\]

kambalo nāgarāje nā sāsnāyāṃ ca kuthe kṛmau \|

api syāduttarāsaṅge klībaṃ payasi kambalam \|\| 72 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 73 \[unit 2082\]

karālo danture tuṅge bhīṣaṇe'pyabhidheyacat \|

karālo dhūnataile syātkarālaṃ tu kuṭherake \|\| 73 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 74 \[unit 2083\]

kallolaḥ khyāta ullolaḥ pramodaparipanthiṣu \|

kākolo droṇākāke syādviṣabhedakulālayoḥ \|\| \|\| 74 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 75 \[unit 2084\]

api kākolakākolyau syātāmoṣadhibhedayoḥ \|

kākīlastu kalājīve kāmakelipraṇālayoḥ \|\| 75 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 76 \[unit 2085\]

apāśrayamanohāritarucchāyārthakopyayam \|

kāmalaḥ kāmuke rogabhede maruvasantayoḥ \|\| 76 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 77 \[unit 2086\]

kāhalī tu taruṇyāṃ syātkāhalaṃ bhṛśaśuṣkayoḥ \|

kāhalā vādyabhāṇḍasya viśeṣe kāhalaḥ khale \|\| 77 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 78 \[unit 2087\]

kiṭṭālastāmrakalaśe lohagūthe'pyayaṃ pumān \|

kīlālaṃ rudhire'pi syātpānīye'pi napuṃsakam \|\| 78 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 79 \[unit 2088\]

kukūlaṃ śaṅkusaṅkīrṇaśvabhre puṃsi tuṣānale \|

kucelā viddhakarṇyāṃ syātkucelo malināṃśuke \|\| 79 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 80 \[unit 2089\]

kuṭilaṃ vācyavaddurge kuṭilā nimnagāntare \|

kuṇḍalaṃ karṇabhūṣāyāṃ tathā valayapāśayoḥ \|\| 80 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 81 \[unit 2090\]

kāñcanadrau guḍūcyāṃ ca kuṇḍalī vartate striyām \|

kuddālo yugapatre syātkuddālo bhūmidāraṇe \|\| 81 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 82 \[unit 2091\]

kuntalāḥ syurjanapade deśe keśe ca kuntalaḥ \|

kuntalo lāṅgale'pi syādyave bhāle'pi dṛśyate \|\| 82 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 83 \[unit 2092\]

ślokacchāyāhare caure śyāle mīne ca kumbhilaḥ \|

kuralaḥ pakṣibhede syātkuralaścūrṇakuntale \|\| 83 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 84 \[unit 2093\]

kulālaḥ kumbhakāre'pi kukkubhe kauśikepi ca \|

kuvalaṃ tūtpale muktāphale'pi badarīphale \|\| 84 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 85 \[unit 2094\]

kuśalaṃ dharmaparyāptikṣemeṣu triṣu śikṣite \|

vācyavatkevalastvekakṛtsnayoḥ kuhane'pi ca \|\| 85 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 86 \[unit 2095\]

niṇīṃte kevalaṃ jñānabhede syātkevalī na nā \|

mataḥ kau vārike keśadrumajāte'pi kaiśilaḥ \|\| 86 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 87 \[unit 2096\]

komalaṃ mṛdule nīre munau madye ca kohalaḥ \|

gandholī varaṭāyāṃ syādbhadrāśaṭyorapi smṛtā \|\| 87 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 88 \[unit 2097\]

viṣe māne'pi garalaṃ garalaṃ tṛṇapūlake \|

gokilo musale sīre gopālo gopabhūpayoḥ \|\| 88 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 89 \[unit 2098\]

gairilo lohacūrṇe syādgaurilo gaurasarṣape \|

granthilastriṣu saṃgranthau nā karīre vikaṅkate \|\| 89 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 90 \[unit 2099\]

cañcalā ca taḍillakṣmyoścañcalaścalakāminoḥ \|

vāte puṃsyatha catvālaḥ syādgarme hemakuṇḍale \|\| 90 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 91 \[unit 2100\]

candrilaścandramaulau ca vāstūke nāpite'pi ca \|

capalaḥ kṣaṇike śīghre cañcale'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 91 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 92 \[unit 2101\]

capalaḥ pārade mīne śilābhede'pi corake \|

capalā kamalā vidyutpuṃścalīpippalīṣvapi \|\| 92 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 93 \[unit 2102\]

cūḍālā cakralāyāṃ syādvācyavaccūḍayānvite \|

chagalī chāgayoṣāyāṃ chagalī vṛddhadārake \|\| 93 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 94 \[unit 2103\]

chagalastu mataśchāge chagalaṃ nīlavāsasi \|

jagalo medake madye kaitave madanadrume \|\| 94 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 95 \[unit 2104\]

jaṅgalastriṣu nirvārideśe'strī jaṅgalaṃ pale \|

jaṭilastu jaṭāyukte jaṭilā māṃsikauṣadhau \|\| 95 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 96 \[unit 2105\]

jambhalaḥ puṃsi jambīre jambhalo devatāntare \|

jambūlo jambuviṭape jambūlaḥ krakacacchade \|\| 96 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 97 \[unit 2106\]

jambālaḥ śaivale paṅke jāṅgalastu kapiñjale \|

vācyavajjaṅgalodbhūte śūkaśimbyāṃ tu jāṅgalī \|\| 97 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 98 \[unit 2107\]

jāṅgulī viṣavidyāyāṃ jāṅgulaṃ jālinīphale \|

syāttaṇḍulastu dhānyādinikare'pi viḍaṅgake \|\| 98 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 99 \[unit 2108\]

tamālaḥ khaḍge tāpicche tilake varuṇadrume \|

taralaścañcale khaḍge bhāsure triṣu puṃsi tu \|\| 99 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 100 \[unit 2109\]

hāramadhyamaṇau madyayavāgvostaralā striyām \|

tāmbūlī nāgavallyāṃ syāttāmbūlaṃ kramuke matam \|\| 100 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 101 \[unit 2110\]

tumulaṃ raṇasaṅghaṭṭe tumulastu kalidrume \|

taitilo gaṇḍake pusi taitilaṃ karaṇāntare \|\| 101 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 102 \[unit 2111\]

dukūlamadvayoḥ kṣaume dukūlaḥ sūkṣmavāsasi \|

dhavalaḥ sundare śvete triṣu puṃsi mahāvṛṣe \|\| 102 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 103 \[unit 2112\]

dhavalī saurabheyyāṃ syānnakulaḥ pāṇḍavāntare \|

babhrau ca nakulī tu syātkukkuṭyāṃ māṃsikauṣadhau \|\| 103 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 104 \[unit 2113\]

nākulī kukkuṭīkande nākulī cavyarāsnayoḥ \|

nābhīlaṃ nābhigarbhāṇḍe vaṅkṣaṇe cottamastriyaḥ \|\| 104 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 105 \[unit 2114\]

niculastu nicole syānniculo hijjaladrume \|

nirmālye'pyabhrake klībaṃ vimale triṣu nirmalam \|\| 105 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 106 \[unit 2115\]

niṣkalastu kalāśūnye naṣṭabīje'pi vācyavat \|

niṣkalā tu matā tasyāṃ yā nārī vigatārttavā \|\| 106 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 107 \[unit 2116\]

vartule'pi cale'pi syānnistalaṃ vācyaliṅgakam \|

naipālī navamālyāṃ syātkunaṭīsuvahākhyayoḥ \|\| 107 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 108 \[unit 2117\]

pañcālī putrikāgītyoḥ pañcālo janadeśayoḥ \|

paṭalaṃ tu chadirnetrarukpiṭake paricchade \|\| 108 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 109 \[unit 2118\]

na puṃsi vṛnde paṭalaṃ paṭolaṃ karkaśacchade \|

paṭolaṃ vastrabhede syājjyotsnikāyāṃ paṭolyapi \|\| 109 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 110 \[unit 2119\]

tilapūrṇe pale paṅke palalaṃ rākṣase pumān \|

pākalaṃ kuṣṭhabhaiṣajye pākalaḥ kuñjarajvare \|\| 110 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 111 \[unit 2120\]

kuṭapūrvaśca tatraiva navapāke tu pākalī \|

pācalo rādhanadravye dahane pavane'pi ca \|\| 111 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 112 \[unit 2121\]

pāṭalā pāṭalitarau puṣpe syātpāṭalā na nā \|

pāṭalī pāṭalāyāṃ syādāśuvrīhau tu pāṭalaḥ \|\| 112 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 113 \[unit 2122\]

pāṭalaḥ śvetarakte'pi tadvati triṣu pāṭalam \|

mṛtpātrabhede vāmāyāṃ vāgurāyāṃ ca pātilī \|\| 113 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 114 \[unit 2123\]

pātālaṃ bhūtale'pyaurve bandhakyāṃ bhuvi pāṃśulā \|

pāṃśulaḥ puṃścale śambhukhaṭvāṅge pāṃśusaṃyute \|\| 114 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 115 \[unit 2124\]

piṅgalo munibhede'gnau caṇḍāṃśoḥ pāripārśvike \|

nidhibhede kapau rudre piṅgalaḥ kapile'nyavat \|\| 115 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 116 \[unit 2125\]

striyāṃ karāyikāveśyā kumudastrīṣu piṅgalā \|

piculo jhabuke puṃsi nicule vārivāyase \|\| 116 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 117 \[unit 2126\]

picchilā śālmalau sindhubhedaśiṃśapayoḥ striyām \|

striyāmupodikāyāṃ ca picchilo vijile triṣu \|\| 117 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 118 \[unit 2127\]

piñjalaṃ kuśapatre syātpīte'pi triṣu piñjalam \|

pittalaṃ taijasadravye pittayukte tu vācyavat \|\| 118 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 119 \[unit 2128\]

pippalā jalapippalyāṃ bodhivṛkṣe tu pippalaḥ \|

niraṃśule pakṣibhede pippalaḥ pippalaṃ jale \|\| 119 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 120 \[unit 2129\]

vasanacchedabhede'pi kaṇāyāṃ tu ca pippalī \|

pudgalaḥ sundarākāre dehe cātmani pudgalaḥ \|\| 120 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 121 \[unit 2130\]

peśalo rucire dakṣe cāruśīle'pi vācyavat \|

praskhalo vājisannāhe triṣu hyantaścale cale \|\| 121 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 122 \[unit 2131\]

pratalaḥ syātsaṃhatayorvāmadakṣiṇahastayoḥ \|

pātālaloke pratalastatāṅgulikare'pi ca \|\| 122 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 123 \[unit 2132\]

vīṇādaṇḍe pravālo'strī vidrume navapallave \|

phenilo'riṣṭavṛkṣe syātphenilaṃ badarīphale \|\| 123 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 124 \[unit 2133\]

madanadruphale caiva saphene phenilastriṣu \|

bandhalastvāmale puñje palvale mattakuñjare \|\| 124 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 125 \[unit 2134\]

bahulaṃ vyomni bahulā tvelānīlikayorbhuvi \|

bahulāḥ kṛttikāsu syuḥ kṛṣṇapakṣe'nale pumān \|\| 125 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 126 \[unit 2135\]

vahulastu mataḥ prājye kṛṣṇavarṇe'pi vācyavat \|

vārdalo durdine puṃsi masīghāne'pi vārddalaḥ \|\| 126 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 127 \[unit 2136\]

maṅgalā śvetadūrvāyāṃ maṅgalastu mahīsute \|

maṅgalaṃ śreyasi klībaṃ tathā labdhārtharakṣaṇe \|\| 127 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 128 \[unit 2137\]

mañjulo jalaraṅkau syānmañjau tu triṣu peṣalaḥ \|

mañjulaṃ śaivale kuñje bimbeṣu triṣu maṇḍalam \|\| 128 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 129 \[unit 2138\]

maṇḍalaṃ nikurumbe'pi deśe dvādaśarājake \|

kuṣṭhāhibhede paridhau cakravāle ca maṇḍalam \|\| 129 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 130 \[unit 2139\]

maṇḍalaṃ syānmaṇḍalake sārameye tu maṇḍalaḥ \|

mahilā tu mahelāyāṃ mahilā'bhīrugundrayoḥ \|\| 130 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 131 \[unit 2140\]

mācalo vandicaure syādāmaye grāhayādasoḥ \|

dhattūre sāmake vrīhau madanadrau ca mātulaḥ \|\| 131 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 132 \[unit 2141\]

samantāttālamūlyākhukarṇyostu musalī striyām \|

musalī gṛhagodhāyāmayogre musalaṃ matam \|\| 132 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 133 \[unit 2142\]

kāñcyāṃ śailanitambe ca khaḍgabandhe ca mekhalā \|

mekhalā kaṭideśe ca rasālaḥ sarase triṣu \|\| 133 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 134 \[unit 2143\]

rasāla ikṣau cūte ca rasālaṃ belisihlayoḥ \|

rasālā mārjitāyāṃ syājjihvādūrvāvidāriṣu \|\| 134 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 135 \[unit 2144\]

rāmilo ramaṇe kāme lāṅgūlaṃ pucchaśephayoḥ \|

lāṅgalī jalapippalyāṃ lāṅgalaṃ kusumāntare \|\| 135 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 136 \[unit 2145\]

gṛhadāruviśeṣe ca sīre tāle ca lāṅgalam \|

lohalaḥ śṛṅkhalādhārye triṣu tvavyaktabhāṣiṇi \|\| 136 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 137 \[unit 2146\]

vaṇṭālaḥ śūrayoryuddhe puṃsi naukākhanitrake \|

vātulo vātasaṃghāte vātale mārutā'sahe \|\| 137 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 138 \[unit 2147\]

vātalaṃ rājakūṣmāṇḍabījakolāsthibījayoḥ \|

vāmilo dāmbhike'pi syāttriṣu vāme'pi vāmilaḥ \|\| 138 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 139 \[unit 2148\]

viḍālaḥ puṃsi mārjāre viḍālo vihagāntare \|

vipulaḥ pṛthule'gādhe merupaścimaparvate \|\| 139 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 140 \[unit 2149\]

vimalā śātalābhūmimedayornirmmale triṣu \|

viśālo vṛkṣabhede syādviśāle vipule'nyavat \|\| 140 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 141 \[unit 2150\]

viśālā tvindravāruṇyāmujjayinyāṃ ca dṛśyate \|

vṛṣalaḥ puṃsi śūdre syāccandragupte'pi rājani \|\| 141 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 142 \[unit 2151\]

śakalaṃ valkale khaṇḍe rāgavastutvacorapi \|

klībaṃ pātheyakulayormmatsare triṣu śambalam \|\| 142 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 143 \[unit 2152\]

śayāluḥ śunyajagare nidrāśīle tu vācyavat \|

śarālaṃ nīrasopāne vāstupote'pi pañjare \|\| 143 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 144 \[unit 2153\]

ṛjau vakre ca śīle ca śārdūlo rākṣasāntare \|

aṣṭāpade'pi vyāghe'pi śreṣṭhe syāduttarasthitaḥ \|\| 144 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 145 \[unit 2154\]

śālmalistu dvayorvṛkṣabhede dvīpāntare'pi ca \|

śītalaṃ śailaje puṣpe kāśīśe malayodbhave \|\| 145 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 146 \[unit 2155\]

śīte cāsanaparṇyāṃ ca śītalaḥ śītale triṣu \|

śevāle śītalaṃ klībaṃ śaileye'pi ca śītalam \|\| 146 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 147 \[unit 2156\]

śṛgālī tu śivābhītyoḥ śṛgālaḥ pherudaityayoḥ \|

śṛṅkhalā nigaḍe'pi syātpuṃskaṭīvastrabandhane \|\| 147 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 148 \[unit 2157\]

śevāle padmakāṣṭhe'pi śaivalaṃ matamadvayoḥ \|

śauṣkalaḥ śuṣkamāṃsasya pāṇike piśitāśini \|\| 148 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 149 \[unit 2158\]

śmāmalastvasite'svacche śyāmavarṇe tu vācyavat \|

śraddhālurdohadinyāṃ syādvācyavacchraddhayānvite \|\| 149 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 150 \[unit 2159\]

śrīphalī nīlikādhātryormmālūre śrīphalī pumān \|

paṇḍālī tu sarojinyā kāmukītailamānayoḥ \|\| 150 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latṛtīyam / 151 \[unit 2160\]

saṅkulaṃ vācyavadvyāpte'spaṣṭārthavacane'pi ca \|

sandhilā tu suraṅgāyāṃ nadīmadirayorapi \|\| 151 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 152 \[unit 2161\]

lacaturtham \|

balābhede tvatibalā prabale'tibalastriṣu \|

akṣamālā vijānīyādarundhatyakṣasūtrayoḥ \|\| 152 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 153 \[unit 2162\]

udūkhalaṃ guggulau syādulūkhalamulūkhale \|

ekāṣṭhīlā striyāṃ puṃsi pāpacelyāṃ buke kramāt \|\| 153 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 154 \[unit 2163\]

kacamālo marudvāhe nāgabhede jaṭāntare \|

kandarālaḥ pumāngarddabhāṇḍe'kṣaplakṣavṛkṣayoḥ \|\| 154 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 155 \[unit 2164\]

astrī kamaṇḍaluḥ kuṇḍyāṃ parkkaṭīpādape pumān \|

klībaṃ karmmaphalaṃ karmmaraṅgakarmmavipākayoḥ \|\| 155 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 156 \[unit 2165\]

puṃsi kolāhale sarjarase kalakalaḥ smṛtaḥ \|

kutūhalaṃ kautuke syāttriṣu śaste kutūhalam \|\| 156 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 157 \[unit 2166\]

kṛtāñjalistu bhaiṣajye vihito yena cāñjaliḥ \|

khatamālaḥ pumāndhūme khatamālo balāhake \|\| 157 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 158 \[unit 2167\]

gaṇḍaśailo giribhraṣṭasthūlopalakapolayoḥ \|

striyāṃ gandhaphalī phalyāṃ tathā campakakorake \|\| 158 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 159 \[unit 2168\]

golāṃgūlaṃ tu gopucche golāṅgūlaḥ kapau pumān \|

cakrabālo girerbhede cakravālaṃ tu maṇḍale \|\| 159 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 160 \[unit 2169\]

jalāñcalaṃ tu śaivāle svataḥ pānīyanirgame \|

dalāmalaṃ marubake damane'pi dalāmalam \|\| 160 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 161 \[unit 2170\]

dhvaninālā tu vīṇāyāṃ veṇukāhalayorapi \|

bhavetparimalaścittahārigandhavimardayoḥ \|\| 161 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 162 \[unit 2171\]

ratāmardasamunmīladaṅgarāgādisaurabhe \|

pīṭhakeliḥ pīṭhamardde karakākekṣirāgayoḥ \|\| 162 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 163 \[unit 2172\]

daurgatau vārivāhe ca pīṭhakelipadābhidhā \|

strīpuṃsayorbahuphalā malayūnīpayoḥ kramāt \|\| 163 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 164 \[unit 2173\]

bṛhannalo guḍākeśe mahāpoṭagale'pi ca \|

bhadrakālī tu pārvatyāṃ gandholyāmoṣadhībhidi \|\| 164 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 165 \[unit 2174\]

bhasmatūlaṃ hime pāṃśuvarṣaṇagrāmakūṭayoḥ \|

maṇimālā matā yoṣiddaśanakṣatahārayoḥ \|\| 165 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 166 \[unit 2175\]

madakalaḥ syānmattebhe madenā'vyaktavāci ca \|

mahākālo mahādeve kimpāke pramathāntare \|\| 166 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 167 \[unit 2176\]

mahānīlo nāgabhede mahānīlaśca mārkave \|

mahābalaṃ sīsake ca balaprauḍhe tu vācyavat \|\| 167 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 168 \[unit 2177\]

gorakṣataṇḍulāyāṃ tu striyāmeva mahābalā \|

muktāphalaṃ tu muktāyāṃ karṇapūre bale phale \|\| 168 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 169 \[unit 2178\]

syātkadalyāṃ mṛtyuphalī mahākālatarau pumān \|

pumānyavaphalo veṇau kuṭaje māṃsikauṣadhau \|\| 169 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 170 \[unit 2179\]

rajasvalastu mahiṣe puṣpavatyāṃ rajasvalā \|

vātakeliḥ kalālāpe ṣiḍgānāṃ dantakhaṇḍane \|\| 170 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 171 \[unit 2180\]

klībaṃ vāyuphalaṃ śakrakārmuke varṣaṇopale \|

pumānvicakilo mallībhede damanake'pi ca \|\| 171 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 172 \[unit 2181\]

udumbare skandhaphale nālikere sadāphalaḥ \|

haritālī nabhorekhākhaḍgadūrvāsu dṛśyate \|\| 172 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacaturtham / 173 \[unit 2182\]

halāhalo brahmasarppe jyeṣṭhikāyāṃ viṣāntare \|

airāvate hastimallo hastimallo vināyake \|\| 173 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lapaṃcamam

## lāntavargaḥ / lapaṃcamam / 174 \[unit 2183\]

lapaṃcamam \|

āsutovalaśabdastu mato yajvani śauṇḍike \|

bhaveduddaṇḍapālastu matsyasarppaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 174 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lapaṃcamam / 175 \[unit 2184\]

rājarāje'pi kālindībhedanepyekakuṇḍalaḥ \|

gajapittajvare pāke pavane kūṭapākalaḥ \|\| 175 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lapaṃcamam / 176 \[unit 2185\]

kṛpīṭapālaḥ puṃsyeva kenipātasamudrayoḥ \|\| 176 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lapaṃcamam / 177 \[unit 2186\]

syātpāṇḍukambalaḥ śvetakamvale grāvadantare \|

vivāhadinasambandhaśiromālye'pi sammatā \|\| 177 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lapaṃcamam / 178 \[unit 2187\]

matā suratatālī tu dūtikāmastakasrajoḥ \|

mantracūrṇalamicchanti vaśīkaraṇavedini \|\| 178 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lapaṃcamam / 179 \[unit 2188\]

ḍākinīmokṣamantrajñe kuśāmbuprokṣaṇe'pi ca \|\| 179 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lapaṃcamam / fragment 2189

iti viśvalocane'parābhidhānāyāṃ muktāvalyāṃ lakārāntavargaḥ \|

## vāntavargaḥ

## vāntavargaḥ / fragment 2190

atha vāntavargaḥ \|

## vāntavargaḥ / vaikam \|

## vāntavargaḥ / vaikam \| / 1 \[unit 2191\]

vaḥ kumbhe varuṇe va syādivārthe sāṃtvane'vyayam \|

vā vātatātayorgranthau viḥ khagākāśayoḥ pumān \|\| 1 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vaikam \| / fragment 2192

svo jñātāvātmani svaṃ tu triṣvātmīye ghane'striyām \|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 2193\]

vadvitīyam \|

kaviḥ śukre'pi vālmīke sūrau kāvyakare pumān \|\| 2 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 2194\]

kiṇvaṃ pāpe surābīje klībaḥ paṇḍe'pyavikrame \|

kharvo hrasve nyagarthepi kharvaḥ syādabhidheyavat \|\| 3 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 2195\]

grīvā grīvāśirāyāṃ syādgrīvā syātkandharābhidhā \|

chaviḥ syādapi śobhāyāṃ dyutāvapi mataśchaviḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 2196\]

onḍrapuṣpe javā vege javo vegini vācyavat \|

jīvo vācaspatau vṛkṣaprabhede prāṇimātrayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 2197\]

jīvā jīvantikāmaurvīkṣitiśiñjitavṛttiṣu \|

matā jīvā vacāyāṃ ca jīvā jīvaṃ ca jīvite \|\| 6 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 2198\]

tattvaṃ svarūpe nṛtyasya prabhede paramātmani \|

davo dāvaśca puṃsyeva vane'pi vanapāvake \|\| 7 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 2199\]

divaṃ svarge'ntarikṣe ca dyaurdyaurdivi ca khe striyām \|

devo rājñi sure meghe devaṃ syādindriye matam \|\| 8 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 2200\]

devī bhaṭṭārikāyāṃ ca tejanīpṛkkayorapi \|

nāṭyokyāṃ cābhiṣiktāyāṃ devī devī nṛpastriyām \|\| 9 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 2201\]

dravaḥ syānnarmaṇi rase pradrāve vidrave gatau \|

dvandvaṃ tu mithune yugme dvandvaḥ kalahaguhyayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 2202\]

dhavaḥ patyau pumānvṛkṣabhede dhūrtte nare'pi ca \|

dhruvaḥ klībe śive śaṅkau munau yoge vaṭai vasau \|\| 11 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 2203\]

dhruvaṃ tu niścite tarkke nityaniścalayostriṣu \|

dhruvā mūrvāśāliparṇyorgītisrugbhedayorapi \|\| 12 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 2204\]

navaḥ kāke stutau puṃsi navaṃ navye'bhidheyavat \|

nīvī tu strīkaṭīvastragranthau mūladhane'striyām \|\| 13 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 2205\]

mataṃ pakvaṃ pariṇate vināśābhimukhe triṣu \|

pārśvaṃ kakṣā'dhare cakropānte parśugaṇā'ntike \|\| 14 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 2206\]

pṛthvī bhuvi pṛthau hiṅgupatrikākṛṣṇajīrayoḥ \|

prādhvaṃ tu bandhane prahve'pyatidūrapathe tathā \|\| 15 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 16 \[unit 2207\]

plavaḥ kāraṇḍave bheke melake vārivāyase \|

plakṣe plutigatau śabde niṣāde kulake kapau \|\| 16 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 17 \[unit 2208\]

kramanimnakṣitau gandhatṛṇe'pi na dvayoḥ plavam \|

bhavaḥ śrīkaṇṭhasaṃsāraśreyaḥsattāptijanmasu \|\| 17 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 18 \[unit 2209\]

bhāvaḥ svabhāvaceṣṭā'bhiprāyasattvātmajanmani \|

bhāvaḥ kriyāyāṃ līlāyāṃ padārthe'bhinayāntare \|\| 18 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 19 \[unit 2210\]

jantau budhe vibhūtau ca nāṭyoktyā paṇḍite'pi ca \|

revā jaṃbālinībhede revā nīlīsmarastriyoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 20 \[unit 2211\]

matā laghvī tu hrasvāyāṃ prakāre syandanasya ca \|

laṭvā karaṃjabhede syātphale vādye khagāntare \|\| 20 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 21 \[unit 2212\]

lavo leśe vilāse ca cchedane rāmanandane \|

śrīphale'pi phale bilvaṃ viśve deveṣu nāgare \|\| 21 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 22 \[unit 2213\]

viśvā viṣāyāṃ sarvasminviśvaṃ syādabhidheyavat \|

viśvaṃ tu viṣṭape klībaṃ śivirbhūrje nṛpāntare \|\| 22 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 23 \[unit 2214\]

śivo hare yogabhede vede kīle'pi bāluke \|

guggule puṇḍarīkadrau śivaṃ mokṣe sukhe jale \|\| 23 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 24 \[unit 2215\]

kuśale'pi śivā tu syādgauryāmalakahetuṣu \|

śivā jhāṭāmalāpathyākroṣṭrīsaktuphalāsu ca \|\| 24 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 25 \[unit 2216\]

sattvaṃ jantuṣu na strī syātsattvaṃ prāṇātmabhāvayoḥ \|

dravye bale piśācādau sattāyāṃ guṇavittayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 26 \[unit 2217\]

svabhāve vyavasāye ca sattvamityabhidhīyate \|

savaṃ jalāḍhyayoḥ snāne savaḥ sandhānayajñayoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 27 \[unit 2218\]

sāntvaṃ dākṣiṇyamānne'pi sāṃtvaṃ sāmani ca smṛtam \|

sruvā srugbhedaśallakyormūrvāyāṃ ca matā sruvā \|\| 27 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvitīyam / 28 \[unit 2219\]

havaḥ syādadhvarāhvānanideśeṣu mataḥ pumān \|

hrasvaḥ kharve nyagarthepi rājikāyāṃ kṣute kṣavaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 29 \[unit 2220\]

vatṛtīyam \|

abhāvaḥ syādasattāyāmabhāvo maraṇe'pi ca \|

akṣīvastriṣvamande syādakṣīvo'vasare pumān \|\| 29 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 30 \[unit 2221\]

ārttavaṃ puṣparajasoḥ samudbhūte tu vācyavat \|

āśravaḥ syātpratijñāyāṃ kleśe'pi vacanasthite \|\| 30 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 31 \[unit 2222\]

āhavastu pumānyāge saṅgare'pyāhavastathā \|

utsavo maha utsedha icchāprasarakopayoḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 32 \[unit 2223\]

uddhavastūtsave kṛṣṇamātule yajñapāvake \|

kāravī dīpyamadhurātvakpatrīkṛṣṇajīrake \|\| 32 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 33 \[unit 2224\]

kitavaḥ puṃsi dhustūre mattavañcakayorapi \|

punnāge mādhave puṃsi keśāḍhye triṣu keśavaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 34 \[unit 2225\]

kaitavaṃ tu chale dyūte kairavaḥ śatrudhūrttayoḥ \|

kairavaṃ kumude klībaṃ candrikāyāṃ tu kairavī \|\| 34 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 35 \[unit 2226\]

kauṭṭavī caṇḍikāyāṃ syāttathā nagnastriyāmapi \|

gāṇḍīvagāṇḍivau na strī kārmuke'rjunakārmuke \|\| 35 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 36 \[unit 2227\]

gālavastu munau lodhre tāṇḍavaṃ tṛṇanṛtyayoḥ \|

svarge'ntarikṣe tridivastridivā saridantare \|\| 36 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 37 \[unit 2228\]

dīdivistridaśācārye bhavedanne'pi dīdiviḥ \|

dvijihvaḥ pannage puṃsi sūcake tvabhidheyavat \|\| 37 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 38 \[unit 2229\]

niṣpāvaḥ śūrppapavane pacane ca kaḍaṅgare \|

niṣpāvo nirvikalpe'pi śimbikārājamāṣayoḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 39 \[unit 2230\]

apalāpe'pi nikṛtāvaviśvāse'pi nihnavaḥ \|

pañcatvaṃ syāttu pañcānāṃ bhāve'pi nidhane'pi ca \|\| 39 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 40 \[unit 2231\]

pallavo vistare khaṅge śṛṅgārelaktarāgayoḥ \|

cale'pyastrī tu kisale viṭape'pi ca pallavaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 41 \[unit 2232\]

tugāyāṃ pārthivī bhūpe pumānbhūvikṛtau triṣu \|

puṅgavo vṛṣabhe śreṣṭhe gavomeṣajalāntare \|\| 41 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 42 \[unit 2233\]

prabhavo janmahetau syādapāṃmūle parākrame \|

prabhavaḥ kiṃvadantīnāṃ sañcāragatikārake \|\| 42 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 43 \[unit 2234\]

ādyopalabdhaye sthāne prabhāvaḥ śaktitejasoḥ \|

prasavo garbhamokṣe syādvṛkṣāṇāṃ phalapuṣpayoḥ \|\| 43 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 44 \[unit 2235\]

paraṃparāprasaṅge ca lokotpāde ca putrayoḥ \|

prasevo vallakīvādyakāṣṭhe syūte'pi dṛśyate \|\| 44 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 45 \[unit 2236\]

pheravo rākṣase pherau vallavaḥ sūdagopayoḥ \|

bhīmasene'pyatha pumānbandhau suhṛdi bāndhavaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 46 \[unit 2237\]

bhārgavaḥ śukragajayoḥ paraśurāme sudhanvani \|

bhārgavī pārvatīlakṣmīsitadūrvāsu sammatā \|\| 46 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 47 \[unit 2238\]

bhairavaḥ puṃsi bharge syādbhairavaṃ bhīṣaṇe triṣu \|

mādhavaḥ keśave rādhe vasante'tha mādhavī \|\| 47 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 48 \[unit 2239\]

madhūtthaśarkkarāmadyakuṭṭanīṣvatimuktake \|

rāghavastu mahāmīnaprabhede raghuvaṃśaje \|\| 48 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 49 \[unit 2240\]

rājīvo matsyamṛgayostriṣu rājopajīvini \|

klībaṃ padme rauravastu narake triṣu bhairave \|\| 49 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 50 \[unit 2241\]

vaḍavā'śvākumbhadāsyoḥ strīviśeṣe dvijastriyām \|

vāḍavā vaḍavāsaṅghe strīṇāṃ ca karaṇāntare \|\| 50 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 51 \[unit 2242\]

pātāle na striyāmaurvaṃ vipre ca nari vāḍavaḥ \|

padravo'pakrame buddhau vibhavo nirvṛtau ghane \|\| 51 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 52 \[unit 2243\]

vibhāvaḥ syātparicaye kāmasyoddīpane'pi ca \|

śatrūṇāṃ bhāvasaṃhatyoḥ śātravaṃ śātravo dviṣi \|\| 52 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 53 \[unit 2244\]

suṣavī kāravelle syājjīrake kṛṣṇajīrake \|

ṣāḍavastu rase nāge'pyāśuvrīhiprasūnayoḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 54 \[unit 2245\]

naukāyāṃ vāsane cātha sacivo bhṛtyamantriṇoḥ \|

sambhavaḥ smṛta utpattau hetau sattve ca melake \|\| 54 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / 55 \[unit 2246\]

ādhārānatiraktatve ādheyasya ca sambhavaḥ \|

sugrīvo vānarapatau cārugrīve tu vācyavat \|\| 55 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatṛtīyam / fragment 2247

saindhavo māṇimanthe'śve sindhudeśabhave triṣu \|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacaturtham

## vāntavargaḥ / vacaturtham / 56 \[unit 2248\]

vacaturtham \|

anubhāvaḥ prabhāve syānniścaye bhāvasūcake \|

apahnavo'palāpe'pi puṃsi snehe'pyapahnavaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacaturtham / 57 \[unit 2249\]

snāne'pi madyasandhāne yajñe cābhiṣavaḥ pumān \|

ādīnavastu doṣe syātparikliṣṭadurantayoḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacaturtham / 58 \[unit 2250\]

utpāte viplave caiva saiṃhikeye'pyupaplavaḥ \|

valmīkajanmani naṭe yācake ca kuśīlavaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacaturtham / 59 \[unit 2251\]

ekayoktyā matau rāmaputrayośca kuśīlavau \|

jalabilvo mataḥ kūrmme karkkaṭe jalacatvare \|\| 59 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacaturtham / 60 \[unit 2252\]

jīvaṃjīvaścakore syātpakṣibhede drumāntare \|

dolājīvo vārddhuṣike mithyājñānapraharṣite \|\| 60 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacaturtham / 61 \[unit 2253\]

dhāmārgavastvapāmārge devadālyāmapi smṛtaḥ \|

cañcale vyākule'pi syādvācyaliṅgaḥ pariplavaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacaturtham / 62 \[unit 2254\]

parābhavastiraskāre vināśe ca parābhavaḥ \|

mataḥ pāraśavaḥ pārastraiṇe śūdrāsute dvijāt \|\| 62 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacaturtham / 63 \[unit 2255\]

śastre'pyatha puṭagrīvo gargarītāmrakumbhayoḥ \|

vārddhuṣike valadevaḥ syādbaladevo bale'nile \|\| 63 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacaturtham / 64 \[unit 2256\]

rohitāśvo hariścandratanaye jātavedasi \|

śaileye saindhave klībaṃ miśyāṃ śītaśivaḥ pumān \|\| 64 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacaturtham / 65 \[unit 2257\]

sahadevā balādaṇḍotpalayoḥ śārivauṣadhau \|

sahadevī bhujaṅgākṣyāṃ sahadevastu pāṇḍave \|\| 65 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vapañcamam

## vāntavargaḥ / vapañcamam / 66 \[unit 2258\]

vapaṃcamam \|

syādāśitaṃbhavastṛptāvannādye tvāśitaṃbhavam \|\| 66 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vapañcamam / fragment 2259

iti viśvalocane'parābhidhānāyāṃ muktāvalyāṃ vakārāntavargaḥ \|

## śāntavargaḥ

## śāntavargaḥ / fragment 2260

atha śāntavargaḥ \|

## śāntavargaḥ / śaikam

## śāntavargaḥ / śaikam / 1 \[unit 2261\]

śaikam \|

śaḥ śatāyuṣi hiṃsāyāṃ śaṃ dharmme śā tu mātari \|

śī strīṣu svaparastrīṣu śīḥ syātsadananidrayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 2262\]

śadvitīyam \|

āśā tṛṣṇādiśorāśurvrīhau klībaṃ tu satvare \|

īśā lāṅgaladaṇḍe syādīśaḥ syādīśvare prabhau \|\| 2 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 2263\]

aṃśustviṣi ravau leśe kāśastu kṣavathau tṛṇe \|

vārāṇasyāṃ tu kāśī syātkīśo markkaṭanagnayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 2264\]

kuśo rāmasute dvīpe yoktre darbhe tu na striyām \|

kuśo matte'pi pāpiṣṭhe triṣu klībe tu vāriṇi \|\| 4 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 2265\]

matā kuśā tu balāyāṃ kuśī phāle prakīrtitā \|

keśo bāle'pi hrībere daityabhedapracetasoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 2266\]

kleśo duḥkhe'pi rogādau vyavasāye ca dṛśyate \|

darśastu daśame puṃsi darśaḥ sūryendusaṅgame \|\| 6 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 2267\]

pakṣāntavaidikavidhau daśā tu vasanāṃśuke \|

daśā karmavipāke'pi syāddaśā varttyavasthayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 2268\]

dṛg darśane ca netre strī jñātṛdarśakayostriṣu \|

daṃśaḥ sannāhavanamakṣikayorbhujagakṣate \|\| 8 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 2269\]

doṣe'pi khaṇḍane daṃśo daṃśo marmmaṇi ca smṛtaḥ \|

nāśaḥ palāyane'pi syānnidhanānupalambhayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 2270\]

syānniśā nigaḍe kvāpi striyāṃ rātriharidrayoḥ \|

niśā dāruharidrāyāṃ mahāpūrvā niśārddhake \|\| 10 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 2271\]

paśurmṛgādau ca pramathe paśurmāṃsārikātmani \|

ajñāne chāgamātre'pi paśu havyarthamavyayam \|\| 11 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 2272\]

pāśaḥ pakṣādibandhe syāccayārthastu kacātparaḥ \|

chātrādyante ca nindārthaḥ karṇāṃte śobhanārthakaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 2273\]

pāṃśurdhūliṣu śasyārthacirasañcitagomaye \|

peśī palalapiṇḍyāṃ syānmāsīkhaḍgapidhānayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 2274\]

supakvakaṇike peśī peśī maṇḍāntare'pi ca \|

rāśistu puñje puṃsyeva tathā meṣavṛṣādiṣu \|\| 14 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 2275\]

vaśastriṣu syādvivaśe vaśaṃ vāñchāprabhutvayoḥ \|

vaśā yoṣāsutāvandhyāstrīgavīkariṇīṣvapi \|\| 15 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 16 \[unit 2276\]

viṭ puṃsi vaiśye manuje praveśe tu striyāmiyam \|

veśaḥ praveśe nepathye veśo veśyāgṛhe gṛhe \|\| 16 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 17 \[unit 2277\]

vaṃśo veṇau kule varge pṛṣṭhasyāvayavāsthani \|

nāsāvivaradeśe'pi vādyabhāṇḍāntare'pi ca \|\| 17 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 18 \[unit 2278\]

śaśaḥ paśau gandharase puruṣāntaralodhrayoḥ \|

mataḥ śaśa iti kvāpi śītāṃśorapi lāñchane \|\| 18 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvitīyam / 19 \[unit 2279\]

sparśastu sparśane dāne rujāyāṃ sparśake'pi ca \|

sparśaḥ syātpuṃsi saṅgrāme praṇidhau ca mato hyayam \|\| 19 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 20 \[unit 2280\]

śatṛtīyam \|

ādarśaḥ puṃsi mukure ṭīkāyāṃ pratipustake \|

uḍḍīśaḥ pārvatīkānte granthabhede ca sa smṛtaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 21 \[unit 2281\]

upāṃśurjāpabhede syādupāṃśu vijane'vyayam \|

mādhavyāṃ kapiśā śyāve triṣu puṃsi ca sihlake \|\| 21 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 22 \[unit 2282\]

kampillakāsamarddekṣukṛpāṇe puṃsi karkkaśaḥ \|

nirdaye paruṣe krūre dṛḍhe sāhasike triṣu \|\| 22 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 23 \[unit 2283\]

kuliśo matsyabhede'sthisaṃhāre kuliśaṃ paśau \|

girīśaḥ śaṅkare vācaspatāvadripatāvapi \|\| 23 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 24 \[unit 2284\]

tuṅgīśastu hare candre duḥsparśaḥ syādyavāsake \|

kaṇṭakāryāṃ tu duḥsparśā kharasparśe tu vācyavat \|\| 24 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 25 \[unit 2285\]

nideśaḥ syādupānte'pi śāsane bhāṣaṇe pumān \|

nirveśo vetane bhoge nirveśo mūrchane'pi ca \|\| 25 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 26 \[unit 2286\]

niveśaḥ śibire puṃsi tathodvāhavināśayoḥ \|

nistriṃśo nirdaye khaḍge nīkāśo niścaye same \|\| 26 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 27 \[unit 2287\]

palāśaḥ kiṃśuke śaṭyāṃ palāśo nikaṣātmaje \|

klībaṃ palāśaṃ chadane palāśo hariti triṣu \|\| 27 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 28 \[unit 2288\]

pakṣīśo garuḍe kṛṣṇe piṅgāśaṃ jātyakāñcane \|

matsye pallīpatau puṃsi piṅgāśī nīlikauṣadhau \|\| 28 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 29 \[unit 2289\]

prakāśo'tiprasiddhe ca prahāse cā''tape sphuṭe \|

pradeśo deśabhittyoḥ syāttarjanyaṅguṣṭhasammite \|\| 29 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 30 \[unit 2290\]

bāliśastu śiśau bālyaliṅge mūrkhe'pi bāliśaḥ \|

bhūkeśyavalguje'pi syādbhūkeśaḥ śaivale vaṭe \|\| 30 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 31 \[unit 2291\]

lomaśastu pumānmeṣe vācyavallomasaṃyute \|

śṛgālīmarkaṭīmāṃsīśūkaśimbiṣu lomaśā \|\| 31 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 32 \[unit 2292\]

lomaśā kākajaṅghāyāṃ kāśīśe śākinībhidi \|

mahābhedātibalayorvīkāśastu vikāśavat \|\| 32 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 33 \[unit 2293\]

prakāśe syādvikasane vijane'pi mataḥ pumān \|

vikośaḥ paṭavarttau syādvikāśe vikace triṣu \|\| 33 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 34 \[unit 2294\]

vipāśā tu nadībheda triṣu pāśasamudgate \|

vivaśo vihvale'pi syādavaśyātmani ca triṣu \|\| 34 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 35 \[unit 2295\]

saṅkāśaḥ sannidhau tulye sadṛśaṃ tūcite same \|

sadeśaḥ sannidhau deśe sadeśo deśavatyapi \|\| 35 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / 36 \[unit 2296\]

sukhāśo rājatiniśe varuṇe sumanorathe \|

āsane'pi ca saṃveśaḥ saveśaḥ śayane'pi ca \|\| 36 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatṛtīyam / fragment 2297

hatāśo vācyavatkrūre nierddaye nirvāñchite \|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacaturtham

## śāntavargaḥ / śacaturtham / 37 \[unit 2298\]

śacaturtham \|

apadeśaḥ smṛto lakṣye nimittavyājayorapi \|\| 37 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacaturtham / 38 \[unit 2299\]

apabhraṃśo duṣpatane bhāṣābhedāpaśabdayoḥ \|

āśrayāśo bṛhadbhānau triṣvevāśrayanāśake \|\| 38 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacaturtham / 39 \[unit 2300\]

upadaṃśaḥ pumānmeḍhre pīḍāyāṃ ca vidaṃśane \|

upasparśastu saṃsparśe snānācamanayorapi \|\| 39 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacaturtham / 40 \[unit 2301\]

krūradṛk syātkhale vakre khaṇḍaparśuḥ pinākini \|

rāhau khaṇḍāmalakayorlepakṛtparśurāmayoḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacaturtham / 41 \[unit 2302\]

jīviteśo yame kānte jīvātau jīviteśvare \|

nāgapāśaḥ smṛtaḥ strīṇāṃ karaṇe varuṇāyudhe \|\| 41 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacaturtham / 42 \[unit 2303\]

vasetpañcadaśī paurṇamāsyamāvasyayormatā \|

pariveśaḥ parivṛtau bhānoścābhyarṇamaṇḍale \|\| 42 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacaturtham / 43 \[unit 2304\]

palaṃkaśā tu muṇḍīryāṃ lākṣāyāṃ puṃsi guggule \|

pādapāśī caṭukāyāṃ śṛṅkhalākaṭuke'pi ca \|\| 43 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacaturtham / 44 \[unit 2305\]

puroḍāśo havirbhede tathā somalatārase \|

piṣṭakasya camasyāṃ ca hutaśeṣe ca sammataḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacaturtham / 45 \[unit 2306\]

vārtāhare puroge ca sahāye ca pratiṣkaśaḥ \|

bhūmispṛk sammato vaiśye bhūmispṛgmanujepi ca \|\| 45 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacaturtham / fragment 2307

iti viśvalocane'parābhidhānāyāṃ muktāvalyāṃ śāntavargaḥ \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ

## ṣāntavargaḥ / fragment 2308

atha ṣāntavargaḥ \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣaikam

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣaikam / fragment 2309

ṣaikam \|

ṣakārastu mataḥ śreṣṭhe'pi syādgarbhavimocane \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 2310\]

ṣadvitīyam \|

uṣā bāṇasutāyāṃ syātprabhāte'pi vibhāvarau \|

uṣastu kāmuke puṃsi guggulādāvuṣaḥ pumān \|\| 1 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 2311\]

ṛṣiśchande vasiṣṭhādau dīdhitau tu ṛṣiḥ striyām \|

karṣaḥ palacaturthāṃśe karṣaḥ syātkarṣaṇe'pi ca \|\| 2 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 2312\]

karṣūḥ puṃsi karīṣāgnau karṣūḥ kulyābhidhāyinī \|

koṣo'strī kuḍmale divye peśyāṃ śabdādisaṅgrahe \|\| 3 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 2313\]

arthaughe jātikośe ca pātrakhaḍgapidhānayoḥ \|

panasādiphalasyāpi koṣaḥ syānmadhyavarttini \|\| 4 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 2314\]

ghoṣā tu śatapuṣpāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ kāṃsyembudadhvanau \|

ghoṣaḥ syādghoṣakābhīranisvanābhīrapalliṣu \|\| 5 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 2315\]

jhaṣā nāgabalāyāṃ syājjhaṣo vaisāriṇi smṛtaḥ \|

pipāsālikṣayostarṣastuṣo dhānyatvagakṣayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 2316\]

tṛṭ tṛṣā ca pipāsāyāṃ lipsāyāṃ ca striyāmubhe \|

tviṭ kāntau ruci bhāratyāṃ vyavasāyajigīṣayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 2317\]

doṣastu dūṣaṇe pāpe doṣā rātrau bhuje'pi ca \|

pauṣo māsaviśeṣe syātpauṣamuddhavayuddhayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 2318\]

pauṣī tu pauṣapaurṇamyāṃ puṣyayuktā bhavedyadi \|

praiṣastu preṣaṇonmānamarddanakleśavācakaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 2319\]

bhāṣā giri sarasvatyāṃ vikalpārthe vipūrvake \|

māṣo vrīhyantare māne mūrkhe tvagdūṣaṇāntare \|\| 10 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 2320\]

miṣastu sparddhane vyāje nimeṣe tu nipūrvakaḥ \|

meṣaḥ syāduraṇe rāśibhedabhaiṣajyabhedayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 2321\]

meṣa utpūrvako vedhe varṣāḥ syuḥ prāvṛṣi striyām \|

varṣamastrī varṣaṇe'bde jambūdvīpe ghane pumān \|\| 12 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 2322\]

viṣā tvativiṣāyāṃ syādviṣaṃ tu garale jale \|

viḍ vyāpane purīṣe ca vṛṣo mūṣakadharmmayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 2323\]

vṛṣabhe vāsake śreṣṭhe rāśau śṛṅgyāṃ ca śukrale \|

śukre puruṣabhede'pi vratināmāsane vṛṣī \|\| 14 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / 15 \[unit 2324\]

vṛṣā mūṣakaparṇyāṃ syātkapikacchvāmapi smṛtā \|

śuṣiḥ śoṣe bile khyātaḥ śeṣaḥ saṅkarṣaṇe vadhe \|\| 15 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvitīyam / fragment 2325

anante'pyavaśiṣṭe'pi śeṣā nirmmālyabhidyapi \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 16 \[unit 2326\]

ṣatṛtīyam \|

abhīṣuḥ puṃsi bhāsi syādabhīṣuḥ pragrahe'pi ca \|\| 16 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 17 \[unit 2327\]

ākarṣastvindriye khyāto dyūtākarṣaṇayorapi \|

pāśake śāriphalake kodaṇḍābhyāsavastuni \|\| 17 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 18 \[unit 2328\]

klībamāmiṣamutkoce māṃse sambhogalobhayoḥ \|

āmiṣaṃ suṃdarākārarūpādau viṣaye'pi ca \|\| 18 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 19 \[unit 2329\]

uṣṇīṣaṃ tu śiroveṣṭe kirīṭe lakṣaṇāntare \|

kalmāṣo rākṣase kṛṣṇakṛṣṇapāṇḍarayorapi \|\| 19 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 20 \[unit 2330\]

kaluṣaṃ kilbiṣe klībamāvile kaluṣaṃ triṣu \|

kilbiṣaṃ vṛjine roge'pyaparādhe'pi kilviṣam \|\| 20 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 21 \[unit 2331\]

kulmāṣo yavake puṃsi caṇake yavaṣaṣṭake \|

kulmāṣaṃ kāñjike klībaṃ gaṇḍūṣaḥ prasṛtonmite \|\| 21 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 22 \[unit 2332\]

gaṇḍūṣo mukhapūre'pi karihastāṅgulāvapi \|

jigīṣā jetumicchāyāṃ vyavasāyaprakarṣayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 23 \[unit 2333\]

tarīṣaḥ śobhanākāre bhelebdhivyavasāyayoḥ \|

tāviṣastu sarinnāthe kanakasvargayorapi \|\| 23 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 24 \[unit 2334\]

nahuṣo rājabhede syānnahuṣo bhujagāntare \|

nikaṣaḥ kaṣapāṣāṇe nikaṣā yātumātari \|\| 24 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 25 \[unit 2335\]

nimeṣanimiṣau kālabhede netranimīlane \|

paruṣaṃ karbure rūkṣe triṣu niṣṭhuravācyapi \|\| 25 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 26 \[unit 2336\]

puruṣaḥ punnāgamātaṅge mādhave paramātmani \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 27 \[unit 2337\]

ūrddhvavistṛtadoḥpāṇinṛmāne triṣu pauruṣam \|

pratyūṣo'harmukhe puṃsi pratyūṣo vasudaivate \|\| 27 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 28 \[unit 2338\]

pradoṣaḥ puṃsi doṣe syānnāṭyoktyārye ca māriṣaḥ \|

rauhiṣaṃ kattṛṇe puṃsi mṛgabhede tu rauhiṣaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 29 \[unit 2339\]

viśeṣo bhedamātre'pi viśeṣastilake'pi ca \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 30 \[unit 2340\]

vyākarṣaḥ śāriphalake dyūtākṣākarṣaṇeṣu ca \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatṛtīyam / 31 \[unit 2341\]

kuśīlaveṣe śailūṣaḥ śailūṣo bilvapādape \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / pacaturtham / 32 \[unit 2342\]

pacaturtham \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / pacaturtham / 33 \[unit 2343\]

sure matsye'pyanimiṣaḥ sure matsye'nimeṣavat \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / pacaturtham / 34 \[unit 2344\]

mārttaṇḍe khaṇḍaparaśau kapītanakiśorayoḥ \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / pacaturtham / 35 \[unit 2345\]

alambuṣā tu muṇḍīrīsvargaveśyāprabhedayoḥ \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / pacaturtham / 36 \[unit 2346\]

nandighoṣaḥ pārtharathe stutipāṭhakaghoṣaṇe \|

palaṅkaṣā gaukṣurake lākṣāguggulakiṃśuke \|

śṛṅgībhede mahāghoṣā puṃsi haṭṭe'tighoṣayoḥ \|

## sāntavargaḥ / saikam

## sāntavargaḥ / saikam / 1 \[unit 2351\]

saikam \|

sā puṃsyandhau ramāyāṃ syādratyāṃ se śrīśrute'pi saḥ \|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 2352\]

sadvitīyam \|

kāsūrvikalavāci syātkāsūḥ śaktyāyudhe striyām \|

kaṃso daityāntare kāṃsye kāṃsyabhājanamānayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 2353\]

syādgutsaḥ stabake stambe hārabhidgranthiparṇayoḥ \|

gosaḥ prabhāte puṃsyeva goso gandharase'pi ca \|\| 3 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 2354\]

cāsaḥ suvarṇacūḍe syātprabheda ikṣuparvaṇaḥ \|

maṇidoṣe bhaye trāso dāso bhṛtye'pi dhīvare \|\| 4 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 2355\]

śūdre'pi dānapātre'pi ceṭīsiṃnakayoḥ striyām \|

nāsā tu nāsikāyāṃ syānnāsā dvārorddhvadāruṇi \|\| 5 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 2356\]

prasūrmātari kandalyāmaśvāyāṃ puṃsi vīrudhi \|

vasurnā devabhede ca yoktre vahnau yudhe triṣu \|

vasu vṛddhyauṣadhe ratne'pi śyāme haṭṭake ghane \|\| 6 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 2357\]

vācyavanmadhure'pi syādbhāḥ prabhāve ruci striyām \|

bhāsastu bhāsi gṛdhre ca goṣṭhakukkuṭake'pi ca \|\| 7 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 2358\]

māṃsaṃ syādāmiṣe māṃsī kakkolījaṭayoḥ striyām \|

māḥ sudhīdīdhitau māse candre candrātparo'pi saḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 2359\]

misiḥ strī madhurīmāṃsyoḥ śatapuṣpājamodayoḥ \|

prasastu muhimūhe syānmūso māsyāmapi smṛtaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 2360\]

rasaḥ svāde'pi tiktādau śṛṅgārādau drave viṣe \|

pārade dhātuvīryāmburāge gandharase tanau \|\| 10 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 2361\]

raso ghṛtādāvāhārapariṇāmodbhave'pi ca \|

rasā jihvāsruvāpāṭhāśallakīkaṅguṣu striyām \|\| 11 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 2362\]

rāsastu gopakrīḍāyāṃ bhāṣāśṛṅkhalake dhvanau \|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 2363\]

vāso gṛhe'pyavasthāne vāsā syādāṭarūṣake \|

munivistārayorvyāsaḥ śaṃsā vacanavāñchayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 2364\]

hiṃsā cauryādivadhayoḥ haṃsaḥ sūryamarālayoḥ \|

yogamantrādibhede ca matsare turagāntare \|

guṇe kope'pyabhimataṃ taraḥ syādbalavegayoḥ \|

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 24 \[unit 2375\]

tejaḥ parākrame dīptau prabhāve balaśukrayoḥ \|

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 25 \[unit 2376\]

dhanurdhanurdhare'pi syāddhanurarjunabhūruhe \|

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 26 \[unit 2377\]

varṣāsu śrāvaṇe ghrāṇe nabhāḥ palitamastake \|

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 27 \[unit 2378\]

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 28 \[unit 2379\]

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 29 \[unit 2380\]

punararthe'vyayaṃ bhūyo bhūyāṃstu bahuṣu triṣu \|

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 30 \[unit 2381\]

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 31 \[unit 2382\]

harṣe vege ca rabhasastattve guhye rate rahaḥ \|

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 32 \[unit 2383\]

retaḥ śukre rase rephāḥ krūre'pi kṛpaṇe'dhame \|

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 34 \[unit 2385\]

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 36 \[unit 2387\]

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 37 \[unit 2388\]

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 39 \[unit 2390\]

## sāntavargaḥ / satṛtīyam / 40 \[unit 2391\]

## sāntavargaḥ / sacaturtham / 53 \[unit 2404\]

## sāntavargaḥ / sacaturtham / 56 \[unit 2407\]

matā madhurasā mūrvā drākṣādagdhikayorapi \|

mahārasastu kharjūre kośakāre kaserūṇi \|

hadvivīyam \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 2422\]

naṣṭendukalādarśepi pikālāpe striyāṃ kuhūḥ \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 2423\]

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 2424\]

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 2425\]

prāho nipuṇatarke'pi prauho hastyāṃghriparvaṇoḥ \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 2426\]

vāhāvāhau haye vāhau vāhaḥ syādvṛṣamānayoḥ \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 2427\]

moho mūḍhatvamātre'pi syādahammatimūrcchayoḥ \|

lohastu śastre lohaṃ tu joṅgake sarvataijase \|\| 7 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 2428\]

varhaṃ mayūrapicche'pi dale'pi syānnapuṃsakam \|

vaho gandhavahe skandhadeśe syādvṛṣabhasya ca \|\| 8 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 2429\]

vyūhastu balavinyāse vṛnde nirmāṇatarkayoḥ \|

saho bale ca bhūmyāṃ tu mudgaparṇyāṃ nakhauṣadhe \|\| 9 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 2430\]

sahadevākumāryośca sahaḥ kṣāntiyute triṣu \|

siṃhaḥ kaṇṭhīrave rāśibhede śreṣṭhe parasthitaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvitīyam / fragment 2431

siṃhī bṛhatyāṃ vārttākau rāhumātari vāsake \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 11 \[unit 2432\]

hatṛtīyam \|

ārohastu nitambe syāddīrghatve ca samucchraye \|\| 11 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 12 \[unit 2433\]

avarohe hastipake mānārohaṇayorapi \|

utsāhastūdyame sūtratantāvapi pumānayam \|\| 12 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 13 \[unit 2434\]

kaṭāho ghṛtatailādipākāmatre'pi karpare \|

dīpe'pi kūrmmapṛṣṭhe'pi kaṭāho mahiṣīśiśau \|\| 13 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 14 \[unit 2435\]

kalaho bhaṇḍane yuddhe khaḍgakoṣe varāṭake \|

dātyūhaḥ kālakaṇṭhe'pi tathā vandivihaṅgame \|\| 14 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 15 \[unit 2436\]

navāho nūtanadine navāhaḥ pratipattithau \|

nigraho bhartsane bandhe maryādāyāṃ ca nigrahaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 16 \[unit 2437\]

niryūho dvāri niryāse śikhare nāgadantake \|

nirūho bastibhede syāttarkkaniścitayorapi \|\| 16 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 17 \[unit 2438\]

paṭahastu samārambhe na strī paṭahamānake \|

pragrahastu tulāsūtre bandhe ca niyame bhuje \|\| 17 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 18 \[unit 2439\]

raśmau hayādiraśmau ca vandyāṃ svarṇālunīpayoḥ \|

pragrāhastu tulāsūtre varṣādipragrahe'pi ca \|\| 18 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 19 \[unit 2440\]

pravāho jalavege syātpāraṃparyānuvarttane \|

varāhaḥ kirimustādriviṣṇumegheṣu mānake \|\| 19 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 20 \[unit 2441\]

vārāhī mātṛkābuddhadevyorgṛṣṭyākhyabheṣaje \|

kāyasaṅgrāmavistārapravibhāgeṣu vigrahaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 21 \[unit 2442\]

vigrahaḥ syātsamāse'pi videho mithile pumān \|

videhā mithilāyāṃ syāddehaśūnye'pi vācyavat \|\| 21 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 22 \[unit 2443\]

vaidehī rocanāsītāvaṇigyoṣitsu pippalau \|

saṅgraho bṛhadyuttuṅge muṣṭau saṅgrahaṇe'pi ca \|\| 22 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / 23 \[unit 2444\]

suvahastu suvāte syātpuṃsi samyagvahe triṣu \|

elāparṇyāṃ tu suvahā sallakīrāsnayopi \|\| 23 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatṛtīyam / fragment 2445

suvahā vallakīhaṃsapadīśephālikāsu ca \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham / 24 \[unit 2446\]

hacaturtham \|

abhigraho'bhigrahaṇe'pyabhiyoge'pi gaurave \|\| 24 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham / 25 \[unit 2447\]

avaroho'vataraṇe mato mūlāllatodgame \|

śākhāśiphāyāṃ tridive'vagrahastu gajālike \|\| 25 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham / 26 \[unit 2448\]

vṛṣṭirodhe pratibandhe'pyasvātantrye'pyavagrahaḥ \|

avagrāho bhavedvṛṣṭirodhahastilalāṭayoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham / 27 \[unit 2449\]

aśvārohā'śvagandhāyāmaśvāroho'śvavārake \|

pumānupagraho vandyāmupayoge'nukūlane \|\| 27 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham / 28 \[unit 2450\]

upanāhastu vīṇāyāṃ bandhane vraṇalepane \|

nāsikāyāṃ gandhavahā vāte gandhavahaḥ pumān \|\| 28 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham / 29 \[unit 2451\]

tanūruhaṃ tu garuti syāllomni ca tanūruham \|

tamopaho jine sūrye dahane mṛgalakṣmaṇi \|\| 29 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham / 30 \[unit 2452\]

sūto devasaho devasahā daṇḍotpalauṣadhau \|

parigrahaḥ parijane patnyāṃ svīkāraśāpayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham / 31 \[unit 2453\]

mūle'pi paribarhastu rājayogye paricchade \|

parīvāho jalocchvāse bhūpālocitavastuni \|\| 31 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham / 32 \[unit 2454\]

pitāmahaḥ pitustāte brahmaṇyapi pitāmahaḥ \|

pratigrahaḥ svīkaraṇe sainyapṛṣṭhe grahāntare \|\| 32 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham / 33 \[unit 2455\]

mahadbhyo vidhivaddeye tadgrahe ca patadgrahe \|

varārohā kaṭau nāryāṃ puṃsi sādyavarohayoḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hacaturtham / fragment 2456

mahāsahā māsaparṇyāmamlāne'pi mahāsahāḥ \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hapañcamam / 34 \[unit 2457\]

hapañcamam \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hapañcamam / fragment 2458

atha kṣāntavargaḥ \|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣaikam

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣaikam / fragment 2460

kṣaikam \|

rākṣase kṣetramātre'pi kṣakāraḥ parikīrttitaḥ \|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 1 \[unit 2461\]

kṣadvitīyam \|

akṣastu pāśake cakre śakaṭe ca bibhītake \|\| 1 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 2 \[unit 2462\]

ācāre vyavahāre ca cullāvātmajñakarṣayoḥ \|

akṣaṃ syādindriye klībaṃ tutthe sauvarcale'pi ca \|\| 2 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 3 \[unit 2463\]

ṛkṣastu puṃsi bhallūke śoṇaphe kṛtavedhane \|

ṛṣibhede'dribhede ca tārāyāmṛkṣamastriyām \|\| 3 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 4 \[unit 2464\]

kakṣaḥ sairibhadormūlakacche śuṣkavane tṛṇe \|

gulminyāmapi kakṣā tu gṛhe kāñcīprakoṣṭhayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 5 \[unit 2465\]

paridhāne parīdhāne paścādañcalapallave \|

sparddhodgāravaratrāsu gajarajjau rathāṃśake \|\| 5 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 6 \[unit 2466\]

raukṣaṃ gīte tvanyavat syāttīkṣṇe śucimanojñayoḥ \|

dakṣo munau haravṛṣe kukkuṭe'gnau ca dhātari \|\| 6 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 7 \[unit 2467\]

dakṣaḥ syāddakṣiṇabhuje pragalbhe'nalase triṣu \|

dakṣā pṛthivyāmākhyātā dhvāṅkṣī kakkolikauṣadhau \|\| 7 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 8 \[unit 2468\]

dhvāṅkṣastu vāyase kaṅke gṛhe takṣakabhikṣuke \|

nyakṣaḥ paraśurāme syānnyukṣaḥ kārtsnyanikṛṣṭayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 9 \[unit 2469\]

pakṣaḥ keśātparo vṛnde pakṣo māsārddhapārśvayoḥ \|

gṛhabhittau grahe bhṛtye sakhyau rājagaje bale \|\| 9 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 10 \[unit 2470\]

sādhye garuti dehāṅge cullirandhravirodhayoḥ \|

nyāyānusārake prekṣaḥ prekṣā nṛtyekṣaṇe gatau \|\| 10 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 11 \[unit 2471\]

plakṣastu pippale jaṅghadvārapārśve gṛhasya ca \|

dvīpabhede garddabhāṇḍe bhikṣukītiviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 12 \[unit 2472\]

bhikṣā bhṛtyarthanāsevāsvapi bhikṣitavastuni \|

mokṣo'pavarge mṛtau ca mokṣo muṣkakapādape \|\| 12 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 13 \[unit 2473\]

kubere guhyake yakṣo rakṣā rakṣaṇalākṣayoḥ \|

rūkṣo vṛkṣāntare premaśūnyakarkkaśayostriṣu \|\| 13 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvitīyam / 14 \[unit 2474\]

lakṣaṃ na puṃsi saṅkhyāyāṃ klībaṃ chadmaśaravyayoḥ \|

lakṣaṃ vitastau ca klībaṃ vīkṣaṃ dṛśye'bhidheyavat \|\| 14 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣatṛtīyam

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣatṛtīyam / 15 \[unit 2475\]

kṣatṛtīyam \|

adhyakṣaḥ syādadhikṛte pratyakṣe'pyabhidheyat \|

ārakṣaṃ rakṣaṇīye'pi śirokarmmaṇi dantinām \|\| 15 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣatṛtīyam / 16 \[unit 2476\]

utprekṣā tu matā kāvyā'laṅkārā'navadhānayoḥ \|

gavākṣī tvindravāruṇyāṃ puṃsi jālakakīśayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣatṛtīyam / 17 \[unit 2477\]

gorakṣo nāgaraṅge syādgavāṃ ca parirakṣake \|

mṛgākṣī mṛganetrāyāmindravāruṇikāminoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣatṛtīyam / 18 \[unit 2478\]

raktākṣaḥ sairibhe krūre pārāvatacakorayoḥ \|

samīkṣā tattve buddhau syādgranthabhede nibhālane \|\| 18 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacaturtham

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacaturtham / 19 \[unit 2479\]

kṣacaturtham \|

devavṛkṣaḥ saptaparṇe mandārādiṣu guggule \|

vīravṛkṣastu bhallātapādape kakubhadrume \|\| 19 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacaturtham / 20 \[unit 2480\]

bhūtavṛkṣastu śākhoṭayakṣaśyonākapādape \|

vikhyāto rājavṛkṣastu suvarṇālupiyālayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacaturtham / 21 \[unit 2481\]

viśālākṣo hare tārkṣye viśālākṣī varastriyām \|

sakaṭākṣo dhavadrau syātkaṭākṣasahite triṣu \|\| 21 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacaturtham / 22 \[unit 2482\]

aṇāditavyādiguṇādiyogātpadaṃ bahuvrīhimataṃ ca vīkṣya \|

anuktaliṅgaṃ ca samūhanīyaṃ kṛtaṃ yadi kvāpi bahutvabhīteḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacaturtham / fragment 2483

iti viśvalocane'parābhidhānāyāṃ muktāvalyāṃ kṣakārāntavargaḥ \|

athāvyayāni \|

## avyayāni / ajantāni

## avyayāni / ajantāni / 1 \[unit 2485\]

ajantāni \|

akārādikamapyevamidānīṃ samanukramāt \|

mayā nānārthakāṇḍe'sminvidhīyante'vyayāni ca \|\| 1 \|\|

## avyayāni / ajantāni / 2 \[unit 2486\]

aḥ śrīkaṇṭhe'vyayaṃ tulyābhāvayorāḥ pitāmahe \|

ā pragṛhyaḥ smṛtau vākye'tyalpe'vyayamathā'vyayam \|\| 2 \|\|

## avyayāni / ajantāni / 3 \[unit 2487\]

āṅīṣadarthe'bhivyāptau sīmāyāṃ dhātuyogaje \|

santāpe ca prakope ca bhavedāḥ smṛtamavyayam \|\| 3 \|\|

## avyayāni / ajantāni / 4 \[unit 2488\]

istu kāme pumānkhede ruṣoktau cāvyayaṃ bhavet \|

ī lakṣmyāmavyayaṃ tvī syādduḥkhabhāvanakopayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## avyayāni / ajantāni / 5 \[unit 2489\]

uḥ śive nā'vyayaṃ tu syātsambuddhau roṣamāpaṇe \|

ūḥ syādanavyayaṃ rakṣārakṣasū triṣu rakṣake \|\| 5 \|\|

## avyayāni / ajantāni / 6 \[unit 2490\]

sūtikriyāyāṃ sūtau ca vākyārambhe tvasaṅkhyakam \|

ṛrdevamātari strī syādavyayaṃ vākyakutsayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## avyayāni / ajantāni / 7 \[unit 2491\]

ṝśca strī devatāmbāyāṃ syādeḥ puṃsi caturbhuje \|

smṛtisambodhanāhvāne'vyayamaistu śive pumān \|\| 7 \|\|

## avyayāni / ajantāni / 8 \[unit 2492\]

avyayaṃ tvai samākhyātaṃ smṛtyāmantraṇahūtiṣu \|

oḥ pumānbrahmaṇi khyāte'vyayamāmantraṇāhvayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## avyayāni / ajantāni / 9 \[unit 2493\]

aurnabhasyavyayaṃ tu syātsambuddhyāhvānayormatam \|

parabrahmaṇyanumatāvaḥ syādaśca tathā'vyayam \|\| 9 \|\|

## avyayāni / ajantāni / fragment 2494

aḥ puṃsi śaṅkare khyātaḥ kādikhyātamatovyayam \|

ca0

tiryaktirorthe ca kule vihagādiṣvanavyayam \|\| 13 \|\|

## avyayāni / cāntāni / 14 \[unit 2499\]

nanuca praśnaduṣṭoktyoḥ prāk syādigdeśakālataḥ \|

## avyayāni / tāntāni

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 17 \[unit 2505\]

ta0

tvati stutau \|

nitāntā'saṃpratikṣepaprakarṣe laṅghanepyati \|\| 17 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 18 \[unit 2506\]

ato'padeśe nirdeśe pañcamyante ca kāraṇe \|

antataḥ śāsane paṃcamyarthe sambhāvanāṅgayoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 19 \[unit 2507\]

astu syādabhyanujñāne'pyasūyāmātrayorapi \|

ahobata mataṃ khede sambuddhau cānukampane \|\| 19 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 20 \[unit 2508\]

ahobatādbhute'pi syādārāddūrasamīpayoḥ \|

itastu pañcamyarthe syādite niyamabhāgayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 21 \[unit 2509\]

iti hetau prakāre ca prakāśādyanukarṣayoḥ \|

iti prakaraṇe'pi syātsamāptau ca nidarśane \|\| 21 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 22 \[unit 2510\]

uta praśne vitarkerthe'pyutātyarthavikalpayoḥ \|

kintu syātpraśnamātre'pi kintu kāmavitarkayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 23 \[unit 2511\]

kimutā'tiśaye praśne vikalpārthe'pi kīrttitaḥ \|

kutaḥ syānnihnute praśne pañcamyarthe kutaḥ smṛtam \|\| 23 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 24 \[unit 2512\]

te tavārthe tvayārthe ca me ca mamamayārthayoḥ \|

tu pādapūraṇe bhedā'vadhāraṇasamuccaye \|\| 24 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 25 \[unit 2513\]

pakṣāntare niyoge ca praśaṃsāyāṃ vinigrahe \|

tata ādau paripraśne pañcamyarthe kathāntare \|\| 25 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 26 \[unit 2514\]

ānantarye'pi tāvattu kārtsnye mānāvadhāraṇe \|

paricchade tu paścāttu pratīcyāṃ carame'pi ca \|\| 26 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 27 \[unit 2515\]

purastātprathame prācyāmagrato'rthapurārthayoḥ \|

prati syātpratidāne ca prati pratinidhāvapi \|

pradhāne sambhave vīpsālakṣaṇādau prayogataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 28 \[unit 2516\]

mātrārthe cābhimukhye ca prakāśe ca smṛtaṃ prati \|

bata khede kṛpānindāsantoṣā''mantraṇādbhute \|\| 28 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 29 \[unit 2517\]

yataḥśabdastu niyame pañcamyarthavibhāgayoḥ \|

yadvatpraśne vitarke ca yāvanmāne'vadhāraṇe \|\| 29 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 30 \[unit 2518\]

sīmni kārtsnye paricchede śaśvatpunaḥsahārthayoḥ \|

svitpraśne ca vitarke ca sakṛtsahaikavārayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 31 \[unit 2519\]

yuktārthe bahumātrārthepyadhunārthe'pi samprati \|

pratyakṣavācakaḥ sākṣātsākṣāttulyārthavācakaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / 32 \[unit 2520\]

svastyāśīḥkṣemapuṇyeṣu mataṃ svasti sukhādiṣu \|

hanta harṣe'nukampāyāṃ vākyārambhaviṣādayoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## avyayāni / tāntāni / fragment 2521

vivāde śobhanārthe ca hantaśabdaḥ prayujyate \|
