tha0

athā'tho ca śubhe praśne sākalyārambhasaṃśaye \|\| 33 \|\|

## avyayāni / thāntāni / 34 \[unit 2523\]

anantare'pyanyathātvaparārthavitathārthayoḥ \|

tathā sādṛśyanirdeśaniścayeṣu samuccaye \|\| 34 \|\|

## avyayāni / thāntāni / 35 \[unit 2524\]

kāraṇasyopapattāvapyuddeśaprativākyayoḥ \|

yathā'numāne sādṛśye nirdeśoddeśayorapi \|\| 35 \|\|

## avyayāni / thāntāni / 36 \[unit 2525\]

kāraṇasyopapattau ca vṛthā tu vidhivarjite \|

vṛthā niṣkāraṇe vandhye sarvathā hetuvādayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## avyayāni / thāntāni / 37 \[unit 2526\]

utprādhānye prakāśe ca mokṣabandhorddhvakarmmasu \|

prābalyalābhabhāveṣu vibhāgā'svāsthyaśaktiṣu \|\| 37 \|\|

## avyayāni / thāntāni / fragment 2527

tatkāraṇe tadātve ca hetuyadyarthayostu yat \|

## avyayāni / nāntāni

## avyayāni / nāntāni / 38 \[unit 2528\]

na0

anu tvanukrame hīne paścādarthasahārthayoḥ \|

āyāme'pi samīpārthe sādṛśye lakṣaṇādiṣu \|\| 38 \|\|

## avyayāni / nāntāni / 39 \[unit 2529\]

kinnu praśne vitarke ca nanu praśnāvadhāraṇe \|

nanvanujñāvitarkāyamantreṣvanunaye nanu \|\| 39 \|\|

## avyayāni / nāntāni / 40 \[unit 2530\]

nānā vinārthe'pi mataṃ nānā'nekobhayārthayoḥ \|

niḥ syānnityabhṛśāścaryavinyāsakṣeparāśiṣu \|\| 40 \|\|

## avyayāni / nāntāni / 41 \[unit 2531\]

antarbhāve'pyadhobhāve darśane dānakarmmaṇi \|

bandhoparamasāmīpyamokṣakauśalasaṃyame \|\| 41 \|\|

## avyayāni / nāntāni / 42 \[unit 2532\]

niveśe'pyatha nu praśne'tīte'nunayavārthayoḥ \|

sthāne tu yuktasādṛśyakāraṇārtheṣu dṛśyate \|\| 42 \|\|

## avyayāni / pāntāni

## avyayāni / pāntāni / 43 \[unit 2533\]

pa0

apa syādapakṛṣṭārthe varjanārthe viparyaye \|

viyoge vikṛtau caurye harṣanirdeśayorapi \|\| 43 \|\|

## avyayāni / pāntāni / 44 \[unit 2534\]

api sambhāvanāśaṅkāpraśnagarhāsamuccaye \|

api yuktapadārtheṣu kāmakārakriyāsvapi \|\| 44 \|\|

## avyayāni / pāntāni / 45 \[unit 2535\]

upa hīne'dhike vyāptau śaktau cārambhapūjayoḥ \|

ācāryakaraṇe dāne dākṣiṇye vyatyaye'pi ca \|\| 45 \|\|

## avyayāni / pāntāni / 46 \[unit 2536\]

tadyoge doṣakathane maraṇārthodyamārthayoḥ \|

samāsanne'pi lipsāyāmupaśabdaḥ prakīrttitaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|

## avyayāni / vāntāni

## avyayāni / vāntāni / 47 \[unit 2537\]

va0

vaśabda upamāyāṃ syādvaruṇe vaḥ pumānayam \|

vā syādvikalpopamayorevārthe'pi samuccaye \|\| 47 \|\|

## avyayāni / vāntāni / fragment 2538

vai pādapūraṇe sambodhane'pyanunaye dhruve \|

## avyayāni / vāntāni / 70 \[unit 2563\]

va0

ava vyāptau paribhave viyogālambaśuddhiṣu \|

īṣadarthe'pi vijñānepyevaupamye'vadhāraṇe \|\| 70 \|\|

## avyayāni / vāntāni / 71 \[unit 2564\]

vastu yuṣmākamityarthe varttate bhedane tu vi \|

vi syādatīte nānārthe śreṣṭhe vistu khage pumān \|\| 71 \|\|

## avyayāni / bhāntāni

## avyayāni / bhāntāni / 48 \[unit 2539\]

bha0

abhītthaṃbhūtakathane'pyativīpsā'bhimukhyayoḥ \|\| 48 \|\|

## avyayāni / bhāntāni / 49 \[unit 2540\]

abhīkṣṇaṃ tu muhuḥśīghraprakarṣe'pyatisantate \|

syādabhīkṣṇaṃ tathā paunaḥpunyasantatayormatam \|\| 49 \|\|

## avyayāni / māntāni

## avyayāni / māntāni / 50 \[unit 2541\]

ma0

amā sahārthā'ntikayoramāvāsyāmamā striyām \|

alaṃ bhūṣaṇaparyāptiśaktivāraṇaniṣphale \|\| 50 \|\|

## avyayāni / māntāni / 51 \[unit 2542\]

yatne nitye'pyavaśyaṃ syādāsmṛtāvavadhāraṇe \|

idānīṃ vākyabhūṣāyāṃ sampratyarthe ca sammatam \|\| 51 \|\|

## avyayāni / māntāni / 52 \[unit 2543\]

iṃ duḥsvabhāvane krodhe pratyakṣe sannidhāvapi \|

uṃ praśneṅgīkṛtau roṣe ūṃ praśne roṣabhāṣaṇe \|\| 52 \|\|

## avyayāni / māntāni / 53 \[unit 2544\]

evaṃ prakrāropamayoraṅgīkāre'vadhāraṇe \|

oṃ syādanumatau proktaṃ praṇave cāpyupakrame \|\| 53 \|\|

## avyayāni / māntāni / 54 \[unit 2545\]

kaṃ śiraḥsukhanīreṣu kathaṃ praśnaprakārayoḥ \|

sambhrame sambhave cātha kāmaṃ tvanumatau matam \|\| 54 \|\|

## avyayāni / māntāni / 55 \[unit 2546\]

prakāmānugamā'sūyāsvatha kiṃ praśnakutsayoḥ \|

joṣaṃ tu tūṣṇīṃsukhayoḥ praśaṃsāyāṃ ca laṅghane \|\| 55 \|\|

## avyayāni / māntāni / 56 \[unit 2547\]

taddinaṃ dinamadhye syāttaddinaṃ prativāsare \|

tūṣṇīkāṃ maunamātre syāttūṣṇīkaṃ triṣu maunini \|\| 56 \|\|

## avyayāni / māntāni / 57 \[unit 2548\]

nāma prākāśyasambhāvyakutsā'bhyupagame krudhi \|

nūnaṃ tarke tu vikhyātaṃ nūnaṃ syādarthaniścaye \|\| 57 \|\|

## avyayāni / māntāni / 58 \[unit 2549\]

prādhvaṃ narme'nukūle'pi prakarṣātyarthayorbhṛśam \|

śaṃ kalyāṇe sukhe cātha smā'tīte pādapūraṇe \|\| 58 \|\|

## avyayāni / māntāni / 59 \[unit 2550\]

saṃ saḍgārthe śobhanārthe prahṛṣṭārthasamārthayoḥ \|

sāmi nindārddhayoryukte'pyadhunārthe'pi sāmpratam \|\| 59 \|\|

## avyayāni / māntāni / 60 \[unit 2551\]

haṃ ruṣoktāvanunaye huṃ syātpraśnavitarkayoḥ \|

hūṃ vikrame cānumatau tajjane'pi kvacinmatam \|\| 60 \|\|

## avyayāni / yāntāni

## avyayāni / yāntāni / 61 \[unit 2552\]

ya0

aye smṛtau viṣāde syādaye sambhramakopayoḥ \|

ayi kākukulālāpasambodhapremabhāṣite \|\| 61 \|\|

## avyayāni / yāntāni / fragment 2553

ayi praśnānunayayoḥ samayā'ntikamadhyayoḥ \|

## avyayāni / rāntāni

## avyayāni / rāntāni / 62 \[unit 2554\]

ra0

antarā tu vinārthe syānmadhyārthanikaṭārthayoḥ \|\| 62 \|\|

## avyayāni / rāntāni / 63 \[unit 2555\]

antaḥ prāntārthamadhyārthasvīkārārthe tu varjane \|

uraryururīvadūrī vistāre'ṅgīkṛtau trayam \|\| 63 \|\|

## avyayāni / rāntāni / 64 \[unit 2556\]

durniṣedhe'pi kaṣṭe'pi gatādyarthā'prakarṣayoḥ \|

nirniḥśeṣe niṣedhe ca krāntādyarthe ca niścaye \|\| 64 \|\|

## avyayāni / rāntāni / 65 \[unit 2557\]

parā gatau vadhe prātilomyaprādhānyagharṣaṇe \|

ābhimukhye vimokṣe ca bhṛśārthe vikrame'pi ca \|\| 65 \|\|

## avyayāni / rāntāni / 66 \[unit 2558\]

pari syātsarvatobhāve vīpsāyāṃ lakṣaṇādiṣu \|

āliṅgane nirasane vyāpane vyādhiśokayoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## avyayāni / rāntāni / 67 \[unit 2559\]

pūjoparamabhūṣāsu doṣākhyāne'pi varjane \|

punarbhidā'prathamayoḥ purā bhāvipurāṇayoḥ \|\| 67 \|\|

## avyayāni / rāntāni / fragment 2560

prabandhe nikaṭe'tīte svaḥ svargaparalokayoḥ \|

## avyayāni / lāntāni

## avyayāni / lāntāni / 68 \[unit 2561\]

la0

kila tvarucau vārttāyāṃ sambhāvyānunayārthayoḥ \|\| 68 \|\|

## avyayāni / lāntāni / 69 \[unit 2562\]

khalu syādvākyabhūṣāyāṃ khalu vīpsāniṣedhayoḥ \|

niścite sāntvane maune jijñāsādau khalu smṛtam \|\| 69 \|\|

## avyayāni / ṣāntāni

## avyayāni / ṣāntāni / 72 \[unit 2565\]

ṣa0

uṣā'saṅkhyaṃ sasaṅkhyaṃ ca niśāntaniśayormatam \|

doṣā rātrimukhe rātrāvatrānavyayamapyasau \|\| 72 \|\|

## avyayāni / ṣāntāni / 73 \[unit 2566\]

nikaṣā tvantike madhye rakṣomātaryanavyayam \|

vibhāṣā tu striyāṃ kvāpi vikalpārthe samuccaye \|\| 73 \|\|

## avyayāni / sāntāni

## avyayāni / sāntāni / 74 \[unit 2567\]

sa0

agrataḥ prathame'gre syādañjasā tattvatūrṇayoḥ \|

abhito'ntikasākalyasammukhomayato drute \|\| 74 \|\|

## avyayāni / sāntāni / 75 \[unit 2568\]

tiro'ntarddhau tiryagarthe nis niścayaniṣedhayoḥ \|

sākalyātītayoścātha nīcaiḥ svairālpayormmatam \|\| 75 \|\|

## avyayāni / sāntāni / 76 \[unit 2569\]

puro'gre prathame ca syātpurataḥ prathamāgrayoḥ \|

prātarddine'pi pūrvedyuḥ pūrvedyurddharmmavāsare \|\| 76 \|\|

## avyayāni / sāntāni / 77 \[unit 2570\]

pūrvatrārthe'pi pūrvedyurbhūyastu syātpunaḥpunaḥ \|

anavyayaṃ prabhūtārthe mithonyonyaṃ mitho rahaḥ \|\| 77 \|\|

## avyayāni / sāntāni / 78 \[unit 2571\]

prāduḥ syātprakaṭībhāve prāduḥ sambhāvyamātrake \|

śanaiḥ śanaiścare khyātaṃ svaire'pi ca śanairiti \|\| 78 \|\|

## avyayāni / sāntāni / 79 \[unit 2572\]

su pūjāyāṃ bhṛśārthā'numatikṛcchrasamṛddhiṣu \|

tatkālamātre sahasā sahasā''kasmike'pi ca \|\| 79 \|\|

## avyayāni / hāntāni

## avyayāni / hāntāni / 80 \[unit 2573\]

ha0

ahā śoke dhigarthe ca viṣādakaruṇārthayoḥ \|

aho praśne vicāre syādahahādbhutakhedayoḥ \|\| 80 \|\|

## avyayāni / hāntāni / 81 \[unit 2574\]

ahaha syādanuśaye parikleśaprakarṣayoḥ \|

āha kṣepaniyogārthe'pyutāho praśnatarkayoḥ \|\| 81 \|\|

## avyayāni / hāntāni / 82 \[unit 2575\]

sahaśabdastu sākalyayaugapadyasamṛddhiṣu \|

sādṛśye vidyamāne'pi sambandhe'pi saha smṛtam \|\| 82 \|\|

## avyayāni / hāntāni / 83 \[unit 2576\]

ha pādapūraṇe sambodhane hīre tvanavyayam \|

hā viṣāde'pi duḥkhe'pi śoke hāhā tu khedane \|\| 83 \|\|

## avyayāni / hāntāni / 84 \[unit 2577\]

gandharve'navyayaṃ hāhā hi viśeṣe'vadhāraṇe \|

hi pādapūraṇe hetau hī vismayaviṣādayoḥ \|\| 84 \|\|

## avyayāni / hāntāni / 85 \[unit 2578\]

hī harṣe duḥkhahetau ca hīhī vismayahāsyayoḥ \|

hūhū harṣe'pi gandharve gandharve kintvanavyayam \|\| 85 \|\|

## avyayāni / hāntāni / 86 \[unit 2579\]

hehe vyastau samastau ca saṃsmṛtyāmāntrahūtiṣu \|

ho ca hau ca samastau ca sambuddhyādhyānayormatau \|\| 86 \|\|

## avyayāni / hāntāni / 87 \[unit 2580\]

maṅkṣuḥ śīghre bhṛśārthe'pi maṅkṣu tattve'pi kutracit \|\| 87 \|\|

## avyayāni / hāntāni / closing

iti viśvalocane'parāmiyānāyāṃ muktāvalyāmavyayānekārthavargaḥ \|

iti śrīpaṇḍitaśrīśrīdharasenaviracite viśvalocane'parābhidhānāyāṃ muktāvalyāṃ nānārthakāṇḍaḥ samāptaḥ \|

śrī \|\| śrī \|\| vikrama saṃvat 1969 \|\|

samāpto'yaṃ granthaḥ \|
